Saya akan buat sebagai peta dekomposisi praktis untuk aplikasi Jersey/JAX-RS: mulai dari model dependency injection, context lifecycle, configuration, sampai file/resource loading. Saya juga akan pisahkan mana yang native Jersey/HK2, mana yang CDI/Weld, dan mana yang sebaiknya dianggap architectural concern agar tidak tercampur.

Temuan penting yang perlu masuk ke peta: pada Jakarta RESTful Web Services 4.0, `@Context` sudah didepresiasi dan arahnya adalah delegasi injection ke CDI. Jadi untuk onboarding jangka panjang, model CDI/Weld perlu dipahami, bukan hanya HK2/Jersey binding.

# Decomposition Map - Dependency Injection, Contexts, AOP, Profiles, Properties, Env, File Resources in Jersey/JAX-RS

## 1. Introduction

Di aplikasi **Jersey/JAX-RS**, area ini sebaiknya dipahami sebagai **composition layer**: tempat resource REST, service, repository, configuration, context request, interceptor, dan runtime environment disusun menjadi aplikasi yang bisa dijalankan secara konsisten di local, on-prem, Docker, Kubernetes, AWS, atau Azure.

Untuk stack modern berbasis `jakarta.*`, konteks terbarunya penting: Jakarta EE 11 mencakup Jakarta RESTful Web Services 4.0, CDI 4.1, Interceptors 2.2, Dependency Injection 2.0, dan mendukung Java 17 atau lebih tinggi. ([Jakarta EE](https://jakarta.ee/release/11/)) Jersey 4.0.x adalah jalur yang kompatibel dengan Jakarta EE 11 / Jakarta REST 4.0, sementara Jersey 3.x umumnya berada di generasi Jakarta EE 10 / Jakarta REST 3.x; jangan mencampur `javax.*` dan `jakarta.*` dalam satu aplikasi modern kecuali sedang migrasi terkontrol. ([projects.eclipse.org](https://projects.eclipse.org/projects/ee4j.jersey/releases/4.0.0-0?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Poin arsitektural paling penting: **HK2** adalah jalur DI yang sangat dekat dengan Jersey runtime, sedangkan **CDI/Weld** adalah jalur DI standar Jakarta untuk lifecycle, scopes, producer, event, interceptor, dan context model. Jakarta REST 4.0 bahkan sudah menandai `@Context` sebagai deprecated dan mengarah ke CDI untuk pekerjaan injection ke depan. ([Jakarta EE](https://jakarta.ee/specifications/restful-ws/4.0/jakarta-restful-ws-spec-4.0))

## 2. Diagram

```text
Jersey / JAX-RS Application Composition
│
├─ 1. Application Bootstrap
│  ├─ Application / ResourceConfig
│  ├─ package scanning / explicit registration
│  ├─ providers, filters, features, exception mappers
│  └─ application-wide properties
│
├─ 2. Dependency Injection Model
│  ├─ HK2 / Jersey Injection
│  │  ├─ AbstractBinder / Binder
│  │  ├─ Factory / Supplier
│  │  ├─ PerLookup / Singleton / RequestScoped
│  │  └─ custom injectable objects
│  │
│  └─ CDI / Weld
│     ├─ @Inject
│     ├─ @ApplicationScoped / @RequestScoped / @Dependent
│     ├─ @Produces / @Disposes
│     ├─ @Qualifier / @Alternative
│     ├─ events
│     └─ lifecycle callbacks
│
├─ 3. Contexts
│  ├─ HTTP request context
│  ├─ application context
│  ├─ tenant/security/user context
│  ├─ transaction/correlation context
│  └─ process/workflow context, e.g. Camunda correlation
│
├─ 4. AOP / Cross-Cutting
│  ├─ CDI / Jakarta Interceptors
│  │  ├─ business method interception
│  │  ├─ lifecycle interception
│  │  └─ timeout interception, mostly EE/EJB-related
│  │
│  └─ JAX-RS Extension Chain
│     ├─ ContainerRequestFilter
│     ├─ ContainerResponseFilter
│     ├─ ReaderInterceptor / WriterInterceptor
│     ├─ DynamicFeature
│     └─ ExceptionMapper
│
├─ 5. Configuration
│  ├─ application profile
│  ├─ application properties
│  ├─ JVM system properties
│  ├─ environment variables
│  ├─ config files / classpath resources
│  └─ secret / external config source
│
└─ 6. Runtime Deployment
   ├─ local Maven run
   ├─ Docker image
   ├─ Kubernetes ConfigMap / Secret
   ├─ on-prem VM / systemd
   ├─ AWS runtime
   └─ Azure runtime
```

## 3. Tabular Decomposition

| Area | Item / Skill | Fungsi Singkat | Yang Perlu Dikuasai | Common Failure |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Application bootstrap | `Application` / `ResourceConfig` | Titik komposisi utama Jersey: register resource, provider, feature, binder, property. `Application#getProperties()` mengekspos custom application-wide properties ke konfigurasi server-side feature dan komponen JAX-RS. ([Jakarta EE](https://jakarta.ee/specifications/restful-ws/4.0/apidocs/jakarta.ws.rs/jakarta/ws/rs/core/application?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) | Manual registration vs package scanning, urutan registration, explicit config untuk production. | Resource tidak terdeteksi, provider tidak aktif, property tertimpa. |
| Jersey config | `ResourceConfig#property` / `addProperties` | Menaruh configuration key-value pada Jersey runtime. `ResourceConfig` mendukung penambahan properties dan registration component. ([eclipse-ee4j.github.io](https://eclipse-ee4j.github.io/jersey.github.io/apidocs/3.1.7/jersey/org/glassfish/jersey/server/ResourceConfig.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) | Bedakan app config domain vs Jersey server properties. | Semua config dicampur di `ResourceConfig`, sulit dites dan sulit override per environment. |
| HK2 DI | HK2 / Jersey injection path | Cocok untuk binding dependency yang dipakai langsung oleh Jersey-managed components. Jersey custom injection biasanya dilakukan lewat `AbstractBinder`, `Factory`/`Supplier`, lalu didaftarkan ke `ResourceConfig`. ([eclipse-ee4j.github.io](https://eclipse-ee4j.github.io/jersey.github.io/documentation/latest31x/ioc.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) | Binding interface-to-implementation, scope, factory, custom injection. | `UnsatisfiedDependencyException`, dependency tidak terdaftar, singleton memegang request object. |
| HK2 binder | `AbstractBinder` / `Binder` | Menentukan object graph untuk service internal aplikasi. Jersey docs menekankan custom binder harus diregister ke `ResourceConfig`. ([eclipse-ee4j.github.io](https://eclipse-ee4j.github.io/jersey.github.io/documentation/latest31x/ioc.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) | Binding contract, named binding, factory binding, lifecycle scope. | Binding tersebar di banyak tempat tanpa module boundary. |
| HK2 scope | `Singleton`, `RequestScoped`, `PerLookup` | Mengontrol lifetime dependency. Default factory/binder dapat jatuh ke model per lookup bila scope tidak didefinisikan eksplisit. ([eclipse-ee4j.github.io](https://eclipse-ee4j.github.io/jersey.github.io/documentation/latest31x/ioc.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) | Pilih scope berdasarkan statefulness, thread-safety, dan request affinity. | Singleton menyimpan mutable state request/user/tenant. |
| CDI/Weld | CDI standard DI | CDI memberi lifecycle context, typesafe injection, event notification, interceptor/decorator model. ([docs.jboss.org](https://docs.jboss.org/weld/reference/6.0.0.Final/en-US/html_single/?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) | `@Inject`, scopes, producer, qualifier, alternatives, events, lifecycle callback. | Campur HK2 dan CDI tanpa boundary: object ada di container A tapi diinjeksi dari container B. |
| Weld SE / Servlet | Weld integration | Weld bisa berjalan di Java SE, servlet engine seperti Tomcat/Jetty, atau full Jakarta EE server. ([docs.jboss.org](https://docs.jboss.org/weld/reference/6.0.3.Final/en-US/html_single/?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) | Bootstrap CDI container, request scope binding, packaging WAR/JAR. | `@RequestScoped` tidak aktif karena request context tidak dibuka/ditutup dengan benar. |
| Jersey + CDI | CDI request scope binding | Jersey CDI integration bergantung pada CDI/Servlet integration dan request scope binding yang benar; tanpa itu, CDI request-scoped bean tidak dapat dipakai dengan benar di Jersey app. ([eclipse-ee4j.github.io](https://eclipse-ee4j.github.io/jersey.github.io/documentation/latest/cdi.support.html)) | Servlet deployment, CDI bootstrap, request scope lifecycle. | Bean request-scoped bocor, context not active, lifecycle callback tidak terpanggil. |
| Jakarta REST context | `@Context` | Legacy/context injection untuk object JAX-RS seperti `UriInfo`, `HttpHeaders`, `SecurityContext`. Namun Jakarta REST 4.0 menandai `@Context` deprecated dan arah masa depannya adalah CDI. ([Jakarta EE](https://jakarta.ee/specifications/restful-ws/4.0/jakarta-restful-ws-spec-4.0)) | Tahu kapan masih aman memakai `@Context`, dan kapan lebih baik wrap ke service/context object. | Terlalu banyak resource bergantung langsung pada request internals. |
| Context modeling | Request/user/tenant/correlation context | Membawa metadata teknis lintas layer: `requestId`, `correlationId`, tenant, actor, locale, auth principal. | Immutable context object, propagation, logging MDC, audit trace. | Context disimpan di static/thread-local tanpa cleanup, bocor antar request/thread. |
| AOP - business layer | Jakarta Interceptors / CDI interceptors | Jakarta Interceptors mendefinisikan interception untuk business method invocation, lifecycle event, dan timeout event. ([Jakarta EE](https://jakarta.ee/specifications/interceptors/2.2/jakarta-interceptors-spec-2.2?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) | `@InterceptorBinding`, `@AroundInvoke`, ordering, idempotency. | Dipakai untuk logic bisnis utama, bukan cross-cutting concern. |
| AOP - HTTP layer | JAX-RS filters/interceptors | Untuk cross-cutting di boundary HTTP: auth, request logging, header normalization, response header, entity read/write transformation. | Request filter vs response filter vs reader/writer interceptor vs exception mapper. | Menganggap JAX-RS filter sama dengan CDI AOP; efeknya salah layer. |
| Dynamic extension | `DynamicFeature` | Register filter/interceptor secara kondisional berdasarkan resource method/class. | Annotation-driven HTTP behavior, selective auth/logging/rate limit. | Semua endpoint terkena filter yang seharusnya hanya untuk sebagian endpoint. |
| Exception policy | `ExceptionMapper` | Menerjemahkan exception domain/infra menjadi HTTP response yang konsisten. | Error taxonomy, status mapping, correlation ID, audit-safe message. | Stack trace bocor ke client, status HTTP tidak konsisten. |
| Application profile | `local/dev/test/staging/prod` | Jersey/Jakarta tidak punya “profile” standar seperti Spring profile; biasanya dibuat sebagai convention melalui env var, JVM property, config file, atau external config. | Profile resolution order, immutable config, explicit fail-fast. | Profile default ke dev di production. |
| Application properties | Typed config object | Bentuk aman dari raw string config: DB URL, pool size, timeout, feature flag, external endpoint. | Validation saat startup, default eksplisit, masking secret. | Config dibaca langsung dari `System.getenv()` di banyak class. |
| JVM system properties | `System.getProperty` | Membaca property JVM, biasanya dari `-Dkey=value`. Java API menyediakan `System#getProperty`. ([Oracle Docs](https://docs.oracle.com/en/java/javase/17/docs/api/java.base/java/lang/System.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) | Precedence terhadap env/config file, naming convention. | Runtime behavior berubah diam-diam karena `-D` berbeda antar environment. |
| Environment variables | `System.getenv` | Membaca environment variable dari OS/container. Java API menyediakan akses environment melalui `System#getenv`. ([Oracle Docs](https://docs.oracle.com/javase/8/docs/api/java/lang/System.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) | Docker/Kubernetes env injection, naming uppercase, secret handling. | Env var hilang/typo menyebabkan fallback tidak aman. |
| File resources | Classpath resources | Resource seperti YAML/properties/templates/static metadata dibaca dari classpath memakai `Class#getResource` / `getResourceAsStream`. ([Oracle Docs](https://docs.oracle.com/en/java/javase/17/docs/api/java.base/java/lang/Class.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) | Classpath path convention, packaging JAR/WAR, resource filtering Maven. | File ada di source tree tapi tidak masuk artifact runtime. |
| External files | Mounted config/resource | File dari volume Docker/Kubernetes/on-prem path, bukan classpath. | Path config, read-only mount, reload policy, permission. | Aplikasi bergantung pada absolute path lokal developer. |
| Secrets | Secret source | Password/token/key harus datang dari secret manager, Kubernetes Secret, vault, atau injected env/file; jangan hardcode. | Secret rotation, masking logs, least privilege. | Secret masuk Git, logs, Docker image layer. |
| Dependency compatibility | Jersey/HK2/CDI/Jakarta alignment | Untuk Jakarta EE 11/Jakarta REST 4.0, gunakan dependency yang memang sejalan; Jersey 4.0.x dinyatakan kompatibel dengan Jakarta EE 11/Jakarta REST 4.0. ([projects.eclipse.org](https://projects.eclipse.org/projects/ee4j.jersey/releases/4.0.0-0?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) | BOM, Maven dependency tree, no mixed `javax.*`/`jakarta.*`. | `ClassNotFoundException`, `NoSuchMethodError`, duplicate API jars. |
| Testability | Container-lite tests | Pisahkan config resolution, binder module, service layer, resource layer agar bisa dites tanpa full runtime. | Unit test config, JerseyTest/resource test, integration test with Testcontainers. | Semua test harus start full stack, lambat dan flaky. |

---

## 4. Mental Model yang Direkomendasikan

### HK2 vs CDI/Weld

| Pertanyaan | HK2 / Jersey Injection | CDI / Weld |
|---|---|---|
| Posisi | Dekat dengan Jersey runtime. | Standar Jakarta untuk DI/context lifecycle. |
| Cocok untuk | Binding dependency yang spesifik ke Jersey resource/provider. | Service application, domain service, producer, interceptor, event, lifecycle. |
| Scope thinking | `Singleton`, `RequestScoped`, `PerLookup`. | `@ApplicationScoped`, `@RequestScoped`, `@Dependent`, custom context. |
| Risiko utama | Terlalu Jersey-specific. | Integrasi request scope salah jika berjalan di servlet/SE tanpa bootstrap benar. |
| Rekomendasi | Gunakan untuk Jersey composition layer. | Gunakan sebagai default mental model jangka panjang bila aplikasi ingin Jakarta-standard. |

### AOP vs JAX-RS Filter

| Concern | Gunakan |
|---|---|
| Logging HTTP request/response | `ContainerRequestFilter` / `ContainerResponseFilter` |
| Auth header parsing | Request filter |
| Response security headers | Response filter |
| JSON/entity transformation | `ReaderInterceptor` / `WriterInterceptor` |
| Business audit annotation di service method | CDI / Jakarta Interceptor |
| Transaction boundary di service method | CDI interceptor / framework transaction layer |
| Error-to-HTTP mapping | `ExceptionMapper` |

Rule praktis: **JAX-RS filter bekerja di HTTP boundary; CDI interceptor bekerja di method/business boundary.**

---

## 5. Roadmap Alur Belajar Singkat

| Urutan | Fokus | Output yang Harus Bisa Dibuat |
|---:|---|---|
| 1 | `Application` / `ResourceConfig` | Satu bootstrap class yang eksplisit register resource, provider, feature, binder. |
| 2 | HK2 binding | Interface service bisa diinjeksi ke resource melalui binder. |
| 3 | Scope & lifecycle | Bisa menjelaskan kapan object dibuat/dibuang: singleton, request, per-lookup. |
| 4 | CDI/Weld | Bisa bootstrap CDI dan memakai `@Inject`, `@ApplicationScoped`, `@RequestScoped`, producer. |
| 5 | Context propagation | Bisa membuat `RequestContext` / `UserContext` / `CorrelationContext` aman per request. |
| 6 | Cross-cutting | Bisa memilih antara CDI interceptor, JAX-RS filter, reader/writer interceptor, exception mapper. |
| 7 | Configuration | Bisa membuat config resolution order: env → JVM property → file → default, dengan validation fail-fast. |
| 8 | Runtime hardening | Bisa menjalankan config yang sama di local, Docker, Kubernetes, on-prem, AWS/Azure tanpa hardcode path/secret. |

## Response:
7/12/2026, 11:44:28 PM

Saya mulai dari pola implementasi, bukan teori ulang. Fokusnya: bagaimana menyusun DI module, scope, config, request context, dan extension point agar aman untuk aplikasi Jersey/JAX-RS production.

# Cheatsheet - Jersey/JAX-RS DI Implementation Patterns

## 1. Scope

Cheatsheet ini fokus pada **cara implementasi dependency injection dan composition layer** di aplikasi Jersey/JAX-RS production:

```text
ResourceConfig / Application
    ↓
DI modules: HK2 atau CDI/Weld
    ↓
Service / Repository / Config / Context
    ↓
JAX-RS resources, filters, interceptors, exception mappers
```

Catatan versi penting: **Jersey 4.0.x adalah jalur Jakarta EE 11 / Jakarta REST 4.0**, tetapi ada **API breakage** dan perubahan terkait injection-related classes, termasuk perpindahan beberapa binding/injection class ke package terkait HK2. Jadi import dan artifact harus dicek terhadap versi Jersey aktual di project. ([projects.eclipse.org](https://projects.eclipse.org/projects/ee4j.jersey/releases/4.0.0-0))

---

## 2. Mental Model Utama

| Layer | Tanggung Jawab | Contoh |
|---|---|---|
| **JAX-RS boundary** | HTTP mapping, request/response, provider, filter | `@Path`, `ContainerRequestFilter`, `ExceptionMapper` |
| **Composition root** | Register resource, provider, binder, feature, property | `ResourceConfig` |
| **DI container** | Membuat, menyambung, dan mengelola lifecycle object | HK2 binder, CDI/Weld beans |
| **Application runtime config** | Profile, properties, env vars, mounted file, secrets | `AppConfig`, `DbConfig`, `FeatureFlags` |
| **Context model** | Metadata per request / user / tenant / correlation | `RequestContext`, `SecurityContext`, MDC |

Rule senior-level: **Jangan jadikan resource JAX-RS sebagai tempat komposisi object graph.** Resource sebaiknya tipis: validasi input, panggil service, map response.

---

## 3. HK2 vs CDI/Weld: Kapan Dipakai?

| Kebutuhan | Pilihan Lebih Natural | Alasan |
|---|---|---|
| Binding service sederhana ke Jersey resource | HK2 | Jersey historically memakai HK2 untuk lifecycle dan DI sejak Jersey 2.0. ([eclipse-ee4j.github.io](https://eclipse-ee4j.github.io/jersey.github.io/documentation/latest31x/ioc.html)) |
| Custom injectable object untuk Jersey component | HK2 / Jersey binder | Jersey docs mendokumentasikan pattern `Factory`/`Supplier` + binder + register ke `ResourceConfig`. ([eclipse-ee4j.github.io](https://eclipse-ee4j.github.io/jersey.github.io/documentation/latest31x/ioc.html)) |
| Standard Jakarta DI, scopes, producers, interceptors, events | CDI/Weld | CDI menyediakan scoping, typesafe resolution, lifecycle callbacks, interception, dan event notification. ([Jakarta EE](https://jakarta.ee/specifications/cdi/4.1/jakarta-cdi-spec-4.1)) |
| Arah modern Jakarta REST 4.0+ | CDI/Weld | Jakarta EE 11 mencatat `@Context` deprecated di Jakarta RESTful Web Services 4.0; arah injection makin condong ke CDI. ([Jakarta EE](https://jakarta.ee/release/11/)) |
| Servlet container tanpa full Jakarta EE server | CDI/Weld butuh setup eksplisit | Jersey CDI integration memerlukan CDI/Servlet integration dan request scope binding yang benar. ([eclipse-ee4j.github.io](https://eclipse-ee4j.github.io/jersey.github.io/documentation/latest31x/cdi.support.html)) |

---

## 4. Recommended Architecture

```text
com.company.app
│
├─ api
│  ├─ CustomerResource.java
│  ├─ OrderResource.java
│  ├─ ErrorMapper.java
│  ├─ AuthFilter.java
│  └─ CorrelationIdFilter.java
│
├─ bootstrap
│  ├─ AppResourceConfig.java
│  ├─ Hk2Module.java
│  ├─ CdiBootstrap.java          optional
│  └─ RuntimeProfile.java
│
├─ config
│  ├─ AppConfig.java
│  ├─ DbConfig.java
│  ├─ HttpClientConfig.java
│  └─ ConfigLoader.java
│
├─ context
│  ├─ RequestContext.java
│  ├─ RequestContextFactory.java
│  └─ CorrelationId.java
│
├─ service
│  ├─ CustomerService.java
│  └─ OrderService.java
│
├─ repository
│  ├─ CustomerRepository.java
│  └─ OrderRepository.java
│
└─ infrastructure
   ├─ DataSourceFactory.java
   ├─ JsonFactory.java
   └─ ExternalClientFactory.java
```

Prinsipnya: **bootstrap menyusun dependency**, **resource memakai dependency**, **service menjalankan use case**, **repository mengakses data**, dan **context/config tidak dibaca sembarang dari seluruh layer**.

---

## 5. Pattern 1 - `ResourceConfig` sebagai Composition Root

```java
public final class AppResourceConfig extends ResourceConfig {

    public AppResourceConfig() {
        packages("com.company.app.api");

        register(new Hk2Module());

        register(CorrelationIdFilter.class);
        register(AuthFilter.class);
        register(ErrorMapper.class);

        property("app.name", "order-service");
    }
}
```

`ResourceConfig` cocok dijadikan titik eksplisit untuk register resource, provider, feature, dan binder. Dalam Jersey custom injection, binder/supplier/factory harus didaftarkan ke `ResourceConfig` agar aktif di runtime. ([eclipse-ee4j.github.io](https://eclipse-ee4j.github.io/jersey.github.io/documentation/latest31x/ioc.html))

**Senior rule:** jangan terlalu mengandalkan package scanning untuk production-critical component. Untuk filter, mapper, security, dan binder, lebih aman register eksplisit agar startup behavior mudah diaudit.

---

## 6. Pattern 2 - HK2 Binder untuk Service Wiring

```java
public final class Hk2Module extends AbstractBinder {

    @Override
    protected void configure() {
        bind(CustomerServiceImpl.class)
            .to(CustomerService.class)
            .in(Singleton.class);

        bind(CustomerRepositoryPostgres.class)
            .to(CustomerRepository.class)
            .in(Singleton.class);

        bindFactory(AppConfigFactory.class)
            .to(AppConfig.class)
            .in(Singleton.class);

        bindFactory(RequestContextFactory.class)
            .to(RequestContext.class)
            .in(RequestScoped.class);
    }
}
```

Pattern HK2 yang paling umum adalah: **bind implementation ke interface**, atau **bind factory/supplier ke type yang ingin diinjeksi**. Jersey docs juga menyebut default scope factory/binder dapat jatuh ke `PerLookup` bila scope tidak didefinisikan eksplisit, jadi scope harus disengaja untuk object penting. ([eclipse-ee4j.github.io](https://eclipse-ee4j.github.io/jersey.github.io/documentation/latest31x/ioc.html))

### Scope decision

| Object | Scope Disarankan | Alasan |
|---|---|---|
| Stateless service | Singleton | Aman jika tidak menyimpan mutable request state. |
| Repository wrapper | Singleton | Biasanya thread-safe bila hanya memegang datasource/client pool. |
| Config object immutable | Singleton | Dibaca banyak komponen, dibuat sekali saat startup. |
| Request/user/tenant context | RequestScoped | Metadata harus unik per HTTP request. |
| Mapper stateless | Singleton | Tidak perlu dibuat per request. |
| Builder mutable | PerLookup / local variable | Jangan dishare antar thread. |

---

## 7. Pattern 3 - CDI/Weld untuk Jakarta-Standard DI

```java
@ApplicationScoped
public class CustomerServiceImpl implements CustomerService {

    @Inject
    CustomerRepository repository;

    public CustomerDetail getCustomer(String id) {
        return repository.findById(id);
    }
}
```

CDI memberi model standar: bean punya type, qualifier, scope, optional name, interceptor bindings, dan implementasi; container menyediakan lifecycle, injection, scoped resolution, lifecycle callbacks, method interception, dan event notification. ([Jakarta EE](https://jakarta.ee/specifications/cdi/4.1/jakarta-cdi-spec-4.1))

### CDI producer untuk config

```java
@ApplicationScoped
public class ConfigProducer {

    @Produces
    @ApplicationScoped
    public AppConfig appConfig() {
        return ConfigLoader.loadAndValidate();
    }
}
```

Gunakan producer ketika object tidak natural dibuat via constructor biasa, misalnya `DataSource`, HTTP client, config object, serializer, atau client ke service eksternal.

### CDI qualifier untuk multiple implementation

```java
@Qualifier
@Retention(RUNTIME)
@Target({ FIELD, PARAMETER, METHOD, TYPE })
public @interface PrimaryDatabase {}
```

```java
@Produces
@PrimaryDatabase
@ApplicationScoped
public DataSource primaryDataSource(AppConfig config) {
    return DataSourceFactory.create(config.database());
}
```

Qualifiers penting ketika ada lebih dari satu implementasi dengan type sama, misalnya primary vs replica datasource, internal vs external client, mock vs real adapter.

---

## 8. Pattern 4 - Request Context yang Aman

Jangan menyebarkan `HttpServletRequest`, `HttpHeaders`, atau `SecurityContext` ke semua service. Buat context object internal.

```java
public record RequestContext(
    String correlationId,
    String requestId,
    String actorId,
    String tenantId,
    String sourceIp
) {}
```

```java
public final class RequestContextFactory implements Supplier<RequestContext> {

    @Inject
    private HttpHeaders headers;

    @Inject
    private SecurityContext securityContext;

    @Override
    public RequestContext get() {
        return new RequestContext(
            firstHeader("X-Correlation-Id"),
            firstHeader("X-Request-Id"),
            securityContext.getUserPrincipal().getName(),
            firstHeader("X-Tenant-Id"),
            firstHeader("X-Forwarded-For")
        );
    }

    private String firstHeader(String name) {
        return headers.getHeaderString(name);
    }
}
```

Di Jakarta REST/Jersey modern, jangan mendesain code baru yang bergantung terlalu dalam pada `@Context`; Jakarta EE 11 sudah mencatat `@Context` deprecated di Jakarta RESTful Web Services 4.0. Gunakan wrapper internal seperti `RequestContext` agar migrasi ke CDI-style injection lebih mudah. ([Jakarta EE](https://jakarta.ee/release/11/))

---

## 9. Pattern 5 - Configuration Resolution

### Recommended precedence

```text
1. Explicit runtime override
2. Environment variable
3. JVM system property
4. External mounted file
5. Classpath default file
6. Safe hardcoded default, only for non-sensitive values
```

Java menyediakan `System.getProperty(...)` untuk JVM system property dan `System.getenv(...)` untuk environment variable. Oracle Java docs juga menekankan env var lebih global dan dapat memiliki efek samping lintas proses, sehingga system property sering lebih cocok ketika informasi hanya ditujukan ke subprocess Java. ([Oracle Docs](https://docs.oracle.com/en/java/javase/17/docs/api/java.base/java/lang/System.html))

```java
public final class ConfigLoader {

    public static AppConfig loadAndValidate() {
        String profile = read("APP_PROFILE", "app.profile", "local");

        DbConfig db = new DbConfig(
            required("DB_URL", "db.url"),
            required("DB_USERNAME", "db.username"),
            required("DB_PASSWORD", "db.password")
        );

        AppConfig config = new AppConfig(profile, db);
        config.validate();

        return config;
    }

    private static String read(String envKey, String sysPropKey, String defaultValue) {
        String env = System.getenv(envKey);
        if (env != null && !env.isBlank()) return env;

        String prop = System.getProperty(sysPropKey);
        if (prop != null && !prop.isBlank()) return prop;

        return defaultValue;
    }

    private static String required(String envKey, String sysPropKey) {
        String value = read(envKey, sysPropKey, null);
        if (value == null || value.isBlank()) {
            throw new IllegalStateException(
                "Missing required config: " + envKey + " / " + sysPropKey
            );
        }
        return value;
    }
}
```

**Senior rule:** config harus **fail-fast saat startup**, bukan gagal saat endpoint pertama dipanggil.

---

## 10. Pattern 6 - File / Classpath Resource Loading

Untuk default config, template, metadata, atau schema yang ikut ter-package dalam JAR/WAR, gunakan classpath resource.

```java
public final class ResourceLoader {

    public static String readClasspathResource(String path) {
        try (InputStream in = ResourceLoader.class.getResourceAsStream(path)) {
            if (in == null) {
                throw new IllegalArgumentException("Resource not found: " + path);
            }
            return new String(in.readAllBytes(), StandardCharsets.UTF_8);
        } catch (IOException e) {
            throw new UncheckedIOException(e);
        }
    }
}
```

`Class#getResource` dan `getResourceAsStream` mencari resource berdasarkan nama; bila path diawali `/`, nama absolut dibentuk dari path setelah `/`, sedangkan path relatif dihitung dari package class tersebut. Method ini bisa mengembalikan `null` bila resource tidak ditemukan atau tidak terbuka dari module. ([Oracle Docs](https://docs.oracle.com/en/java/javase/17/docs/api/java.base/java/lang/Class.html))

### Praktik aman

| Resource Type | Lokasi | Catatan |
|---|---|---|
| Default app config | classpath | Aman untuk default non-secret. |
| Secret | external secret source | Jangan masuk JAR, Git, atau Docker image layer. |
| Large file | mounted volume/object storage | Jangan dibaca penuh ke memory jika besar. |
| Runtime override | mounted file/env | Cocok untuk Kubernetes ConfigMap/Secret. |
| SQL migration | migration tool folder | Lebih baik dikelola Flyway/Liquibase, bukan custom loader. |

---

## 11. Pattern 7 - AOP vs JAX-RS Extension Point

CDI mendukung interceptor untuk memisahkan cross-cutting concern dari business logic; CDI Lite mendukung forms seperti interceptor class dengan interceptor binding, `@AroundInvoke`, lifecycle callback, dan ordering dengan `@Priority`. ([Jakarta EE](https://jakarta.ee/specifications/cdi/4.1/jakarta-cdi-spec-4.1))

| Concern | Gunakan | Jangan Gunakan |
|---|---|---|
| Correlation ID header | `ContainerRequestFilter` | CDI interceptor |
| Auth token extraction | Request filter | Service method interceptor |
| Response security header | `ContainerResponseFilter` | Resource method manual |
| Exception to HTTP response | `ExceptionMapper` | `try/catch` di setiap resource |
| Audit business operation | CDI interceptor | HTTP filter saja |
| Transaction boundary | CDI/interceptor/framework tx | Manual begin/commit di resource |
| JSON body transform | `ReaderInterceptor` / `WriterInterceptor` | Business service |

### Example - request filter

```java
@Provider
@Priority(Priorities.AUTHENTICATION)
public final class CorrelationIdFilter implements ContainerRequestFilter {

    @Override
    public void filter(ContainerRequestContext requestContext) {
        String correlationId = requestContext.getHeaderString("X-Correlation-Id");

        if (correlationId == null || correlationId.isBlank()) {
            correlationId = UUID.randomUUID().toString();
        }

        requestContext.setProperty("correlationId", correlationId);
    }
}
```

### Example - business interceptor

```java
@InterceptorBinding
@Retention(RUNTIME)
@Target({ TYPE, METHOD })
public @interface Audited {}
```

```java
@Audited
@Interceptor
@Priority(Interceptor.Priority.APPLICATION)
public class AuditInterceptor {

    @AroundInvoke
    public Object around(InvocationContext ctx) throws Exception {
        long start = System.nanoTime();
        try {
            return ctx.proceed();
        } finally {
            long elapsed = System.nanoTime() - start;
            // write audit event without leaking sensitive data
        }
    }
}
```

---

## 12. Failure Modes yang Harus Cepat Dikenali

| Symptom | Kemungkinan Penyebab | Cara Investigasi |
|---|---|---|
| `UnsatisfiedDependencyException` | Binding tidak terdaftar / CDI bean tidak ditemukan | Cek `ResourceConfig`, binder, `beans.xml`, package scanning. |
| `Ambiguous dependency` | Ada lebih dari satu bean kandidat | Tambahkan qualifier atau pilih implementation eksplisit. |
| Request data bocor antar user | Request context disimpan di singleton/static/thread-local tanpa cleanup | Audit stateful field di singleton. |
| Config berubah tapi app tidak ikut berubah | Config dibaca sekali saat startup | Pastikan memang intended; untuk dynamic config butuh reload strategy. |
| Local jalan, container gagal | Resource file tidak ikut masuk artifact / path absolute | Cek `target/classes`, Docker image, working directory. |
| CDI `@RequestScoped` error | Request scope tidak aktif | Cek CDI/Servlet integration dan request scope binding. Jersey CDI integration bergantung pada request scope binding yang benar. ([eclipse-ee4j.github.io](https://eclipse-ee4j.github.io/jersey.github.io/documentation/latest31x/cdi.support.html)) |
| `NoSuchMethodError` / `ClassNotFoundException` | Campur versi Jersey/Jakarta/HK2/CDI | Jalankan `mvn dependency:tree`, cek BOM, cek `javax.*` vs `jakarta.*`. |
| Filter tidak jalan | Tidak diregister / salah package scanning / salah priority | Register eksplisit di `ResourceConfig`. |
| Interceptor tidak aktif | Binding annotation salah / belum enable / bean bukan CDI-managed | Pastikan target class dikelola CDI. |
| Secret muncul di log | Config object di-`toString()` penuh | Masking field secret dan jangan log raw config. |

---

## 13. Production Checklist

| Checklist | Target |
|---|---|
| Semua dependency critical diregister eksplisit | Resource, provider, mapper, filter, binder jelas. |
| Scope setiap binding disengaja | Tidak ada mutable request state di singleton. |
| Config immutable dan tervalidasi saat startup | Fail-fast, bukan fail-on-first-request. |
| Secret tidak ada di source code, artifact, atau log | Env/secret manager/mounted secret. |
| Request context immutable | Correlation, actor, tenant, request id jelas. |
| `@Context` tidak menyebar ke service layer | Dibungkus di boundary/factory. |
| HK2 dan CDI tidak dicampur sembarang | Ada boundary jelas: mana Jersey-managed, mana CDI-managed. |
| Dependency tree bersih | Tidak ada campuran `javax.*` dan `jakarta.*`. |
| Tests punya config profile sendiri | Tidak bergantung env lokal developer. |
| Startup log menampilkan profile non-secret | Mudah audit runtime environment. |

---

## 14. Roadmap Belajar Singkat

| Urutan | Fokus | Target Praktik |
|---:|---|---|
| 1 | `ResourceConfig` | Bisa membuat bootstrap eksplisit tanpa magic scanning berlebihan. |
| 2 | HK2 binder | Bisa inject service/repository/config ke resource. |
| 3 | Scope correctness | Bisa membedakan singleton, request, per-lookup, dependent. |
| 4 | Config loader | Bisa load env/sysprop/file dan validate saat startup. |
| 5 | Request context | Bisa membuat context immutable per request. |
| 6 | CDI/Weld | Bisa memakai `@Inject`, producer, qualifier, scope. |
| 7 | Cross-cutting | Bisa memilih filter, interceptor, exception mapper dengan tepat. |
| 8 | Failure diagnosis | Bisa debug injection/config/scope issue dari stack trace dan dependency tree. |

---

Lanjut ke **cheatsheet: “Jersey/JAX-RS Configuration Architecture - application profile, env var, JVM property, external file, Kubernetes ConfigMap/Secret, and fail-fast validation.”**

- [Jakarta Contexts and Dependency Injection](https://jakarta.ee/specifications/cdi/4.1/jakarta-cdi-spec-4.1)

## Response:
7/13/2026, 12:00:40 AM

# Cheatsheet - Jersey/JAX-RS Configuration Architecture

## 1. Scope

Cheatsheet ini membahas cara mendesain **configuration architecture** untuk aplikasi Jersey/JAX-RS:

```text id="0wdtpi"
Application Profile
    ↓
Configuration Resolution
    ↓
Typed Config Object
    ↓
Validation at Startup
    ↓
Injection into Services / Repositories / Clients
    ↓
Runtime Deployment: local, Docker, Kubernetes, on-prem, AWS, Azure
```

Target akhirnya: aplikasi bisa jalan di berbagai environment tanpa hardcode, tanpa config tersebar, tanpa secret bocor, dan tanpa behavior yang berubah diam-diam.

---

## 2. Prinsip Utama

| Prinsip | Penjelasan |
|---|---|
| **Configuration is runtime contract** | Config adalah kontrak antara aplikasi dan environment. Jangan dianggap sekadar file `.properties`. |
| **Fail fast** | Config wajib divalidasi saat startup, bukan saat endpoint pertama dipanggil. |
| **Typed config** | Jangan oper raw `String` dari env var ke seluruh layer. Ubah menjadi object seperti `DbConfig`, `HttpClientConfig`, `SecurityConfig`. |
| **No secret in artifact** | Secret tidak boleh masuk Git, JAR/WAR, Docker image, atau log. |
| **Explicit precedence** | Urutan sumber config harus jelas dan stabil. |
| **Profile is not magic** | Jersey/JAX-RS tidak punya konsep profile standar seperti Spring profile; profile perlu dibuat sendiri atau lewat config library. |

---

## 3. Configuration Source Map

```text id="zwgejp"
Configuration Sources
│
├─ 1. Command / Runtime Override
│  ├─ CLI argument
│  ├─ explicit config path
│  └─ deployment override
│
├─ 2. Environment Variables
│  ├─ APP_PROFILE
│  ├─ DB_URL
│  ├─ DB_USERNAME
│  ├─ DB_PASSWORD
│  └─ EXTERNAL_API_BASE_URL
│
├─ 3. JVM System Properties
│  ├─ -Dapp.profile=prod
│  ├─ -Ddb.url=...
│  └─ -Dconfig.file=/etc/app/application.properties
│
├─ 4. External Mounted Files
│  ├─ /etc/app/application.properties
│  ├─ /etc/app/secrets/db-password
│  └─ /etc/app/features.yaml
│
├─ 5. Classpath Resources
│  ├─ application-default.properties
│  ├─ error-codes.json
│  └─ templates/*.json
│
└─ 6. Safe Defaults
   ├─ non-sensitive timeout default
   ├─ non-sensitive retry count
   └─ local-only fallback
```

Java menyediakan `System.getenv()` untuk environment variable dan `System.getProperty()` untuk JVM system property. Environment variable berasal dari environment proses, sedangkan system property berada di dalam runtime JVM dan sering dipass lewat `-Dkey=value`. ([Oracle Java 17 System API](https://docs.oracle.com/en/java/javase/17/docs/api/java.base/java/lang/System.html))

---

## 4. Recommended Precedence

Gunakan urutan yang konsisten:

```text id="5kwgnx"
1. Explicit runtime override
2. Environment variable
3. JVM system property
4. External mounted config file
5. Classpath default config
6. Hardcoded safe default
```

Kenapa env var biasanya lebih tinggi daripada file? Karena di Docker/Kubernetes/cloud runtime, env var sering dipakai sebagai deployment override yang paling eksplisit. Kubernetes juga mendukung ConfigMap sebagai env var atau file volume, sedangkan Secret juga bisa diekspos sebagai env var atau mounted volume. ([Kubernetes ConfigMap docs](https://kubernetes.io/docs/concepts/configuration/configmap/), [Kubernetes Secret docs](https://kubernetes.io/docs/concepts/configuration/secret/))

---

## 5. Config Domain Model

Jangan punya satu `Map<String, String>` global. Bentuk config menjadi object domain.

```java id="zry7sf"
public record AppConfig(
    String profile,
    ServerConfig server,
    DbConfig database,
    HttpClientConfig httpClient,
    SecurityConfig security,
    FeatureFlags featureFlags
) {
    public void validate() {
        requireNonBlank(profile, "profile");
        server.validate();
        database.validate();
        httpClient.validate();
        security.validate();
        featureFlags.validate();
    }

    private static void requireNonBlank(String value, String name) {
        if (value == null || value.isBlank()) {
            throw new IllegalStateException("Missing config: " + name);
        }
    }
}
```

```java id="hf28ri"
public record DbConfig(
    String url,
    String username,
    String password,
    int maxPoolSize,
    Duration connectionTimeout
) {
    public void validate() {
        requireNonBlank(url, "database.url");
        requireNonBlank(username, "database.username");
        requireNonBlank(password, "database.password");

        if (maxPoolSize <= 0) {
            throw new IllegalStateException("database.maxPoolSize must be > 0");
        }

        if (connectionTimeout == null || connectionTimeout.isNegative() || connectionTimeout.isZero()) {
            throw new IllegalStateException("database.connectionTimeout must be positive");
        }
    }

    private static void requireNonBlank(String value, String name) {
        if (value == null || value.isBlank()) {
            throw new IllegalStateException("Missing config: " + name);
        }
    }
}
```

**Rule:** config object sebaiknya immutable. Di Java modern, `record` cocok untuk config value object.

---

## 6. Profile Model

Karena Jersey/JAX-RS tidak menyediakan profile standard, buat enum sendiri.

```java id="0b6xwz"
public enum RuntimeProfile {
    LOCAL,
    DEV,
    TEST,
    STAGING,
    PROD;

    public static RuntimeProfile parse(String value) {
        if (value == null || value.isBlank()) {
            return LOCAL;
        }

        return RuntimeProfile.valueOf(value.trim().toUpperCase(Locale.ROOT));
    }

    public boolean isProductionLike() {
        return this == STAGING || this == PROD;
    }
}
```

### Profile policy

| Profile | Tujuan | Boleh Default? | Catatan |
|---|---|---:|---|
| `LOCAL` | Developer machine | Ya | Boleh pakai local DB/container. |
| `DEV` | Shared development | Terbatas | Jangan pakai default secret. |
| `TEST` | Automated test | Ya | Biasanya isolated DB/Testcontainers. |
| `STAGING` | Pre-prod | Tidak | Harus mirip production. |
| `PROD` | Production | Tidak | Semua config critical wajib eksplisit. |

Anti-pattern paling bahaya: **production jatuh ke local default karena `APP_PROFILE` kosong.**

---

## 7. Config Loader Pattern

```java id="fmu72f"
public final class ConfigLoader {

    public static AppConfig load() {
        ConfigSource source = ConfigSource.composite(
            ConfigSource.environment(),
            ConfigSource.systemProperties(),
            ConfigSource.externalPropertiesFile(optional("CONFIG_FILE")),
            ConfigSource.classpathProperties("/application-default.properties")
        );

        RuntimeProfile profile = RuntimeProfile.parse(
            source.get("APP_PROFILE", "app.profile", "LOCAL")
        );

        AppConfig config = new AppConfig(
            profile.name(),
            loadServer(source),
            loadDatabase(source),
            loadHttpClient(source),
            loadSecurity(source),
            loadFeatureFlags(source)
        );

        config.validate();
        return config;
    }

    private static DbConfig loadDatabase(ConfigSource source) {
        return new DbConfig(
            source.required("DB_URL", "db.url"),
            source.required("DB_USERNAME", "db.username"),
            source.required("DB_PASSWORD", "db.password"),
            source.intValue("DB_MAX_POOL_SIZE", "db.maxPoolSize", 10),
            source.duration("DB_CONNECTION_TIMEOUT", "db.connectionTimeout", Duration.ofSeconds(5))
        );
    }

    private static Optional<String> optional(String envKey) {
        String value = System.getenv(envKey);
        return value == null || value.isBlank() ? Optional.empty() : Optional.of(value);
    }
}
```

---

## 8. ConfigSource Abstraction

Buat abstraction kecil agar source config tidak bocor ke business code.

```java id="63xjqp"
public interface ConfigSource {

    Optional<String> find(String envKey, String propertyKey);

    default String get(String envKey, String propertyKey, String defaultValue) {
        return find(envKey, propertyKey).orElse(defaultValue);
    }

    default String required(String envKey, String propertyKey) {
        return find(envKey, propertyKey)
            .filter(value -> !value.isBlank())
            .orElseThrow(() -> new IllegalStateException(
                "Missing required config: " + envKey + " / " + propertyKey
            ));
    }

    default int intValue(String envKey, String propertyKey, int defaultValue) {
        return find(envKey, propertyKey)
            .map(Integer::parseInt)
            .orElse(defaultValue);
    }

    default Duration duration(String envKey, String propertyKey, Duration defaultValue) {
        return find(envKey, propertyKey)
            .map(Duration::parse)
            .orElse(defaultValue);
    }
}
```

```java id="6j403f"
public final class EnvironmentConfigSource implements ConfigSource {

    @Override
    public Optional<String> find(String envKey, String propertyKey) {
        return Optional.ofNullable(System.getenv(envKey));
    }
}
```

```java id="fyjxla"
public final class SystemPropertyConfigSource implements ConfigSource {

    @Override
    public Optional<String> find(String envKey, String propertyKey) {
        return Optional.ofNullable(System.getProperty(propertyKey));
    }
}
```

```java id="0dpzff"
public final class CompositeConfigSource implements ConfigSource {

    private final List<ConfigSource> sources;

    public CompositeConfigSource(List<ConfigSource> sources) {
        this.sources = List.copyOf(sources);
    }

    @Override
    public Optional<String> find(String envKey, String propertyKey) {
        for (ConfigSource source : sources) {
            Optional<String> value = source.find(envKey, propertyKey);
            if (value.isPresent() && !value.get().isBlank()) {
                return value;
            }
        }
        return Optional.empty();
    }
}
```

Dengan pola ini, service tidak pernah tahu config berasal dari env var, file, JVM property, Kubernetes ConfigMap, atau Secret.

---

## 9. Inject Config ke Jersey/HK2

```java id="5birys"
public final class AppBinder extends AbstractBinder {

    @Override
    protected void configure() {
        AppConfig appConfig = ConfigLoader.load();

        bind(appConfig)
            .to(AppConfig.class);

        bind(appConfig.database())
            .to(DbConfig.class);

        bind(appConfig.httpClient())
            .to(HttpClientConfig.class);

        bindFactory(DataSourceFactory.class)
            .to(DataSource.class)
            .in(Singleton.class);
    }
}
```

```java id="jmrmd4"
public final class AppResourceConfig extends ResourceConfig {

    public AppResourceConfig() {
        register(new AppBinder());

        register(CustomerResource.class);
        register(ErrorMapper.class);
        register(CorrelationIdFilter.class);

        property("app.profile", System.getenv().getOrDefault("APP_PROFILE", "LOCAL"));
    }
}
```

`ResourceConfig` menyediakan operasi untuk register component/resource/provider dan menyimpan custom properties pada konfigurasi Jersey runtime. ([Jersey ResourceConfig API](https://eclipse-ee4j.github.io/jersey.github.io/apidocs/3.0.0/jersey/org/glassfish/jersey/server/ResourceConfig.html))

**Boundary rule:** `ResourceConfig.property(...)` cocok untuk Jersey runtime properties, bukan sebagai pengganti typed application config.

---

## 10. Inject Config dengan CDI/Weld

```java id="n34n20"
@ApplicationScoped
public class AppConfigProducer {

    private final AppConfig config = ConfigLoader.load();

    @Produces
    @ApplicationScoped
    public AppConfig appConfig() {
        return config;
    }

    @Produces
    @ApplicationScoped
    public DbConfig dbConfig() {
        return config.database();
    }

    @Produces
    @ApplicationScoped
    public HttpClientConfig httpClientConfig() {
        return config.httpClient();
    }
}
```

```java id="bkkrpy"
@ApplicationScoped
public class CustomerRepositoryPostgres implements CustomerRepository {

    private final DbConfig dbConfig;

    @Inject
    public CustomerRepositoryPostgres(DbConfig dbConfig) {
        this.dbConfig = dbConfig;
    }
}
```

CDI cocok bila aplikasi ingin memakai model Jakarta-standard: scopes, producer, qualifiers, interceptors, events, dan lifecycle. CDI 4.1 adalah bagian dari Jakarta EE 11. ([Jakarta EE 11 release page](https://jakarta.ee/release/11/))

---

## 11. Properties File Pattern

Contoh classpath default:

```properties id="91qb1h"
app.profile=LOCAL

server.port=8080
server.basePath=/api

db.maxPoolSize=10
db.connectionTimeout=PT5S

http.connectTimeout=PT3S
http.readTimeout=PT10S

feature.newOrderFlow=false
```

Contoh external override:

```properties id="wum7ll"
app.profile=STAGING

db.url=jdbc:postgresql://postgres-staging:5432/orderdb
db.username=order_app
db.maxPoolSize=30

http.connectTimeout=PT2S
http.readTimeout=PT8S

feature.newOrderFlow=true
```

Jangan simpan ini di file default:

```properties id="qrvjmk"
db.password=secret
jwt.privateKey=secret
external.api.token=secret
```

Secret harus berasal dari Secret Manager, Kubernetes Secret, mounted secret file, atau secret injection mechanism lain.

---

## 12. Docker Pattern

### Dockerfile

```dockerfile id="1bfhnu"
FROM eclipse-temurin:17-jre

WORKDIR /app

COPY target/order-service.jar /app/order-service.jar

ENV APP_PROFILE=LOCAL

ENTRYPOINT ["java", "-jar", "/app/order-service.jar"]
```

### docker-compose local

```yaml id="334yfw"
services:
  order-service:
    image: order-service:local
    environment:
      APP_PROFILE: LOCAL
      DB_URL: jdbc:postgresql://postgres:5432/orderdb
      DB_USERNAME: order_app
      DB_PASSWORD: order_password
      DB_MAX_POOL_SIZE: "10"
    ports:
      - "8080:8080"

  postgres:
    image: postgres:16
    environment:
      POSTGRES_DB: orderdb
      POSTGRES_USER: order_app
      POSTGRES_PASSWORD: order_password
```

Untuk local development, env var di compose masih wajar. Untuk production, jangan hardcode secret di manifest yang masuk Git.

---

## 13. Kubernetes ConfigMap + Secret Pattern

### ConfigMap untuk non-secret

```yaml id="5yiqio"
apiVersion: v1
kind: ConfigMap
metadata:
  name: order-service-config
data:
  APP_PROFILE: "PROD"
  DB_URL: "jdbc:postgresql://postgres-prod:5432/orderdb"
  DB_USERNAME: "order_app"
  DB_MAX_POOL_SIZE: "30"
  DB_CONNECTION_TIMEOUT: "PT5S"
```

ConfigMap memang ditujukan untuk non-confidential key-value data dan dapat dikonsumsi oleh Pod sebagai env var, command-line argument, atau file konfigurasi dalam volume. ([Kubernetes ConfigMap docs](https://kubernetes.io/docs/concepts/configuration/configmap/))

### Secret untuk sensitive value

```yaml id="mdva3i"
apiVersion: v1
kind: Secret
metadata:
  name: order-service-secret
type: Opaque
stringData:
  DB_PASSWORD: "replace-me"
```

Kubernetes Secret dapat dipakai sebagai mounted data volume atau diekspos sebagai environment variable ke container. ([Kubernetes Secret docs](https://kubernetes.io/docs/concepts/configuration/secret/))

### Deployment env injection

```yaml id="8ipx1t"
apiVersion: apps/v1
kind: Deployment
metadata:
  name: order-service
spec:
  replicas: 3
  selector:
    matchLabels:
      app: order-service
  template:
    metadata:
      labels:
        app: order-service
    spec:
      containers:
        - name: order-service
          image: registry.example.com/order-service:1.0.0
          envFrom:
            - configMapRef:
                name: order-service-config
            - secretRef:
                name: order-service-secret
```

### Mounted file alternative

```yaml id="s0iy5l"
apiVersion: apps/v1
kind: Deployment
metadata:
  name: order-service
spec:
  template:
    spec:
      containers:
        - name: order-service
          image: registry.example.com/order-service:1.0.0
          volumeMounts:
            - name: app-config
              mountPath: /etc/order-service
              readOnly: true
      volumes:
        - name: app-config
          configMap:
            name: order-service-config-files
```

Use env var untuk simple scalar config. Use mounted file untuk structured config seperti YAML, JSON, policy file, template, certificate bundle, atau feature matrix.

---

## 14. Secret Handling Rules

| Rule | Penjelasan |
|---|---|
| Jangan log raw config | `AppConfig#toString()` harus mask field secret. |
| Jangan taruh secret di classpath resource | File classpath ikut masuk artifact. |
| Jangan taruh secret di Dockerfile | Secret bisa masuk image layer/history. |
| Jangan taruh secret di Git | Bahkan private repo tetap risk. |
| Gunakan short-lived credentials bila memungkinkan | Lebih mudah rotate dan mengurangi blast radius. |
| Pisahkan config dan secret | ConfigMap untuk non-secret, Secret/Secret Manager untuk secret. |

Contoh masking:

```java id="x4stbk"
public record SecretValue(String value) {

    public SecretValue {
        if (value == null || value.isBlank()) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("Secret must not be blank");
        }
    }

    @Override
    public String toString() {
        return "****";
    }
}
```

```java id="c3bvq8"
public record DbConfig(
    String url,
    String username,
    SecretValue password,
    int maxPoolSize,
    Duration connectionTimeout
) {}
```

---

## 15. Startup Validation Pattern

```java id="vg7s9w"
public final class StartupValidator {

    public static void validate(AppConfig config) {
        config.validate();

        if ("PROD".equals(config.profile())) {
            assertNoLocalhost(config.database().url(), "db.url");
            assertNoDefaultPassword(config.database().password());
            assertProductionTimeouts(config.httpClient());
        }
    }

    private static void assertNoLocalhost(String value, String name) {
        if (value.contains("localhost") || value.contains("127.0.0.1")) {
            throw new IllegalStateException(name + " must not point to localhost in PROD");
        }
    }

    private static void assertNoDefaultPassword(SecretValue password) {
        if ("password".equals(password.value()) || "changeme".equals(password.value())) {
            throw new IllegalStateException("Default password is forbidden in PROD");
        }
    }

    private static void assertProductionTimeouts(HttpClientConfig config) {
        if (config.readTimeout().compareTo(Duration.ofSeconds(60)) > 0) {
            throw new IllegalStateException("HTTP read timeout too high for PROD");
        }
    }
}
```

Validation bukan hanya “field tidak null”, tapi juga **environment-specific invariant**.

---

## 16. Config Observability

Saat startup, log config summary yang aman:

```text id="jki7f4"
Starting order-service
profile=PROD
version=1.12.4
db.url=jdbc:postgresql://postgres-prod:5432/orderdb
db.username=order_app
db.password=****
db.maxPoolSize=30
http.connectTimeout=PT2S
http.readTimeout=PT8S
feature.newOrderFlow=true
```

Jangan log:

```text id="jttc8o"
jwt.privateKey=...
db.password=...
external.api.token=...
```

Senior production habit: log enough to debug deployment mismatch, never enough to leak credentials.

---

## 17. Application File Resources

Ada dua jenis file resource:

```text id="6bf5ec"
Classpath Resource
    packaged inside JAR/WAR
    stable, versioned with app
    good for default templates/schema/non-secret metadata

External Runtime File
    mounted or present in filesystem
    environment-specific
    good for config, secrets, certificates, policy files
```

Java `Class#getResourceAsStream` bisa dipakai untuk membaca classpath resource; method ini akan mengembalikan `null` bila resource tidak ditemukan. ([Oracle Java 17 Class API](https://docs.oracle.com/en/java/javase/17/docs/api/java.base/java/lang/Class.html))

```java id="gfbmf8"
public final class ClasspathResources {

    public static String readUtf8(String path) {
        try (InputStream input = ClasspathResources.class.getResourceAsStream(path)) {
            if (input == null) {
                throw new IllegalArgumentException("Classpath resource not found: " + path);
            }
            return new String(input.readAllBytes(), StandardCharsets.UTF_8);
        } catch (IOException e) {
            throw new UncheckedIOException(e);
        }
    }
}
```

Untuk external file:

```java id="1lvnej"
public final class ExternalFiles {

    public static String readUtf8(Path path) {
        try {
            if (!Files.exists(path)) {
                throw new IllegalArgumentException("File not found: " + path);
            }
            return Files.readString(path, StandardCharsets.UTF_8);
        } catch (IOException e) {
            throw new UncheckedIOException(e);
        }
    }
}
```

---

## 18. Failure Modes

| Symptom | Root Cause | Diagnosis |
|---|---|---|
| Local jalan, Kubernetes gagal | Env var / Secret / ConfigMap tidak terpasang | `kubectl describe pod`, cek env, cek mount path. |
| Config berubah tapi app tidak berubah | App membaca config hanya saat startup | Restart pod atau implement reload mechanism. |
| Production pakai local config | Default profile terlalu permisif | Larang fallback `LOCAL` untuk production deployment. |
| Secret muncul di log | `toString()` config tidak masking | Review config logging dan exception handling. |
| `NumberFormatException` saat startup | Env var numerik invalid | Tambahkan typed parser dengan error message jelas. |
| `Duration.parse` gagal | Format bukan ISO-8601 duration | Dokumentasikan format seperti `PT5S`, `PT1M`. |
| File tidak ditemukan dalam JAR | Resource tidak masuk `src/main/resources` | Cek artifact build output. |
| Mounted file tidak terbaca | Permission/path salah | Cek UID container, `readOnly`, `mountPath`. |
| ConfigMap updated tapi env tidak berubah | Env var dari ConfigMap tidak otomatis update di proses | Restart pod untuk env-based config. |
| Behavior beda antar pod | Config tidak immutable saat rollout | Gunakan versioned ConfigMap/Secret atau rollout terkontrol. |

---

## 19. Production Checklist

| Checklist | Harus Terpenuhi |
|---|---|
| Config source precedence terdokumentasi | Ya |
| Semua required config divalidasi saat startup | Ya |
| Config object immutable | Ya |
| Secret dimodelkan sebagai `SecretValue` atau masked type | Ya |
| Tidak ada secret di default properties | Ya |
| Tidak ada direct `System.getenv()` tersebar di service | Ya |
| Runtime profile eksplisit | Ya |
| Production tidak boleh fallback ke local | Ya |
| Startup log hanya non-secret summary | Ya |
| Kubernetes ConfigMap dan Secret dipisah | Ya |
| File path external configurable | Ya |
| Error message config jelas | Ya |

---

## 20. Roadmap Belajar Singkat

| Urutan | Fokus | Target |
|---:|---|---|
| 1 | Env var vs JVM property | Paham kapan pakai `APP_PROFILE` vs `-Dapp.profile`. |
| 2 | Typed config | Bisa membuat `AppConfig`, `DbConfig`, `HttpClientConfig`. |
| 3 | Config resolution | Bisa implement precedence source secara deterministic. |
| 4 | Fail-fast validation | Bisa menolak startup bila config invalid. |
| 5 | Secret handling | Bisa masking, externalize, dan memisahkan secret dari config. |
| 6 | Docker config | Bisa menjalankan app via env var di compose. |
| 7 | Kubernetes config | Bisa memakai ConfigMap/Secret sebagai env atau volume. |
| 8 | Production hardening | Bisa membuat invariant khusus prod/staging. |

## Rekomendasi Materi Selanjutnya

Lanjut ke **cheatsheet: “Jersey/JAX-RS Context Propagation - request context, correlation ID, tenant/user context, MDC logging, thread boundary, async calls, and failure cases.”**

## Response:
7/13/2026, 12:03:56 AM

# Cheatsheet - Jersey/JAX-RS Context Propagation

## 1. Scope

Cheatsheet ini membahas cara membawa **context** secara aman di aplikasi Jersey/JAX-RS:

```text id="7m8e2x"
Incoming HTTP Request
    ↓
Correlation / Trace / Request ID
    ↓
Auth Principal / User / Tenant Context
    ↓
JAX-RS Filter
    ↓
RequestContext Object
    ↓
Service / Repository / External Client
    ↓
Logs / Audit / Metrics / Downstream HTTP / Async Task
```

Targetnya: setiap request bisa dilacak dari **API gateway → Jersey resource → service layer → database/external service → log/trace/audit**, tanpa context bocor antar request, antar thread, atau antar tenant.

---

## 2. Core Mental Model

| Context Type | Isi | Tujuan |
|---|---|---|
| **Request context** | `requestId`, method, path, source IP, user agent | Melacak satu HTTP request lokal. |
| **Correlation context** | `correlationId` | Menghubungkan beberapa service call dalam satu business flow. |
| **Trace context** | `traceId`, `spanId`, `traceparent` | Distributed tracing lintas service/process. OpenTelemetry menjelaskan propagation sebagai mekanisme untuk membawa context antar service/process agar trace, log, dan metric bisa dikorelasikan. ([OpenTelemetry](https://opentelemetry.io/docs/concepts/context-propagation/)) |
| **Security context** | actor, principal, roles, scopes | Authorization dan audit. |
| **Tenant context** | `tenantId`, organization, realm | Multi-tenancy dan data isolation. |
| **Workflow context** | process instance id, business key, correlation key | Berguna untuk Camunda/BPMN atau long-running workflow. |
| **Logging context** | MDC fields | Memperkaya log dengan request/correlation/user/tenant metadata. MDC pada Logback bersifat per-thread dan perlu dibersihkan karena server biasa mendaur ulang thread. ([logback.qos.ch](https://logback.qos.ch/manual/mdc.html)) |

---

## 3. Diagram

```text id="5vrzva"
Jersey Request Context Propagation
│
├─ 1. Inbound Boundary
│  ├─ HTTP headers
│  │  ├─ X-Request-Id
│  │  ├─ X-Correlation-Id
│  │  ├─ traceparent
│  │  ├─ Authorization
│  │  └─ X-Tenant-Id
│  │
│  └─ ContainerRequestFilter
│     ├─ validate / normalize headers
│     ├─ generate missing IDs
│     ├─ extract actor / tenant
│     ├─ create RequestContext
│     └─ populate MDC
│
├─ 2. Application Layer
│  ├─ Resource method
│  ├─ Service layer
│  ├─ Repository layer
│  └─ Audit/event publisher
│
├─ 3. Outbound Boundary
│  ├─ Jersey Client / OpenFeign / HTTP client
│  ├─ copy correlation headers
│  ├─ inject trace context
│  └─ avoid leaking internal-only fields
│
├─ 4. Thread Boundary
│  ├─ executor service
│  ├─ async response
│  ├─ CompletableFuture
│  ├─ scheduler
│  └─ message listener
│
└─ 5. Cleanup
   ├─ clear MDC
   ├─ close request scope
   ├─ remove ThreadLocal
   └─ avoid context bleed
```

---

## 4. Context Data Model

Buat context internal yang immutable. Jangan sebar raw `HttpHeaders`, `ContainerRequestContext`, `HttpServletRequest`, atau `SecurityContext` ke seluruh service layer.

```java id="x03n9q"
public record RequestContext(
    String requestId,
    String correlationId,
    String traceId,
    String actorId,
    String tenantId,
    String sourceIp,
    String userAgent,
    Instant receivedAt
) {
    public void validate() {
        require(requestId, "requestId");
        require(correlationId, "correlationId");
        require(tenantId, "tenantId");
    }

    private static void require(String value, String field) {
        if (value == null || value.isBlank()) {
            throw new IllegalStateException("Missing request context field: " + field);
        }
    }
}
```

**Rule:** context object boleh dibaca oleh service, repository, client, audit, dan logger adapter; tapi jangan dijadikan tempat menyimpan mutable business state.

---

## 5. Inbound Context Filter

Jersey/JAX-RS request filter berjalan sebelum resource method, dan filter bisa memanipulasi request parameter/header/entity atau menghentikan request dengan `abortWith`. Response filter tetap bisa berjalan untuk response yang dibuat setelah abort. ([Eclipse EE4J](https://eclipse-ee4j.github.io/jersey.github.io/documentation/latest/filters-and-interceptors.html))

```java id="97p0jz"
@Provider
@Priority(Priorities.AUTHENTICATION)
public final class RequestContextFilter implements ContainerRequestFilter, ContainerResponseFilter {

    public static final String REQUEST_CONTEXT_PROPERTY = "app.requestContext";

    @Override
    public void filter(ContainerRequestContext request) {
        RequestContext context = buildContext(request);
        context.validate();

        request.setProperty(REQUEST_CONTEXT_PROPERTY, context);

        MDC.put("requestId", context.requestId());
        MDC.put("correlationId", context.correlationId());
        MDC.put("tenantId", context.tenantId());
        MDC.put("actorId", safe(context.actorId()));
    }

    @Override
    public void filter(ContainerRequestContext request, ContainerResponseContext response) {
        try {
            RequestContext context =
                (RequestContext) request.getProperty(REQUEST_CONTEXT_PROPERTY);

            if (context != null) {
                response.getHeaders().putSingle("X-Request-Id", context.requestId());
                response.getHeaders().putSingle("X-Correlation-Id", context.correlationId());
            }
        } finally {
            MDC.clear();
        }
    }

    private RequestContext buildContext(ContainerRequestContext request) {
        String requestId = valueOrGenerate(request.getHeaderString("X-Request-Id"));
        String correlationId = valueOrGenerate(request.getHeaderString("X-Correlation-Id"));

        return new RequestContext(
            requestId,
            correlationId,
            extractTraceId(request.getHeaderString("traceparent")),
            extractActor(request),
            requiredTenant(request),
            sourceIp(request),
            request.getHeaderString("User-Agent"),
            Instant.now()
        );
    }

    private static String valueOrGenerate(String value) {
        return value == null || value.isBlank()
            ? UUID.randomUUID().toString()
            : value;
    }

    private static String safe(String value) {
        return value == null ? "anonymous" : value;
    }
}
```

**Catatan penting:** pada Jakarta REST 4.0, arah spesifikasi makin mendorong injection melalui CDI; `@Context` sudah masuk jalur deprecation. Karena itu, untuk code baru lebih aman membungkus request metadata ke `RequestContext` internal daripada menyebarkan `@Context` ke banyak class. ([Jakarta EE](https://jakarta.ee/specifications/restful-ws/3.1/jakarta-restful-ws-spec-3.1.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 6. Access Pattern di Resource dan Service

### Resource tipis

```java id="9p3j4k"
@Path("/orders")
@Produces(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
public final class OrderResource {

    private final OrderService orderService;

    @Inject
    public OrderResource(OrderService orderService) {
        this.orderService = orderService;
    }

    @GET
    @Path("/{id}")
    public Response getOrder(@PathParam("id") String id,
                             @Context ContainerRequestContext request) {
        RequestContext context = (RequestContext) request.getProperty(
            RequestContextFilter.REQUEST_CONTEXT_PROPERTY
        );

        OrderDto result = orderService.getOrder(id, context);
        return Response.ok(result).build();
    }
}
```

### Service explicit context

```java id="985smk"
public final class OrderService {

    private final OrderRepository repository;
    private final AuditPublisher auditPublisher;

    public OrderDto getOrder(String orderId, RequestContext context) {
        authorizeRead(orderId, context);

        Order order = repository.findById(orderId, context.tenantId());

        auditPublisher.publish(AuditEvent.orderViewed(
            orderId,
            context.actorId(),
            context.tenantId(),
            context.correlationId()
        ));

        return OrderDto.from(order);
    }
}
```

Untuk service layer, **explicit parameter** sering lebih aman daripada `ThreadLocal`, karena dependency context terlihat di method signature dan lebih mudah dites.

---

## 7. Context Propagation Style

| Style | Cocok Untuk | Kelebihan | Risiko |
|---|---|---|---|
| Explicit parameter | Service/domain/application logic | Jelas, testable, minim magic | Signature bisa panjang bila context terlalu gemuk. |
| Request-scoped bean | Jersey/CDI managed object | Ergonomis untuk resource/provider | Bisa gagal jika request scope tidak aktif. |
| `ContainerRequestContext#setProperty` | Boundary JAX-RS | Natural di filter/resource | Jangan bocorkan object ini ke service. |
| MDC | Logging | Otomatis masuk log pattern | Per-thread; wajib cleanup dan tidak otomatis lintas thread. |
| ThreadLocal | Legacy/internal adapter | Mudah untuk library yang tidak menerima parameter | Rawan leak di thread pool. |
| OpenTelemetry context | Distributed tracing | Standar observability modern | Jangan dipakai untuk authorization domain state. |

---

## 8. MDC Logging Pattern

MDC bagus untuk log enrichment, bukan untuk business logic. Logback menyatakan MDC mengelola contextual information secara per-thread, dan child thread tidak otomatis mewarisi MDC parent thread. ([logback.qos.ch](https://logback.qos.ch/manual/mdc.html))

### Log pattern

```properties id="kg23zx"
logging.pattern=%d{ISO8601} %-5level [%thread] \
correlationId=%X{correlationId} requestId=%X{requestId} \
tenantId=%X{tenantId} actorId=%X{actorId} \
logger=%logger{36} - %msg%n
```

### Safe usage

```java id="m944nj"
try {
    MDC.put("correlationId", context.correlationId());
    MDC.put("requestId", context.requestId());
    MDC.put("tenantId", context.tenantId());

    service.execute(command, context);
} finally {
    MDC.clear();
}
```

### Anti-pattern

```java id="87wjil"
// BAD: no cleanup
MDC.put("tenantId", tenantId);
service.execute();
```

Pada servlet/Jersey runtime, thread pool bisa mendaur ulang thread. Kalau MDC tidak dibersihkan, log request berikutnya bisa membawa context request sebelumnya.

---

## 9. Outbound HTTP Propagation

Saat aplikasi memanggil service lain, context yang perlu diteruskan biasanya:

```text id="k55gzp"
X-Request-Id       usually new per service request or preserved by policy
X-Correlation-Id   preserved across business flow
traceparent        propagated by OpenTelemetry/instrumentation
Authorization      only if delegation policy allows it
X-Tenant-Id        only if downstream is tenant-aware and trusted
```

OpenTelemetry menggunakan propagation untuk memindahkan context antar service/process; default propagator memakai header dari W3C Trace Context, termasuk `traceparent`. ([OpenTelemetry](https://opentelemetry.io/docs/concepts/context-propagation/))

### Jersey Client filter

```java id="5pklsw"
public final class OutboundContextFilter implements ClientRequestFilter {

    private final RequestContextProvider contextProvider;

    public OutboundContextFilter(RequestContextProvider contextProvider) {
        this.contextProvider = contextProvider;
    }

    @Override
    public void filter(ClientRequestContext requestContext) {
        RequestContext context = contextProvider.current();

        requestContext.getHeaders().putSingle(
            "X-Correlation-Id",
            context.correlationId()
        );

        requestContext.getHeaders().putSingle(
            "X-Request-Id",
            UUID.randomUUID().toString()
        );

        requestContext.getHeaders().putSingle(
            "X-Tenant-Id",
            context.tenantId()
        );
    }
}
```

Jersey membedakan server-side container filters dan client filters; client request filter bisa mencegah request dikirim, sedangkan client response filter memproses response dari client invocation. ([Eclipse EE4J](https://eclipse-ee4j.github.io/jersey.github.io/documentation/latest/filters-and-interceptors.html))

---

## 10. Thread Boundary dan Async Calls

Masalah utama context propagation muncul ketika eksekusi pindah thread:

```text id="9r5dnv"
HTTP thread
    ↓
ExecutorService / CompletableFuture / async callback
    ↓
Different worker thread
    ↓
MDC / ThreadLocal context hilang atau salah
```

Karena MDC berbasis per-thread dan child thread tidak otomatis mewarisi copy MDC parent, context harus dipassing eksplisit atau dibungkus saat submit task. ([logback.qos.ch](https://logback.qos.ch/manual/mdc.html))

### Context-aware executor wrapper

```java id="d84c5h"
public final class ContextAwareExecutor {

    private final ExecutorService delegate;

    public ContextAwareExecutor(ExecutorService delegate) {
        this.delegate = delegate;
    }

    public <T> CompletableFuture<T> supplyAsync(
        Supplier<T> supplier,
        RequestContext context
    ) {
        Map<String, String> parentMdc = MDC.getCopyOfContextMap();

        return CompletableFuture.supplyAsync(() -> {
            Map<String, String> previous = MDC.getCopyOfContextMap();

            try {
                if (parentMdc != null) {
                    MDC.setContextMap(parentMdc);
                }

                return supplier.get();
            } finally {
                MDC.clear();

                if (previous != null) {
                    MDC.setContextMap(previous);
                }
            }
        }, delegate);
    }
}
```

### Better service style

```java id="jb29za"
CompletableFuture<OrderRisk> risk =
    contextAwareExecutor.supplyAsync(
        () -> riskService.calculate(orderId, context),
        context
    );
```

Rule: **context business/security tetap dipass eksplisit; MDC hanya disalin untuk logging.**

---

## 11. Tenant Context Rules

Tenant context lebih berbahaya daripada correlation ID karena berhubungan dengan data isolation.

| Rule | Penjelasan |
|---|---|
| Tenant wajib tervalidasi | Jangan percaya `X-Tenant-Id` mentah dari client publik. |
| Tenant harus cocok dengan token/claim | Header tenant harus diverifikasi terhadap identity/authz context. |
| Repository wajib menerima tenant boundary | Query harus memfilter tenant atau memakai schema/role terpisah. |
| Jangan fallback tenant | Missing tenant sebaiknya `401/403/400`, bukan default tenant. |
| Audit harus menyimpan tenant | Supaya forensic trail bisa dipisahkan. |

```java id="v9q2mb"
private String requiredTenant(ContainerRequestContext request) {
    String tenantId = request.getHeaderString("X-Tenant-Id");

    if (tenantId == null || tenantId.isBlank()) {
        throw new BadRequestException("Missing X-Tenant-Id");
    }

    if (!tenantPolicy.isAllowedForPrincipal(tenantId, currentPrincipal(request))) {
        throw new ForbiddenException("Principal is not allowed for tenant");
    }

    return tenantId;
}
```

---

## 12. Correlation vs Request vs Trace ID

| ID | Scope | Generated By | Propagation |
|---|---|---|---|
| `requestId` | Satu HTTP request di satu service | Gateway atau service lokal | Bisa baru setiap hop. |
| `correlationId` | Satu business transaction / user action | Gateway atau first service | Dipertahankan lintas service. |
| `traceId` | Distributed trace | OpenTelemetry/tracing SDK | Dipropagasikan via trace context. |
| `spanId` | Satu operation dalam trace | Tracing SDK | Berubah per span. |
| `businessKey` | Domain/workflow instance | Application/domain layer | Dipakai untuk audit/workflow, bukan logging teknis saja. |

Jangan menyamakan semuanya menjadi satu ID. Untuk debugging cepat, correlation ID berguna; untuk distributed tracing, trace ID/span ID lebih tepat; untuk regulatory/audit/business process, business key harus tetap eksplisit.

---

## 13. Failure Modes

| Symptom | Penyebab Umum | Diagnosis / Fix |
|---|---|---|
| Log tenant/user salah | MDC tidak dibersihkan di thread pool | `MDC.clear()` di `finally`. |
| Async log kehilangan correlation ID | Eksekusi pindah thread | Copy MDC atau pass context eksplisit. |
| Downstream service tidak bisa ditrace | `traceparent`/correlation header tidak diteruskan | Tambahkan client filter atau OpenTelemetry instrumentation. |
| Data tenant bocor | Tenant header dipercaya tanpa validasi | Cocokkan tenant dengan auth principal/claim. |
| Service sulit dites | Context tersembunyi di ThreadLocal | Jadikan `RequestContext` explicit parameter. |
| `NullPointerException` context | Filter tidak register / urutan filter salah | Register eksplisit dan set priority. |
| Header internal bocor ke external API | Outbound propagation terlalu agresif | Whitelist header per downstream. |
| Duplicate request ID | ID di-generate tidak unik atau reused | Pakai UUID/ULID; bedakan request ID vs correlation ID. |
| Audit tidak bisa direkonstruksi | Audit hanya menyimpan actor, tidak correlation/business key | Simpan actor, tenant, correlation ID, business key, timestamp. |
| Context terlalu besar | Semua hal dimasukkan ke `RequestContext` | Pisahkan technical context, security context, workflow context. |

---

## 14. Production Checklist

| Checklist | Target |
|---|---|
| Inbound filter membuat/validasi `requestId` dan `correlationId` | Ya |
| Response selalu mengembalikan `X-Request-Id` / `X-Correlation-Id` | Ya |
| MDC diisi di awal dan dibersihkan di `finally` | Ya |
| Tenant divalidasi terhadap principal/token | Ya |
| Service menerima `RequestContext` eksplisit untuk operasi penting | Ya |
| Outbound client meneruskan correlation/trace context secara whitelist | Ya |
| Async execution punya context propagation wrapper | Ya |
| ThreadLocal tidak menjadi sumber kebenaran business/security | Ya |
| Audit event menyimpan actor, tenant, correlation ID, business key | Ya |
| OpenTelemetry trace context tidak dicampur dengan authorization state | Ya |

---

## 15. Roadmap Belajar Singkat

| Urutan | Fokus | Target Praktik |
|---:|---|---|
| 1 | Inbound filter | Bisa generate/validate correlation ID dan request ID. |
| 2 | Immutable context object | Bisa membuat `RequestContext` yang aman dipass ke service. |
| 3 | MDC logging | Bisa enrich log dan cleanup dengan benar. |
| 4 | Tenant/security context | Bisa validasi tenant terhadap principal. |
| 5 | Outbound propagation | Bisa meneruskan correlation/trace headers ke downstream. |
| 6 | Async boundary | Bisa propagasi context ke `ExecutorService` / `CompletableFuture`. |
| 7 | Audit linkage | Bisa menghubungkan API request, business event, dan workflow/process instance. |
| 8 | Failure modeling | Bisa mendeteksi context leak, missing context, dan cross-tenant risk. |

---
