## Response:
7/13/2026, 12:07:43 AM

Saya lanjut ke cross-cutting architecture: bukan sekadar “pakai filter”, tapi pemetaan concern ke extension point yang tepat, urutan eksekusi, dan failure mode production.

# Cheatsheet - Jersey/JAX-RS AOP & Cross-Cutting Architecture

## 1. Scope

Cheatsheet ini membahas cara menaruh **cross-cutting concern** di aplikasi Jersey/JAX-RS tanpa membuat resource, service, dan repository tercemar logic horizontal.

```text id="7qgyy8"
Cross-Cutting Concerns
│
├─ Security
├─ Authentication / Authorization
├─ Correlation ID
├─ Logging / Audit
├─ Metrics / Tracing
├─ Error Mapping
├─ Validation Boundary
├─ Transaction Boundary
├─ Request / Response Header Policy
├─ Compression / Encoding
├─ Entity Transformation
└─ Idempotency / Rate Limit / Feature Gate
```

Di konteks Jersey/JAX-RS, “AOP” biasanya bukan satu mekanisme tunggal. Ada dua keluarga besar: **JAX-RS extension points** untuk boundary HTTP, dan **Jakarta/CDI interceptors** untuk method/business boundary. Jakarta REST mendefinisikan container/client filters, reader/writer interceptors, binding, priority, dan exception mapping; Jakarta Interceptors mendefinisikan interception untuk business method invocation, lifecycle event, dan timeout event pada managed classes. ([Jakarta EE](https://jakarta.ee/specifications/restful-ws/4.0/jakarta-restful-ws-spec-4.0))

---

## 2. Mental Model

```text id="endfrq"
HTTP Boundary
    ↓
Pre-matching request filter
    ↓
Resource matching
    ↓
Name-bound / dynamic request filters
    ↓
ReaderInterceptor
    ↓
MessageBodyReader
    ↓
Resource method
    ↓
CDI / Jakarta Interceptor around service method
    ↓
Business service / repository / transaction
    ↓
ExceptionMapper if exception occurs
    ↓
WriterInterceptor
    ↓
MessageBodyWriter
    ↓
Response filter
    ↓
HTTP response
```

Rule paling penting:

```text id="0msthu"
JAX-RS filter/interceptor = HTTP boundary concern
CDI/Jakarta interceptor = managed method/business concern
ExceptionMapper = exception-to-response policy
```

Jangan memaksa semua concern masuk satu tempat. Security header cocok di response filter; audit business operation cocok di CDI interceptor atau service-level event; error taxonomy cocok di `ExceptionMapper`; request body transformation cocok di `ReaderInterceptor`.

---

## 3. Extension Point Decision Table

| Concern | Extension Point | Kenapa |
|---|---|---|
| Correlation ID | `ContainerRequestFilter` + `ContainerResponseFilter` | Dibaca dari inbound header, dikembalikan di response. |
| Authentication token parsing | `ContainerRequestFilter` | Harus terjadi sebelum resource method. |
| Authorization per endpoint | Name-bound filter / `DynamicFeature` / service policy | Tergantung apakah rule berbasis HTTP annotation atau domain rule. |
| Response security headers | `ContainerResponseFilter` | Concern murni response boundary. |
| Request/response logging | Request + response filter | Butuh metadata HTTP dan status response. |
| Body encryption/decryption | `ReaderInterceptor` / `WriterInterceptor` | Membungkus entity read/write. |
| GZIP/custom encoding | `ReaderInterceptor` / `WriterInterceptor` | Entity transformation, bukan business logic. |
| Exception-to-HTTP mapping | `ExceptionMapper<T>` | Kontrak resmi untuk map Java exception ke `Response`. |
| Business audit | CDI/Jakarta interceptor atau domain event | Harus dekat dengan use case, bukan hanya HTTP call. |
| Transaction boundary | CDI/Jakarta interceptor / framework transaction | Boundary method/service, bukan resource manual. |
| Feature gate per endpoint | Name-bound filter / `DynamicFeature` | Bisa dipasang selektif pada method/class. |
| Downstream HTTP header propagation | `ClientRequestFilter` | Concern outbound client request. |
| Downstream response normalization | `ClientResponseFilter` | Concern outbound client response. |

Jakarta REST menyatakan client filter mengimplementasikan `ClientRequestFilter`/`ClientResponseFilter`, container filter mengimplementasikan `ContainerRequestFilter`/`ContainerResponseFilter`, dan entity interceptor mengimplementasikan `ReaderInterceptor`/`WriterInterceptor`; semuanya adalah provider dan dapat ditemukan otomatis dengan `@Provider`. ([Jakarta EE](https://jakarta.ee/specifications/restful-ws/4.0/jakarta-restful-ws-spec-4.0))

---

## 4. Cross-Cutting Classification

```text id="lf77em"
Cross-Cutting Architecture
│
├─ HTTP Pipeline Concern
│  ├─ headers
│  ├─ auth token extraction
│  ├─ request logging
│  ├─ response decoration
│  └─ abort unauthorized request
│
├─ Entity Concern
│  ├─ compression
│  ├─ encryption
│  ├─ body checksum
│  ├─ stream wrapping
│  └─ serialization-adjacent behavior
│
├─ Resource Selection Concern
│  ├─ annotation-based endpoint behavior
│  ├─ method/class-based binding
│  └─ conditional filter registration
│
├─ Business Method Concern
│  ├─ audit
│  ├─ transaction
│  ├─ idempotency guard
│  ├─ policy enforcement
│  └─ performance timing
│
└─ Error Concern
   ├─ domain exception mapping
   ├─ validation error mapping
   ├─ infrastructure error mapping
   ├─ safe error body
   └─ correlation-aware error response
```

---

## 5. Container Request Filter

Gunakan untuk concern yang harus terjadi **sebelum resource method**.

```java id="qsx0sp"
@Provider
@Priority(Priorities.AUTHENTICATION)
public final class AuthenticationFilter implements ContainerRequestFilter {

    private final TokenVerifier tokenVerifier;

    @Inject
    public AuthenticationFilter(TokenVerifier tokenVerifier) {
        this.tokenVerifier = tokenVerifier;
    }

    @Override
    public void filter(ContainerRequestContext requestContext) {
        String authorization = requestContext.getHeaderString(HttpHeaders.AUTHORIZATION);

        if (authorization == null || authorization.isBlank()) {
            requestContext.abortWith(
                Response.status(Response.Status.UNAUTHORIZED)
                    .entity(ErrorResponse.of("AUTH_REQUIRED", "Authentication is required"))
                    .build()
            );
            return;
        }

        Principal principal = tokenVerifier.verify(authorization);
        requestContext.setProperty("principal", principal);
    }
}
```

`ContainerRequestContext` bersifat mutable dan menyediakan informasi request-specific seperti URI, headers, entity, dan request-scoped properties; request filter juga bisa menghentikan chain dengan `abortWith(Response)`. ([Jakarta EE](https://jakarta.ee/specifications/restful-ws/4.0/jakarta-restful-ws-spec-4.0))

### Cocok untuk

| Use Case | Catatan |
|---|---|
| Extract bearer token | Jangan lakukan business authorization mendalam di sini. |
| Validate mandatory header | Bisa langsung `400`. |
| Correlation ID setup | Set property/MDC. |
| Tenant header normalization | Tetap validasi tenant terhadap principal. |
| Early reject | `abortWith` untuk unauthorized/bad request. |

---

## 6. Container Response Filter

Gunakan untuk concern yang harus terjadi **sebelum HTTP response keluar**.

```java id="wg5lfe"
@Provider
@Priority(Priorities.HEADER_DECORATOR)
public final class SecurityHeadersFilter implements ContainerResponseFilter {

    @Override
    public void filter(ContainerRequestContext request,
                       ContainerResponseContext response) {
        response.getHeaders().putSingle("X-Content-Type-Options", "nosniff");
        response.getHeaders().putSingle("X-Frame-Options", "DENY");
        response.getHeaders().putSingle("Referrer-Policy", "no-referrer");

        Object correlationId = request.getProperty("correlationId");
        if (correlationId != null) {
            response.getHeaders().putSingle("X-Correlation-Id", correlationId.toString());
        }
    }
}
```

Response filter dieksekusi dalam response pipeline sebelum response dikirim ke network. Untuk chain response, urutan priority kebalik dibanding request chain: response filters disortir descending sehingga response filter berjalan dalam reverse order dari request filter. ([Jakarta EE](https://jakarta.ee/specifications/restful-ws/4.0/jakarta-restful-ws-spec-4.0))

---

## 7. Pre-Matching Filter

Gunakan `@PreMatching` hanya jika filter perlu berjalan **sebelum resource matching**, misalnya method override, path rewrite, atau normalization yang memengaruhi matching.

```java id="2obwd3"
@Provider
@PreMatching
@Priority(Priorities.HEADER_DECORATOR)
public final class HttpMethodOverrideFilter implements ContainerRequestFilter {

    @Override
    public void filter(ContainerRequestContext request) {
        String override = request.getHeaderString("X-HTTP-Method-Override");

        if (override != null && !override.isBlank()) {
            request.setMethod(override.toUpperCase(Locale.ROOT));
        }
    }
}
```

Global `ContainerRequestFilter` normalnya dieksekusi setelah resource matching, kecuali diberi `@PreMatching`; filter pre-matching bisa mengubah input ke matching algorithm, tetapi resource-specific context tertentu belum tentu tersedia pada tahap itu. ([Jakarta EE](https://jakarta.ee/specifications/restful-ws/4.0/jakarta-restful-ws-spec-4.0))

### Jangan pakai pre-matching untuk

| Jangan | Alasan |
|---|---|
| Authorization berbasis annotation resource | Resource method belum matched. |
| Logic business | Terlalu awal dan salah layer. |
| Request body processing | Lebih cocok reader interceptor/resource validation. |
| Audit final outcome | Response belum diketahui. |

---

## 8. Reader / Writer Interceptor

Gunakan untuk concern yang membungkus proses **entity read/write**, bukan sekadar metadata HTTP.

```java id="qbdvgc"
@Provider
@Priority(Priorities.ENTITY_CODER)
public final class ChecksumReaderInterceptor implements ReaderInterceptor {

    @Override
    public Object aroundReadFrom(ReaderInterceptorContext context)
        throws IOException, WebApplicationException {

        InputStream original = context.getInputStream();
        InputStream wrapped = new DigestInputStream(original, MessageDigest.getInstance("SHA-256"));

        context.setInputStream(wrapped);

        try {
            return context.proceed();
        } catch (NoSuchAlgorithmException e) {
            throw new InternalServerErrorException(e);
        }
    }
}
```

```java id="hqwuh0"
@Provider
@Priority(Priorities.ENTITY_CODER)
public final class ChecksumWriterInterceptor implements WriterInterceptor {

    @Override
    public void aroundWriteTo(WriterInterceptorContext context)
        throws IOException, WebApplicationException {

        context.getHeaders().putSingle("X-Body-Processed", "true");
        context.proceed();
    }
}
```

Jakarta REST mendefinisikan `ReaderInterceptor` sebagai wrapper di sekitar `MessageBodyReader.readFrom`, dan `WriterInterceptor` sebagai wrapper di sekitar `MessageBodyWriter.writeTo`; implementation wajib memanggil registered interceptors saat mapping representasi ke Java type dan sebaliknya. ([Jakarta EE](https://jakarta.ee/specifications/restful-ws/4.0/jakarta-restful-ws-spec-4.0))

### Cocok untuk

| Use Case | Catatan |
|---|---|
| GZIP/custom compression | Entity stream concern. |
| Envelope encryption/decryption | Hati-hati memory usage dan streaming. |
| Body checksum | Jangan baca full body jika besar. |
| Payload audit hash | Simpan hash, bukan raw sensitive body. |
| Media-type-specific transformation | Pastikan tidak merusak serializer/deserializer. |

---

## 9. Name Binding

Gunakan `@NameBinding` untuk filter/interceptor yang hanya berlaku pada resource atau method tertentu.

```java id="2twmcn"
@NameBinding
@Retention(RetentionPolicy.RUNTIME)
@Target({ElementType.TYPE, ElementType.METHOD})
public @interface RequireAdmin {}
```

```java id="evw75q"
@Provider
@RequireAdmin
@Priority(Priorities.AUTHORIZATION)
public final class AdminAuthorizationFilter implements ContainerRequestFilter {

    @Override
    public void filter(ContainerRequestContext request) {
        Principal principal = (Principal) request.getProperty("principal");

        if (principal == null || !principal.hasRole("ADMIN")) {
            throw new ForbiddenException("Admin role is required");
        }
    }
}
```

```java id="0ftqwi"
@Path("/admin/orders")
@RequireAdmin
public final class AdminOrderResource {

    @GET
    public Response listOrders() {
        return Response.ok().build();
    }
}
```

Name binding mengikat filter/interceptor ke resource class atau method menggunakan annotation yang dideklarasikan dengan meta-annotation `@NameBinding`; beberapa binding bisa dipasang sekaligus, dan jika filter memiliki beberapa binding maka semua annotation tersebut harus ada agar binding berlaku. ([Jakarta EE](https://jakarta.ee/specifications/restful-ws/4.0/jakarta-restful-ws-spec-4.0))

---

## 10. DynamicFeature

Gunakan `DynamicFeature` saat binding perlu ditentukan berdasarkan metadata resource method secara programmatic.

```java id="g08udq"
@Provider
public final class FeatureGateDynamicFeature implements DynamicFeature {

    @Override
    public void configure(ResourceInfo resourceInfo, FeatureContext context) {
        Method method = resourceInfo.getResourceMethod();

        if (method.isAnnotationPresent(BetaFeature.class)) {
            BetaFeature feature = method.getAnnotation(BetaFeature.class);
            context.register(new FeatureGateFilter(feature.value()));
        }
    }
}
```

```java id="wf42nl"
@Retention(RetentionPolicy.RUNTIME)
@Target({ElementType.METHOD, ElementType.TYPE})
public @interface BetaFeature {
    String value();
}
```

```java id="ikn98x"
public final class FeatureGateFilter implements ContainerRequestFilter {

    private final String featureName;

    public FeatureGateFilter(String featureName) {
        this.featureName = featureName;
    }

    @Override
    public void filter(ContainerRequestContext request) {
        if (!FeatureFlags.isEnabled(featureName)) {
            throw new NotFoundException();
        }
    }
}
```

`DynamicFeature` adalah provider yang dipakai untuk menambah filter/entity interceptor yang bound ke resource method; JAX-RS implementation sebaiknya resolve dynamic features sekali untuk setiap resource method dan direkomendasikan memprosesnya pada deployment time. ([Jakarta EE](https://jakarta.ee/specifications/restful-ws/4.0/jakarta-restful-ws-spec-4.0))

### Name Binding vs DynamicFeature

| Kebutuhan | Pakai |
|---|---|
| Satu annotation sederhana, behavior fixed | `@NameBinding` |
| Filter butuh parameter dari annotation | `DynamicFeature` |
| Rule berdasarkan class/method/reflection | `DynamicFeature` |
| Binding global | Register langsung di `ResourceConfig` |
| Binding client-side | Register via client/configurable API, bukan name binding |

---

## 11. ExceptionMapper

Gunakan `ExceptionMapper<T>` untuk membangun error response konsisten.

```java id="yg3uf5"
@Provider
public final class DomainExceptionMapper implements ExceptionMapper<DomainException> {

    @Override
    public Response toResponse(DomainException exception) {
        ErrorResponse body = new ErrorResponse(
            exception.code(),
            exception.safeMessage(),
            RequestContextHolder.correlationId()
        );

        return Response.status(mapStatus(exception))
            .type(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
            .entity(body)
            .build();
    }

    private Response.Status mapStatus(DomainException exception) {
        return switch (exception.category()) {
            case NOT_FOUND -> Response.Status.NOT_FOUND;
            case VALIDATION -> Response.Status.BAD_REQUEST;
            case CONFLICT -> Response.Status.CONFLICT;
            case FORBIDDEN -> Response.Status.FORBIDDEN;
            default -> Response.Status.INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR;
        };
    }
}
```

`ExceptionMapper` adalah kontrak provider untuk memetakan Java exception ke `Response`, dan provider tersebut harus diregister programmatically atau diberi `@Provider` agar ditemukan otomatis. Method `toResponse` mengembalikan response hasil mapping; jika mengembalikan `null`, hasilnya `204 No Content`, sedangkan runtime exception dari mapper menghasilkan `500 Internal Server Error`. ([Jakarta EE](https://jakarta.ee/specifications/restful-ws/4.0/apidocs/jakarta.ws.rs/jakarta/ws/rs/ext/exceptionmapper))

### Error taxonomy

| Exception Type | HTTP Status | Body Policy |
|---|---:|---|
| `ValidationException` | 400 | Field errors aman. |
| `AuthenticationException` | 401 | Jangan ungkap detail token. |
| `AuthorizationException` | 403 | Jangan ungkap rule internal. |
| `EntityNotFoundException` | 404 | Aman bila resource existence tidak sensitive. |
| `ConflictException` | 409 | Jelaskan conflict domain. |
| `RateLimitException` | 429 | Sertakan retry hint bila aman. |
| `ExternalDependencyException` | 502/503/504 | Jangan bocorkan downstream internals. |
| Unknown exception | 500 | Generic message + correlation ID. |

---

## 12. CDI / Jakarta Interceptor untuk Business AOP

Gunakan CDI/Jakarta interceptor untuk concern di sekitar method service, bukan HTTP pipeline.

```java id="v606b3"
@InterceptorBinding
@Retention(RetentionPolicy.RUNTIME)
@Target({ElementType.TYPE, ElementType.METHOD})
public @interface Audited {
    String action();
}
```

```java id="itbzh1"
@Audited(action = "")
@Interceptor
@Priority(Interceptor.Priority.APPLICATION)
public class AuditInterceptor {

    @Inject
    AuditPublisher auditPublisher;

    @AroundInvoke
    public Object around(InvocationContext context) throws Exception {
        long started = System.nanoTime();

        try {
            Object result = context.proceed();

            Audited audited = findAuditedAnnotation(context);
            auditPublisher.publishSuccess(
                audited.action(),
                context.getMethod().getName(),
                System.nanoTime() - started
            );

            return result;
        } catch (Exception e) {
            auditPublisher.publishFailure(
                context.getMethod().getName(),
                e.getClass().getSimpleName()
            );
            throw e;
        }
    }

    private Audited findAuditedAnnotation(InvocationContext context) {
        Audited methodAnnotation = context.getMethod().getAnnotation(Audited.class);
        if (methodAnnotation != null) return methodAnnotation;

        return context.getTarget().getClass().getAnnotation(Audited.class);
    }
}
```

```java id="zd8s88"
@ApplicationScoped
public class OrderApprovalService {

    @Audited(action = "APPROVE_ORDER")
    public ApprovalResult approve(ApproveOrderCommand command) {
        // business operation
        return ApprovalResult.approved(command.orderId());
    }
}
```

Jakarta Interceptors mendefinisikan interception atas business method invocation dan lifecycle/timeout events pada Jakarta EE components dan managed classes. CDI memperluas model ini dengan typesafe interceptor bindings, serta mendukung `@AroundInvoke`, lifecycle callbacks, dan ordering interceptor dengan `@Priority` pada CDI Lite. ([Jakarta EE](https://jakarta.ee/specifications/interceptors/2.2/jakarta-interceptors-spec-2.2))

---

## 13. Priority & Ordering

```text id="4ryv7j"
Request-side chain:
lower @Priority value = runs earlier

Response-side chain:
higher @Priority value = runs earlier
```

Untuk application-supplied providers, `@Priority(1)` lebih tinggi daripada `@Priority(10)`, dan default priority provider adalah `jakarta.ws.rs.Priorities.USER`. Untuk filter/interceptor chains, request-side chains seperti `ContainerRequest`, `PreMatchContainerRequest`, `ClientRequest`, `ReadFrom`, dan `WriteTo` disortir ascending; response-side chains seperti `ContainerResponse` dan `ClientResponse` disortir descending. Jika priority sama, urutannya implementation-dependent. ([Jakarta EE](https://jakarta.ee/specifications/restful-ws/4.0/jakarta-restful-ws-spec-4.0))

### Suggested ordering

| Stage | Priority | Concern |
|---|---:|---|
| Pre-matching normalization | `Priorities.HEADER_DECORATOR` atau custom low number | Method/path/header normalization. |
| Authentication | `Priorities.AUTHENTICATION` | Token parse/verify. |
| Authorization | `Priorities.AUTHORIZATION` | Role/scope/endpoint policy. |
| Header decoration | `Priorities.HEADER_DECORATOR` | Response headers. |
| Entity coding | `Priorities.ENTITY_CODER` | Compression/encryption/body wrapping. |
| User filters | `Priorities.USER` | App-specific default behavior. |

Jangan mengandalkan urutan antar filter dengan priority sama. Untuk production-critical ordering, beri priority eksplisit.

---

## 14. ResourceConfig Registration Pattern

```java id="dzdzip"
public final class AppResourceConfig extends ResourceConfig {

    public AppResourceConfig() {
        // resources
        register(OrderResource.class);
        register(AdminOrderResource.class);

        // global filters
        register(CorrelationIdFilter.class);
        register(AuthenticationFilter.class);
        register(SecurityHeadersFilter.class);

        // name-bound filters
        register(AdminAuthorizationFilter.class);

        // dynamic binding
        register(FeatureGateDynamicFeature.class);

        // entity interceptors
        register(ChecksumReaderInterceptor.class);
        register(ChecksumWriterInterceptor.class);

        // exception mappers
        register(DomainExceptionMapper.class);
        register(ValidationExceptionMapper.class);
        register(UnhandledExceptionMapper.class);
    }
}
```

Provider discovery bisa dilakukan via `@Provider`, tetapi untuk komponen production-critical seperti security filters, error mappers, dan dynamic features, explicit registration di composition root biasanya lebih mudah diaudit. Jakarta REST menyatakan automatic discovery dengan `@Provider` dipakai runtime untuk menemukan provider class melalui class scanning, sedangkan services seperti `Feature` dan `DynamicFeature` juga bisa ditemukan melalui service loading jika service loading aktif. ([Jakarta EE](https://jakarta.ee/specifications/restful-ws/4.0/jakarta-restful-ws-spec-4.0))

---

## 15. Transaction Boundary Pattern

Untuk aplikasi Jersey standalone tanpa full Jakarta EE transaction manager, transaction boundary sering dibuat eksplisit via service wrapper/interceptor di application layer.

```java id="alxhu2"
@InterceptorBinding
@Retention(RetentionPolicy.RUNTIME)
@Target({ElementType.TYPE, ElementType.METHOD})
public @interface TransactionalOperation {}
```

```java id="2s76f2"
@TransactionalOperation
@Interceptor
@Priority(Interceptor.Priority.APPLICATION + 100)
public class TransactionInterceptor {

    @Inject
    TransactionManager transactionManager;

    @AroundInvoke
    public Object around(InvocationContext context) throws Exception {
        Transaction tx = transactionManager.begin();

        try {
            Object result = context.proceed();
            tx.commit();
            return result;
        } catch (Exception e) {
            tx.rollback();
            throw e;
        }
    }
}
```

```java id="lwxvcf"
@ApplicationScoped
public class CaseLifecycleService {

    @TransactionalOperation
    public CaseDecision decide(CaseDecisionCommand command) {
        // update state
        // insert audit
        // enqueue outbox event
        return CaseDecision.accepted(command.caseId());
    }
}
```

Rule: transaction boundary sebaiknya membungkus **use case**, bukan satu repository call kecil dan bukan keseluruhan HTTP pipeline. Jika memakai full Jakarta EE/JTA atau framework transaction lain, ikuti mekanisme container/runtime tersebut.

---

## 16. Security Architecture Pattern

```text id="gzawzg"
Authentication Filter
    ↓
Principal / Claims extracted
    ↓
RequestContext / SecurityContext populated
    ↓
Authorization Filter for endpoint-level policy
    ↓
Service-level domain authorization
    ↓
Audit event emitted
```

Pisahkan:

| Layer | Tanggung Jawab |
|---|---|
| Authentication filter | Membuktikan token/session valid. |
| Endpoint authorization filter | Mengecek role/scope/annotation endpoint. |
| Service authorization | Mengecek rule domain: owner, tenant, state, permission, ACL. |
| Audit | Mencatat actor, action, target, decision, correlation ID. |

Anti-pattern: authorization hanya di resource/filter, padahal rule sebenarnya bergantung pada entity state di database.

---

## 17. Audit Pattern

```java id="u1f1ih"
@InterceptorBinding
@Retention(RetentionPolicy.RUNTIME)
@Target({ElementType.METHOD, ElementType.TYPE})
public @interface AuditAction {
    String value();
}
```

```java id="5y242p"
@AuditAction("ESCALATE_CASE")
public EscalationResult escalate(EscalateCaseCommand command, RequestContext context) {
    CaseRecord record = caseRepository.get(command.caseId(), context.tenantId());

    policy.requireCanEscalate(context.actorId(), record);

    EscalationResult result = escalationEngine.escalate(record, command.reason());

    outbox.insert(AuditEvent.caseEscalated(
        context.correlationId(),
        context.tenantId(),
        context.actorId(),
        command.caseId(),
        command.reason()
    ));

    return result;
}
```

Untuk sistem regulatory/case-management, audit biasanya tidak cukup sebagai log HTTP. Audit harus merekam **business action**, **actor**, **tenant**, **target entity**, **before/after atau decision**, **correlation ID**, dan **waktu**.

---

## 18. Failure Modes

| Symptom | Root Cause | Fix |
|---|---|---|
| Filter tidak jalan | Tidak diregister / `@Provider` tidak discan | Register eksplisit di `ResourceConfig`. |
| Authorization filter jalan untuk semua endpoint | Binding global tidak sengaja | Gunakan `@NameBinding` atau `DynamicFeature`. |
| Filter butuh resource method tapi `null` | Dipasang `@PreMatching` | Jangan pre-matching jika butuh `ResourceInfo`. |
| Response header hilang pada error | Exception/abort path tidak lewat filter yang diharapkan | Pastikan response filter global dan cek exception mapping path. |
| Error mapper tidak terpanggil | Exception sudah ditangkap manual / mapper tidak registered | Register mapper, kurangi catch-all di resource. |
| `ExceptionMapper` menghasilkan 500 | Mapper sendiri throw runtime exception | Mapper harus defensif dan dependency minimal. |
| Interceptor CDI tidak aktif | Target bukan CDI-managed bean | Pastikan service dibuat oleh CDI/Weld, bukan `new`. |
| Audit dobel | Filter + interceptor sama-sama emit audit event | Pisahkan HTTP access log vs business audit. |
| Transaction terlalu luas | Transaction dimulai di request filter | Pindahkan ke use-case method. |
| Ordering flaky | Priority sama | Set `@Priority` eksplisit. |
| Sensitive data bocor | Logging filter log full body/header | Redact token, cookie, secret, PII. |
| Body habis terbaca | Filter membaca entity stream tanpa reset | Gunakan reader interceptor / buffering hati-hati. |
| Tenant leak | Tenant hanya dari header, tidak diverifikasi | Cocokkan tenant dengan principal/claims. |

---

## 19. Production Checklist

| Checklist | Status Target |
|---|---|
| Security filters diregister eksplisit | Wajib |
| Filter priority eksplisit untuk auth/authz/correlation | Wajib |
| `@PreMatching` hanya untuk concern sebelum matching | Wajib |
| Name-bound filter untuk endpoint-specific concern | Wajib |
| DynamicFeature untuk annotation/metadata-driven behavior | Wajib jika rule conditional |
| Error response punya taxonomy konsisten | Wajib |
| Unknown exception tidak bocor stack trace | Wajib |
| Response error membawa correlation ID | Wajib |
| Audit business action tidak dicampur dengan access log | Wajib |
| Transaction boundary di service/use-case | Wajib |
| Request/response body logging dibatasi dan redacted | Wajib |
| Priority sama tidak dipakai untuk ordering penting | Wajib |
| Cross-cutting behavior dites dengan integration test | Wajib |

---

## 20. Roadmap Belajar Singkat

| Urutan | Fokus | Target Praktik |
|---:|---|---|
| 1 | Request/response filter | Bisa membuat correlation/auth/security-header filter. |
| 2 | Priority & ordering | Bisa menjelaskan urutan request dan response chain. |
| 3 | Name binding | Bisa membuat annotation endpoint-specific seperti `@RequireAdmin`. |
| 4 | DynamicFeature | Bisa bind filter berdasarkan annotation/method metadata. |
| 5 | Reader/writer interceptor | Bisa membedakan entity transformation vs HTTP metadata. |
| 6 | ExceptionMapper | Bisa membuat error taxonomy konsisten. |
| 7 | CDI/Jakarta interceptor | Bisa membuat audit/transaction method interceptor. |
| 8 | Failure modeling | Bisa debug kenapa filter/interceptor/mapper tidak aktif. |

## Rekomendasi Materi Selanjutnya

Lanjut ke **cheatsheet: “Jersey/JAX-RS Security Cross-Cutting Implementation - authentication filter, authorization filter, principal/context model, endpoint annotation, service-level policy, and audit trail.”**

**Sources:**

- [Jakarta RESTful Web Services](https://jakarta.ee/specifications/restful-ws/4.0/jakarta-restful-ws-spec-4.0)

## Response:
7/13/2026, 12:09:29 AM

# Cheatsheet - Jersey/JAX-RS Security Cross-Cutting Implementation

## 1. Scope

Cheatsheet ini fokus pada desain security cross-cutting di aplikasi **Jersey/JAX-RS**:

```text id="pncbtg"
HTTP Request
  ↓
Authentication Filter
  ↓
Principal / Claims / SecurityContext
  ↓
Endpoint Authorization Filter
  ↓
RequestContext / TenantContext
  ↓
Service-Level Policy
  ↓
Repository/Data Boundary
  ↓
Audit Trail
  ↓
HTTP Response / Error Mapping
```

Tujuan utamanya: **authentication membuktikan siapa aktornya**, **authorization menentukan apa yang boleh dilakukan**, **service policy menjaga aturan domain**, dan **audit trail merekam keputusan security secara defensible**.

OWASP API Security Top 10 2023 menempatkan **Broken Object Level Authorization** dan **Broken Authentication** sebagai risiko utama API; artinya, filter auth saja tidak cukup bila object-level/domain-level authorization tidak diterapkan di service layer. ([OWASP Foundation](https://owasp.org/www-project-api-security/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 2. Security Layering Model

```text id="jv02qg"
Security Responsibilities
│
├─ 1. Transport Security
│  ├─ HTTPS / TLS termination
│  ├─ trusted proxy headers
│  └─ secure channel detection
│
├─ 2. Authentication
│  ├─ parse Authorization header
│  ├─ verify token/session/client certificate
│  ├─ validate issuer/audience/expiry/signature
│  └─ create Principal / ClaimsPrincipal
│
├─ 3. Endpoint Authorization
│  ├─ role
│  ├─ scope
│  ├─ permission
│  ├─ endpoint annotation
│  └─ coarse access decision
│
├─ 4. Domain Authorization
│  ├─ object ownership
│  ├─ tenant boundary
│  ├─ case/order/process state
│  ├─ ACL / permission matrix
│  └─ regulatory rule
│
├─ 5. Data Boundary
│  ├─ tenant filter in query
│  ├─ row/schema/database boundary
│  ├─ no implicit fallback tenant
│  └─ least privilege DB account
│
└─ 6. Audit Trail
   ├─ actor
   ├─ tenant
   ├─ action
   ├─ target resource
   ├─ decision
   ├─ reason
   └─ correlation ID
```

Rule: **endpoint authorization menjawab “boleh memanggil API ini?”; domain authorization menjawab “boleh melakukan aksi ini terhadap object ini dalam state ini?”**

---

## 3. Core Security Objects

```java id="v44zta"
public record AuthenticatedPrincipal(
    String subject,
    String displayName,
    Set<String> roles,
    Set<String> scopes,
    Set<String> permissions,
    Set<String> tenantIds,
    Map<String, Object> claims
) implements Principal {

    @Override
    public String getName() {
        return subject;
    }

    public boolean hasRole(String role) {
        return roles.contains(role);
    }

    public boolean hasScope(String scope) {
        return scopes.contains(scope);
    }

    public boolean hasPermission(String permission) {
        return permissions.contains(permission);
    }

    public boolean canAccessTenant(String tenantId) {
        return tenantIds.contains(tenantId);
    }
}
```

```java id="ndogho"
public record SecurityDecision(
    boolean allowed,
    String reasonCode,
    String safeMessage
) {
    public static SecurityDecision allow() {
        return new SecurityDecision(true, "ALLOW", "Allowed");
    }

    public static SecurityDecision deny(String reasonCode, String safeMessage) {
        return new SecurityDecision(false, reasonCode, safeMessage);
    }
}
```

JAX-RS menyediakan `SecurityContext` sebagai interface injectable untuk informasi security request, termasuk principal, role check, secure-channel check, dan authentication scheme. ([Jakarta EE](https://jakarta.ee/specifications/restful-ws/4.0/apidocs/jakarta.ws.rs/jakarta/ws/rs/core/securitycontext))

---

## 4. Authentication Filter

Authentication filter bertugas **membaca credential**, **memverifikasi credential**, lalu **mengisi principal/security context**. Jangan taruh rule domain di sini.

```java id="sbb8mm"
@Provider
@Priority(Priorities.AUTHENTICATION)
public final class BearerAuthenticationFilter implements ContainerRequestFilter {

    public static final String PRINCIPAL_PROPERTY = "app.security.principal";

    private final TokenVerifier tokenVerifier;

    @Inject
    public BearerAuthenticationFilter(TokenVerifier tokenVerifier) {
        this.tokenVerifier = tokenVerifier;
    }

    @Override
    public void filter(ContainerRequestContext request) {
        String authorization = request.getHeaderString(HttpHeaders.AUTHORIZATION);

        if (authorization == null || authorization.isBlank()) {
            abortUnauthorized(request, "AUTH_MISSING", "Authentication is required");
            return;
        }

        if (!authorization.startsWith("Bearer ")) {
            abortUnauthorized(request, "AUTH_SCHEME_UNSUPPORTED", "Bearer token is required");
            return;
        }

        String token = authorization.substring("Bearer ".length()).trim();

        TokenClaims claims;
        try {
            claims = tokenVerifier.verify(token);
        } catch (TokenVerificationException e) {
            abortUnauthorized(request, "AUTH_INVALID", "Invalid authentication token");
            return;
        }

        AuthenticatedPrincipal principal = PrincipalMapper.from(claims);

        request.setProperty(PRINCIPAL_PROPERTY, principal);
        request.setSecurityContext(new PrincipalSecurityContext(
            principal,
            request.getSecurityContext().isSecure()
        ));
    }

    private void abortUnauthorized(ContainerRequestContext request,
                                   String code,
                                   String message) {
        request.abortWith(
            Response.status(Response.Status.UNAUTHORIZED)
                .header(HttpHeaders.WWW_AUTHENTICATE, "Bearer")
                .type(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
                .entity(new ErrorResponse(code, message))
                .build()
        );
    }
}
```

`ContainerRequestContext#setSecurityContext(...)` dapat mengganti security context injectable untuk request saat ini, dan `abortWith(...)` menghentikan filter chain lalu mengirim response yang tetap melewati response filters yang applicable. ([Jakarta EE](https://jakarta.ee/specifications/restful-ws/4.0/apidocs/jakarta.ws.rs/jakarta/ws/rs/container/containerrequestcontext?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 5. Custom `SecurityContext`

```java id="ul4nl5"
public final class PrincipalSecurityContext implements SecurityContext {

    private final AuthenticatedPrincipal principal;
    private final boolean secure;

    public PrincipalSecurityContext(AuthenticatedPrincipal principal, boolean secure) {
        this.principal = principal;
        this.secure = secure;
    }

    @Override
    public Principal getUserPrincipal() {
        return principal;
    }

    @Override
    public boolean isUserInRole(String role) {
        return principal != null && principal.hasRole(role);
    }

    @Override
    public boolean isSecure() {
        return secure;
    }

    @Override
    public String getAuthenticationScheme() {
        return "Bearer";
    }
}
```

`SecurityContext#getUserPrincipal()` harus mengembalikan principal user saat authenticated, `isUserInRole(...)` mengecek role logical, `isSecure()` menunjukkan secure channel seperti HTTPS, dan `getAuthenticationScheme()` mengembalikan skema autentikasi. ([Jakarta EE](https://jakarta.ee/specifications/restful-ws/4.0/apidocs/jakarta.ws.rs/jakarta/ws/rs/core/securitycontext))

---

## 6. Token Verification Contract

Jangan biarkan resource/filter mengetahui detail library JWT/OAuth. Buat contract kecil.

```java id="fchlk7"
public interface TokenVerifier {
    TokenClaims verify(String rawToken) throws TokenVerificationException;
}
```

```java id="z7i7dx"
public record TokenClaims(
    String issuer,
    String subject,
    String audience,
    Instant expiresAt,
    Instant issuedAt,
    Set<String> roles,
    Set<String> scopes,
    Set<String> permissions,
    Set<String> tenantIds,
    Map<String, Object> rawClaims
) {}
```

Untuk bearer token, sifat dasarnya adalah siapa pun yang memegang token dapat menggunakannya; RFC 6750 menekankan bearer token tidak membutuhkan proof-of-possession cryptographic key material dari pemegang token. Karena itu token harus diproteksi, tidak dilog, dan wajib dikirim lewat channel aman. ([IETF Datatracker](https://datatracker.ietf.org/doc/html/rfc6750?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Untuk JWT, validasi minimal yang sering wajib: signature, algorithm allowlist, issuer, audience, expiry, not-before, token type/use, dan key id. RFC 7519 menyatakan JWT dengan `aud` harus ditolak bila penerima token tidak termasuk audience yang dituju; RFC 8725 juga menekankan algorithm verification dan penggunaan algorithm yang tepat. ([IETF Datatracker](https://datatracker.ietf.org/doc/html/rfc7519?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 7. Endpoint Authorization Annotation

Gunakan annotation untuk coarse-grained endpoint authorization.

```java id="uy5xfk"
@NameBinding
@Retention(RetentionPolicy.RUNTIME)
@Target({ElementType.TYPE, ElementType.METHOD})
public @interface RequireScope {
    String[] value();
}
```

```java id="xlvy4k"
@Provider
@RequireScope({})
@Priority(Priorities.AUTHORIZATION)
public final class ScopeAuthorizationFilter implements ContainerRequestFilter {

    @Context
    ResourceInfo resourceInfo;

    @Override
    public void filter(ContainerRequestContext request) {
        RequireScope annotation = resolveAnnotation();

        if (annotation == null || annotation.value().length == 0) {
            return;
        }

        AuthenticatedPrincipal principal =
            (AuthenticatedPrincipal) request.getProperty(
                BearerAuthenticationFilter.PRINCIPAL_PROPERTY
            );

        if (principal == null) {
            throw new NotAuthorizedException("Authentication is required");
        }

        for (String requiredScope : annotation.value()) {
            if (!principal.hasScope(requiredScope)) {
                throw new ForbiddenException("Required scope is missing");
            }
        }
    }

    private RequireScope resolveAnnotation() {
        RequireScope methodAnnotation =
            resourceInfo.getResourceMethod().getAnnotation(RequireScope.class);

        if (methodAnnotation != null) {
            return methodAnnotation;
        }

        return resourceInfo.getResourceClass().getAnnotation(RequireScope.class);
    }
}
```

```java id="a32vk7"
@Path("/orders")
@Produces(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
public final class OrderResource {

    private final OrderService orderService;

    @Inject
    public OrderResource(OrderService orderService) {
        this.orderService = orderService;
    }

    @GET
    @Path("/{id}")
    @RequireScope("orders:read")
    public Response getOrder(@PathParam("id") String orderId,
                             @Context ContainerRequestContext request) {
        AuthenticatedPrincipal principal =
            (AuthenticatedPrincipal) request.getProperty(
                BearerAuthenticationFilter.PRINCIPAL_PROPERTY
            );

        OrderDto result = orderService.getOrder(orderId, principal);
        return Response.ok(result).build();
    }
}
```

Gunakan annotation seperti ini hanya untuk **coarse access**. Object-level decision tetap harus di service layer karena perlu data domain: owner, tenant, state, ACL, delegation, atau process status.

---

## 8. Service-Level Authorization Policy

Service-level policy adalah tempat utama untuk mencegah **Broken Object Level Authorization**.

```java id="x06b8l"
@ApplicationScoped
public class OrderAuthorizationPolicy {

    public SecurityDecision canReadOrder(AuthenticatedPrincipal principal, Order order) {
        if (principal == null) {
            return SecurityDecision.deny("AUTH_REQUIRED", "Authentication is required");
        }

        if (!principal.canAccessTenant(order.tenantId())) {
            return SecurityDecision.deny("TENANT_DENIED", "Tenant access denied");
        }

        if (principal.hasPermission("orders:read:any")) {
            return SecurityDecision.allow();
        }

        if (principal.hasPermission("orders:read:own")
            && order.ownerId().equals(principal.subject())) {
            return SecurityDecision.allow();
        }

        return SecurityDecision.deny("ORDER_READ_DENIED", "Order access denied");
    }
}
```

```java id="ya5dnh"
@ApplicationScoped
public class OrderService {

    private final OrderRepository repository;
    private final OrderAuthorizationPolicy policy;
    private final AuditPublisher auditPublisher;

    @Inject
    public OrderService(OrderRepository repository,
                        OrderAuthorizationPolicy policy,
                        AuditPublisher auditPublisher) {
        this.repository = repository;
        this.policy = policy;
        this.auditPublisher = auditPublisher;
    }

    public OrderDto getOrder(String orderId, AuthenticatedPrincipal principal) {
        Order order = repository.findById(orderId)
            .orElseThrow(() -> new NotFoundException("Order not found"));

        SecurityDecision decision = policy.canReadOrder(principal, order);

        auditPublisher.publish(AuditEvent.authorizationDecision(
            principal.subject(),
            order.tenantId(),
            "READ_ORDER",
            order.id(),
            decision.allowed(),
            decision.reasonCode()
        ));

        if (!decision.allowed()) {
            throw new ForbiddenException(decision.safeMessage());
        }

        return OrderDto.from(order);
    }
}
```

Pattern penting: **load object → authorize against object → execute action**. Jangan authorize hanya dari path parameter `orderId`, karena attacker bisa mengganti ID object milik user/tenant lain.

---

## 9. Tenant Boundary

Tenant boundary jangan hanya mengandalkan header.

```java id="1cyc0z"
public final class TenantResolver {

    public String resolveTenant(ContainerRequestContext request,
                                AuthenticatedPrincipal principal) {
        String tenantId = request.getHeaderString("X-Tenant-Id");

        if (tenantId == null || tenantId.isBlank()) {
            throw new BadRequestException("Missing tenant");
        }

        if (!principal.canAccessTenant(tenantId)) {
            throw new ForbiddenException("Tenant access denied");
        }

        return tenantId;
    }
}
```

```java id="xf6x8m"
public interface OrderRepository {
    Optional<Order> findByIdAndTenant(String orderId, String tenantId);
}
```

Untuk multi-tenant system, repository method sebaiknya membawa tenant boundary secara eksplisit. Kalau repository menyediakan `findById(id)` tanpa tenant, developer mudah lupa memasukkan filter tenant di query.

---

## 10. Permission Model

```text id="nfz26e"
Role
  = coarse grouping
  example: ADMIN, CASE_WORKER, SUPERVISOR

Scope
  = API/client capability
  example: orders:read, orders:write

Permission
  = application/domain capability
  example: ORDER_READ_OWN, ORDER_APPROVE_HIGH_RISK

ACL
  = object-specific grant
  example: user X can view case Y

Policy
  = decision logic using role/scope/permission/ACL/state/tenant
```

| Model | Cocok Untuk | Jangan Dipakai Untuk |
|---|---|---|
| Role | UI/menu/coarse grouping | Object ownership kompleks |
| Scope | OAuth/API-level capability | Domain decision lengkap |
| Permission | Domain capability | Identity proof |
| ACL | Per-object sharing/delegation | Semua endpoint sederhana |
| Policy | Rule yang butuh state/data | Sekadar parse token |

Rule praktis: **role/scope boleh membuka pintu endpoint; policy menentukan boleh tidaknya aksi terhadap object tertentu.**

---

## 11. Authorization Failure Semantics

| Kondisi | Status | Catatan |
|---|---:|---|
| Tidak ada credential | 401 | Sertakan `WWW-Authenticate` bila memakai Bearer. |
| Token invalid/expired | 401 | Jangan detailkan alasan token terlalu spesifik ke client publik. |
| Credential valid, hak kurang | 403 | Principal dikenal tapi tidak boleh. |
| Object tidak ada | 404 | Aman bila existence tidak sensitive. |
| Object ada tapi tidak boleh diketahui | 404 atau 403 | Pilih policy konsisten; 404 dapat mengurangi enumeration. |
| Tenant tidak valid untuk principal | 403 | Jangan fallback ke tenant default. |
| Request security malformed | 400 | Misalnya tenant header format invalid. |

Bearer token error handling perlu hati-hati karena detail error bisa membantu attacker. RFC 6750 mendefinisikan penggunaan `WWW-Authenticate` untuk Bearer dan parameter error seperti `invalid_request`, `invalid_token`, dan `insufficient_scope`, tetapi informasi yang diberikan tetap harus dibatasi sesuai exposure policy. ([IETF Datatracker](https://datatracker.ietf.org/doc/html/rfc6750?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 12. Audit Trail

Audit security harus mencatat **decision**, bukan hanya request masuk.

```java id="bv1fgc"
public record AuditEvent(
    String eventId,
    Instant occurredAt,
    String correlationId,
    String actorId,
    String tenantId,
    String action,
    String targetType,
    String targetId,
    boolean allowed,
    String reasonCode,
    Map<String, Object> attributes
) {
    public static AuditEvent authorizationDecision(
        String actorId,
        String tenantId,
        String action,
        String targetId,
        boolean allowed,
        String reasonCode
    ) {
        return new AuditEvent(
            UUID.randomUUID().toString(),
            Instant.now(),
            Correlation.currentId(),
            actorId,
            tenantId,
            action,
            "ORDER",
            targetId,
            allowed,
            reasonCode,
            Map.of()
        );
    }
}
```

Untuk regulatory/case-management platform, audit idealnya menyimpan: actor, tenant, action, target entity, decision, reason code, correlation ID, timestamp, dan versi policy/rule bila tersedia.

---

## 13. Security Header Filter

```java id="jnkmil"
@Provider
@Priority(Priorities.HEADER_DECORATOR)
public final class SecurityHeadersFilter implements ContainerResponseFilter {

    @Override
    public void filter(ContainerRequestContext request,
                       ContainerResponseContext response) {
        response.getHeaders().putSingle("X-Content-Type-Options", "nosniff");
        response.getHeaders().putSingle("X-Frame-Options", "DENY");
        response.getHeaders().putSingle("Referrer-Policy", "no-referrer");
        response.getHeaders().putSingle("Cache-Control", "no-store");
    }
}
```

Untuk API yang mengembalikan data sensitive, `Cache-Control: no-store` sering lebih aman daripada membiarkan client/proxy menyimpan response. Namun jangan memasang policy secara buta untuk static/public resource yang memang cacheable.

---

## 14. Error Mapper untuk Security

```java id="rls8oh"
@Provider
public final class SecurityExceptionMapper implements ExceptionMapper<SecurityException> {

    @Override
    public Response toResponse(SecurityException exception) {
        ErrorResponse body = new ErrorResponse(
            "ACCESS_DENIED",
            "Access denied"
        );

        return Response.status(Response.Status.FORBIDDEN)
            .type(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
            .entity(body)
            .build();
    }
}
```

```java id="7o5kzp"
@Provider
public final class ForbiddenExceptionMapper implements ExceptionMapper<ForbiddenException> {

    @Override
    public Response toResponse(ForbiddenException exception) {
        return Response.status(Response.Status.FORBIDDEN)
            .type(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
            .entity(new ErrorResponse("FORBIDDEN", "Access denied"))
            .build();
    }
}
```

Security error body harus **safe by default**. Jangan kirim detail seperti “user belongs to tenant A but tried tenant B”, “missing permission X:Y:Z internal”, atau raw token parsing exception.

---

## 15. ResourceConfig Registration

```java id="a33t7a"
public final class AppResourceConfig extends ResourceConfig {

    public AppResourceConfig() {
        register(OrderResource.class);

        // Authentication / authorization
        register(BearerAuthenticationFilter.class);
        register(ScopeAuthorizationFilter.class);

        // Context / headers
        register(SecurityHeadersFilter.class);

        // Error mapping
        register(SecurityExceptionMapper.class);
        register(ForbiddenExceptionMapper.class);
        register(AuthenticationExceptionMapper.class);

        // DI
        register(new AppSecurityBinder());
    }
}
```

Security component sebaiknya diregister eksplisit. Jangan berharap package scanning menemukan semua filter security yang critical.

---

## 16. Implementation Checklist

| Area | Checklist |
|---|---|
| Authentication | Parse `Authorization`, validasi scheme, verifikasi token, set principal. |
| JWT/OAuth token | Validate signature, algorithm, issuer, audience, expiry, nbf, token use. |
| SecurityContext | Set via `request.setSecurityContext(...)`. |
| Endpoint auth | Gunakan annotation untuk scope/role coarse-grained. |
| Domain auth | Policy service mengecek tenant, owner, ACL, state, permission. |
| Tenant | Tenant dari request harus cocok dengan principal/claims. |
| Repository | Query harus membawa tenant/data boundary. |
| Error | 401 vs 403 vs 404 konsisten. |
| Audit | Rekam decision allow/deny, bukan hanya success. |
| Logs | Jangan log token, cookie, secret, PII mentah. |
| Response | Tambahkan security headers sesuai jenis API. |
| Tests | Cover missing token, expired token, wrong tenant, wrong owner, missing permission. |

---

## 17. Failure Modes

| Symptom | Kemungkinan Root Cause | Fix |
|---|---|---|
| User bisa akses object user lain | Authorization hanya role/scope endpoint | Tambahkan object-level policy di service. |
| Cross-tenant data leak | Repository `findById` tanpa tenant | Wajib `findByIdAndTenant` atau DB-level tenant guard. |
| Token expired masih diterima | Verifier tidak validasi `exp` | Tambahkan expiry validation. |
| Token untuk service lain diterima | Tidak validasi `aud` | Wajib cek audience. |
| Algorithm confusion | Algorithm mengikuti header token mentah | Gunakan allowlist algorithm dan key binding. |
| Endpoint public jadi protected | Filter global tanpa allowlist | Tambahkan `@PermitAll`/dynamic feature/public route registry. |
| Endpoint protected jadi public | Annotation lupa dipasang | Default-deny untuk route sensitif atau test coverage. |
| Audit tidak lengkap | Hanya audit success | Audit allow dan deny decision. |
| Error bocor detail | Mapper mengirim exception message mentah | Safe error message + correlation ID. |
| SecurityContext null | Authentication filter tidak jalan / ordering salah | Register eksplisit dan priority authentication. |

---

## 18. Testing Matrix

| Test Case | Expected |
|---|---|
| No `Authorization` header | 401 |
| Wrong auth scheme | 401 |
| Invalid signature | 401 |
| Expired token | 401 |
| Wrong issuer | 401 |
| Wrong audience | 401 |
| Missing scope | 403 |
| Scope exists but object belongs to another tenant | 403/404 sesuai policy |
| Scope exists but object owned by another user | 403/404 sesuai policy |
| Admin permission reads any object in allowed tenant | 200 |
| Admin permission reads object in disallowed tenant | 403 |
| Security error response includes correlation ID | Yes |
| Token value appears in logs | Never |
| Denied access produces audit event | Yes |

---

## 19. Roadmap Belajar Singkat

| Urutan | Fokus | Target Praktik |
|---:|---|---|
| 1 | Authentication filter | Bisa parse Bearer token dan set `SecurityContext`. |
| 2 | Token verifier | Bisa validasi signature, issuer, audience, expiry, algorithm. |
| 3 | Principal model | Bisa map claims ke roles/scopes/permissions/tenants. |
| 4 | Endpoint annotation | Bisa membuat `@RequireScope` / `@RequirePermission`. |
| 5 | Service policy | Bisa implement object-level authorization. |
| 6 | Tenant boundary | Bisa enforce tenant di service dan repository. |
| 7 | Security errors | Bisa bedakan 401/403/404 tanpa bocor detail. |
| 8 | Audit trail | Bisa rekam allow/deny decision dengan correlation ID. |

---
