- [The OAuth 2.0 Authorization Framework: Bearer Token Usage](https://datatracker.ietf.org/doc/html/rfc6750?utm_source=chatgpt.com)

## Response:
7/13/2026, 12:12:27 AM

Saya lanjut ke authorization deep dive: fokusnya membedakan RBAC, ABAC, ACL, permission matrix, tenant isolation, dan object-level authorization supaya tidak berhenti di “cek role di filter”.

# Cheatsheet - Jersey/JAX-RS Authorization Deep Dive  
## RBAC, ABAC, ACL, Permission Matrix, Tenant Isolation, Object-Level Authorization, Policy Testing

## 1. Scope

Cheatsheet ini membahas **authorization design** untuk aplikasi Jersey/JAX-RS, terutama pada microservice yang punya domain kompleks seperti order management, case lifecycle, workflow, approval, escalation, dan multi-tenant access.

```text id="wbysti"
Authentication
  = siapa aktornya?

Authorization
  = aktor ini boleh melakukan aksi ini terhadap object ini,
    dalam tenant ini, pada state ini, melalui channel ini?

Access Control Enforcement
  = di mana decision itu benar-benar dipaksa?

Audit
  = bagaimana decision itu bisa dibuktikan setelah kejadian?
```

Kunci utamanya: **authorization tidak cukup di filter HTTP**. OWASP API Security 2023 menempatkan **Broken Object Level Authorization / BOLA** sebagai risiko API nomor satu; pola serangannya biasanya sederhana: attacker mengganti object ID di path, query, header, atau payload untuk mengakses object milik orang lain. ([OWASP Foundation](https://owasp.org/API-Security/editions/2023/en/0xa1-broken-object-level-authorization/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 2. Mental Model Authorization

```text id="tbtgpe"
Authorization Decision =
    Subject
  + Action
  + Object
  + Tenant
  + Context
  + Policy
  + Data State
```

| Elemen | Contoh | Pertanyaan |
|---|---|---|
| **Subject** | user, service account, system actor | Siapa yang melakukan aksi? |
| **Action** | read, approve, cancel, escalate, assign | Aksi apa yang diminta? |
| **Object** | order, case, task, document, process instance | Object apa yang ditargetkan? |
| **Tenant** | organization, customer, business unit, realm | Dalam boundary tenant mana? |
| **Context** | channel, time, IP, risk level, workflow state | Dalam kondisi apa? |
| **Policy** | rule, permission, ACL, delegation | Aturan apa yang berlaku? |
| **Data State** | status order, case phase, lock state | Object sedang dalam state apa? |

Rule senior-level:

```text id="tnix1v"
Endpoint-level authorization is necessary.
Object-level authorization is mandatory.
State-aware authorization is what makes it correct.
Audit is what makes it defensible.
```

---

## 3. Authorization Model Map

```text id="ia879t"
Authorization Models
│
├─ 1. RBAC - Role-Based Access Control
│  ├─ user → role
│  ├─ role → permission
│  ├─ coarse-grained access
│  └─ good for menus / endpoint grouping
│
├─ 2. Permission-Based Access
│  ├─ user/service → permission
│  ├─ permission → action capability
│  ├─ finer than role
│  └─ good for API operation gating
│
├─ 3. ABAC - Attribute-Based Access Control
│  ├─ subject attributes
│  ├─ object attributes
│  ├─ action attributes
│  ├─ environment attributes
│  └─ good for contextual policy
│
├─ 4. ACL - Access Control List
│  ├─ object → subject → grant
│  ├─ per-object exception/sharing
│  └─ good for delegated access
│
├─ 5. ReBAC - Relationship-Based Access Control
│  ├─ subject relationship to object
│  ├─ owner / member / manager / assignee
│  └─ good for graph-like ownership
│
├─ 6. Tenant Isolation
│  ├─ tenant boundary in token/context
│  ├─ tenant boundary in service policy
│  ├─ tenant boundary in query
│  └─ tenant boundary in audit
│
└─ 7. Policy Engine / Matrix
   ├─ role + permission matrix
   ├─ state transition policy
   ├─ object-level policy
   └─ testable decision table
```

---

## 4. RBAC - Role-Based Access Control

RBAC cocok untuk **coarse-grained grouping**: admin, supervisor, case worker, read-only user, integration service.

NIST menjelaskan model RBAC melalui elemen dasar seperti **users, roles, permissions, operations, objects**, serta relasi antar elemen tersebut. ([NIST Computer Security Resource Center](https://csrc.nist.gov/projects/role-based-access-control?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

```text id="6wvxfy"
User
  ↓ assigned to
Role
  ↓ grants
Permission
  ↓ allows
Operation on Object
```

### Contoh

| Role | Permissions |
|---|---|
| `ORDER_VIEWER` | `ORDER_READ` |
| `ORDER_OPERATOR` | `ORDER_READ`, `ORDER_UPDATE`, `ORDER_CANCEL` |
| `ORDER_APPROVER` | `ORDER_READ`, `ORDER_APPROVE` |
| `CASE_SUPERVISOR` | `CASE_READ`, `CASE_ASSIGN`, `CASE_ESCALATE` |
| `SYSTEM_INTEGRATION` | `ORDER_SYNC`, `CASE_SYNC` |

### Java model

```java id="lz3hfv"
public enum Role {
    ORDER_VIEWER,
    ORDER_OPERATOR,
    ORDER_APPROVER,
    CASE_SUPERVISOR,
    SYSTEM_INTEGRATION
}
```

```java id="xaujh8"
public enum Permission {
    ORDER_READ,
    ORDER_UPDATE,
    ORDER_CANCEL,
    ORDER_APPROVE,
    CASE_READ,
    CASE_ASSIGN,
    CASE_ESCALATE,
    ORDER_SYNC,
    CASE_SYNC
}
```

```java id="retkcb"
public final class RolePermissionRegistry {

    private static final Map<Role, Set<Permission>> ROLE_PERMISSIONS = Map.of(
        Role.ORDER_VIEWER, Set.of(Permission.ORDER_READ),

        Role.ORDER_OPERATOR, Set.of(
            Permission.ORDER_READ,
            Permission.ORDER_UPDATE,
            Permission.ORDER_CANCEL
        ),

        Role.ORDER_APPROVER, Set.of(
            Permission.ORDER_READ,
            Permission.ORDER_APPROVE
        ),

        Role.CASE_SUPERVISOR, Set.of(
            Permission.CASE_READ,
            Permission.CASE_ASSIGN,
            Permission.CASE_ESCALATE
        )
    );

    public static Set<Permission> permissionsFor(Set<Role> roles) {
        return roles.stream()
            .flatMap(role -> ROLE_PERMISSIONS
                .getOrDefault(role, Set.of())
                .stream())
            .collect(Collectors.toUnmodifiableSet());
    }
}
```

### Kapan RBAC cukup?

| Situasi | RBAC Cukup? |
|---|---:|
| Menampilkan menu | Ya |
| Membatasi endpoint admin | Ya |
| Membatasi operasi API umum | Kadang |
| Mengecek owner object | Tidak |
| Mengecek tenant object | Tidak |
| Mengecek workflow state | Tidak |
| Mengecek delegation sementara | Tidak |

RBAC bagus untuk gerbang awal, tapi lemah untuk domain authorization yang bergantung pada object state.

---

## 5. Permission-Based Authorization

Permission lebih detail dari role. Role adalah grouping; permission adalah capability.

```text id="2t69se"
BAD:
  if user.role == ADMIN

BETTER:
  if principal.hasPermission(ORDER_APPROVE)

BEST:
  if policy.canApproveOrder(principal, order, context)
```

### Permission naming

Gunakan format yang stabil dan eksplisit.

```text id="sbijhe"
domain:resource:action:scope
```

Contoh:

| Permission | Makna |
|---|---|
| `order:read:any` | Bisa baca order mana pun dalam tenant yang valid. |
| `order:read:own` | Bisa baca order milik sendiri. |
| `order:update:assigned` | Bisa update order yang assigned ke dirinya. |
| `order:approve:low-risk` | Bisa approve order low-risk. |
| `order:approve:high-risk` | Bisa approve order high-risk. |
| `case:escalate:assigned` | Bisa escalate case yang assigned ke dirinya. |
| `case:reassign:any` | Bisa reassign case dalam tenant. |

### Principal

```java id="bxryi9"
public record PrincipalContext(
    String subjectId,
    Set<String> roles,
    Set<String> permissions,
    Set<String> tenantIds
) {
    public boolean hasPermission(String permission) {
        return permissions.contains(permission);
    }

    public boolean canAccessTenant(String tenantId) {
        return tenantIds.contains(tenantId);
    }
}
```

---

## 6. ABAC - Attribute-Based Access Control

ABAC menentukan authorization berdasarkan atribut subject, object, action, dan environment. NIST SP 800-162 mendefinisikan ABAC sebagai metodologi access control yang mengevaluasi atribut subject, object, operation, dan kadang environment condition terhadap policy/rules/relationships untuk menentukan operasi yang diizinkan. ([NIST Computer Security Resource Center](https://csrc.nist.gov/pubs/sp/800/162/upd2/final?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

```text id="fq1wp2"
ABAC Decision =
    subject.department == object.department
AND subject.clearance >= object.classification
AND action == APPROVE
AND environment.riskScore <= threshold
AND object.status == PENDING_APPROVAL
```

### ABAC attributes

| Attribute Type | Contoh |
|---|---|
| Subject attributes | role, department, clearance, region, tenant, employment status |
| Object attributes | owner, tenant, status, risk level, classification, amount |
| Action attributes | read, update, approve, cancel, assign |
| Environment attributes | time, IP, device trust, channel, emergency mode |
| Relationship attributes | owner, assignee, supervisor, delegated approver |

### Java policy example

```java id="2lyl2l"
public final class OrderApprovalPolicy {

    public AuthorizationDecision canApprove(
        PrincipalContext principal,
        Order order,
        RequestContext request
    ) {
        if (!principal.canAccessTenant(order.tenantId())) {
            return AuthorizationDecision.deny("TENANT_DENIED");
        }

        if (!order.status().equals(OrderStatus.PENDING_APPROVAL)) {
            return AuthorizationDecision.deny("INVALID_ORDER_STATE");
        }

        if (order.riskLevel() == RiskLevel.HIGH
            && !principal.hasPermission("order:approve:high-risk")) {
            return AuthorizationDecision.deny("HIGH_RISK_APPROVAL_DENIED");
        }

        if (order.riskLevel() == RiskLevel.LOW
            && !principal.hasPermission("order:approve:low-risk")) {
            return AuthorizationDecision.deny("LOW_RISK_APPROVAL_DENIED");
        }

        if (order.amount().compareTo(request.approvalLimit()) > 0) {
            return AuthorizationDecision.deny("APPROVAL_LIMIT_EXCEEDED");
        }

        return AuthorizationDecision.allow();
    }
}
```

### Kapan ABAC cocok?

| Cocok | Tidak Cocok |
|---|---|
| Rule banyak bergantung state object | Permission sederhana |
| Multi-tenant + regional policy | Hanya admin/user biasa |
| Risk-based approval | Static menu access |
| Case lifecycle / enforcement workflow | CRUD sederhana |
| Compliance-driven decision | Sistem kecil tanpa domain rule |

ABAC kuat, tapi bisa sulit diaudit bila rule tersebar. Solusinya: policy harus terpusat, eksplisit, dan punya test matrix.

---

## 7. ACL - Access Control List

ACL cocok untuk **per-object grant**, misalnya dokumen/case/order tertentu dibagikan ke user/team tertentu.

```text id="4bbvgo"
Object: CASE-123
  ├─ user:alice → READ
  ├─ user:bob → READ, COMMENT
  ├─ team:fraud-review → READ, UPDATE
  └─ role:case-supervisor → READ, ASSIGN
```

### ACL table model

```sql id="5q1a6w"
CREATE TABLE object_acl (
    id              BIGSERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
    tenant_id       TEXT NOT NULL,
    object_type     TEXT NOT NULL,
    object_id       TEXT NOT NULL,
    subject_type    TEXT NOT NULL, -- USER, TEAM, ROLE, SERVICE
    subject_id      TEXT NOT NULL,
    permission      TEXT NOT NULL,
    granted_by      TEXT NOT NULL,
    granted_at      TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    expires_at      TIMESTAMPTZ NULL,

    UNIQUE (
        tenant_id,
        object_type,
        object_id,
        subject_type,
        subject_id,
        permission
    )
);
```

### ACL check

```java id="djrbuk"
public final class AclService {

    private final AclRepository aclRepository;

    public boolean hasObjectPermission(
        PrincipalContext principal,
        String tenantId,
        String objectType,
        String objectId,
        String permission
    ) {
        if (!principal.canAccessTenant(tenantId)) {
            return false;
        }

        return aclRepository.existsActiveGrant(
            tenantId,
            objectType,
            objectId,
            subjectCandidates(principal),
            permission,
            Instant.now()
        );
    }

    private List<AclSubject> subjectCandidates(PrincipalContext principal) {
        List<AclSubject> subjects = new ArrayList<>();

        subjects.add(new AclSubject("USER", principal.subjectId()));

        for (String role : principal.roles()) {
            subjects.add(new AclSubject("ROLE", role));
        }

        return subjects;
    }
}
```

### ACL failure risk

| Risiko | Penjelasan |
|---|---|
| ACL terlalu granular | Query dan maintenance mahal. |
| ACL tidak expire | Delegation sementara menjadi permanen. |
| ACL tidak tenant-aware | Cross-tenant access leak. |
| ACL tidak diaudit | Sulit tahu siapa memberi akses. |
| ACL dicampur role global | Rule menjadi sulit diprediksi. |

---

## 8. ReBAC - Relationship-Based Access Control

ReBAC berguna saat access bergantung relasi:

```text id="u7a4s3"
user is owner of order
user is assignee of case
user is supervisor of assignee
user is member of investigation team
user is delegated approver for department
```

### Example

```java id="4c48lc"
public final class CaseAccessPolicy {

    public AuthorizationDecision canViewCase(
        PrincipalContext principal,
        CaseRecord caseRecord
    ) {
        if (!principal.canAccessTenant(caseRecord.tenantId())) {
            return AuthorizationDecision.deny("TENANT_DENIED");
        }

        if (caseRecord.ownerId().equals(principal.subjectId())) {
            return AuthorizationDecision.allow();
        }

        if (caseRecord.assigneeId().equals(principal.subjectId())) {
            return AuthorizationDecision.allow();
        }

        if (principal.hasPermission("case:read:any")) {
            return AuthorizationDecision.allow();
        }

        if (principal.hasPermission("case:read:supervised")
            && caseRecord.supervisorIds().contains(principal.subjectId())) {
            return AuthorizationDecision.allow();
        }

        return AuthorizationDecision.deny("CASE_VIEW_DENIED");
    }
}
```

ReBAC sering muncul di case management, workflow approval, CPQ/order management, dan organisasi berhierarki.

---

## 9. Tenant Isolation

Tenant isolation harus muncul di beberapa lapis sekaligus.

```text id="3gdm2z"
Tenant Isolation Layers
│
├─ Token / Principal
│  └─ principal has allowed tenant IDs
│
├─ Request Context
│  └─ selected tenant must be explicit
│
├─ Service Policy
│  └─ selected tenant must match object tenant
│
├─ Repository Query
│  └─ WHERE tenant_id = ?
│
├─ Database Constraint
│  └─ tenant_id NOT NULL, FK includes tenant when possible
│
├─ Cache Key
│  └─ tenant_id part of key
│
├─ Event / Message
│  └─ tenant_id included in payload/metadata
│
└─ Audit
   └─ tenant_id recorded in every decision
```

### Repository pattern

```java id="n7t7p4"
public interface OrderRepository {

    Optional<Order> findByIdAndTenant(String orderId, String tenantId);

    int updateStatus(
        String orderId,
        String tenantId,
        OrderStatus expectedStatus,
        OrderStatus newStatus
    );
}
```

```sql id="2y4cng"
SELECT *
FROM orders
WHERE id = :order_id
  AND tenant_id = :tenant_id;
```

Anti-pattern:

```java id="sr7ed2"
// BAD
repository.findById(orderId);
```

Kalau tenant boundary tidak dipaksa di repository method signature, developer akan lupa.

---

## 10. Object-Level Authorization / BOLA Prevention

BOLA terjadi ketika aplikasi hanya mengecek bahwa user authenticated, tetapi tidak mengecek apakah user berhak atas object ID yang diminta. OWASP menekankan object-level authorization harus dipertimbangkan pada setiap function yang mengakses data source menggunakan ID dari user. ([OWASP Foundation](https://owasp.org/www-project-api-security/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

### Vulnerable pattern

```java id="nah40h"
@GET
@Path("/orders/{id}")
public Response getOrder(@PathParam("id") String id) {
    Order order = repository.findById(id)
        .orElseThrow(NotFoundException::new);

    return Response.ok(OrderDto.from(order)).build();
}
```

Masalah: user bisa mengganti `/orders/1001` menjadi `/orders/1002`.

### Correct pattern

```java id="8rn72w"
@GET
@Path("/orders/{id}")
public Response getOrder(@PathParam("id") String id,
                         @Context ContainerRequestContext request) {
    PrincipalContext principal = principalFrom(request);
    RequestContext context = requestContextFrom(request);

    OrderDto dto = orderService.getOrder(id, principal, context);

    return Response.ok(dto).build();
}
```

```java id="l33r53"
public OrderDto getOrder(
    String orderId,
    PrincipalContext principal,
    RequestContext context
) {
    Order order = repository.findByIdAndTenant(orderId, context.tenantId())
        .orElseThrow(() -> new NotFoundException("Order not found"));

    AuthorizationDecision decision =
        orderPolicy.canRead(principal, order, context);

    auditAuthorization("ORDER_READ", order.id(), principal, context, decision);

    if (!decision.allowed()) {
        throw new ForbiddenException("Access denied");
    }

    return OrderDto.from(order);
}
```

### Object-level authorization checklist

| Check | Required |
|---|---:|
| Object loaded with tenant boundary | Yes |
| Principal tenant matches object tenant | Yes |
| Subject permission/role checked | Yes |
| Ownership/assignment/delegation checked | If applicable |
| Object state checked | If action is state-dependent |
| Decision audited | Yes |
| Deny path tested | Yes |

---

## 11. Object Property-Level Authorization

Authorization juga bisa gagal pada level field/property. OWASP API Security 2023 juga mencakup **Broken Object Property Level Authorization**, yaitu API mengekspos atau menerima property object yang tidak seharusnya dapat diakses atau dimodifikasi oleh user tertentu. ([OWASP Foundation](https://owasp.org/API-Security/editions/2023/en/0xa3-broken-object-property-level-authorization/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

### Example: response field filtering

```java id="79p0nb"
public final class OrderDtoMapper {

    public OrderDto toDto(Order order, PrincipalContext principal) {
        return new OrderDto(
            order.id(),
            order.status(),
            order.amount(),
            canViewMargin(principal) ? order.margin() : null,
            canViewInternalNote(principal) ? order.internalNote() : null
        );
    }

    private boolean canViewMargin(PrincipalContext principal) {
        return principal.hasPermission("order:field:margin:read");
    }

    private boolean canViewInternalNote(PrincipalContext principal) {
        return principal.hasPermission("order:field:internal-note:read");
    }
}
```

### Example: update field authorization

```java id="bqvyr3"
public AuthorizationDecision canUpdateField(
    PrincipalContext principal,
    Order order,
    String fieldName
) {
    return switch (fieldName) {
        case "status" -> principal.hasPermission("order:status:update")
            ? AuthorizationDecision.allow()
            : AuthorizationDecision.deny("STATUS_UPDATE_DENIED");

        case "internalNote" -> principal.hasPermission("order:internal-note:update")
            ? AuthorizationDecision.allow()
            : AuthorizationDecision.deny("INTERNAL_NOTE_UPDATE_DENIED");

        case "discount" -> principal.hasPermission("order:discount:update")
            ? AuthorizationDecision.allow()
            : AuthorizationDecision.deny("DISCOUNT_UPDATE_DENIED");

        default -> AuthorizationDecision.deny("FIELD_UPDATE_DENIED");
    };
}
```

---

## 12. Permission Matrix

Untuk sistem kompleks, permission matrix harus eksplisit, bukan tersembunyi dalam `if` acak.

| Action | Viewer | Operator | Approver | Supervisor | Admin |
|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| Read own order | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes |
| Read any order in tenant | No | No | No | Yes | Yes |
| Update assigned order | No | Yes | No | Yes | Yes |
| Cancel order | No | Yes | No | Yes | Yes |
| Approve low-risk order | No | No | Yes | Yes | Yes |
| Approve high-risk order | No | No | No | Yes | Yes |
| Reassign order | No | No | No | Yes | Yes |
| Delete order | No | No | No | No | Maybe never |

### Matrix-as-code

```java id="gya9q8"
public enum Action {
    READ_ORDER,
    UPDATE_ORDER,
    CANCEL_ORDER,
    APPROVE_LOW_RISK_ORDER,
    APPROVE_HIGH_RISK_ORDER,
    REASSIGN_ORDER
}
```

```java id="ne57io"
public final class PermissionMatrix {

    private static final Map<Action, String> REQUIRED_PERMISSION = Map.of(
        Action.READ_ORDER, "order:read",
        Action.UPDATE_ORDER, "order:update",
        Action.CANCEL_ORDER, "order:cancel",
        Action.APPROVE_LOW_RISK_ORDER, "order:approve:low-risk",
        Action.APPROVE_HIGH_RISK_ORDER, "order:approve:high-risk",
        Action.REASSIGN_ORDER, "order:reassign"
    );

    public boolean hasBasePermission(PrincipalContext principal, Action action) {
        String permission = REQUIRED_PERMISSION.get(action);
        return permission != null && principal.hasPermission(permission);
    }
}
```

Matrix sebaiknya tidak menggantikan policy object. Matrix menjawab “punya capability dasar?”, policy menjawab “boleh pada object/state/context ini?”

---

## 13. State-Aware Authorization

Di domain workflow/case/order, authorization sering bergantung pada state.

```text id="kvbutc"
Order State:
DRAFT
  → SUBMITTED
  → PENDING_APPROVAL
  → APPROVED
  → FULFILLED
  → CLOSED

Authorization:
- DRAFT can be edited by creator
- SUBMITTED can be withdrawn by creator
- PENDING_APPROVAL can be approved by approver
- APPROVED cannot be modified except by supervisor override
- CLOSED read-only
```

### State transition policy

```java id="cynp7v"
public final class OrderStatePolicy {

    public AuthorizationDecision canTransition(
        PrincipalContext principal,
        Order order,
        OrderStatus targetStatus
    ) {
        if (!principal.canAccessTenant(order.tenantId())) {
            return AuthorizationDecision.deny("TENANT_DENIED");
        }

        return switch (order.status()) {
            case DRAFT -> canTransitionFromDraft(principal, order, targetStatus);
            case SUBMITTED -> canTransitionFromSubmitted(principal, order, targetStatus);
            case PENDING_APPROVAL -> canTransitionFromPendingApproval(principal, order, targetStatus);
            case APPROVED -> canTransitionFromApproved(principal, order, targetStatus);
            case CLOSED -> AuthorizationDecision.deny("ORDER_CLOSED");
        };
    }

    private AuthorizationDecision canTransitionFromPendingApproval(
        PrincipalContext principal,
        Order order,
        OrderStatus targetStatus
    ) {
        if (targetStatus == OrderStatus.APPROVED
            && principal.hasPermission("order:approve")) {
            return AuthorizationDecision.allow();
        }

        if (targetStatus == OrderStatus.REJECTED
            && principal.hasPermission("order:reject")) {
            return AuthorizationDecision.allow();
        }

        return AuthorizationDecision.deny("TRANSITION_DENIED");
    }
}
```

Untuk senior engineer, ini penting: **authorization dan state machine sering saling terkait**. Jangan validasi state di satu tempat dan authorization di tempat lain tanpa kontrak yang jelas.

---

## 14. Enforcement Placement

```text id="3ke4kr"
Where to enforce?
│
├─ API Gateway
│  └─ coarse authn/authz, rate limit, token validation
│
├─ JAX-RS Filter
│  └─ endpoint-level role/scope/authn
│
├─ Resource
│  └─ minimal orchestration only
│
├─ Service Policy
│  └─ object/state/domain authorization
│
├─ Repository
│  └─ tenant/data boundary
│
├─ Database
│  └─ constraints, RLS if used, least privilege
│
└─ Audit
   └─ decision evidence
```

OWASP Authorization Cheat Sheet menekankan bahwa permission harus divalidasi dengan benar pada setiap request, karena satu access control check yang terlewat bisa mengkompromikan confidentiality atau integrity resource. ([OWASP Cheat Sheet Series](https://cheatsheetseries.owasp.org/cheatsheets/Authorization_Cheat_Sheet.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

### Placement table

| Concern | Gateway | Filter | Service Policy | Repository | DB |
|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| Token presence | Yes | Yes | No | No | No |
| Token signature | Yes/Maybe | Yes | No | No | No |
| Scope endpoint | Maybe | Yes | Maybe | No | No |
| Tenant selected | No | Yes | Yes | Yes | Maybe |
| Object ownership | No | No | Yes | Maybe | Maybe |
| Object state | No | No | Yes | No | No |
| Field-level access | No | No | Yes | Maybe | No |
| Query tenant filter | No | No | No | Yes | Yes |
| Audit decision | No | Maybe | Yes | No | Maybe |

---

## 15. Deny-by-Default

Deny-by-default berarti sistem menolak akses kecuali ada rule eksplisit yang memperbolehkan.

```java id="xef8wx"
public final class AuthorizationDecision {

    private final boolean allowed;
    private final String reasonCode;

    private AuthorizationDecision(boolean allowed, String reasonCode) {
        this.allowed = allowed;
        this.reasonCode = reasonCode;
    }

    public static AuthorizationDecision allow() {
        return new AuthorizationDecision(true, "ALLOW");
    }

    public static AuthorizationDecision deny(String reasonCode) {
        return new AuthorizationDecision(false, reasonCode);
    }

    public boolean allowed() {
        return allowed;
    }

    public String reasonCode() {
        return reasonCode;
    }
}
```

```java id="3f5f9t"
public AuthorizationDecision canRead(PrincipalContext principal, Order order) {
    if (principal == null) {
        return AuthorizationDecision.deny("NO_PRINCIPAL");
    }

    if (!principal.canAccessTenant(order.tenantId())) {
        return AuthorizationDecision.deny("TENANT_DENIED");
    }

    if (principal.hasPermission("order:read:any")) {
        return AuthorizationDecision.allow();
    }

    if (principal.hasPermission("order:read:own")
        && order.ownerId().equals(principal.subjectId())) {
        return AuthorizationDecision.allow();
    }

    return AuthorizationDecision.deny("ORDER_READ_DENIED");
}
```

OWASP Authorization guidance juga menekankan prinsip least privilege dan deny-by-default sebagai kontrol penting dalam access control. ([OWASP Cheat Sheet Series](https://cheatsheetseries.owasp.org/cheatsheets/Authorization_Cheat_Sheet.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 16. Policy Object Pattern

Jangan letakkan authorization rule langsung di resource.

### Bad

```java id="yj8o1x"
if (principal.hasRole("ADMIN") || order.ownerId().equals(principal.subjectId())) {
    return Response.ok(order).build();
}
throw new ForbiddenException();
```

### Better

```java id="nyunvl"
AuthorizationDecision decision =
    orderAccessPolicy.canRead(principal, order, requestContext);

if (!decision.allowed()) {
    throw new ForbiddenException("Access denied");
}
```

### Policy object

```java id="mrsl2k"
@ApplicationScoped
public final class OrderAccessPolicy {

    public AuthorizationDecision canRead(
        PrincipalContext principal,
        Order order,
        RequestContext context
    ) {
        if (principal == null) {
            return AuthorizationDecision.deny("AUTH_REQUIRED");
        }

        if (!principal.canAccessTenant(order.tenantId())) {
            return AuthorizationDecision.deny("TENANT_DENIED");
        }

        if (!context.tenantId().equals(order.tenantId())) {
            return AuthorizationDecision.deny("REQUEST_TENANT_MISMATCH");
        }

        if (principal.hasPermission("order:read:any")) {
            return AuthorizationDecision.allow();
        }

        if (principal.hasPermission("order:read:own")
            && order.ownerId().equals(principal.subjectId())) {
            return AuthorizationDecision.allow();
        }

        if (principal.hasPermission("order:read:assigned")
            && order.assigneeId().equals(principal.subjectId())) {
            return AuthorizationDecision.allow();
        }

        return AuthorizationDecision.deny("ORDER_READ_DENIED");
    }
}
```

Benefits:

| Benefit | Kenapa Penting |
|---|---|
| Testable | Policy bisa dites tanpa HTTP server. |
| Auditable | Rule authorization terkonsentrasi. |
| Reusable | Bisa dipakai REST, message consumer, batch job. |
| Safer | Tidak tergantung developer ingat menulis `if` di setiap resource. |

---

## 17. Policy Testing Matrix

Authorization harus dites sebagai matrix, bukan hanya happy path.

### Example matrix

| Case | Principal | Object | Action | Expected |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Owner reads own order | owner | same owner, same tenant | read | allow |
| Owner reads another order | owner | other owner, same tenant | read | deny |
| Supervisor reads tenant order | supervisor | same tenant | read | allow |
| Supervisor reads other tenant | supervisor | different tenant | read | deny |
| Approver approves pending low-risk | approver | pending, low risk | approve | allow |
| Approver approves high-risk | approver low-risk only | pending, high risk | approve | deny |
| Admin updates closed order | admin | closed | update | deny or override-only |
| Missing principal | none | any | read | deny |
| Tenant mismatch | valid user | object other tenant | read | deny |

### JUnit-style example

```java id="b1l2pr"
class OrderAccessPolicyTest {

    private final OrderAccessPolicy policy = new OrderAccessPolicy();

    @Test
    void ownerCanReadOwnOrderInSameTenant() {
        PrincipalContext principal = principal(
            "user-1",
            Set.of("order:read:own"),
            Set.of("tenant-a")
        );

        Order order = order(
            "order-1",
            "tenant-a",
            "user-1",
            "assignee-2"
        );

        RequestContext context = requestContext("tenant-a");

        AuthorizationDecision decision =
            policy.canRead(principal, order, context);

        assertTrue(decision.allowed());
    }

    @Test
    void ownerCannotReadOrderInDifferentTenant() {
        PrincipalContext principal = principal(
            "user-1",
            Set.of("order:read:own"),
            Set.of("tenant-a")
        );

        Order order = order(
            "order-1",
            "tenant-b",
            "user-1",
            "assignee-2"
        );

        RequestContext context = requestContext("tenant-a");

        AuthorizationDecision decision =
            policy.canRead(principal, order, context);

        assertFalse(decision.allowed());
        assertEquals("TENANT_DENIED", decision.reasonCode());
    }
}
```

---

## 18. API Security Test Patterns

### BOLA test

```text id="swjsdz"
Given:
  user A owns order-100
  user B owns order-200

Test:
  login as user A
  call GET /orders/order-200

Expected:
  403 or 404
```

### Tenant isolation test

```text id="r6qxuo"
Given:
  user A belongs to tenant-1
  order X belongs to tenant-2

Test:
  login as user A
  send X-Tenant-Id: tenant-2
  call GET /orders/X

Expected:
  403
```

### Field-level test

```text id="dobk0o"
Given:
  operator lacks permission order:field:margin:read

Test:
  GET /orders/{id}

Expected:
  response does not include margin field
```

### State-aware test

```text id="8hps76"
Given:
  order is CLOSED

Test:
  user with order:update permission calls PATCH /orders/{id}

Expected:
  deny because state is closed
```

---

## 19. Audit Model for Authorization

```java id="fwbiu2"
public record AuthorizationAuditEvent(
    String eventId,
    Instant occurredAt,
    String correlationId,
    String actorId,
    String tenantId,
    String action,
    String objectType,
    String objectId,
    boolean allowed,
    String reasonCode,
    String policyName,
    String policyVersion
) {}
```

Audit authorization harus bisa menjawab:

| Pertanyaan | Field |
|---|---|
| Siapa aktornya? | `actorId` |
| Tenant mana? | `tenantId` |
| Aksi apa? | `action` |
| Object apa? | `objectType`, `objectId` |
| Diizinkan atau ditolak? | `allowed` |
| Kenapa? | `reasonCode` |
| Rule mana? | `policyName`, `policyVersion` |
| Request/flow mana? | `correlationId` |
| Kapan? | `occurredAt` |

Untuk sistem regulatory, audit authorization deny juga penting. Jangan hanya audit success.

---

## 20. Failure Modes

| Symptom | Root Cause | Fix |
|---|---|---|
| User bisa baca object user lain | BOLA: hanya cek authenticated/scope | Tambahkan object-level policy. |
| User bisa akses tenant lain | Tenant hanya dari header | Cocokkan header dengan principal dan object tenant. |
| Admin bisa update closed object | Permission tidak state-aware | Tambahkan state transition policy. |
| Endpoint aman, batch job bocor | Authorization hanya di JAX-RS filter | Policy harus reusable di service layer. |
| Field sensitive muncul di response | Tidak ada property-level authorization | Tambahkan DTO mapper berbasis permission. |
| User bisa update field terlarang | PATCH/PUT tidak validate field-level permission | Whitelist field update per permission. |
| ACL grant tidak expire | Tidak ada `expires_at` atau cleanup | Tambahkan expiry dan audit grant. |
| Policy sulit dites | Rule tersebar di resource/service | Ekstrak policy object. |
| 404/403 tidak konsisten | Tidak ada failure semantics | Definisikan exposure policy. |
| Audit tidak defensible | Hanya log HTTP | Simpan decision audit dengan reason code. |
| Cache bocor antar tenant | Cache key tidak pakai tenant | Tambahkan tenant ke cache key. |
| Event bocor antar tenant | Event payload tidak menyimpan tenant | Tenant wajib di event metadata. |

---

## 21. Production Checklist

| Area | Checklist |
|---|---|
| RBAC | Role hanya dipakai untuk grouping/coarse access. |
| Permission | Permission eksplisit dan tidak bergantung hardcoded role string. |
| ABAC | Policy membaca subject/object/action/environment attributes secara jelas. |
| ACL | ACL tenant-aware, audited, dan bisa expire. |
| Tenant | Tenant divalidasi di principal, service, repository, audit. |
| Object-level | Semua endpoint dengan object ID punya object-level authorization. |
| Property-level | Sensitive fields punya read/write permission. |
| State-aware | Action penting mengecek state machine object. |
| Deny default | Semua policy default deny. |
| Service policy | Authorization rule utama ada di policy/service layer, bukan resource. |
| Repository | Query membawa tenant boundary. |
| Audit | Allow dan deny decision direkam. |
| Testing | Ada matrix test untuk role, permission, tenant, ownership, state, field. |
| Error | 401/403/404 policy konsisten dan tidak bocor detail. |

---

## 22. Roadmap Belajar Singkat

| Urutan | Fokus | Target Praktik |
|---:|---|---|
| 1 | RBAC vs permission | Bisa memisahkan role grouping dari permission capability. |
| 2 | Object-level authorization | Bisa mencegah BOLA pada endpoint berbasis object ID. |
| 3 | Tenant isolation | Bisa enforce tenant di principal, service, repository, audit. |
| 4 | ABAC | Bisa membuat policy berbasis subject/object/action/environment. |
| 5 | ACL | Bisa mendesain per-object grant yang audited dan expirable. |
| 6 | State-aware policy | Bisa menggabungkan authorization dengan state machine. |
| 7 | Field-level authorization | Bisa membatasi field response/update. |
| 8 | Policy testing | Bisa membuat authorization decision matrix dan automated tests. |

## Response:
7/13/2026, 12:14:56 AM

# Cheatsheet - Jersey/JAX-RS Policy Testing & Security Test Matrix  
## BOLA Tests, Tenant Isolation, Permission Matrix, Negative Cases, Audit Verification

## 1. Scope

Cheatsheet ini fokus pada **cara mengetes authorization policy** di aplikasi Jersey/JAX-RS, terutama untuk domain yang punya object-level access, multi-tenancy, permission matrix, workflow state, dan audit trail.

```text id="ufnply"
Security Policy Testing
│
├─ Unit Test
│  └─ policy object / decision logic
│
├─ Resource/API Test
│  └─ endpoint authn/authz behavior
│
├─ Integration Test
│  └─ DB tenant boundary + repository query
│
├─ Negative Security Test
│  └─ forbidden object / wrong tenant / missing permission
│
├─ Audit Verification
│  └─ allow/deny decision recorded
│
└─ Regression Matrix
   └─ every role/action/state/tenant combination
```

Intinya: **authorization test bukan hanya memastikan user yang benar bisa akses**, tapi juga memastikan user yang salah **tidak bisa akses**. OWASP API Security 2023 menempatkan **Broken Object Level Authorization** sebagai risiko API utama; serangannya sering berupa manipulasi object ID di path, query, header, atau payload. ([OWASP Foundation](https://owasp.org/API-Security/editions/2023/en/0xa1-broken-object-level-authorization/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 2. Mental Model Testing

```text id="bk0blh"
Authorization Test Case =
    Actor
  + Tenant
  + Permission / Role / Scope
  + Object
  + Object State
  + Requested Action
  + Expected Decision
  + Expected HTTP Status
  + Expected Audit Event
```

Untuk aplikasi production, jangan hanya mengetes:

```text id="mg1n6v"
admin can access order
```

Test yang lebih defensible:

```text id="tf93i0"
user with order:read:own
from tenant A
cannot read order owned by another user
even if order belongs to same tenant
and deny decision is audited
```

---

## 3. Test Pyramid untuk Authorization

```text id="kk2wct"
Authorization Test Pyramid
│
├─ 1. Policy Unit Tests
│  ├─ fast
│  ├─ no HTTP
│  ├─ no DB
│  └─ tests pure decision logic
│
├─ 2. Service Tests
│  ├─ object loaded
│  ├─ policy invoked
│  ├─ audit emitted
│  └─ exception semantics checked
│
├─ 3. Resource / Jersey Tests
│  ├─ filters active
│  ├─ annotations active
│  ├─ HTTP status checked
│  └─ error body checked
│
├─ 4. Integration Tests
│  ├─ real DB or Testcontainers
│  ├─ tenant query boundary
│  ├─ ACL table
│  └─ audit/outbox persisted
│
└─ 5. Contract / Regression Matrix
   ├─ role-action matrix
   ├─ tenant matrix
   ├─ object-state matrix
   └─ negative cases
```

OWASP Authorization Cheat Sheet menekankan bahwa permission harus divalidasi pada setiap request dan access control sebaiknya bisa dikonfigurasi secara global/application-wide, karena satu check yang terlewat cukup untuk membahayakan confidentiality atau integrity resource. ([OWASP Cheat Sheet Series](https://cheatsheetseries.owasp.org/cheatsheets/Authorization_Cheat_Sheet.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 4. Authorization Test Matrix Template

| Dimension | Values |
|---|---|
| Actor | anonymous, owner, assignee, supervisor, admin, service account |
| Tenant | same tenant, different tenant, missing tenant, invalid tenant |
| Permission | none, read own, read assigned, read any, approve low risk, approve high risk |
| Object ownership | owned, assigned, unrelated |
| Object state | draft, submitted, pending approval, approved, closed |
| Action | read, update, cancel, approve, assign, escalate |
| Expected decision | allow, deny |
| Expected HTTP | 200, 201, 204, 400, 401, 403, 404, 409 |
| Audit | allow event, deny event, no event for malformed auth, security alert |

---

## 5. BOLA Test Matrix

BOLA / IDOR test wajib ada untuk semua endpoint yang menerima object ID dari user. OWASP menjelaskan object ID bisa berada di path, query string, header, atau payload; semua itu harus dianggap sebagai input yang bisa dimanipulasi attacker. ([OWASP Foundation](https://owasp.org/API-Security/editions/2023/en/0xa1-broken-object-level-authorization/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

### Matrix

| Case | Actor | Object | Expected |
|---|---|---|---|
| Owner reads own object | user A | object owned by user A | allow |
| Owner reads another user object | user A | object owned by user B | deny |
| Same tenant, different owner | user A | user B object in same tenant | deny unless permission allows |
| Different tenant | user A tenant-1 | object tenant-2 | deny |
| Admin same tenant | admin tenant-1 | object tenant-1 | allow |
| Admin different tenant | admin tenant-1 | object tenant-2 | deny unless cross-tenant admin explicitly allowed |
| Service account allowed object | service account | allowed integration object | allow |
| Service account random object | service account | unrelated object | deny |

### BOLA API test example

```java id="x3kjaz"
@Test
void userCannotReadAnotherUsersOrder() {
    String userAToken = tokenForUser(
        "user-a",
        "tenant-a",
        Set.of("order:read:own")
    );

    String orderOwnedByUserB = seedOrder(
        "tenant-a",
        "user-b",
        OrderStatus.SUBMITTED
    );

    given()
        .auth().oauth2(userAToken)
        .header("X-Tenant-Id", "tenant-a")
    .when()
        .get("/orders/{id}", orderOwnedByUserB)
    .then()
        .statusCode(403)
        .body("code", equalTo("FORBIDDEN"));
}
```

REST Assured mendukung request HTTP seperti GET, POST, PUT, DELETE, OPTIONS, PATCH, dan HEAD, sehingga cocok untuk resource/API-level test pada REST service. ([GitHub](https://github.com/rest-assured/rest-assured/wiki/usage?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 6. Tenant Isolation Test Matrix

Tenant isolation tidak boleh hanya diuji di filter. Test harus membuktikan tenant boundary berlaku di **principal**, **request context**, **service policy**, **repository query**, **cache key**, dan **audit**.

| Case | Principal Tenant | Request Tenant | Object Tenant | Expected |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Same tenant | A | A | A | allow if permission valid |
| Request tenant mismatch | A | B | A | deny |
| Object tenant mismatch | A | A | B | deny/not found |
| Principal multi-tenant valid | A,B | B | B | allow if permission valid |
| Principal lacks tenant | A | B | B | deny |
| Missing tenant header | A | missing | A | 400 or default policy-specific deny |
| Invalid tenant format | A | malformed | A | 400 |
| Admin same tenant | A | A | A | allow |
| Admin cross-tenant without permission | A | B | B | deny |
| System account explicit tenant | service | B | B | allow only if service policy allows |

### Repository integration test

```java id="hddj9p"
@Test
void repositoryMustNotReturnObjectFromDifferentTenant() {
    seedOrder("order-1", "tenant-a", "user-a");
    seedOrder("order-2", "tenant-b", "user-b");

    Optional<Order> result =
        repository.findByIdAndTenant("order-2", "tenant-a");

    assertTrue(result.isEmpty());
}
```

### Anti-test smell

```java id="isrbij"
// Weak test: this does not prove tenant isolation.
repository.findById("order-2");
```

Repository API harus memaksa tenant boundary di signature, misalnya `findByIdAndTenant(id, tenantId)`.

---

## 7. Permission Matrix Tests

Permission matrix test memastikan role/permission mapping tidak berubah diam-diam.

```text id="5n1hr5"
Role
  ↓
Permissions
  ↓
Actions
  ↓
Policy Decision
```

### Example matrix

| Role | Permission | Read Own | Read Any | Update Assigned | Approve Low | Approve High |
|---|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| Viewer | `order:read:own` | Yes | No | No | No | No |
| Operator | `order:update:assigned` | Yes | No | Yes | No | No |
| Approver | `order:approve:low-risk` | Yes | No | No | Yes | No |
| Supervisor | `order:read:any`, `order:approve:high-risk` | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes |
| Admin | explicit admin permissions | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes |

### JUnit parameterized test

```java id="34hb08"
@ParameterizedTest
@CsvSource({
    "VIEWER, order:read:own, true",
    "VIEWER, order:read:any, false",
    "OPERATOR, order:update:assigned, true",
    "APPROVER, order:approve:low-risk, true",
    "APPROVER, order:approve:high-risk, false",
    "SUPERVISOR, order:approve:high-risk, true"
})
void rolePermissionMatrixIsStable(
    Role role,
    String permission,
    boolean expected
) {
    Set<String> permissions =
        RolePermissionRegistry.permissionsFor(Set.of(role));

    assertEquals(expected, permissions.contains(permission));
}
```

JUnit mendukung parameterized tests dan `@CsvSource` untuk mengekspresikan argument list sebagai CSV literal, sehingga cocok untuk permission matrix yang eksplisit dan mudah direview. ([JUnit User Guide](https://docs.junit.org/6.1.1/writing-tests/parameterized-classes-and-tests.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 8. Object-State Authorization Tests

Untuk domain workflow/case/order, permission saja tidak cukup. State object harus diuji.

| State | Action | Actor | Expected |
|---|---|---|---|
| `DRAFT` | update | creator | allow |
| `DRAFT` | approve | approver | deny |
| `SUBMITTED` | withdraw | creator | allow |
| `PENDING_APPROVAL` | approve low-risk | approver low-risk | allow |
| `PENDING_APPROVAL` | approve high-risk | approver low-risk | deny |
| `APPROVED` | update | operator | deny |
| `CLOSED` | update | admin | deny unless explicit override |
| `CLOSED` | read | viewer | allow if object-level allowed |

### Policy unit test

```java id="9if2mg"
@ParameterizedTest
@CsvSource({
    "DRAFT, UPDATE, CREATOR, true",
    "DRAFT, APPROVE, APPROVER, false",
    "PENDING_APPROVAL, APPROVE_LOW_RISK, APPROVER, true",
    "PENDING_APPROVAL, APPROVE_HIGH_RISK, APPROVER, false",
    "APPROVED, UPDATE, OPERATOR, false",
    "CLOSED, UPDATE, ADMIN, false"
})
void orderStateAuthorizationMatrix(
    OrderStatus status,
    Action action,
    ActorKind actorKind,
    boolean expectedAllowed
) {
    PrincipalContext principal = principalFor(actorKind);
    Order order = orderWithStatus(status);

    AuthorizationDecision decision =
        orderStatePolicy.canPerform(principal, order, action);

    assertEquals(expectedAllowed, decision.allowed());
}
```

---

## 9. Field-Level Authorization Tests

OWASP API Security 2023 juga memasukkan **Broken Object Property Level Authorization**, yaitu user bisa membaca atau mengubah property object yang tidak seharusnya. Field-level test wajib untuk data sensitive seperti margin, internal note, risk score, regulatory flag, PII, dan decision rationale.

### Read field matrix

| Field | Permission Required | No Permission | With Permission |
|---|---|---:|---:|
| `amount` | basic read | visible | visible |
| `margin` | `order:field:margin:read` | hidden/null | visible |
| `internalNote` | `order:field:internal-note:read` | hidden/null | visible |
| `riskScore` | `order:field:risk-score:read` | hidden/null | visible |
| `customerPii` | `customer:pii:read` | redacted | visible |

### API response test

```java id="6wvvl2"
@Test
void responseMustNotExposeMarginWithoutPermission() {
    String token = tokenForUser(
        "user-a",
        "tenant-a",
        Set.of("order:read:any")
    );

    String orderId = seedOrderWithMargin("tenant-a", "user-b");

    given()
        .auth().oauth2(token)
        .header("X-Tenant-Id", "tenant-a")
    .when()
        .get("/orders/{id}", orderId)
    .then()
        .statusCode(200)
        .body("$", not(hasKey("margin")));
}
```

### Update field matrix

| Field | Actor | Expected |
|---|---|---|
| `status` | operator with status permission | allow |
| `status` | viewer | deny |
| `internalNote` | operator without internal-note permission | deny |
| `discount` | operator with discount limit exceeded | deny |
| `riskScore` | any non-risk-service user | deny |

---

## 10. Endpoint Annotation Tests

Kalau memakai annotation seperti `@RequireScope`, `@RequirePermission`, `@RequireAdmin`, test harus memastikan annotation benar-benar aktif.

### Test missing scope

```java id="e1bh5g"
@Test
void endpointRequiresOrderReadScope() {
    String token = tokenForUser(
        "user-a",
        "tenant-a",
        Set.of() // no order:read
    );

    given()
        .auth().oauth2(token)
        .header("X-Tenant-Id", "tenant-a")
    .when()
        .get("/orders/{id}", "order-1")
    .then()
        .statusCode(403);
}
```

### Test route accidentally public

```java id="cejggh"
@Test
void protectedEndpointMustRejectAnonymousRequest() {
    given()
        .header("X-Tenant-Id", "tenant-a")
    .when()
        .get("/orders/{id}", "order-1")
    .then()
        .statusCode(401);
}
```

### Annotation coverage scan

```java id="leib3c"
@Test
void allNonPublicResourceMethodsMustDeclareSecurityAnnotation() {
    Set<Method> unsecuredMethods =
        ResourceSecurityScanner.findUnsecuredMethods("com.company.app.api");

    assertTrue(
        unsecuredMethods.isEmpty(),
        "Unsecured resource methods: " + unsecuredMethods
    );
}
```

Test ini berguna jika policy internal adalah **default protected**. Untuk public endpoint, gunakan annotation eksplisit seperti `@PublicEndpoint`.

---

## 11. Negative Case Matrix

Security test yang bagus punya negative cases lebih banyak daripada positive cases.

| Negative Case | Expected |
|---|---|
| No token | 401 |
| Malformed token | 401 |
| Expired token | 401 |
| Wrong issuer | 401 |
| Wrong audience | 401 |
| Missing scope | 403 |
| Wrong tenant | 403 |
| Missing tenant | 400/403 sesuai policy |
| Object belongs to other user | 403/404 |
| Object belongs to other tenant | 403/404 |
| State transition invalid | 409/403 sesuai policy |
| Field update forbidden | 403 |
| ACL expired | 403 |
| Permission revoked | 403 |
| Service account calls human-only endpoint | 403 |
| User calls service-only endpoint | 403 |

### Why negative tests matter

OWASP Authorization guidance menekankan **deny by default** dan least privilege; test matrix harus membuktikan bahwa tidak ada fallback permissive ketika rule tidak cocok. ([OWASP Cheat Sheet Series](https://cheatsheetseries.owasp.org/cheatsheets/Authorization_Cheat_Sheet.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 12. Audit Verification Tests

Authorization test belum lengkap kalau tidak mengecek audit trail, terutama untuk platform regulatory/case-management.

### Audit event schema

```java id="4g9nop"
public record AuthorizationAuditEvent(
    String eventId,
    Instant occurredAt,
    String correlationId,
    String actorId,
    String tenantId,
    String action,
    String objectType,
    String objectId,
    boolean allowed,
    String reasonCode,
    String policyName,
    String policyVersion
) {}
```

### Allow audit test

```java id="szg3z9"
@Test
void allowedReadMustProduceAuditEvent() {
    PrincipalContext principal = principal(
        "user-a",
        Set.of("order:read:own"),
        Set.of("tenant-a")
    );

    Order order = order("order-1", "tenant-a", "user-a");

    OrderDto result = orderService.getOrder(
        order.id(),
        principal,
        requestContext("tenant-a", "corr-123")
    );

    AuthorizationAuditEvent event =
        auditRepository.findLatestByCorrelationId("corr-123").orElseThrow();

    assertEquals("user-a", event.actorId());
    assertEquals("tenant-a", event.tenantId());
    assertEquals("ORDER_READ", event.action());
    assertEquals("order-1", event.objectId());
    assertTrue(event.allowed());
    assertEquals("ALLOW", event.reasonCode());
}
```

### Deny audit test

```java id="4axfdq"
@Test
void deniedReadMustProduceAuditEvent() {
    PrincipalContext principal = principal(
        "user-a",
        Set.of("order:read:own"),
        Set.of("tenant-a")
    );

    Order order = order("order-1", "tenant-a", "user-b");

    assertThrows(
        ForbiddenException.class,
        () -> orderService.getOrder(
            order.id(),
            principal,
            requestContext("tenant-a", "corr-456")
        )
    );

    AuthorizationAuditEvent event =
        auditRepository.findLatestByCorrelationId("corr-456").orElseThrow();

    assertEquals("ORDER_READ_DENIED", event.reasonCode());
    assertFalse(event.allowed());
}
```

Audit deny penting karena banyak insiden security justru terlihat dari pola percobaan akses yang ditolak.

---

## 13. Service-Level Policy Unit Test

Policy harus bisa dites tanpa Jersey, tanpa HTTP server, dan tanpa DB.

```java id="fl90lm"
class OrderAccessPolicyTest {

    private final OrderAccessPolicy policy = new OrderAccessPolicy();

    @Test
    void ownerWithReadOwnPermissionCanReadOwnOrder() {
        PrincipalContext principal = principal(
            "user-a",
            Set.of("order:read:own"),
            Set.of("tenant-a")
        );

        Order order = order(
            "order-1",
            "tenant-a",
            "user-a",
            OrderStatus.SUBMITTED
        );

        AuthorizationDecision decision =
            policy.canRead(principal, order, requestContext("tenant-a"));

        assertTrue(decision.allowed());
    }

    @Test
    void ownerCannotReadOwnOrderFromDifferentRequestTenant() {
        PrincipalContext principal = principal(
            "user-a",
            Set.of("order:read:own"),
            Set.of("tenant-a")
        );

        Order order = order(
            "order-1",
            "tenant-a",
            "user-a",
            OrderStatus.SUBMITTED
        );

        AuthorizationDecision decision =
            policy.canRead(principal, order, requestContext("tenant-b"));

        assertFalse(decision.allowed());
        assertEquals("REQUEST_TENANT_MISMATCH", decision.reasonCode());
    }
}
```

Rule: setiap policy method minimal punya test untuk **allow**, **deny karena tenant**, **deny karena permission**, **deny karena ownership**, dan **deny karena state**.

---

## 14. Service Test dengan Mock Repository + Audit

Service test memastikan urutan ini benar:

```text id="4sgmel"
load object with tenant boundary
    ↓
evaluate policy
    ↓
audit decision
    ↓
allow: return result
deny: throw safe exception
```

```java id="g1zjxd"
@Test
void serviceMustAuditBeforeThrowingForbidden() {
    OrderRepository repository = mock(OrderRepository.class);
    AuditPublisher auditPublisher = mock(AuditPublisher.class);
    OrderAccessPolicy policy = new OrderAccessPolicy();

    Order order = order("order-1", "tenant-a", "user-b");

    when(repository.findByIdAndTenant("order-1", "tenant-a"))
        .thenReturn(Optional.of(order));

    OrderService service = new OrderService(
        repository,
        policy,
        auditPublisher
    );

    PrincipalContext principal = principal(
        "user-a",
        Set.of("order:read:own"),
        Set.of("tenant-a")
    );

    assertThrows(
        ForbiddenException.class,
        () -> service.getOrder("order-1", principal, requestContext("tenant-a"))
    );

    verify(auditPublisher).publish(argThat(event ->
        event.action().equals("ORDER_READ")
            && event.objectId().equals("order-1")
            && !event.allowed()
            && event.reasonCode().equals("ORDER_READ_DENIED")
    ));
}
```

---

## 15. API-Level Test Structure

Untuk Jersey/JAX-RS resource test, targetnya adalah memastikan filter, annotation, mapper, dan HTTP semantics bekerja.

```text id="832s7u"
API Test should verify:
- authentication filter active
- authorization filter active
- endpoint annotation active
- policy denial becomes correct HTTP status
- error body safe
- correlation ID returned
- audit event created if expected
```

### Example

```java id="4kq7rb"
@Test
void forbiddenResponseMustBeSafeAndContainCorrelationId() {
    String token = tokenForUser(
        "user-a",
        "tenant-a",
        Set.of("order:read:own")
    );

    String otherUserOrder = seedOrder("tenant-a", "user-b");

    given()
        .auth().oauth2(token)
        .header("X-Tenant-Id", "tenant-a")
        .header("X-Correlation-Id", "corr-test-001")
    .when()
        .get("/orders/{id}", otherUserOrder)
    .then()
        .statusCode(403)
        .header("X-Correlation-Id", "corr-test-001")
        .body("code", equalTo("FORBIDDEN"))
        .body("message", equalTo("Access denied"))
        .body("message", not(containsString("user-b")))
        .body("message", not(containsString("tenant-a")));
}
```

---

## 16. Contract-First Authorization Test Cases

Jika memakai OpenAPI contract-first, tambahkan metadata security requirement dan turunkan test matrix dari contract.

```yaml id="v4y1s6"
paths:
  /orders/{orderId}:
    get:
      operationId: getOrder
      security:
        - bearerAuth: [order:read]
      x-authorization:
        objectType: ORDER
        objectIdParam: orderId
        tenantRequired: true
        objectLevelPolicy: OrderAccessPolicy.canRead
```

OpenAPI standard security scheme biasanya tidak cukup untuk menjelaskan object-level authorization. Ini salah satu alasan object-level policy harus dijadikan convention internal, custom extension, atau test metadata sendiri. Riset tentang OpenAPI dan BOLA juga mencatat bahwa properti security OpenAPI tidak secara standar menangani object authorization. ([arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2212.06606?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 17. Regression Matrix as Data

Untuk policy kompleks, simpan matrix sebagai CSV/resource agar bisa direview oleh engineering, QA, security, dan business.

```csv id="3iu7h8"
caseId,actor,permissions,actorTenant,requestTenant,objectTenant,owner,state,action,expected,reason
C001,owner,order:read:own,tenant-a,tenant-a,tenant-a,owner,SUBMITTED,READ,ALLOW,ALLOW
C002,owner,order:read:own,tenant-a,tenant-a,tenant-a,other,SUBMITTED,READ,DENY,ORDER_READ_DENIED
C003,admin,order:read:any,tenant-a,tenant-a,tenant-b,other,SUBMITTED,READ,DENY,TENANT_DENIED
C004,approver,order:approve:low-risk,tenant-a,tenant-a,tenant-a,other,PENDING_APPROVAL,APPROVE_LOW_RISK,ALLOW,ALLOW
C005,approver,order:approve:low-risk,tenant-a,tenant-a,tenant-a,other,PENDING_APPROVAL,APPROVE_HIGH_RISK,DENY,HIGH_RISK_APPROVAL_DENIED
```

```java id="e7irfy"
@ParameterizedTest
@CsvFileSource(resources = "/authorization/order-policy-matrix.csv", numLinesToSkip = 1)
void orderPolicyMatrix(
    String caseId,
    String actor,
    String permissions,
    String actorTenant,
    String requestTenant,
    String objectTenant,
    String owner,
    String state,
    String action,
    String expected,
    String reason
) {
    PrincipalContext principal = principalFromRow(actor, permissions, actorTenant);
    Order order = orderFromRow(objectTenant, owner, state);
    RequestContext context = requestContext(requestTenant);

    AuthorizationDecision decision =
        orderPolicy.canPerform(principal, order, Action.valueOf(action));

    assertEquals("ALLOW".equals(expected), decision.allowed(), caseId);
    assertEquals(reason, decision.reasonCode(), caseId);
}
```

---

## 18. Test Data Design

Authorization test data harus sengaja dibuat untuk menangkap boundary.

```text id="yhy2p0"
Tenants:
  tenant-a
  tenant-b

Users:
  user-a-owner-tenant-a
  user-b-owner-tenant-a
  user-c-owner-tenant-b
  supervisor-tenant-a
  admin-tenant-a
  global-admin-explicit

Objects:
  order-a-owned-by-user-a
  order-b-owned-by-user-b-same-tenant
  order-c-owned-by-user-c-other-tenant
  order-closed
  order-pending-low-risk
  order-pending-high-risk
```

### Seed helpers

```java id="avdkpp"
public final class AuthorizationFixtures {

    public PrincipalContext ownerTenantA() {
        return principal(
            "user-a",
            Set.of("order:read:own"),
            Set.of("tenant-a")
        );
    }

    public PrincipalContext supervisorTenantA() {
        return principal(
            "supervisor-a",
            Set.of("order:read:any", "order:update:assigned"),
            Set.of("tenant-a")
        );
    }

    public Order orderOwnedByUserA() {
        return order("order-a", "tenant-a", "user-a", OrderStatus.SUBMITTED);
    }

    public Order orderOwnedByUserBInSameTenant() {
        return order("order-b", "tenant-a", "user-b", OrderStatus.SUBMITTED);
    }

    public Order orderOwnedByUserCInOtherTenant() {
        return order("order-c", "tenant-b", "user-c", OrderStatus.SUBMITTED);
    }
}
```

---

## 19. Mutation-Style Security Tests

Test bagus harus gagal bila authorization check dihapus.

Contoh mutation yang harus membuat test gagal:

```text id="q9xp2w"
- remove tenant check
- remove owner check
- replace permission with role ADMIN
- return allow by default
- skip audit on deny
- change repository.findByIdAndTenant to findById
- ignore object state
- expose sensitive field by default
```

### Practical approach

| Mutation | Test yang Harus Gagal |
|---|---|
| Remove tenant check | Tenant isolation matrix |
| Remove ownership check | BOLA same-tenant different-owner test |
| Remove state check | State-aware authorization test |
| Remove permission check | Permission matrix test |
| Remove audit deny | Audit verification test |
| Use `findById` | Repository tenant boundary test |
| Default allow | Negative case matrix |

---

## 20. CI Gate

Authorization tests harus jadi CI gate, bukan manual checklist.

```yaml id="imqy15"
name: security-policy-tests

on:
  pull_request:
    branches: [ main ]

jobs:
  authorization-tests:
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest

    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v4

      - uses: actions/setup-java@v4
        with:
          distribution: temurin
          java-version: '17'

      - name: Run authorization policy tests
        run: mvn test -Dgroups=authorization

      - name: Run integration security tests
        run: mvn verify -Pintegration-security
```

Minimum CI rule:

```text id="vq9a1g"
No merge if:
- policy matrix test fails
- BOLA regression test fails
- tenant isolation test fails
- security annotation coverage fails
- audit verification test fails
```

---

## 21. Test Categories

| Category | Speed | Dependencies | Purpose |
|---|---:|---|---|
| Policy unit test | Fast | None | Pure decision correctness. |
| Service authorization test | Fast/medium | Mock repo/audit | Enforcement order. |
| Resource auth test | Medium | Jersey test runtime | Filter/annotation/status mapping. |
| Repository tenant test | Medium | Test DB | Tenant query boundary. |
| End-to-end API security test | Slower | App + DB | Realistic regression. |
| Contract security test | Medium | OpenAPI + scanner/custom rule | Coverage of protected operations. |

---

## 22. Production Checklist

| Checklist | Status |
|---|---|
| Semua endpoint object-ID punya BOLA test | Wajib |
| Semua tenant-sensitive endpoint punya tenant isolation test | Wajib |
| Permission matrix disimpan sebagai data/test eksplisit | Wajib |
| Negative cases lebih lengkap daripada happy path | Wajib |
| Policy unit test tidak butuh HTTP/DB | Wajib |
| Repository method memaksa tenant boundary | Wajib |
| Field-level sensitive data punya read/write tests | Wajib |
| Audit allow dan deny diverifikasi | Wajib |
| Error body security-safe dites | Wajib |
| Annotation coverage dites | Wajib jika pakai annotation security |
| CI memblokir regression authorization | Wajib |
| Test data mencakup same-tenant/different-owner dan different-tenant | Wajib |

---

## 23. Failure Modes

| Symptom | Root Cause | Test yang Harus Menangkap |
|---|---|---|
| User A bisa akses object user B | Missing object-level policy | BOLA test |
| Admin tenant A bisa akses tenant B | Tenant check terlalu longgar | Tenant isolation test |
| Endpoint baru lupa security annotation | No annotation coverage test | Resource security scan |
| Denied access tidak terekam | Audit hanya success path | Audit deny test |
| Field sensitive muncul di response | Mapper tidak permission-aware | Field-level read test |
| User bisa PATCH field terlarang | Update validator tidak field-aware | Field-level write test |
| Closed object bisa diubah | Missing state policy | State-aware test |
| Service account bisa akses endpoint human | Principal type tidak dicek | Negative actor-type test |
| Error message bocor detail tenant/user | Mapper pakai exception message mentah | Error body safety test |
| Cache bocor tenant | Cache key tanpa tenant | Integration/cache test |

---

## 24. Roadmap Belajar Singkat

| Urutan | Fokus | Target Praktik |
|---:|---|---|
| 1 | Policy unit tests | Bisa mengetes pure authorization decision tanpa HTTP/DB. |
| 2 | BOLA tests | Bisa membuktikan object ID manipulation ditolak. |
| 3 | Tenant isolation tests | Bisa membuktikan cross-tenant access tidak mungkin. |
| 4 | Permission matrix tests | Bisa menjadikan role/permission/action matrix sebagai regression suite. |
| 5 | State-aware tests | Bisa mengetes authorization yang bergantung lifecycle object. |
| 6 | Field-level tests | Bisa mencegah data/property exposure. |
| 7 | Audit verification | Bisa membuktikan allow/deny decision tercatat. |
| 8 | CI security gate | Bisa membuat authorization regression memblokir merge. |
