## Response:
7/13/2026, 12:17:19 AM

# Cheatsheet - Jersey/JAX-RS Runtime Profile & Environment Strategy  
## Local / Dev / Test / Staging / Prod, Maven Profile, Docker/Kubernetes Env, Secret Injection, Deployment Invariants

## 1. Scope

Cheatsheet ini membahas cara mendesain **runtime profile dan environment strategy** untuk aplikasi Jersey/JAX-RS.

```text id="mj90l6"
Source Code
  ↓
Build Profile / Maven Profile
  ↓
Artifact: JAR/WAR/Image
  ↓
Runtime Profile
  ↓
Environment Variables / JVM Properties / Mounted Files
  ↓
ConfigMap / Secret / Secret Manager
  ↓
Deployment Invariants
  ↓
Startup Validation
```

Poin paling penting: **build profile dan runtime profile harus dipisahkan**. Maven profile mengubah proses build. Runtime profile mengubah perilaku aplikasi saat dijalankan. Jangan menjadikan Maven profile sebagai satu-satunya mekanisme konfigurasi environment production.

---

## 2. Build-Time vs Runtime-Time

```text id="xsxgyh"
Build-time concern:
  - dependency selection
  - test grouping
  - code generation
  - packaging mode
  - Docker image build
  - migration artifact packaging

Runtime concern:
  - DB URL
  - credentials
  - tenant mode
  - feature flags
  - external service endpoints
  - timeout/retry
  - logging level
  - secret source
```

| Concern | Maven Profile | Runtime Profile |
|---|---:|---:|
| Skip integration tests | Yes | No |
| Activate code generation | Yes | No |
| Choose packaging variant | Yes | No |
| DB URL production | No | Yes |
| DB password | Never | Yes, via secret source |
| Feature flag | Usually no | Yes |
| Log level | No | Yes |
| Kubernetes namespace | No | Yes |
| External API endpoint | No | Yes |

Maven profiles memang bisa aktif berdasarkan property/system property/CLI user property dan kondisi environment tertentu, tetapi itu tetap berada di konteks **build model**, bukan runtime contract aplikasi. ([Apache Maven Profiles](https://maven.apache.org/guides/introduction/introduction-to-profiles.html))

---

## 3. Runtime Profile Model

```text id="vwjcxq"
RuntimeProfile
│
├─ LOCAL
│  ├─ developer machine
│  ├─ docker compose
│  └─ permissive local defaults
│
├─ DEV
│  ├─ shared dev env
│  ├─ unstable integration
│  └─ non-production data
│
├─ TEST
│  ├─ automated test
│  ├─ CI integration test
│  └─ isolated ephemeral resources
│
├─ STAGING
│  ├─ production-like
│  ├─ real deployment topology
│  └─ production invariant rehearsal
│
└─ PROD
   ├─ explicit config only
   ├─ no fallback secret
   ├─ no localhost dependency
   └─ strict validation
```

### Java enum

```java id="be77gl"
public enum RuntimeProfile {
    LOCAL,
    DEV,
    TEST,
    STAGING,
    PROD;

    public static RuntimeProfile parse(String value) {
        if (value == null || value.isBlank()) {
            return LOCAL;
        }

        return RuntimeProfile.valueOf(
            value.trim().toUpperCase(Locale.ROOT)
        );
    }

    public boolean isProductionLike() {
        return this == STAGING || this == PROD;
    }

    public boolean allowsLocalFallbacks() {
        return this == LOCAL || this == TEST;
    }
}
```

**Rule:** `LOCAL` boleh punya default nyaman. `PROD` dan `STAGING` tidak boleh punya fallback yang menyembunyikan salah konfigurasi.

---

## 4. Profile Policy Table

| Profile | Tujuan | Data | Config Strictness | Secret Source | Deployment Style |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `LOCAL` | Developer onboarding | Dummy/local | Low/medium | `.env`, local secret file | Docker Compose / local run |
| `DEV` | Shared integration | Non-prod | Medium | Dev secret store / K8s Secret | Dev namespace |
| `TEST` | CI automation | Ephemeral | Medium/high | CI secret / Testcontainers | CI pipeline |
| `STAGING` | Production rehearsal | Prod-like masked | High | Secret manager / K8s Secret | Production-like cluster |
| `PROD` | Real production | Production | Highest | Secret manager / controlled Secret | Controlled rollout |

Anti-pattern:

```text id="fudoml"
APP_PROFILE missing
  ↓
fallback to LOCAL
  ↓
application starts in production
  ↓
uses local defaults / weak behavior
```

Correct behavior:

```text id="j7t646"
APP_PROFILE missing in production-like deployment
  ↓
startup fails
  ↓
deployment blocked
```

---

## 5. Recommended Runtime Config Precedence

```text id="43j8op"
1. Explicit command-line/runtime override
2. Environment variable
3. JVM system property
4. External mounted config file
5. Classpath default config
6. Hardcoded safe default
```

ConfigMap dapat dikonsumsi Pod sebagai environment variable, command-line argument, atau file konfigurasi dalam volume; Secret juga dapat dimount sebagai volume atau diekspos sebagai environment variable ke container. Pisahkan keduanya: ConfigMap untuk non-secret, Secret untuk sensitive value. ([Kubernetes ConfigMap](https://kubernetes.io/docs/concepts/configuration/configmap/), [Kubernetes Secret](https://kubernetes.io/docs/concepts/configuration/secret/))

### Config keys convention

| Kind | Env Var | JVM Property | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| Profile | `APP_PROFILE` | `app.profile` | Wajib eksplisit di staging/prod. |
| DB URL | `DB_URL` | `db.url` | Non-secret tapi sensitive topology. |
| DB username | `DB_USERNAME` | `db.username` | Non-secret-ish, tetap jangan diumbar. |
| DB password | `DB_PASSWORD` | `db.password` | Secret. |
| HTTP timeout | `HTTP_READ_TIMEOUT` | `http.readTimeout` | Format `PT10S`. |
| Feature flag | `FEATURE_NEW_FLOW` | `feature.newFlow` | Default harus jelas. |
| Config file path | `CONFIG_FILE` | `config.file` | Untuk mounted file. |

---

## 6. AppConfig + Invariants

```java id="bewdt2"
public record AppConfig(
    RuntimeProfile profile,
    DbConfig database,
    HttpClientConfig httpClient,
    SecurityConfig security,
    FeatureFlags featureFlags
) {
    public void validate() {
        database.validate();
        httpClient.validate();
        security.validate();

        if (profile.isProductionLike()) {
            validateProductionLike();
        }
    }

    private void validateProductionLike() {
        if (database.url().contains("localhost")
            || database.url().contains("127.0.0.1")) {
            throw new IllegalStateException(
                "Production-like profile must not use localhost DB"
            );
        }

        if (database.password().isDefaultOrWeak()) {
            throw new IllegalStateException(
                "Production-like profile must not use default DB password"
            );
        }

        if (!security.requireTls()) {
            throw new IllegalStateException(
                "Production-like profile must require TLS-aware deployment"
            );
        }
    }
}
```

### Deployment invariants

| Invariant | Local | Dev | Test | Staging | Prod |
|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| `APP_PROFILE` explicit | Optional | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes |
| DB password required | Optional | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes |
| No localhost DB | No | Prefer | No for CI | Yes | Yes |
| TLS/proxy awareness | Optional | Prefer | Optional | Yes | Yes |
| Secret not in image | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes |
| Startup validation | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes |
| Audit enabled | Optional | Yes | Optional | Yes | Yes |
| Debug endpoint disabled | Optional | Prefer | Optional | Yes | Yes |
| Stack trace hidden from client | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes |

---

## 7. Maven Profile Strategy

Maven profile sebaiknya dipakai untuk **build behavior**, bukan runtime secret/config.

### Good Maven profile usage

```xml id="0md6nf"
<profiles>
    <profile>
        <id>integration-test</id>
        <activation>
            <property>
                <name>runITs</name>
                <value>true</value>
            </property>
        </activation>
        <build>
            <plugins>
                <plugin>
                    <groupId>org.apache.maven.plugins</groupId>
                    <artifactId>maven-failsafe-plugin</artifactId>
                    <version>${maven-failsafe-plugin.version}</version>
                    <executions>
                        <execution>
                            <goals>
                                <goal>integration-test</goal>
                                <goal>verify</goal>
                            </goals>
                        </execution>
                    </executions>
                </plugin>
            </plugins>
        </build>
    </profile>
</profiles>
```

Maven mendukung aktivasi profile lewat property seperti `-DrunITs=true`, JDK, OS, file, dan mekanisme aktivasi lain. Ini berguna untuk mengubah build/test behavior, tetapi jangan digunakan untuk menyisipkan password production ke artifact. ([Apache Maven Profiles](https://maven.apache.org/guides/introduction/introduction-to-profiles.html))

### Dangerous Maven profile usage

```xml id="24pfig"
<!-- BAD -->
<profile>
    <id>prod</id>
    <properties>
        <db.url>jdbc:postgresql://prod-db:5432/app</db.url>
        <db.password>prod-secret</db.password>
    </properties>
</profile>
```

Masalahnya:

```text id="25z710"
secret masuk source control
artifact bisa mengandung config environment
build ulang dibutuhkan untuk ganti runtime config
sulit rotate secret
sulit audit siapa memakai config apa
```

---

## 8. Docker Strategy

### Dockerfile

```dockerfile id="lav1j6"
FROM eclipse-temurin:17-jre

WORKDIR /app

COPY target/order-service.jar /app/order-service.jar

ENV JAVA_OPTS=""
ENV APP_PROFILE="LOCAL"

ENTRYPOINT ["sh", "-c", "java $JAVA_OPTS -jar /app/order-service.jar"]
```

Dockerfile `ENV` menyetel environment variable yang tersedia untuk instruksi berikutnya dan tetap ada ketika container dijalankan dari image; Docker docs juga mengingatkan bahwa persistence environment variable bisa menimbulkan efek samping, dan `ARG` tidak dipersist ke final container seperti `ENV`. ([Dockerfile reference](https://docs.docker.com/reference/dockerfile/))

### Production caution

Jangan letakkan secret sebagai `ENV` di Dockerfile:

```dockerfile id="sx8kpl"
# BAD
ENV DB_PASSWORD="prod-password"
```

Kenapa?

```text id="a6f07g"
Docker image layer dapat tersebar
docker inspect bisa memperlihatkan ENV
secret rotation butuh rebuild image
artifact tidak environment-agnostic
```

---

## 9. Docker Compose Local Strategy

```yaml id="8qlvdm"
services:
  order-service:
    image: order-service:local
    build:
      context: .
    environment:
      APP_PROFILE: LOCAL
      DB_URL: jdbc:postgresql://postgres:5432/orderdb
      DB_USERNAME: order_app
      DB_PASSWORD: order_password
      HTTP_READ_TIMEOUT: PT10S
    ports:
      - "8080:8080"
    depends_on:
      - postgres

  postgres:
    image: postgres:16
    environment:
      POSTGRES_DB: orderdb
      POSTGRES_USER: order_app
      POSTGRES_PASSWORD: order_password
    ports:
      - "5432:5432"
```

Docker Compose dapat menyetel environment variable dengan atribut `environment`, mendukung mapping/list syntax, dan juga mendukung `env_file`; dokumentasinya juga memperingatkan agar tidak memakai environment variable untuk informasi sensitif seperti password dan menyarankan secrets untuk itu. ([Docker Compose environment variables](https://docs.docker.com/compose/how-tos/environment-variables/set-environment-variables/))

### Local `.env`

```properties id="ly9a1z"
APP_PROFILE=LOCAL
DB_URL=jdbc:postgresql://localhost:5432/orderdb
DB_USERNAME=order_app
DB_PASSWORD=order_password
HTTP_READ_TIMEOUT=PT10S
```

`.env` cocok untuk local onboarding. Jangan perlakukan `.env` local sebagai model production.

---

## 10. Kubernetes ConfigMap + Secret Strategy

### ConfigMap untuk non-secret

```yaml id="o01zns"
apiVersion: v1
kind: ConfigMap
metadata:
  name: order-service-config
data:
  APP_PROFILE: "PROD"
  DB_URL: "jdbc:postgresql://postgres-prod:5432/orderdb"
  DB_USERNAME: "order_app"
  HTTP_READ_TIMEOUT: "PT10S"
  HTTP_CONNECT_TIMEOUT: "PT3S"
  FEATURE_NEW_ORDER_FLOW: "false"
```

ConfigMap ditujukan untuk non-confidential key-value data dan membantu memisahkan konfigurasi dari image container. Pod bisa mengonsumsi ConfigMap sebagai env var, argumen command-line, atau file konfigurasi dalam volume. ([Kubernetes ConfigMap](https://kubernetes.io/docs/concepts/configuration/configmap/))

### Secret untuk sensitive value

```yaml id="9rn9yp"
apiVersion: v1
kind: Secret
metadata:
  name: order-service-secret
type: Opaque
stringData:
  DB_PASSWORD: "replace-me"
  JWT_VERIFICATION_KEY: "replace-me"
```

Secret dapat digunakan sebagai mounted volume atau environment variable dalam Pod; Secret juga dapat dipakai oleh komponen lain di cluster untuk berinteraksi dengan sistem eksternal. ([Kubernetes Secret](https://kubernetes.io/docs/concepts/configuration/secret/))

### Deployment envFrom

```yaml id="v4vn3k"
apiVersion: apps/v1
kind: Deployment
metadata:
  name: order-service
spec:
  replicas: 3
  selector:
    matchLabels:
      app: order-service
  template:
    metadata:
      labels:
        app: order-service
    spec:
      containers:
        - name: order-service
          image: registry.example.com/order-service:1.12.4
          envFrom:
            - configMapRef:
                name: order-service-config
            - secretRef:
                name: order-service-secret
```

Kubernetes juga mendukung `env` dan `envFrom` untuk menyetel environment variable container; keduanya punya efek berbeda, `env` untuk entry individual dan `envFrom` untuk mengambil banyak key dari ConfigMap/Secret. ([Kubernetes env vars](https://kubernetes.io/docs/tasks/inject-data-application/define-environment-variable-container/))

---

## 11. Mounted File Strategy

Gunakan mounted file bila config lebih kompleks dari scalar key-value.

```yaml id="3mnghm"
apiVersion: v1
kind: ConfigMap
metadata:
  name: order-service-file-config
data:
  application.yaml: |
    app:
      profile: PROD
    http:
      readTimeout: PT10S
      connectTimeout: PT3S
    feature:
      newOrderFlow: false
```

```yaml id="grz2kc"
apiVersion: apps/v1
kind: Deployment
metadata:
  name: order-service
spec:
  template:
    spec:
      containers:
        - name: order-service
          image: registry.example.com/order-service:1.12.4
          env:
            - name: CONFIG_FILE
              value: /etc/order-service/application.yaml
          volumeMounts:
            - name: app-config
              mountPath: /etc/order-service
              readOnly: true
      volumes:
        - name: app-config
          configMap:
            name: order-service-file-config
```

### Env var vs mounted file

| Need | Env Var | Mounted File |
|---|---:|---:|
| Simple scalar | Good | Overkill |
| DB URL / timeout / flag | Good | Optional |
| JSON/YAML policy | Bad | Good |
| Certificate bundle | Bad | Good |
| Large config | Bad | Good |
| Secret file | Possible but risky | Better, with controlled mount |
| Hot reload | Limited | Possible but app must support reload |

---

## 12. Secret Injection Strategy

```text id="skwn3e"
Secret Sources
│
├─ Local
│  ├─ .env ignored by Git
│  └─ local secret file
│
├─ CI
│  ├─ CI secret variables
│  └─ ephemeral credentials
│
├─ Kubernetes
│  ├─ Secret as env var
│  ├─ Secret as mounted file
│  └─ external secret operator / CSI driver
│
├─ Cloud
│  ├─ AWS Secrets Manager / Parameter Store
│  ├─ Azure Key Vault
│  └─ managed identity / workload identity
│
└─ On-Prem
   ├─ Vault
   ├─ mounted secret file
   └─ systemd environment file with strict permission
```

### Secret handling rules

| Rule | Reason |
|---|---|
| Secret tidak masuk Git | Menghindari exposure permanen. |
| Secret tidak masuk Dockerfile | ENV Dockerfile persist di image. |
| Secret tidak masuk default properties | Classpath resource ikut artifact. |
| Secret tidak di-log | Log sering disalin ke banyak sistem. |
| Secret value tidak muncul di exception | Stack trace bisa terkirim ke log/monitoring. |
| Secret bisa dirotate | Production credential harus bisa diganti tanpa rebuild artifact. |
| Secret dipisah dari config non-secret | Audit dan access control lebih jelas. |

---

## 13. Java Config Loader Pattern

```java id="7ki5gd"
public final class RuntimeConfigLoader {

    public static AppConfig load() {
        RuntimeProfile profile = RuntimeProfile.parse(
            read("APP_PROFILE", "app.profile", null)
        );

        AppConfig config = new AppConfig(
            profile,
            loadDbConfig(),
            loadHttpClientConfig(),
            loadSecurityConfig(),
            loadFeatureFlags()
        );

        config.validate();
        return config;
    }

    private static DbConfig loadDbConfig() {
        return new DbConfig(
            required("DB_URL", "db.url"),
            required("DB_USERNAME", "db.username"),
            SecretValue.of(required("DB_PASSWORD", "db.password"))
        );
    }

    private static String read(
        String envKey,
        String propertyKey,
        String defaultValue
    ) {
        String envValue = System.getenv(envKey);
        if (envValue != null && !envValue.isBlank()) {
            return envValue;
        }

        String propertyValue = System.getProperty(propertyKey);
        if (propertyValue != null && !propertyValue.isBlank()) {
            return propertyValue;
        }

        return defaultValue;
    }

    private static String required(String envKey, String propertyKey) {
        String value = read(envKey, propertyKey, null);

        if (value == null || value.isBlank()) {
            throw new IllegalStateException(
                "Missing required config: " + envKey + " / " + propertyKey
            );
        }

        return value;
    }
}
```

### Production-like guard

```java id="ok6q6i"
public final class DeploymentInvariantValidator {

    public static void validate(AppConfig config) {
        if (!config.profile().isProductionLike()) {
            return;
        }

        assertRequired("APP_PROFILE");
        assertNotLocalhost(config.database().url(), "DB_URL");
        assertSecretNotDefault(config.database().password(), "DB_PASSWORD");
        assertNoDebugFeature(config.featureFlags());
    }

    private static void assertRequired(String envKey) {
        String value = System.getenv(envKey);

        if (value == null || value.isBlank()) {
            throw new IllegalStateException(
                envKey + " must be explicit in production-like profile"
            );
        }
    }

    private static void assertNotLocalhost(String value, String key) {
        if (value.contains("localhost") || value.contains("127.0.0.1")) {
            throw new IllegalStateException(
                key + " must not use localhost in production-like profile"
            );
        }
    }

    private static void assertSecretNotDefault(
        SecretValue value,
        String key
    ) {
        if (value.isDefaultOrWeak()) {
            throw new IllegalStateException(
                key + " must not use default or weak value"
            );
        }
    }

    private static void assertNoDebugFeature(FeatureFlags flags) {
        if (flags.debugEndpointsEnabled()) {
            throw new IllegalStateException(
                "Debug endpoints must be disabled in production-like profile"
            );
        }
    }
}
```

---

## 14. ResourceConfig Integration

```java id="5lyxuz"
public final class AppResourceConfig extends ResourceConfig {

    public AppResourceConfig() {
        AppConfig config = RuntimeConfigLoader.load();
        DeploymentInvariantValidator.validate(config);

        register(new AppBinder(config));

        register(OrderResource.class);
        register(HealthResource.class);
        register(ErrorMapper.class);
        register(SecurityHeadersFilter.class);

        property("app.profile", config.profile().name());
    }
}
```

```java id="mngj1u"
public final class AppBinder extends AbstractBinder {

    private final AppConfig config;

    public AppBinder(AppConfig config) {
        this.config = config;
    }

    @Override
    protected void configure() {
        bind(config).to(AppConfig.class);
        bind(config.database()).to(DbConfig.class);
        bind(config.httpClient()).to(HttpClientConfig.class);

        bindFactory(DataSourceFactory.class)
            .to(DataSource.class)
            .in(Singleton.class);
    }
}
```

Rule: **runtime profile harus diselesaikan sebelum object graph dibuat**. Kalau config invalid, aplikasi gagal startup sebelum menerima traffic.

---

## 15. Health Check Strategy by Profile

| Check | Local | Dev | Test | Staging | Prod |
|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| App boot | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes |
| DB connection | Optional | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes |
| Migration status | Optional | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes |
| External dependency | Mock/optional | Optional | Mock | Yes | Yes |
| Secret presence | Optional | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes |
| Version/build info | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes |
| Config summary non-secret | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes |

### Startup log safe summary

```text id="e7r55u"
Starting order-service
version=1.12.4
profile=PROD
java.version=17
db.url=jdbc:postgresql://postgres-prod:5432/orderdb
db.username=order_app
db.password=****
http.readTimeout=PT10S
feature.newOrderFlow=false
debugEndpointsEnabled=false
```

Jangan log:

```text id="qg2r6b"
DB_PASSWORD=...
JWT_PRIVATE_KEY=...
OAUTH_CLIENT_SECRET=...
```

---

## 16. On-Prem / VM / systemd Strategy

Untuk deployment on-prem tanpa Kubernetes, environment strategy tetap sama: config dan secret harus berada di luar artifact.

### systemd environment file

```ini id="c9s3c2"
# /etc/order-service/order-service.env
APP_PROFILE=PROD
DB_URL=jdbc:postgresql://postgres-prod:5432/orderdb
DB_USERNAME=order_app
DB_PASSWORD=replace-me
HTTP_READ_TIMEOUT=PT10S
```

```ini id="qk3xh8"
[Unit]
Description=Order Service
After=network.target

[Service]
User=order-service
EnvironmentFile=/etc/order-service/order-service.env
ExecStart=/usr/bin/java -jar /opt/order-service/order-service.jar
Restart=always

[Install]
WantedBy=multi-user.target
```

### On-prem checklist

| Area | Rule |
|---|---|
| File permission | Secret file readable only by service user. |
| Artifact | JAR/WAR does not contain environment config. |
| Rotation | Secret replacement procedure exists. |
| Startup | Config validation blocks unsafe startup. |
| Logs | No secret printed. |
| Rollback | Previous artifact and config version known. |

---

## 17. AWS / Azure Runtime Notes

Untuk cloud runtime, pattern utamanya sama:

```text id="8mig91"
Application identity
  ↓
permission to read secret/config
  ↓
config loaded at startup
  ↓
validated
  ↓
injected as typed AppConfig
```

| Cloud Concern | Strategy |
|---|---|
| Secret access | Use managed identity / IAM role / workload identity where available. |
| Secret rotation | Do not bake secret into image. |
| DB credentials | Prefer managed secret source or short-lived credentials where possible. |
| App config | Keep non-secret config separate from secret. |
| Audit | Log profile, version, config source summary, not raw secret. |
| Multi-cloud | Keep app-level config abstraction independent from AWS/Azure API. |

Rule: jangan biarkan business code bergantung langsung pada AWS Secrets Manager atau Azure Key Vault API. Bungkus sebagai `SecretProvider` atau lakukan injection saat startup.

---

## 18. Feature Flag Strategy

Feature flags adalah runtime config, tetapi tidak semua flag aman untuk diubah sembarang.

| Flag Type | Example | Runtime Change? | Risk |
|---|---|---:|---|
| UI visibility | Show new tab | Yes | Low |
| Read-only behavior | Enable new search | Yes | Medium |
| Write path | New order creation flow | Controlled | High |
| Security behavior | Disable auth check | Never as normal flag | Critical |
| Data migration behavior | Use new schema | Controlled rollout | High |
| Workflow transition | New approval rule | Controlled + audited | High |

### Feature flag object

```java id="sk84s5"
public record FeatureFlags(
    boolean newOrderFlow,
    boolean debugEndpointsEnabled,
    boolean strictTenantValidation
) {
    public void validate(RuntimeProfile profile) {
        if (profile.isProductionLike() && debugEndpointsEnabled) {
            throw new IllegalStateException(
                "Debug endpoints must not be enabled in production-like profile"
            );
        }

        if (profile.isProductionLike() && !strictTenantValidation) {
            throw new IllegalStateException(
                "Strict tenant validation must be enabled in production-like profile"
            );
        }
    }
}
```

---

## 19. Failure Modes

| Symptom | Root Cause | Fix |
|---|---|---|
| App production pakai local DB | Fallback `LOCAL` terlalu permisif | Production-like profile wajib explicit dan no-localhost invariant. |
| Secret bocor di image | Secret ditaruh di Dockerfile `ENV` | Secret injection runtime only. |
| ConfigMap berubah tapi app tidak berubah | App baca env saat startup | Restart rollout atau implement reload explicit. |
| Pod crashloop | Required config missing | Good failure; perbaiki manifest/secret. |
| Behavior beda antar pod | Config tidak versioned / rollout parsial | Version ConfigMap/Secret atau enforce rollout strategy. |
| Maven build prod menghasilkan artifact berbeda | Runtime config dimasukkan ke Maven profile | Build artifact environment-agnostic. |
| Staging tidak mirip prod | Staging invariant terlalu longgar | Terapkan production-like validation. |
| Local onboarding sulit | Local config terlalu strict | Izinkan local defaults terbatas. |
| Debug endpoint aktif di prod | Feature flag tidak divalidasi | Tambahkan production invariant. |
| Error config sulit dipahami | Parser lempar exception generik | Error message sebut key yang missing/invalid. |
| Secret rotation butuh rebuild | Secret baked into artifact/image | Pindahkan ke runtime secret source. |

---

## 20. Production Checklist

| Checklist | Status |
|---|---|
| Artifact tidak mengandung env-specific config | Wajib |
| Maven profile hanya untuk build/test/package behavior | Wajib |
| Runtime profile explicit untuk dev/test/staging/prod | Wajib |
| Production-like profile tidak boleh fallback ke local | Wajib |
| ConfigMap untuk non-secret | Wajib |
| Secret untuk sensitive value | Wajib |
| Secret tidak ada di Dockerfile/JAR/default properties | Wajib |
| Config divalidasi saat startup | Wajib |
| Deployment invariant ada untuk prod/staging | Wajib |
| Startup log menampilkan config summary non-secret | Wajib |
| Feature flag high-risk dikontrol dan diaudit | Wajib |
| Tenant/security invariant aktif di prod/staging | Wajib |
| Config source precedence terdokumentasi | Wajib |
| Health check memverifikasi dependency critical | Wajib |
| CI punya test untuk config/profile validation | Wajib |

---

## 21. Roadmap Belajar Singkat

| Urutan | Fokus | Target Praktik |
|---:|---|---|
| 1 | Build vs runtime separation | Bisa menjelaskan kenapa Maven profile bukan runtime config. |
| 2 | Runtime profile enum | Bisa membuat `LOCAL/DEV/TEST/STAGING/PROD` dengan invariant. |
| 3 | Config precedence | Bisa resolve env/JVM/file/default secara deterministik. |
| 4 | Docker local config | Bisa menjalankan service dengan Compose tanpa hardcode artifact. |
| 5 | Kubernetes ConfigMap/Secret | Bisa memisahkan non-secret dan secret di manifest. |
| 6 | Startup validation | Bisa fail-fast jika config production unsafe. |
| 7 | Secret injection | Bisa mencegah secret masuk Git/image/log. |
| 8 | Deployment invariants | Bisa membuat guardrail untuk prod/staging. |

## Rekomendasi Materi Selanjutnya

Lanjut ke **cheatsheet: “Jersey/JAX-RS File Resource & Secret Loading - classpath resources, mounted files, Kubernetes Secret volumes, certificate bundles, config reload policy, and safe logging.”**

## Response:
7/13/2026, 12:25:34 AM

# Cheatsheet - Jersey/JAX-RS File Resource & Secret Loading  
## Classpath Resources, Mounted Files, Kubernetes Secret Volumes, Certificate Bundles, Config Reload Policy, Safe Logging

## 1. Scope

Cheatsheet ini membahas bagaimana aplikasi **Jersey/JAX-RS** membaca resource dan secret secara aman:

```text id="tnb69r"
File / Resource Loading
│
├─ Classpath Resource
│  ├─ packaged inside JAR/WAR
│  ├─ versioned with code
│  └─ non-secret only
│
├─ External Mounted File
│  ├─ environment-specific
│  ├─ Docker/Kubernetes/on-prem mount
│  └─ config / cert / policy file
│
├─ Secret Source
│  ├─ env var
│  ├─ mounted secret volume
│  ├─ secret manager
│  └─ short-lived credential
│
├─ Certificate / Key Material
│  ├─ truststore
│  ├─ keystore
│  ├─ PEM bundle
│  └─ private key
│
└─ Runtime Policy
   ├─ load at startup
   ├─ validate
   ├─ mask logs
   ├─ optional reload
   └─ fail safe
```

Target desainnya: **resource yang stabil ikut artifact**, **config/secret runtime berada di luar artifact**, dan **semua pembacaan file punya error handling, validation, permission awareness, dan safe logging**.

---

## 2. Core Distinction

| Jenis | Lokasi | Lifecycle | Contoh | Boleh Secret? |
|---|---|---|---|---:|
| **Classpath resource** | `src/main/resources`, masuk JAR/WAR | Versioned with code | default config, JSON schema, templates, static metadata | Tidak |
| **External file** | filesystem runtime | Environment-specific | `/etc/app/application.yaml`, mounted policy file | Bisa, dengan kontrol |
| **Environment variable** | process env | Injected by runtime | `DB_URL`, `APP_PROFILE`, `DB_PASSWORD` | Bisa, tapi hati-hati exposure |
| **Mounted secret file** | Docker/K8s/on-prem volume | Injected by runtime | `/run/secrets/db_password` | Ya |
| **Secret manager** | external secret service | Managed outside app | AWS Secrets Manager, Azure Key Vault, Vault | Ya |
| **Generated runtime credential** | identity provider / workload identity | Short-lived | temporary DB token, cloud access token | Ya |

Rule paling penting:

```text id="gdcs1y"
Classpath resource = safe default / static artifact data.
External resource = environment-specific runtime contract.
Secret = never baked into source, artifact, image, or logs.
```

Docker Compose sendiri menyarankan penggunaan secrets agar password/API key tidak disimpan sebagai environment variable, karena env var berisiko terekspos secara tidak sengaja; service hanya dapat mengakses secret jika eksplisit diberi akses lewat atribut `secrets`. ([Docker Documentation](https://docs.docker.com/compose/how-tos/use-secrets/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 3. Decomposition Map

```text id="nuisv1"
Resource & Secret Loading Architecture
│
├─ 1. Resource Type
│  ├─ classpath default
│  ├─ external config file
│  ├─ mounted secret file
│  ├─ certificate bundle
│  ├─ policy/rule file
│  └─ template/schema file
│
├─ 2. Loader
│  ├─ ClasspathResourceLoader
│  ├─ ExternalFileLoader
│  ├─ SecretFileLoader
│  ├─ CertificateLoader
│  └─ ConfigLoader
│
├─ 3. Validation
│  ├─ exists
│  ├─ readable
│  ├─ expected encoding
│  ├─ expected format
│  ├─ size limit
│  ├─ checksum/signature optional
│  └─ permission/ownership check optional
│
├─ 4. Conversion
│  ├─ String
│  ├─ Properties
│  ├─ JSON/YAML object
│  ├─ PEM/certificate
│  ├─ KeyStore
│  └─ typed config object
│
├─ 5. Injection
│  ├─ HK2 binder
│  ├─ CDI producer
│  ├─ singleton immutable config
│  └─ factory for clients/datasource
│
├─ 6. Reload Policy
│  ├─ no reload
│  ├─ reload on restart
│  ├─ polling reload
│  ├─ file watch
│  └─ external config service
│
└─ 7. Observability
   ├─ startup summary
   ├─ masked secret
   ├─ config source name
   ├─ checksum/version
   └─ no raw secret logs
```

---

## 4. Classpath Resource Pattern

Classpath resource dipakai untuk file yang **ikut artifact** dan aman dipublikkan bersama code.

Contoh:

```text id="v8q9ek"
src/main/resources/
├─ application-default.properties
├─ error-codes.json
├─ schemas/order-created.schema.json
├─ templates/email/order-approved.txt
└─ policy/default-validation-rules.json
```

Java `getResource` dan `getResourceAsStream` mencari resource berdasarkan nama dan mengembalikan `null` bila resource tidak ditemukan. ([Oracle Docs](https://docs.oracle.com/javase/8/docs/technotes/guides/lang/resources.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

```java id="ecrj6i"
public final class ClasspathResourceLoader {

    private ClasspathResourceLoader() {}

    public static String readUtf8(String absolutePath) {
        if (!absolutePath.startsWith("/")) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException(
                "Classpath path must be absolute, e.g. /application-default.properties"
            );
        }

        try (InputStream input =
                 ClasspathResourceLoader.class.getResourceAsStream(absolutePath)) {

            if (input == null) {
                throw new IllegalArgumentException(
                    "Classpath resource not found: " + absolutePath
                );
            }

            return new String(input.readAllBytes(), StandardCharsets.UTF_8);
        } catch (IOException e) {
            throw new UncheckedIOException(
                "Failed to read classpath resource: " + absolutePath,
                e
            );
        }
    }
}
```

### Classpath rules

| Rule | Penjelasan |
|---|---|
| Gunakan absolute path dengan `/` | Menghindari kebingungan path relatif package. |
| Fail fast jika resource tidak ditemukan | Jangan return empty config diam-diam. |
| Jangan simpan secret | Resource ikut JAR/WAR dan bisa diekstrak. |
| Jangan pakai `File` untuk classpath resource | Dalam JAR, resource bukan selalu file filesystem biasa. |
| Batasi ukuran resource | Jangan `readAllBytes()` untuk file besar. |
| Test packaging | Pastikan resource benar-benar masuk `target/classes`. |

---

## 5. External File Pattern

External file dipakai untuk runtime-specific config, policy, certificate, atau data yang tidak seharusnya masuk artifact.

```text id="0edmld"
/etc/order-service/
├─ application.yaml
├─ policy-rules.json
├─ trust-bundle.pem
└─ secrets/
   ├─ db-password
   └─ jwt-verification-key
```

Java `Files.readString(Path)` membaca seluruh konten file menjadi string; overload default membaca UTF-8, sedangkan overload dengan `Charset` memakai charset yang diberikan. ([Oracle Docs](https://docs.oracle.com/en/java/javase/23/docs/api/java.base/java/nio/file/Files.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

```java id="hdwiy2"
public final class ExternalFileLoader {

    private static final long DEFAULT_MAX_BYTES = 1024 * 1024; // 1 MiB

    private ExternalFileLoader() {}

    public static String readUtf8(Path path) {
        return readUtf8(path, DEFAULT_MAX_BYTES);
    }

    public static String readUtf8(Path path, long maxBytes) {
        Path normalized = path.toAbsolutePath().normalize();

        try {
            validateReadableRegularFile(normalized, maxBytes);
            return Files.readString(normalized, StandardCharsets.UTF_8);
        } catch (IOException e) {
            throw new UncheckedIOException(
                "Failed to read file: " + normalized,
                e
            );
        }
    }

    private static void validateReadableRegularFile(Path path, long maxBytes)
        throws IOException {

        if (!Files.exists(path)) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("File does not exist: " + path);
        }

        if (!Files.isRegularFile(path)) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("Not a regular file: " + path);
        }

        if (!Files.isReadable(path)) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("File is not readable: " + path);
        }

        long size = Files.size(path);
        if (size > maxBytes) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException(
                "File too large: " + path + ", size=" + size + ", max=" + maxBytes
            );
        }
    }
}
```

### External file rules

| Rule | Kenapa |
|---|---|
| Normalize path | Menghindari path ambiguity. |
| Validate exists/readable/regular file | Error lebih jelas saat startup. |
| Batasi ukuran | Hindari OOM atau accidental large read. |
| Jangan log isi file | Bisa berisi secret/policy sensitive. |
| Gunakan read-only mount | Mengurangi risiko runtime tampering. |
| Fail fast untuk required file | Deployment salah harus gagal jelas. |

---

## 6. Secret Loading Pattern

Secret harus dimodelkan sebagai tipe khusus agar tidak mudah bocor lewat `toString()`.

```java id="ea0nof"
public final class SecretValue {

    private final String value;

    private SecretValue(String value) {
        if (value == null || value.isBlank()) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("Secret value must not be blank");
        }
        this.value = value;
    }

    public static SecretValue of(String value) {
        return new SecretValue(value);
    }

    public String reveal() {
        return value;
    }

    public boolean isDefaultOrWeak() {
        String normalized = value.trim().toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT);
        return normalized.equals("password")
            || normalized.equals("changeme")
            || normalized.equals("secret")
            || normalized.equals("admin");
    }

    @Override
    public String toString() {
        return "****";
    }
}
```

### Read secret from env var

```java id="v0gsmy"
public final class EnvSecretLoader {

    public static SecretValue requiredSecret(String envKey) {
        String value = System.getenv(envKey);

        if (value == null || value.isBlank()) {
            throw new IllegalStateException("Missing required secret env var: " + envKey);
        }

        return SecretValue.of(value);
    }
}
```

### Read secret from mounted file

```java id="5849sn"
public final class SecretFileLoader {

    private static final long MAX_SECRET_BYTES = 64 * 1024;

    public static SecretValue readSecret(Path path) {
        String raw = ExternalFileLoader.readUtf8(path, MAX_SECRET_BYTES);
        String value = trimTrailingLineBreak(raw);

        return SecretValue.of(value);
    }

    private static String trimTrailingLineBreak(String value) {
        return value.replaceFirst("\\R\\z", "");
    }
}
```

Kubernetes Secret dapat dipakai oleh Pod sebagai mounted data volume atau environment variable. ([Kubernetes](https://kubernetes.io/docs/concepts/configuration/secret/?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) Untuk secret yang sensitif, mounted file sering lebih mudah dikontrol daripada environment variable karena bisa diberi permission/mount path tertentu dan tidak perlu muncul di process environment.

---

## 7. Config + Secret Source Resolution

Pattern yang lebih aman: config non-secret dari ConfigMap/file/env, secret dari Secret/file/secret manager.

```text id="4t7jcy"
Config Resolution
│
├─ APP_PROFILE              env
├─ DB_URL                   env / file
├─ DB_USERNAME              env / file
├─ DB_PASSWORD              secret env or mounted file
├─ JWT_VERIFICATION_KEY     mounted file / secret manager
└─ TRUST_BUNDLE_PATH        env points to mounted file
```

```java id="4eddur"
public record DbConfig(
    String url,
    String username,
    SecretValue password
) {
    public void validate(RuntimeProfile profile) {
        if (url == null || url.isBlank()) {
            throw new IllegalStateException("db.url is required");
        }

        if (username == null || username.isBlank()) {
            throw new IllegalStateException("db.username is required");
        }

        if (profile.isProductionLike() && password.isDefaultOrWeak()) {
            throw new IllegalStateException(
                "Default or weak DB password is forbidden in production-like profile"
            );
        }
    }
}
```

```java id="z8yz6r"
public final class SecretResolver {

    public SecretValue resolveDbPassword() {
        String secretFile = System.getenv("DB_PASSWORD_FILE");

        if (secretFile != null && !secretFile.isBlank()) {
            return SecretFileLoader.readSecret(Path.of(secretFile));
        }

        String secretEnv = System.getenv("DB_PASSWORD");

        if (secretEnv != null && !secretEnv.isBlank()) {
            return SecretValue.of(secretEnv);
        }

        throw new IllegalStateException(
            "Missing DB password. Set DB_PASSWORD_FILE or DB_PASSWORD"
        );
    }
}
```

### Recommended precedence for secrets

```text id="e8v5ec"
1. External secret manager / workload identity
2. Mounted secret file
3. Secret environment variable
4. Local-only .env / local secret file
5. Never: classpath default / hardcoded fallback
```

---

## 8. Kubernetes Secret Volume Pattern

### Secret object

```yaml id="51wak8"
apiVersion: v1
kind: Secret
metadata:
  name: order-service-secret
type: Opaque
stringData:
  db-password: "replace-me"
  jwt-verification-key.pem: |
    -----BEGIN PUBLIC KEY-----
    replace-me
    -----END PUBLIC KEY-----
```

### Deployment with mounted secret

```yaml id="r6isg1"
apiVersion: apps/v1
kind: Deployment
metadata:
  name: order-service
spec:
  replicas: 3
  selector:
    matchLabels:
      app: order-service
  template:
    metadata:
      labels:
        app: order-service
    spec:
      containers:
        - name: order-service
          image: registry.example.com/order-service:1.12.4
          env:
            - name: DB_PASSWORD_FILE
              value: /run/secrets/order-service/db-password
            - name: JWT_VERIFICATION_KEY_FILE
              value: /run/secrets/order-service/jwt-verification-key.pem
          volumeMounts:
            - name: order-service-secret-volume
              mountPath: /run/secrets/order-service
              readOnly: true
      volumes:
        - name: order-service-secret-volume
          secret:
            secretName: order-service-secret
```

Kubernetes dokumentasi menyebut Secret bisa diekspos sebagai environment variable atau file dalam volume; tetapi secret masih perlu kontrol tambahan seperti RBAC, encryption at rest, dan least privilege di cluster. ([Kubernetes](https://kubernetes.io/docs/concepts/configuration/secret/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

### Mounted secret rules

| Rule | Kenapa |
|---|---|
| Mount read-only | Secret tidak perlu ditulis aplikasi. |
| Gunakan path konsisten seperti `/run/secrets/...` | Mudah diaudit. |
| Jangan mount secret terlalu luas | Least privilege. |
| Jangan mount semua secret ke semua container | Scope per workload. |
| Gunakan file env var pointer, misalnya `DB_PASSWORD_FILE` | App tidak hardcode path. |
| Jangan print path + content | Path boleh, content tidak. |

---

## 9. Docker Compose Secrets Pattern

Docker Compose menyediakan secrets agar informasi sensitif tidak perlu disimpan sebagai environment variable. ([Docker Documentation](https://docs.docker.com/compose/how-tos/use-secrets/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

```yaml id="vdy6zg"
services:
  order-service:
    image: order-service:local
    environment:
      APP_PROFILE: LOCAL
      DB_URL: jdbc:postgresql://postgres:5432/orderdb
      DB_USERNAME: order_app
      DB_PASSWORD_FILE: /run/secrets/db_password
    secrets:
      - db_password

  postgres:
    image: postgres:16
    environment:
      POSTGRES_DB: orderdb
      POSTGRES_USER: order_app
      POSTGRES_PASSWORD_FILE: /run/secrets/db_password
    secrets:
      - db_password

secrets:
  db_password:
    file: ./secrets/local-db-password.txt
```

### Local secret file rule

```text id="1ksb5t"
./secrets/
├─ local-db-password.txt     ignored by Git
└─ README.md                 explains how to create local secret file
```

`.gitignore`:

```gitignore id="5c9v10"
secrets/*
!secrets/README.md
```

---

## 10. Certificate Bundle Loading

Certificate/key material sebaiknya diperlakukan seperti runtime resource sensitif.

```text id="os9uir"
Certificate / Key Material
│
├─ Trust material
│  ├─ CA bundle
│  ├─ truststore
│  └─ public certificates
│
├─ Identity material
│  ├─ client certificate
│  ├─ private key
│  └─ keystore
│
└─ Verification material
   ├─ JWT public key
   ├─ JWKS cache
   └─ signing certificate
```

### Read PEM public key

```java id="7b1ozm"
public final class PemKeyLoader {

    public static String readPem(Path path) {
        String pem = ExternalFileLoader.readUtf8(path, 256 * 1024);

        if (!pem.contains("-----BEGIN")) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("Invalid PEM file: missing BEGIN marker");
        }

        if (!pem.contains("-----END")) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("Invalid PEM file: missing END marker");
        }

        return pem;
    }
}
```

### Truststore config

```java id="c00ys3"
public record TlsConfig(
    Path trustStorePath,
    SecretValue trustStorePassword,
    Path keyStorePath,
    SecretValue keyStorePassword
) {
    public void validate() {
        requireReadable(trustStorePath, "trustStorePath");

        if (keyStorePath != null) {
            requireReadable(keyStorePath, "keyStorePath");
        }
    }

    private static void requireReadable(Path path, String name) {
        if (path == null || !Files.isReadable(path)) {
            throw new IllegalStateException(name + " is not readable: " + path);
        }
    }
}
```

### Certificate material rules

| Material | Sensitivity | Storage |
|---|---:|---|
| CA bundle / public cert | Medium | ConfigMap or image if stable, but prefer runtime-managed for rotation. |
| Truststore password | Secret | Secret source. |
| Client private key | High | Secret volume / secret manager. |
| JWT public key | Medium | ConfigMap/Secret depending policy. |
| JWT private key | Critical | Secret manager / HSM/KMS, avoid plain file if possible. |

---

## 11. Application Policy / Rule File Loading

Policy file bisa berupa JSON/YAML yang mendefinisikan rule, mapping, threshold, atau matrix.

```json id="zbbeyi"
{
  "policyName": "order-approval-policy",
  "policyVersion": "2026-07-01",
  "rules": [
    {
      "action": "APPROVE_LOW_RISK_ORDER",
      "requiredPermission": "order:approve:low-risk",
      "allowedStates": ["PENDING_APPROVAL"],
      "maxAmount": 100000
    }
  ]
}
```

### Typed policy config

```java id="hwszcj"
public record PolicyConfig(
    String policyName,
    String policyVersion,
    List<PolicyRule> rules
) {
    public void validate() {
        if (policyName == null || policyName.isBlank()) {
            throw new IllegalStateException("policyName is required");
        }

        if (policyVersion == null || policyVersion.isBlank()) {
            throw new IllegalStateException("policyVersion is required");
        }

        if (rules == null || rules.isEmpty()) {
            throw new IllegalStateException("At least one policy rule is required");
        }
    }
}
```

### Policy loading rules

| Rule | Penjelasan |
|---|---|
| Version every policy file | Audit harus tahu rule mana yang dipakai. |
| Validate schema | Jangan parse lalu percaya. |
| Log policy name/version, not whole policy if sensitive | Audit-friendly tanpa leak. |
| Decide reload policy explicitly | Policy perubahan runtime bisa berisiko. |
| Test with matrix | Rule file harus punya regression tests. |

---

## 12. Config Reload Policy

Tidak semua file perlu reload. Banyak production system lebih aman dengan **reload on restart**.

```text id="z4nipc"
Reload Policy
│
├─ No reload
│  └─ immutable runtime config after startup
│
├─ Restart reload
│  └─ config changes apply after pod/app restart
│
├─ Polling reload
│  └─ app periodically checks file version/checksum
│
├─ WatchService reload
│  └─ app watches filesystem changes
│
└─ External config service
   └─ app receives config update through managed service
```

### Reload decision table

| Resource | Recommended Reload | Reason |
|---|---|---|
| DB URL | Restart only | Connection pool lifecycle. |
| DB password | Restart or controlled rotation | Needs careful pool refresh. |
| HTTP timeout | Restart or dynamic if supported | Safer immutable. |
| Feature flag low-risk | Dynamic possible | But audit changes. |
| Security policy | Controlled reload only | High risk. |
| Authorization matrix | Restart/controlled reload | Must be tested/versioned. |
| TLS trust bundle | Controlled reload | Need client/server refresh. |
| JWT public key | JWKS cache with TTL possible | Standard key rotation pattern. |
| Private key | Restart/secret manager controlled | Critical. |

### Simple checksum-based reload guard

```java id="78kjh7"
public record LoadedFile<T>(
    Path path,
    String checksumSha256,
    Instant loadedAt,
    T value
) {}
```

```java id="wel6r9"
public final class FileChecksum {

    public static String sha256(Path path) {
        try (InputStream input = Files.newInputStream(path)) {
            MessageDigest digest = MessageDigest.getInstance("SHA-256");
            byte[] buffer = new byte[8192];

            int read;
            while ((read = input.read(buffer)) != -1) {
                digest.update(buffer, 0, read);
            }

            return HexFormat.of().formatHex(digest.digest());
        } catch (IOException e) {
            throw new UncheckedIOException(e);
        } catch (NoSuchAlgorithmException e) {
            throw new IllegalStateException("SHA-256 not available", e);
        }
    }
}
```

---

## 13. Safe Startup Logging

Saat startup, log **apa yang cukup untuk audit deployment**, bukan isi secret.

```text id="vtj7ik"
Starting order-service
version=1.12.4
profile=PROD
config.source=env+mounted-file
config.file=/etc/order-service/application.yaml
policy.name=order-approval-policy
policy.version=2026-07-01
policy.sha256=0d7a...
db.url=jdbc:postgresql://postgres-prod:5432/orderdb
db.username=order_app
db.password=****
jwt.key.source=/run/secrets/order-service/jwt-verification-key.pem
jwt.key.content=****
trust.bundle=/etc/ssl/order-service/trust-bundle.pem
```

Jangan log:

```text id="k2w27z"
DB_PASSWORD=actual-password
JWT_PRIVATE_KEY=-----BEGIN PRIVATE KEY-----
Authorization=Bearer ey...
Cookie=session=...
```

### Safe logger helper

```java id="iidm99"
public final class SafeLog {

    private SafeLog() {}

    public static String secret(Object ignored) {
        return "****";
    }

    public static String path(Path path) {
        if (path == null) {
            return "<not-configured>";
        }
        return path.toAbsolutePath().normalize().toString();
    }

    public static String checksum(String sha256) {
        if (sha256 == null || sha256.length() < 8) {
            return "<unknown>";
        }
        return sha256.substring(0, 8) + "...";
    }
}
```

---

## 14. HK2 / CDI Injection Pattern

### HK2 binder

```java id="ufq5gk"
public final class AppBinder extends AbstractBinder {

    private final RuntimeResources resources;

    public AppBinder(RuntimeResources resources) {
        this.resources = resources;
    }

    @Override
    protected void configure() {
        bind(resources.appConfig()).to(AppConfig.class);
        bind(resources.policyConfig()).to(PolicyConfig.class);
        bind(resources.tlsConfig()).to(TlsConfig.class);
    }
}
```

```java id="rvth5c"
public record RuntimeResources(
    AppConfig appConfig,
    PolicyConfig policyConfig,
    TlsConfig tlsConfig
) {}
```

```java id="zprwm8"
public final class AppResourceConfig extends ResourceConfig {

    public AppResourceConfig() {
        RuntimeResources resources = RuntimeResourceLoader.load();
        RuntimeResourceValidator.validate(resources);

        register(new AppBinder(resources));

        register(OrderResource.class);
        register(ErrorMapper.class);
        register(SecurityHeadersFilter.class);
    }
}
```

### CDI producer

```java id="1bp0jc"
@ApplicationScoped
public class RuntimeResourceProducer {

    private final RuntimeResources resources =
        RuntimeResourceLoader.loadAndValidate();

    @Produces
    @ApplicationScoped
    public AppConfig appConfig() {
        return resources.appConfig();
    }

    @Produces
    @ApplicationScoped
    public PolicyConfig policyConfig() {
        return resources.policyConfig();
    }

    @Produces
    @ApplicationScoped
    public TlsConfig tlsConfig() {
        return resources.tlsConfig();
    }
}
```

Rule: **load, parse, validate, then inject typed object**. Jangan biarkan service membaca file sendiri-sendiri.

---

## 15. Resource Loader Composition

```java id="03pf41"
public final class RuntimeResourceLoader {

    public static RuntimeResources loadAndValidate() {
        AppConfig appConfig = loadAppConfig();
        PolicyConfig policyConfig = loadPolicyConfig(appConfig);
        TlsConfig tlsConfig = loadTlsConfig(appConfig);

        RuntimeResources resources =
            new RuntimeResources(appConfig, policyConfig, tlsConfig);

        RuntimeResourceValidator.validate(resources);

        return resources;
    }

    private static AppConfig loadAppConfig() {
        String configFile = System.getenv("CONFIG_FILE");

        if (configFile != null && !configFile.isBlank()) {
            String yaml = ExternalFileLoader.readUtf8(Path.of(configFile));
            return AppConfigParser.parseYaml(yaml);
        }

        String defaultProperties =
            ClasspathResourceLoader.readUtf8("/application-default.properties");

        return AppConfigParser.parseProperties(defaultProperties);
    }

    private static PolicyConfig loadPolicyConfig(AppConfig appConfig) {
        Path policyPath = appConfig.policyFilePath();

        if (policyPath != null) {
            String json = ExternalFileLoader.readUtf8(policyPath);
            return PolicyConfigParser.parseJson(json);
        }

        String defaultJson =
            ClasspathResourceLoader.readUtf8("/policy/default-policy.json");

        return PolicyConfigParser.parseJson(defaultJson);
    }

    private static TlsConfig loadTlsConfig(AppConfig appConfig) {
        return new TlsConfig(
            appConfig.trustStorePath(),
            SecretFileLoader.readSecret(appConfig.trustStorePasswordFile()),
            appConfig.keyStorePath(),
            SecretFileLoader.readSecret(appConfig.keyStorePasswordFile())
        );
    }
}
```

---

## 16. File Resource Testing Strategy

| Test | Target |
|---|---|
| Classpath resource exists | Resource packaged correctly. |
| Missing classpath resource fails | Fail-fast behavior. |
| External file missing fails | Deployment error visible. |
| External file too large fails | Size guard works. |
| Secret `toString()` masked | No accidental leak. |
| Secret file trailing newline trimmed | K8s/Docker secret friendly. |
| Config parse validates required fields | No partial invalid config. |
| Production invariant blocks weak secret | Prod safety. |
| Policy file version required | Audit readiness. |
| Startup log excludes secret | Safe observability. |

### Example unit test

```java id="ijkrgx"
@Test
void secretValueMustNotExposeRawValueInToString() {
    SecretValue secret = SecretValue.of("super-secret-password");

    assertEquals("****", secret.toString());
}
```

```java id="lx4jf2"
@Test
void missingClasspathResourceMustFail() {
    IllegalArgumentException ex = assertThrows(
        IllegalArgumentException.class,
        () -> ClasspathResourceLoader.readUtf8("/missing-file.json")
    );

    assertTrue(ex.getMessage().contains("Classpath resource not found"));
}
```

```java id="l98y2x"
@Test
void secretFileLoaderTrimsSingleTrailingLineBreak() throws IOException {
    Path temp = Files.createTempFile("secret", ".txt");
    Files.writeString(temp, "secret-value\n", StandardCharsets.UTF_8);

    SecretValue secret = SecretFileLoader.readSecret(temp);

    assertEquals("secret-value", secret.reveal());
}
```

---

## 17. Security Failure Modes

| Symptom | Root Cause | Fix |
|---|---|---|
| Local jalan, Docker gagal | File path lokal hardcoded | Gunakan env var path dan mount eksplisit. |
| Resource not found dalam JAR | File tidak masuk `src/main/resources` | Cek artifact packaging. |
| Secret bocor di log | Config/secret `toString()` raw | Gunakan `SecretValue` masking. |
| Secret bocor di image | Secret masuk Dockerfile/classpath | Runtime secret injection only. |
| Pod crash karena file missing | Secret/ConfigMap volume belum mount | Cek manifest dan startup validation. |
| Policy berubah tanpa audit | Dynamic reload tanpa versioning | Wajib policy version/checksum. |
| App pakai config lama | Env-based config berubah tanpa restart | Lakukan rollout restart atau dynamic reload eksplisit. |
| Certificate rotation tidak berlaku | Truststore dibaca sekali | Tentukan reload/restart policy. |
| Path traversal | App menerima path dari user input | Jangan load file berdasarkan user-supplied path. |
| OOM saat baca file | `readAllBytes()` untuk file besar | Batasi ukuran atau streaming. |
| Cross-tenant leak dari policy cache | Cache key tidak include tenant/policy version | Tambahkan tenant/version ke key. |

---

## 18. Production Checklist

| Checklist | Status |
|---|---|
| Classpath resource hanya untuk non-secret | Wajib |
| Secret tidak ada di Git/JAR/WAR/Docker image | Wajib |
| External file path configurable | Wajib |
| Required file divalidasi saat startup | Wajib |
| File size guard ada | Wajib |
| Secret dimodelkan sebagai masked type | Wajib |
| Startup log tidak mencetak secret | Wajib |
| Kubernetes Secret/ConfigMap dipisah | Wajib |
| Secret volume mount read-only | Wajib |
| Policy file punya name/version/checksum | Wajib |
| Reload policy eksplisit | Wajib |
| Certificate/key material punya rotation strategy | Wajib |
| Path dari user tidak dipakai untuk file loading | Wajib |
| Loader terpusat; service tidak membaca file sendiri-sendiri | Wajib |
| Unit/integration test mencakup missing file dan masking | Wajib |

---

## 19. Roadmap Belajar Singkat

| Urutan | Fokus | Target Praktik |
|---:|---|---|
| 1 | Classpath resource | Bisa membaca default config/schema/template dari JAR secara aman. |
| 2 | External file | Bisa membaca mounted file dengan validation dan size guard. |
| 3 | Secret value type | Bisa mencegah secret bocor via `toString()`/logging. |
| 4 | Kubernetes Secret volume | Bisa inject secret sebagai file, bukan hardcode. |
| 5 | Docker Compose secrets | Bisa local run tanpa env var secret mentah. |
| 6 | Certificate bundle | Bisa load truststore/PEM dengan validation. |
| 7 | Reload policy | Bisa menentukan restart-only vs controlled reload. |
| 8 | Safe logging | Bisa log source/version/checksum tanpa isi secret. |

---
