## Response:
7/13/2026, 12:29:10 AM

Saya lanjut ke integrasi HK2 vs CDI/Weld secara lebih dalam: siapa pemilik object, bagaimana scope dijembatani, titik rawan injection gagal, dan strategi migrasi yang aman.

# Cheatsheet - Jersey/JAX-RS HK2 vs CDI/Weld Integration Deep Dive  
## Object Ownership, Scope Bridging, Injection Pitfalls, Migration Path, Testing Strategy

## 1. Scope

Cheatsheet ini membahas integrasi **HK2** dan **CDI/Weld** dalam aplikasi Jersey/JAX-RS:

```text id="6wf079"
Jersey / JAX-RS Runtime
│
├─ HK2
│  ├─ Jersey-native injection path
│  ├─ ResourceConfig binder
│  ├─ Factory / Supplier
│  ├─ Jersey providers/resources
│  └─ PerLookup / Singleton / RequestScoped
│
└─ CDI / Weld
   ├─ Jakarta-standard DI
   ├─ @Inject / @Produces / @Qualifier
   ├─ @ApplicationScoped / @RequestScoped / @Dependent
   ├─ interceptors / events
   └─ lifecycle/context model
```

Konteks versi penting: Jersey 4.0.x adalah jalur untuk Jakarta EE 11 / Jakarta REST 4.0; roadmap Jersey mencatat Jersey 4.0.x kompatibel dengan Jakarta REST 4.0 dan ada perubahan seperti perpindahan `AbstractBinder` ke modul HK2. ([GitHub Jersey Road Map](https://github.com/eclipse-ee4j/jersey/wiki/Road-Map)) Jakarta CDI 4.1 adalah bagian dari Jakarta EE 11 dan mendefinisikan model DI untuk reusability, testability, maintainability, scopes, interceptors, producers, dan context lifecycle. ([Jakarta CDI 4.1](https://jakarta.ee/specifications/cdi/4.1/))

---

## 2. Mental Model Utama

```text id="rms3g7"
Pertanyaan utama bukan:
"Bagaimana supaya @Inject jalan?"

Pertanyaan yang benar:
"Container mana yang memiliki object ini,
scope-nya apa,
lifecycle-nya siapa yang mengelola,
dan apakah object ini aman dipakai lintas boundary?"
```

| Pertanyaan | Kenapa Penting |
|---|---|
| Siapa yang membuat object? | HK2, CDI/Weld, manual `new`, servlet container, atau factory? |
| Siapa yang menyuntik dependency? | `@Inject` terlihat sama, tapi resolver bisa berbeda. |
| Scope-nya apa? | Singleton vs request vs dependent/per-lookup berdampak pada thread-safety. |
| Lifecycle callback aktif? | `@PostConstruct`, interceptor, disposer, proxy hanya aktif bila object container-managed. |
| Apakah CDI interceptor aktif? | Tidak aktif pada object yang dibuat manual/HK2-only. |
| Apakah request scope aktif? | CDI `@RequestScoped` butuh request context yang benar. |
| Apakah resource/provider Jersey dikelola HK2 atau CDI? | Menentukan injection behavior dan extension compatibility. |

---

## 3. Object Ownership Map

```text id="s8y9wv"
Object Ownership
│
├─ Jersey/HK2-Owned
│  ├─ JAX-RS resources registered in ResourceConfig
│  ├─ ContainerRequestFilter
│  ├─ ContainerResponseFilter
│  ├─ ExceptionMapper
│  ├─ MessageBodyReader/Writer
│  ├─ ReaderInterceptor / WriterInterceptor
│  └─ HK2-bound services
│
├─ CDI/Weld-Owned
│  ├─ @ApplicationScoped service
│  ├─ @RequestScoped context bean
│  ├─ @Dependent helper
│  ├─ @Produces-created dependency
│  ├─ CDI interceptors
│  └─ CDI events
│
├─ Manually-Owned
│  ├─ new SomeService()
│  ├─ static singleton
│  ├─ builder-created object
│  └─ library-created callback
│
└─ Runtime-Owned
   ├─ servlet container
   ├─ thread pool
   ├─ JDBC pool
   ├─ HTTP client pool
   └─ external SDK client
```

**Rule:** object yang dibuat dengan `new` tidak otomatis mendapat DI, scope, lifecycle callback, atau interceptor. Ini sumber bug paling sering saat campur HK2/CDI.

---

## 4. HK2 vs CDI/Weld Decision Table

| Kebutuhan | HK2 Lebih Cocok | CDI/Weld Lebih Cocok |
|---|---:|---:|
| Register dependency langsung ke Jersey `ResourceConfig` | Ya | Bisa, tapi butuh integrasi |
| Inject ke Jersey resource/filter/mapper | Ya | Ya, jika CDI integration benar |
| Standard Jakarta DI lintas runtime | Tidak utama | Ya |
| `@Produces`, `@Qualifier`, alternatives | Terbatas/different model | Ya |
| CDI interceptors | Tidak | Ya |
| CDI events | Tidak | Ya |
| Jersey-specific provider injection | Ya | Kadang via bridge/integration |
| Standalone Jersey sederhana | Ya | Optional |
| Full Jakarta EE alignment | Kurang ideal | Ya |
| Migration jangka panjang Jakarta EE 11+ | Transitional | Lebih strategis |

Jersey documentation menyatakan chapter IOC-nya hanya membahas skenario umum dependency injection di Jersey dan menyarankan opsi implementasi; untuk custom injection, pattern umum adalah binder/factory/supplier yang diregister ke `ResourceConfig`. ([Jersey Custom Injection and Lifecycle Management](https://eclipse-ee4j.github.io/jersey.github.io/documentation/latest/ioc.html))

---

## 5. HK2 Composition Pattern

```java id="5em6h8"
public final class AppResourceConfig extends ResourceConfig {

    public AppResourceConfig() {
        register(new AppHk2Binder());

        register(OrderResource.class);
        register(AuthenticationFilter.class);
        register(DomainExceptionMapper.class);
    }
}
```

```java id="u4nngj"
public final class AppHk2Binder extends AbstractBinder {

    @Override
    protected void configure() {
        bind(OrderServiceImpl.class)
            .to(OrderService.class)
            .in(Singleton.class);

        bind(OrderRepositoryPostgres.class)
            .to(OrderRepository.class)
            .in(Singleton.class);

        bindFactory(RequestContextFactory.class)
            .to(RequestContext.class)
            .in(RequestScoped.class);
    }
}
```

### Cocok bila

| Kondisi | Penjelasan |
|---|---|
| Aplikasi Jersey standalone sederhana | Tidak butuh full CDI feature. |
| Object graph kecil | Binding mudah diaudit. |
| Fokus pada JAX-RS provider/resource | HK2 dekat dengan Jersey runtime. |
| Tidak banyak AOP/event/producers | CDI benefit belum dominan. |

### Risiko

| Risiko | Contoh |
|---|---|
| Terkunci ke Jersey/HK2 | Sulit pindah ke runtime Jakarta lain. |
| CDI interceptor tidak aktif | Service bukan CDI-managed. |
| Producer/qualifier pattern tidak seragam | Model berbeda dari CDI. |
| Scope bridging rawan | HK2 request scope ≠ CDI request scope jika tidak diintegrasikan benar. |

---

## 6. CDI/Weld Composition Pattern

```java id="mw6w2t"
@ApplicationScoped
public class OrderServiceImpl implements OrderService {

    private final OrderRepository repository;
    private final OrderAccessPolicy policy;

    @Inject
    public OrderServiceImpl(OrderRepository repository,
                            OrderAccessPolicy policy) {
        this.repository = repository;
        this.policy = policy;
    }
}
```

```java id="x56k3j"
@ApplicationScoped
public class InfrastructureProducer {

    @Produces
    @ApplicationScoped
    public AppConfig appConfig() {
        return RuntimeConfigLoader.loadAndValidate();
    }

    @Produces
    @ApplicationScoped
    public DataSource dataSource(AppConfig config) {
        return DataSourceFactory.create(config.database());
    }
}
```

### Cocok bila

| Kondisi | Penjelasan |
|---|---|
| Ingin Jakarta-standard DI | CDI adalah standar Jakarta. |
| Butuh producer/qualifier/event/interceptor | CDI menyediakan model formal. |
| Domain service ingin reusable di non-Jersey entrypoint | Misalnya batch, message consumer, Camunda delegate. |
| Aplikasi makin kompleks | CDI lebih cocok sebagai application composition model. |

CDI 4.1 mendefinisikan konsep bean, scopes, contextual lifecycle, dependency injection, producers, disposer, decorators, interceptors, dan events; CDI juga memperluas Jakarta Interceptors. ([Jakarta CDI 4.1 Spec](https://jakarta.ee/specifications/cdi/4.1/jakarta-cdi-spec-4.1))

---

## 7. Scope Equivalence: Jangan Disamakan Mentah

| Concept | HK2 / Jersey | CDI / Weld | Catatan |
|---|---|---|---|
| Application singleton | `Singleton` | `@ApplicationScoped` | Mirip, tapi lifecycle/proxy berbeda. |
| Per injection lookup | `PerLookup` | `@Dependent` | Mirip secara efek, tapi ownership/disposal berbeda. |
| Per request | HK2 `RequestScoped` | CDI `@RequestScoped` | Tidak otomatis sama bila integration tidak benar. |
| Factory/producer | HK2 `Factory`/`Supplier` | `@Produces` | CDI producer ikut CDI lifecycle/qualifier. |
| Interceptor | Jersey filter/interceptor atau HK2 facility | CDI/Jakarta Interceptor | Layer berbeda. |
| Qualifier/named binding | HK2 named/contract binding | CDI `@Qualifier` | Jangan campur tanpa strategy. |

**Important:** `@Singleton` object tidak boleh menyimpan mutable request/user/tenant state, baik di HK2 maupun CDI.

---

## 8. Scope Bridging Problem

Masalah umum:

```text id="e0x1vq"
HK2 Singleton
  injects
CDI @RequestScoped bean

atau

CDI @ApplicationScoped service
  injects
HK2 RequestScoped object

atau

Manual singleton
  reads ThreadLocal RequestContext
```

Risikonya:

| Risiko | Penjelasan |
|---|---|
| Context not active | Request scope belum aktif saat bean diakses. |
| Proxy misuse | Proxy request-scoped dipakai di luar request thread. |
| Context leak | Request data tertahan di singleton/static. |
| Lifecycle mismatch | CDI disposer/lifecycle callback tidak terpanggil. |
| Interceptor missing | Object bukan CDI-managed. |

### Better pattern: pass context explicitly

```java id="3kwynv"
public final class OrderService {

    public OrderDto getOrder(
        String orderId,
        PrincipalContext principal,
        RequestContext requestContext
    ) {
        // explicit context
    }
}
```

Alih-alih menginjeksi request context ke singleton service, pass `RequestContext` sebagai parameter untuk operasi yang request-bound.

---

## 9. Resource Ownership Strategy

### Option A - Jersey/HK2-owned resource, CDI-owned service

```text id="joq4o8"
Jersey Resource
  ↓ calls
CDI Service
  ↓ injects
CDI Repository / Producer / Interceptor
```

Ini sering dipilih saat migrasi bertahap.

```java id="k38n6y"
@Path("/orders")
public final class OrderResource {

    private final OrderService orderService;

    @Inject
    public OrderResource(OrderService orderService) {
        this.orderService = orderService;
    }
}
```

Agar ini berhasil, bridge/integration harus memastikan `OrderService` dapat di-resolve dari CDI ke Jersey/HK2 side. Jika tidak, resource akan gagal startup dengan unsatisfied dependency.

### Option B - CDI-owned resource dan service

```text id="cup8op"
CDI Resource
  ↓
CDI Service
  ↓
CDI Repository
```

Ini lebih bersih secara Jakarta-standard, tapi perlu memastikan Jersey/CDI support aktif di deployment.

### Option C - HK2-only

```text id="n48m8o"
HK2 Resource
  ↓
HK2 Service
  ↓
HK2 Repository
```

Ini paling sederhana untuk Jersey standalone, tapi tidak memanfaatkan CDI features.

---

## 10. Jakarta REST 4.0 Direction

Jakarta RESTful Web Services 4.0 adalah release untuk Jakarta EE 11 dan goal rilisnya termasuk menghapus dependency JAXB dan ManagedBean support, serta menjaga backward compatibility. ([Jakarta RESTful Web Services 4.0](https://jakarta.ee/specifications/restful-ws/4.0/)) Dalam roadmap Jersey, Jersey 4.0.x dinyatakan kompatibel dengan Jakarta REST 4.0 dan ada perubahan terkait HK2 module. ([GitHub Jersey Road Map](https://github.com/eclipse-ee4j/jersey/wiki/Road-Map))

Implikasi praktis:

```text id="upzk2x"
Legacy Jersey app:
  HK2-heavy, @Context-heavy, ResourceConfig binder-heavy

Modern Jakarta direction:
  CDI-managed app services,
  explicit request context,
  less direct @Context spread,
  provider/filter still in JAX-RS boundary
```

Jangan langsung rewrite semua. Yang lebih aman: **tarik domain service, config, policy, repository, audit ke CDI-compatible design**, lalu biarkan Jersey boundary tetap tipis.

---

## 11. Injection Pitfalls

| Pitfall | Contoh | Dampak | Fix |
|---|---|---|---|
| Mixed container ownership | HK2 membuat object yang butuh CDI interceptor | Interceptor tidak aktif | Pastikan object CDI-managed. |
| Manual `new` | `new OrderService()` | DI/lifecycle hilang | Constructor injection + container ownership. |
| Singleton holds request context | Field `RequestContext current` | Cross-request leak | Pass context as method parameter. |
| CDI bean injected into HK2 without bridge | `UnsatisfiedDependencyException` | Startup gagal | Aktifkan integration/bridge atau register adapter. |
| HK2 object injected into CDI directly | CDI tidak tahu HK2 binding | Unsatisfied dependency | Buat CDI producer adapter. |
| Multiple implementations | Dua `OrderRepository` | Ambiguous dependency | CDI qualifier / HK2 named binding. |
| Scope mismatch | App-scoped bean depends on raw request object | Context inactive/leak | Inject provider/proxy or pass explicitly. |
| Wrong namespace | `javax.inject` vs `jakarta.inject` | Class mismatch | Konsisten `jakarta.*` untuk Jakarta modern. |
| Version mismatch | Jersey 3 + Jakarta EE 11 API | Runtime error | Align BOM/dependency versions. |
| Provider not registered | Filter/mapper tidak jalan | Security/error gap | Register eksplisit di `ResourceConfig`. |

---

## 12. Adapter Pattern: HK2 to CDI Boundary

Jika perlu memakai CDI service dari HK2/Jersey component, buat adapter yang jelas. Jangan biarkan semua class bergantung cross-container sembarangan.

```java id="j2f0os"
public interface OrderServiceGateway {
    OrderDto getOrder(String orderId, PrincipalContext principal, RequestContext context);
}
```

```java id="3vhtza"
@ApplicationScoped
public class CdiOrderServiceGateway implements OrderServiceGateway {

    private final OrderService orderService;

    @Inject
    public CdiOrderServiceGateway(OrderService orderService) {
        this.orderService = orderService;
    }

    @Override
    public OrderDto getOrder(
        String orderId,
        PrincipalContext principal,
        RequestContext context
    ) {
        return orderService.getOrder(orderId, principal, context);
    }
}
```

Atau, bila Jersey/HK2 tidak bisa langsung resolve CDI bean, buat lookup adapter di satu tempat saja, bukan tersebar di resource.

```text id="at640l"
Allowed:
  Resource -> OrderServiceGateway

Avoid:
  Resource -> CDI.current().select(OrderService.class).get()
  Filter -> CDI.current().select(...)
  Mapper -> CDI.current().select(...)
  Random helper -> CDI.current().select(...)
```

---

## 13. Adapter Pattern: CDI Producer untuk HK2/External Object

Jika object dibuat oleh non-CDI library atau infrastructure, expose ke CDI lewat producer.

```java id="hixd2e"
@ApplicationScoped
public class ExternalObjectProducer {

    @Produces
    @ApplicationScoped
    public AppConfig appConfig() {
        return RuntimeConfigLoader.loadAndValidate();
    }

    @Produces
    @ApplicationScoped
    public DataSource dataSource(AppConfig config) {
        return DataSourceFactory.create(config.database());
    }
}
```

Untuk object yang perlu cleanup:

```java id="97ht5f"
@Produces
@ApplicationScoped
public CloseableHttpClient httpClient(AppConfig config) {
    return HttpClientFactory.create(config.httpClient());
}

public void closeHttpClient(@Disposes CloseableHttpClient client) throws IOException {
    client.close();
}
```

CDI producer/disposer adalah cara bersih untuk menghubungkan object non-CDI ke lifecycle CDI.

---

## 14. Qualifier Strategy

Jika ada banyak implementasi, jangan mengandalkan nama class.

```java id="wvwq3u"
@Qualifier
@Retention(RUNTIME)
@Target({FIELD, PARAMETER, METHOD, TYPE})
public @interface PrimaryDatabase {}
```

```java id="59pp4e"
@Qualifier
@Retention(RUNTIME)
@Target({FIELD, PARAMETER, METHOD, TYPE})
public @interface ReadReplica {}
```

```java id="fn4b5k"
@Produces
@PrimaryDatabase
@ApplicationScoped
public DataSource primaryDataSource(AppConfig config) {
    return DataSourceFactory.create(config.primaryDatabase());
}

@Produces
@ReadReplica
@ApplicationScoped
public DataSource readReplicaDataSource(AppConfig config) {
    return DataSourceFactory.create(config.readReplicaDatabase());
}
```

```java id="v71jja"
@ApplicationScoped
public class OrderRepositoryPostgres {

    private final DataSource primary;
    private final DataSource replica;

    @Inject
    public OrderRepositoryPostgres(
        @PrimaryDatabase DataSource primary,
        @ReadReplica DataSource replica
    ) {
        this.primary = primary;
        this.replica = replica;
    }
}
```

### Qualifier naming rules

| Rule | Reason |
|---|---|
| Qualifier harus domain/infrastructure meaning | `@PrimaryDatabase`, bukan `@DataSource1`. |
| Hindari stringly-typed name | Refactor lebih aman. |
| Jangan overuse qualifier | Kalau cuma satu implementation, tidak perlu. |
| Gunakan untuk ambiguity nyata | Multi datasource, multi client, multi mapper. |

---

## 15. Lifecycle Callback Pitfall

```java id="f6iiot"
public class OrderService {

    @PostConstruct
    void init() {
        // expected to run
    }
}
```

`@PostConstruct` hanya meaningful kalau object dibuat oleh container yang mendukung lifecycle callback. Kalau object dibuat manual:

```java id="ocq67r"
OrderService service = new OrderService(); // BAD for managed lifecycle
```

maka injection dan lifecycle callback tidak otomatis terjadi.

### Checklist lifecycle

| Need | Pastikan |
|---|---|
| `@PostConstruct` aktif | Object container-managed. |
| CDI interceptor aktif | Object CDI bean, method non-final/eligible, invocation lewat proxy. |
| `@Disposes` aktif | Object dibuat via CDI producer. |
| Request scope aktif | Access terjadi dalam request context. |
| Cleanup client/pool | Lifecycle owner jelas. |

---

## 16. Interceptor Pitfall

CDI interceptor hanya bekerja pada CDI-managed invocation. Contoh:

```java id="i6fo2w"
@Audited
public OrderDto approveOrder(String orderId) {
    ...
}
```

Interceptor bisa tidak jalan jika:

| Penyebab | Penjelasan |
|---|---|
| Object dibuat manual | CDI tidak mengelola invocation. |
| Method dipanggil dari dalam class sendiri | Self-invocation bypass proxy/interceptor. |
| Class/method tidak eligible | Tergantung proxying/rules. |
| Binding annotation salah target/retention | Interceptor binding tidak terdeteksi. |
| Interceptor tidak enabled/priority | Tidak masuk chain. |

### Safer pattern

```java id="khl7w4"
@ApplicationScoped
public class OrderApprovalFacade {

    private final OrderApprovalService service;

    @Inject
    public OrderApprovalFacade(OrderApprovalService service) {
        this.service = service;
    }

    @Audited(action = "APPROVE_ORDER")
    public ApprovalResult approve(ApproveOrderCommand command) {
        return service.approve(command);
    }
}
```

Letakkan interceptor pada facade/use-case boundary yang dipanggil dari luar bean, bukan method internal private/helper.

---

## 17. Testing Strategy

```text id="h51mly"
DI Integration Testing
│
├─ Unit Test
│  ├─ constructor injection manually
│  ├─ no container
│  └─ pure service/policy logic
│
├─ HK2/Jersey Test
│  ├─ ResourceConfig
│  ├─ binder registered
│  ├─ filter/mapper active
│  └─ HTTP behavior verified
│
├─ CDI/Weld Test
│  ├─ CDI container boot
│  ├─ producers active
│  ├─ qualifiers resolve
│  ├─ interceptors active
│  └─ scopes tested
│
└─ Full Integration Test
   ├─ Jersey + CDI integration
   ├─ request scope active
   ├─ resource can call CDI service
   ├─ filter can read context
   └─ object lifecycle verified
```

### Unit test: no container

```java id="mrxve7"
@Test
void serviceCanBeTestedWithoutContainer() {
    OrderRepository repository = new FakeOrderRepository();
    OrderAccessPolicy policy = new OrderAccessPolicy();
    AuditPublisher audit = new InMemoryAuditPublisher();

    OrderService service = new OrderService(repository, policy, audit);

    OrderDto result = service.getOrder(
        "order-1",
        principal(),
        requestContext()
    );

    assertEquals("order-1", result.id());
}
```

### Jersey/HK2 test target

```text id="1i5iad"
Verify:
- ResourceConfig registers binder
- resource can inject service
- filter order correct
- exception mapper active
- request context available
```

### CDI/Weld test target

```text id="xpl0w6"
Verify:
- @Produces AppConfig works
- qualifier resolves correct DataSource
- @ApplicationScoped service reused as expected
- @RequestScoped bean requires active context
- @Audited interceptor fires
```

---

## 18. Migration Path: HK2-heavy to CDI-centered

```text id="ip46mt"
Phase 0: Stabilize
  - inventory HK2 bindings
  - list resources/providers/filters/mappers
  - list singleton/request-scoped objects
  - detect manual new/static singleton

Phase 1: Clean Boundaries
  - resource thin
  - service constructor-injected
  - no @Context in service layer
  - RequestContext explicit parameter

Phase 2: Extract Config/Infrastructure
  - AppConfig immutable
  - DataSource/HTTP client factories
  - secret loading centralized
  - safe startup validation

Phase 3: CDI-ready Services
  - add @ApplicationScoped where appropriate
  - use @Produces for infrastructure
  - introduce qualifiers
  - move audit/transaction to CDI interceptor if needed

Phase 4: Jersey/CDI Integration
  - verify resource can inject CDI services
  - verify request scope
  - verify interceptors
  - remove duplicate HK2 binding

Phase 5: Reduce HK2 Surface
  - HK2 limited to Jersey boundary
  - CDI owns service/repository/policy/config
  - adapters only at boundary
```

**Migration rule:** jangan migrasi resource, service, config, filter, interceptor, repository sekaligus. Migrasi ownership object per layer.

---

## 19. Recommended Target Architecture

```text id="5dry3p"
Jersey Boundary
│
├─ Resource
├─ ContainerRequestFilter
├─ ContainerResponseFilter
├─ ExceptionMapper
└─ MessageBody providers

Application Core - CDI/Weld
│
├─ Use-case services
├─ Domain policies
├─ Repositories
├─ Config producers
├─ External clients
├─ Audit publisher
└─ Interceptors/events

Explicit Boundary Objects
│
├─ RequestContext
├─ PrincipalContext
├─ TenantContext
├─ AppConfig
└─ RuntimeResources
```

### Ownership recommendation

| Component | Recommended Owner |
|---|---|
| JAX-RS resource | Jersey or CDI, depending integration maturity |
| JAX-RS filter/mapper | Jersey boundary |
| Business service | CDI |
| Repository | CDI |
| Policy object | CDI or plain object injected into CDI service |
| Config object | CDI producer / bootstrap singleton |
| RequestContext | Explicit parameter or request-scoped boundary object |
| DataSource / HTTP client | CDI producer or bootstrap infrastructure factory |
| Transaction/audit interceptor | CDI if method-level AOP needed |
| Serialization provider | Jersey boundary |

---

## 20. Dependency Version & Namespace Checks

| Check | Reason |
|---|---|
| Use `jakarta.*` consistently | Modern Jakarta stack uses `jakarta.*`, not `javax.*`. |
| Align Jersey version with Jakarta REST version | Jersey 4.x for Jakarta REST 4.0 direction. |
| Check HK2 module artifact/class move | Jersey 4 roadmap notes `AbstractBinder` moved to HK2 module. |
| Avoid duplicate API jars | Duplicate `jakarta.ws.rs-api`, CDI API, inject API can cause classloading issues. |
| Use dependency tree | `mvn dependency:tree` catches transitive mismatch. |
| Do not mix Jersey 2.x `javax.*` with Jakarta 3/4 APIs | High risk of runtime `ClassNotFoundException`. |

```bash id="y8ew66"
mvn dependency:tree \
  -Dincludes=org.glassfish.jersey,jakarta.ws.rs,jakarta.inject,jakarta.enterprise,org.glassfish.hk2,org.jboss.weld
```

---

## 21. Failure Modes

| Symptom | Likely Root Cause | Fix |
|---|---|---|
| `UnsatisfiedDependencyException` in resource | HK2 cannot resolve CDI bean | Configure bridge/integration or register adapter. |
| CDI interceptor not firing | Object not CDI-managed or self-invocation | Ensure CDI ownership and external proxy invocation. |
| `@RequestScoped` context not active | CDI request scope not bound to Jersey request | Fix Jersey/CDI/Servlet integration. |
| Singleton leaks user/tenant | Request object stored in singleton | Pass context explicitly. |
| Duplicate service instances | Same service bound in HK2 and CDI | Single owner per component. |
| Ambiguous dependency | Multiple beans no qualifier | Add CDI qualifier or HK2 named binding. |
| `ClassNotFoundException javax.*` | Mixed namespace | Migrate imports/dependencies to `jakarta.*`. |
| `NoSuchMethodError` | Version mismatch | Align Jersey/HK2/CDI/Jakarta API versions. |
| `@PostConstruct` not called | Object manually created | Let container create object. |
| Config loaded twice | Producer and binder both load | Centralize config creation. |
| RequestContext null | Filter not registered/order wrong | Explicit registration and priority. |
| Tests pass but runtime fails | Unit test bypasses container wiring | Add DI integration tests. |

---

## 22. Production Checklist

| Checklist | Status |
|---|---|
| Setiap component punya satu owner: HK2, CDI, manual, atau runtime | Wajib |
| Service/repository/policy tidak membaca `@Context` langsung | Wajib |
| Request/user/tenant context tidak disimpan di singleton | Wajib |
| Config hanya dibuat sekali dan diinject sebagai immutable object | Wajib |
| HK2 binding dan CDI producer tidak menduplikasi object yang sama | Wajib |
| Qualifier dipakai untuk dependency ganda | Wajib |
| CDI interceptor dites benar-benar aktif | Wajib jika dipakai |
| Request scope dites di runtime Jersey/CDI | Wajib jika pakai `@RequestScoped` |
| Dependency tree bebas campuran `javax.*` dan `jakarta.*` | Wajib |
| `ResourceConfig` eksplisit register security/error components | Wajib |
| Ada migration inventory HK2 binding → CDI bean | Wajib untuk migrasi |
| Integration test membuktikan resource → service → repository chain aktif | Wajib |

---

## 23. Roadmap Belajar Singkat

| Urutan | Fokus | Target Praktik |
|---:|---|---|
| 1 | Object ownership | Bisa menentukan siapa yang membuat dan mengelola tiap object. |
| 2 | HK2 binder | Bisa wire Jersey resource/filter ke dependency dengan scope benar. |
| 3 | CDI producer/qualifier | Bisa expose config, datasource, client, dan multi-implementation. |
| 4 | Scope correctness | Bisa mencegah singleton memegang request state. |
| 5 | Interceptor lifecycle | Bisa memastikan CDI interceptor benar-benar aktif. |
| 6 | Bridge boundary | Bisa membuat adapter HK2 ↔ CDI tanpa menyebar lookup acak. |
| 7 | Migration plan | Bisa mengurangi HK2 surface secara bertahap. |
| 8 | DI integration tests | Bisa membuktikan wiring bekerja di runtime, bukan hanya unit test. |

---

Lanjut ke **cheatsheet: “Jersey/JAX-RS Dependency Injection Failure Diagnostics - dependency tree, namespace mismatch, scope leak, request context inactive, interceptor not firing, and startup debug checklist.”**

## Response:
7/13/2026, 12:33:57 AM

# Cheatsheet - Jersey/JAX-RS Dependency Injection Failure Diagnostics  
## Dependency Tree, Namespace Mismatch, Scope Leak, Request Context Inactive, Interceptor Not Firing, Startup Debug Checklist

## 1. Scope

Cheatsheet ini fokus pada **cara mendiagnosis kegagalan dependency injection** pada aplikasi Jersey/JAX-RS yang memakai HK2, CDI/Weld, Jakarta namespace, Maven, dan runtime modern.

```text id="c6m3sx"
DI Failure Diagnostics
│
├─ Startup failure
│  ├─ Unsatisfied dependency
│  ├─ Ambiguous dependency
│  ├─ ClassNotFoundException
│  ├─ NoSuchMethodError
│  └─ Context not active
│
├─ Runtime failure
│  ├─ null injected field
│  ├─ interceptor not firing
│  ├─ request context leak
│  ├─ wrong implementation injected
│  └─ provider/filter not registered
│
├─ Build/dependency failure
│  ├─ javax/jakarta mismatch
│  ├─ Jersey/HK2/CDI version mismatch
│  ├─ duplicate API jars
│  └─ transitive dependency conflict
│
└─ Operational failure
   ├─ different config per environment
   ├─ classpath/resource missing
   ├─ container profile mismatch
   └─ package scanning difference
```

Targetnya bukan sekadar memperbaiki error satu kali, tapi membangun **diagnostic discipline**: dari stack trace → ownership object → dependency graph → scope → runtime registration → test coverage.

---

## 2. Golden Rule

```text id="4f72br"
Ketika DI gagal, jangan langsung tambah annotation.

Tanyakan berurutan:
1. Object ini dibuat oleh siapa?
2. Dependency ini dicari oleh container mana?
3. Binding/bean-nya ada atau tidak?
4. Scope-nya aktif atau tidak?
5. Versi package dan namespace-nya konsisten atau tidak?
6. Invocation lewat container/proxy atau manual?
```

Banyak bug DI terjadi karena developer menganggap `@Inject` adalah mekanisme tunggal. Dalam aplikasi Jersey, bisa ada HK2, CDI/Weld, servlet container, atau manual factory yang semuanya punya lifecycle berbeda.

---

## 3. Diagnostic Map

```text id="io8jn4"
Symptom
│
├─ A. Startup fails
│  ├─ Unsatisfied dependency
│  ├─ Ambiguous dependency
│  ├─ ClassNotFoundException
│  ├─ NoSuchMethodError
│  └─ Provider not found
│
├─ B. Runtime behavior wrong
│  ├─ injected object null
│  ├─ wrong implementation
│  ├─ interceptor not firing
│  ├─ filter not invoked
│  └─ mapper not invoked
│
├─ C. Scope/lifecycle wrong
│  ├─ request context inactive
│  ├─ singleton stores request state
│  ├─ request data leaks across users
│  └─ @PostConstruct not called
│
├─ D. Dependency graph wrong
│  ├─ javax.* mixed with jakarta.*
│  ├─ Jersey version mismatch
│  ├─ HK2 version mismatch
│  ├─ CDI API/runtime mismatch
│  └─ duplicate transitive API
│
└─ E. Environment-specific
   ├─ local works, Docker fails
   ├─ Docker works, Kubernetes fails
   ├─ test works, production fails
   └─ package scanning differs
```

---

## 4. Common Error Signatures

| Symptom / Error | Bias Diagnosis | First Checks |
|---|---|---|
| `UnsatisfiedDependencyException` | Container tidak menemukan binding/bean | Binding HK2 ada? CDI bean discoverable? Owner object siapa? |
| `AmbiguousResolutionException` | CDI menemukan lebih dari satu bean kandidat | Tambahkan qualifier atau pilih implementation eksplisit. |
| `ContextNotActiveException` | CDI scoped context tidak aktif | Apakah `@RequestScoped` diakses di luar request? |
| `ClassNotFoundException: javax.*` | Namespace mismatch | Campur dependency lama `javax.*` dengan app `jakarta.*`. |
| `ClassNotFoundException: jakarta.*` | Dependency Jakarta API/runtime belum ada atau versi salah | Cek dependency tree dan server runtime. |
| `NoSuchMethodError` | Binary incompatibility / versi transitive konflik | Cek dependency tree, BOM, duplicate jar. |
| `@PostConstruct` tidak jalan | Object dibuat manual | Pastikan object container-managed. |
| CDI interceptor tidak firing | Object bukan CDI bean atau self-invocation | Pastikan invocation lewat CDI proxy. |
| JAX-RS filter tidak jalan | Tidak registered / scanning salah / binding salah | Cek `ResourceConfig`, `@Provider`, `@NameBinding`. |
| Exception mapper tidak jalan | Mapper tidak registered atau exception ditangkap manual | Register mapper eksplisit, cek exception type. |
| Request data bocor | Singleton/static/thread-local menyimpan context | Audit mutable state dan cleanup MDC/ThreadLocal. |
| Config null di resource | Binder/producer tidak register atau config load gagal diam-diam | Fail-fast config loader. |

---

## 5. Dependency Tree Diagnostics

Langkah pertama untuk error versi/namespace: lihat dependency tree, bukan menebak.

Apache Maven Dependency Plugin `dependency:tree` menampilkan dependency tree project dan mendukung format seperti text, DOT, GraphML, TGF, dan JSON; versi dokumentasi plugin saat ini juga mencatat goal ini thread-safe dan mendukung parallel build. ([Apache Maven](https://maven.apache.org/plugins/maven-dependency-plugin/tree-mojo.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

```bash id="mz9s6w"
mvn dependency:tree
```

### Filter dependency penting

```bash id="woqcik"
mvn dependency:tree \
  -Dincludes=org.glassfish.jersey,jakarta.ws.rs,jakarta.inject,jakarta.enterprise,org.glassfish.hk2,org.jboss.weld
```

```bash id="xji47o"
mvn dependency:tree \
  -Dincludes=javax.ws.rs,javax.inject,javax.enterprise,jakarta.ws.rs,jakarta.inject,jakarta.enterprise
```

### Cari duplicate / conflict

```bash id="b7moqd"
mvn dependency:tree -Dverbose
```

```bash id="7qsoi5"
mvn dependency:analyze
```

Maven Dependency Plugin juga memiliki goal analisis untuk menemukan dependency yang digunakan tapi belum dideklarasikan, serta dependency yang dideklarasikan tapi tidak digunakan. ([Apache Maven](https://maven.apache.org/plugins/maven-dependency-plugin/plugin-info.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

### Apa yang dicari?

| Red Flag | Contoh |
|---|---|
| Campur `javax.ws.rs-api` dan `jakarta.ws.rs-api` | App modern pakai `jakarta.*`, transitive lama bawa `javax.*`. |
| Jersey 2.x dengan Jakarta 3/4 API | Jersey 2.x generasi `javax.*`. |
| Jersey 3.x/4.x dengan library lama `javax.*` | Class mismatch. |
| HK2 versi tertarik transitive berbeda | `NoSuchMethodError`, injection failure. |
| CDI API ada tapi runtime tidak ada | Compile sukses, runtime gagal. |
| Weld runtime ada tapi servlet integration tidak aktif | `@RequestScoped` gagal. |
| Duplicate API jar dari app dan server | Runtime classloading conflict. |

---

## 6. Namespace Mismatch: `javax.*` vs `jakarta.*`

```text id="nuxzio"
Jakarta EE 8 and older style:
  javax.ws.rs.*
  javax.inject.*
  javax.enterprise.*

Jakarta EE 9+ style:
  jakarta.ws.rs.*
  jakarta.inject.*
  jakarta.enterprise.*
```

Untuk stack modern Jakarta/Jersey, jangan campur namespace kecuali sedang migrasi terisolasi.

### Diagnostic commands

```bash id="eeljb2"
grep -R "import javax\." -n src/main/java src/test/java
```

```bash id="er97fu"
grep -R "import jakarta\." -n src/main/java src/test/java
```

```bash id="zpyfnb"
mvn dependency:tree | grep -E "javax\.|jakarta\."
```

### Decision table

| Kondisi | Diagnosis | Fix |
|---|---|---|
| Source pakai `jakarta.ws.rs.*`, dependency bawa `javax.ws.rs-api` | Transitive lama | Exclude transitive atau upgrade library. |
| Source pakai `javax.ws.rs.*`, dependency Jersey 3/4 | App belum migrasi | Migrasi source ke `jakarta.*` atau downgrade stack konsisten. |
| Compile OK, runtime `ClassNotFoundException javax.ws.rs...` | Runtime tidak punya `javax.*` | Jangan pakai library lama di runtime Jakarta. |
| Compile OK, runtime `ClassNotFoundException jakarta.ws.rs...` | Runtime/server lama | Upgrade runtime/container atau dependency. |

---

## 7. Object Ownership Diagnostics

Saat error injection muncul, petakan ownership object.

```text id="pvwo3k"
Class: OrderResource
Owner?
  - Registered in ResourceConfig?     → Jersey/HK2 likely
  - Annotated @ApplicationScoped?     → CDI likely, if CDI scans it
  - Created by new?                   → Manual
  - Created by servlet container?     → Servlet/runtime
  - Created by library callback?      → Library-owned
```

### Checklist ownership

| Question | Cara Cek |
|---|---|
| Class didaftarkan di `ResourceConfig`? | Cari `register(OrderResource.class)` atau package scanning. |
| Class punya CDI scope? | Cari `@ApplicationScoped`, `@RequestScoped`, `@Dependent`. |
| Class dibuat manual? | `grep -R "new OrderService" src/main/java`. |
| Class di-produce CDI? | Cari `@Produces`. |
| Class di-bind HK2? | Cari `bind(...).to(...)`, `bindFactory(...)`. |
| Class dibuat library? | Callback/listener/factory external. |
| Ada dua owner? | Class sama di-bind HK2 dan CDI. |

### Anti-pattern

```java id="n4t2y2"
public final class OrderResource {

    private final OrderService service = new OrderService(); // BAD
}
```

Efeknya: dependency `OrderService` tidak diinjeksi, lifecycle tidak aktif, interceptor tidak aktif, config tidak masuk.

---

## 8. HK2 Unsatisfied Dependency Diagnostics

### Typical symptom

```text id="dgcd0t"
org.glassfish.hk2.api.UnsatisfiedDependencyException:
There was no object available for injection at ...
```

### Common causes

| Cause | Check | Fix |
|---|---|---|
| Binder tidak diregister | `ResourceConfig.register(new AppBinder())` | Register eksplisit. |
| Interface tidak di-bind | `bind(Impl.class).to(Contract.class)` | Tambahkan binding. |
| Scope salah import | `jakarta.inject.Singleton` vs HK2/Jersey scope confusion | Konsisten dan cek dokumentasi versi. |
| Factory tidak di-bind | `bindFactory(...)` missing | Tambahkan factory binding. |
| Resource dibuat CDI, dependency hanya HK2 | Ownership mismatch | Pindah binding ke CDI producer atau adapter. |
| Package scanning tidak menemukan provider | `@Provider` package tidak discan | Register eksplisit. |
| Versi HK2/Jersey mismatch | `NoSuchMethodError`/DI failure | Align dependency versions. |

### Minimal HK2 diagnostic binder

```java id="l3v0y7"
public final class DiagnosticBinder extends AbstractBinder {

    @Override
    protected void configure() {
        System.out.println("Registering DiagnosticBinder");

        bind(OrderServiceImpl.class)
            .to(OrderService.class)
            .in(Singleton.class);
    }
}
```

### Safer production pattern

```java id="kfbof2"
public final class AppResourceConfig extends ResourceConfig {

    public AppResourceConfig() {
        register(new AppBinder());

        register(OrderResource.class);
        register(AuthenticationFilter.class);
        register(DomainExceptionMapper.class);
    }
}
```

Jersey documentation untuk custom injection menunjukkan pattern binder/factory/supplier yang didaftarkan ke `ResourceConfig`; jadi bila injection HK2 gagal, `ResourceConfig` adalah lokasi pertama yang harus diaudit. ([Mkyong.com](https://mkyong.com/webservices/jax-rs/jersey-and-hk2-dependency-injection-auto-scanning/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 9. CDI Unsatisfied / Ambiguous Diagnostics

CDI 4.1 mendefinisikan dependency injection sebagai cara memperoleh object untuk meningkatkan reusability, testability, dan maintainability dibanding constructor/factory/service locator tradisional. ([Jakarta EE](https://jakarta.ee/specifications/cdi/4.1/?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) Tetapi model ini hanya bekerja kalau bean discoverable, ownership jelas, dan resolusi type/qualifier tidak ambigu.

### Unsatisfied dependency

```text id="uhgd2y"
jakarta.enterprise.inject.UnsatisfiedResolutionException
```

| Cause | Fix |
|---|---|
| Bean class tidak ter-scan | Pastikan ada bean archive / discovery mode benar. |
| Tidak ada scope/bean-defining annotation | Tambah `@ApplicationScoped`, `@Dependent`, dll sesuai kebutuhan. |
| Salah qualifier | Injection point dan producer harus punya qualifier sama. |
| Producer tidak aktif | Pastikan producer class CDI-managed. |
| Dependency dibuat HK2-only | Expose via CDI producer atau pindahkan ownership. |
| Test tidak boot CDI container | Gunakan Weld/Jakarta test support. |

### Ambiguous dependency

```text id="1bszma"
jakarta.enterprise.inject.AmbiguousResolutionException
```

| Cause | Fix |
|---|---|
| Dua implementation untuk interface sama | Tambahkan qualifier. |
| Producer dan class bean menghasilkan type sama | Pilih satu atau beri qualifier. |
| Test double dan real bean sama-sama aktif | Gunakan alternative/profile/test config. |
| Library auto-register bean | Exclude atau qualify. |

### Example fix with qualifier

```java id="obcpr1"
@Qualifier
@Retention(RUNTIME)
@Target({ FIELD, PARAMETER, METHOD, TYPE })
public @interface PrimaryDatabase {}
```

```java id="4qr57s"
@Produces
@PrimaryDatabase
@ApplicationScoped
DataSource primaryDataSource(AppConfig config) {
    return DataSourceFactory.create(config.primaryDatabase());
}
```

```java id="0ko8dn"
@Inject
public OrderRepository(@PrimaryDatabase DataSource dataSource) {
    this.dataSource = dataSource;
}
```

---

## 10. Request Context Inactive Diagnostics

### Typical symptom

```text id="xq6sn7"
jakarta.enterprise.context.ContextNotActiveException
```

### Bias diagnosis

```text id="1ojtib"
@ApplicationScoped singleton
  tries to use
@RequestScoped bean
  outside active request context
```

atau:

```text id="77n39s"
Async task / CompletableFuture
  runs on worker thread
  request context no longer active
```

### Common causes

| Cause | Example | Fix |
|---|---|---|
| Access request bean during startup | `@PostConstruct` reads `@RequestScoped` bean | Jangan akses request object saat startup. |
| Async thread uses request bean | `CompletableFuture.supplyAsync(() -> requestBean.get())` | Pass immutable context explicitly. |
| CDI/Servlet/Jersey integration incomplete | `@RequestScoped` tidak aktif di request | Perbaiki integration. |
| Request-scoped object stored in singleton | Field menyimpan context | Gunakan method parameter. |
| Test tidak membuka request context | Unit test inject request bean | Gunakan test extension atau hindari request scope. |

### Safer pattern

```java id="39rr2b"
public OrderDto getOrder(
    String orderId,
    PrincipalContext principal,
    RequestContext requestContext
) {
    // request data passed explicitly
}
```

Jangan menjadikan request-scoped bean sebagai dependency langsung dari singleton service kecuali benar-benar paham lifecycle/proxy dan request context propagation.

---

## 11. Scope Leak Diagnostics

### Symptom

```text id="jm57v7"
Log request B berisi correlationId request A
User A melihat data tenant B
Audit actor salah
Random flaky behavior under load
```

### Main suspects

| Suspect | Why |
|---|---|
| `static RequestContext current` | Global state lintas request. |
| Singleton field `RequestContext context` | Singleton dibagi semua request. |
| `ThreadLocal` tanpa cleanup | Thread pool mendaur ulang thread. |
| MDC tanpa `clear()` | Log context bocor. |
| Mutable singleton service | Race condition. |
| Cache key tanpa tenant | Cross-tenant result leak. |

### Code smell search

```bash id="ra9egr"
grep -R "static .*RequestContext\|ThreadLocal\|MDC.put" -n src/main/java
```

### Correct MDC pattern

```java id="kva6am"
try {
    MDC.put("correlationId", context.correlationId());
    MDC.put("tenantId", context.tenantId());

    return chain.proceed();
} finally {
    MDC.clear();
}
```

### Correct service pattern

```java id="2a869t"
@ApplicationScoped
public class OrderService {

    public OrderDto getOrder(
        String orderId,
        PrincipalContext principal,
        RequestContext context
    ) {
        // no request context stored as field
    }
}
```

---

## 12. Interceptor Not Firing Diagnostics

CDI 4.1 builds on Jakarta Interceptors and associates interceptors with beans using typesafe interceptor bindings. ([Jakarta EE](https://jakarta.ee/specifications/cdi/4.1/jakarta-cdi-spec-4.1?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) Jika interceptor tidak firing, biasanya karena target bukan CDI-managed atau invocation tidak lewat proxy.

### Symptom

```text id="ezo57p"
@Audited method executed,
but audit interceptor not called
```

### Root causes

| Cause | Example | Fix |
|---|---|---|
| Object dibuat manual | `new OrderService()` | Let CDI create it. |
| Object HK2-owned only | Service bound only in HK2 | Pindahkan ke CDI atau buat CDI-managed facade. |
| Self-invocation | `this.approve()` dari method class yang sama | Panggil lewat bean lain/facade. |
| Annotation bukan interceptor binding | Missing `@InterceptorBinding` | Tambahkan binding annotation. |
| Annotation retention salah | `RetentionPolicy.CLASS` | Harus runtime. |
| Interceptor tidak enabled | Missing `@Priority` / config | Tambahkan `@Priority` atau enable sesuai runtime. |
| Method tidak eligible | final/private/static tergantung proxy rules | Pindahkan ke public/protected managed method. |

### Diagnostic test

```java id="jbgksf"
@Test
void auditInterceptorMustFire() {
    orderApprovalFacade.approve(command);

    assertTrue(auditSink.containsAction("APPROVE_ORDER"));
}
```

### Recommended structure

```text id="rde6jz"
Resource
  ↓
CDI Facade method annotated @Audited
  ↓
Domain service
```

Jangan taruh interceptor pada helper private/internal method dan berharap self-call memicu AOP.

---

## 13. Filter / Provider Not Invoked Diagnostics

### Symptom

```text id="2d42n0"
AuthenticationFilter tidak jalan
SecurityHeadersFilter tidak menambah header
ExceptionMapper tidak menangkap exception
```

### Common causes

| Cause | Fix |
|---|---|
| Filter tidak registered | Register eksplisit di `ResourceConfig`. |
| Package scanning tidak mencakup package provider | Hindari scanning untuk critical provider. |
| Missing `@Provider` jika mengandalkan scanning | Tambahkan `@Provider`. |
| Name binding tidak cocok | Pastikan annotation ada di filter dan resource/method. |
| `@PreMatching` salah dipakai | Jangan gunakan bila butuh resource method info. |
| Priority salah | Set `@Priority` eksplisit. |
| Exception ditangkap sebelum mapper | Hilangkan catch-all di resource/service boundary. |
| Mapper type terlalu spesifik/salah | Register mapper untuk exception actual atau base domain exception. |

### Diagnostic registration

```java id="xj7y6x"
public final class AppResourceConfig extends ResourceConfig {

    public AppResourceConfig() {
        register(OrderResource.class);

        // explicitly register critical providers
        register(AuthenticationFilter.class);
        register(SecurityHeadersFilter.class);
        register(DomainExceptionMapper.class);
        register(UnhandledExceptionMapper.class);
    }
}
```

### Smoke test

```java id="48tbjz"
@Test
void authFilterMustRejectAnonymousRequest() {
    given()
    .when()
        .get("/orders/123")
    .then()
        .statusCode(401);
}
```

---

## 14. `NoSuchMethodError` / `ClassNotFoundException` Diagnostics

### Pattern

```text id="m8ub0p"
Compile-time classpath ≠ runtime classpath
or
dependency A expects version X,
dependency B provides version Y
```

### Diagnostic steps

```bash id="kybkcb"
mvn -q dependency:tree -Dverbose
```

```bash id="7s95kt"
mvn dependency:tree \
  -Dincludes=org.glassfish.jersey,org.glassfish.hk2,jakarta.ws.rs,jakarta.inject,jakarta.enterprise
```

```bash id="lqrz8o"
jar tf target/app.jar | grep -E "javax/ws/rs|jakarta/ws/rs"
```

### Common fixes

| Problem | Fix |
|---|---|
| Old transitive `javax.ws.rs-api` | Exclude or upgrade dependency. |
| Multiple Jersey versions | Use BOM / dependencyManagement. |
| App packages API jar that server already provides | Mark as `provided` if appropriate for WAR/server deployment. |
| Different HK2 transitive versions | Align via dependency management. |
| Weld API but no implementation | Add runtime implementation/integration. |
| Shaded jar conflict | Inspect final artifact. |

### Maven guardrail

```xml id="4nt9lu"
<dependencyManagement>
    <dependencies>
        <!-- align versions here; use official BOM if your stack provides one -->
    </dependencies>
</dependencyManagement>
```

---

## 15. Startup Debug Checklist

Gunakan checklist ini saat aplikasi gagal start.

| Step | Question | Evidence |
|---:|---|---|---|
| 1 | Error muncul saat compile, startup, atau request pertama? | Stack trace phase. |
| 2 | Class yang gagal dibuat dimiliki HK2/CDI/manual? | `ResourceConfig`, CDI scope, `new`. |
| 3 | Injection point type apa? | Interface/class/qualifier. |
| 4 | Ada binding/bean untuk type itu? | HK2 binder / CDI producer / scoped bean. |
| 5 | Ada lebih dari satu kandidat? | Ambiguous exception / multiple impl. |
| 6 | Scope dependency aktif? | Request scope, app scope, async boundary. |
| 7 | Namespace konsisten? | `javax.*` vs `jakarta.*`. |
| 8 | Dependency tree bersih? | `mvn dependency:tree`. |
| 9 | Provider/filter/mapper registered? | `ResourceConfig`. |
| 10 | Object dibuat manual? | grep `new`. |
| 11 | Interceptor seharusnya aktif? | CDI ownership + proxy invocation. |
| 12 | Local vs container classpath beda? | final artifact / image inspection. |

---

## 16. Runtime Debug Logging yang Aman

Tambahkan startup summary non-secret.

```text id="a5e9ph"
Starting order-service
version=1.12.4
profile=DEV
jersey.version=...
jakarta.ws.rs.version=...
cdi.enabled=true
weld.version=...
hk2.enabled=true
registered.resources=OrderResource, HealthResource
registered.filters=AuthenticationFilter, CorrelationIdFilter
registered.mappers=DomainExceptionMapper, UnhandledExceptionMapper
config.source=env+mounted-file
```

Jangan log secret, token, cookie, raw auth header, atau private key.

### Optional diagnostic endpoint

Untuk non-prod:

```text id="wk54pt"
GET /internal/diagnostics/runtime
```

Isi aman:

```json id="88w3b4"
{
  "profile": "DEV",
  "version": "1.12.4",
  "resources": ["OrderResource", "HealthResource"],
  "filters": ["AuthenticationFilter", "CorrelationIdFilter"],
  "mappers": ["DomainExceptionMapper"],
  "configSources": ["env", "mounted-file"]
}
```

Production: matikan atau batasi keras dengan admin/internal network.

---

## 17. Test Strategy for DI Failures

| Failure Class | Test Type | Example |
|---|---|---|
| Missing HK2 binding | Jersey resource test | Resource endpoint starts and injects service. |
| Missing CDI producer | CDI container test | `AppConfig` bean resolves. |
| Wrong qualifier | CDI unit/integration test | `@PrimaryDatabase` resolves correct datasource. |
| Filter not registered | API smoke test | anonymous request returns 401. |
| Mapper not registered | API error test | domain exception returns expected JSON. |
| Interceptor not firing | CDI integration test | audit event emitted. |
| Request context inactive | async/request test | context passed explicitly, no scoped bean access. |
| Namespace mismatch | Maven enforcer/dependency tree CI | build fails on banned deps. |
| Scope leak | concurrent test | correlation/tenant never crosses requests. |

### Jersey smoke test

```java id="v36aff"
@Test
void applicationStartsWithExpectedProviders() {
    ResourceConfig config = new AppResourceConfig();

    assertTrue(config.getClasses().contains(OrderResource.class));
    assertTrue(config.getClasses().contains(AuthenticationFilter.class));
    assertTrue(config.getClasses().contains(DomainExceptionMapper.class));
}
```

### Service unit test without container

```java id="4tjkkx"
@Test
void serviceShouldNotNeedContainerForBusinessLogic() {
    OrderService service = new OrderService(
        new FakeOrderRepository(),
        new OrderAccessPolicy(),
        new InMemoryAuditPublisher()
    );

    OrderDto dto = service.getOrder(
        "order-1",
        principal(),
        requestContext()
    );

    assertEquals("order-1", dto.id());
}
```

## 18. CI Guardrails

### Dependency tree check

```bash id="13hi0y"
mvn dependency:tree \
  -Dincludes=javax.ws.rs,javax.inject,javax.enterprise
```

Fail build jika modern Jakarta project masih membawa `javax.*` API tidak disengaja.

### Maven Enforcer idea

```xml id="bpo5d7"
<plugin>
    <groupId>org.apache.maven.plugins</groupId>
    <artifactId>maven-enforcer-plugin</artifactId>
    <version>${maven-enforcer-plugin.version}</version>
    <executions>
        <execution>
            <goals>
                <goal>enforce</goal>
            </goals>
            <configuration>
                <rules>
                    <dependencyConvergence />
                    <requireUpperBoundDeps />
                </rules>
            </configuration>
        </execution>
    </executions>
</plugin>
```

### CI checklist

```text id="ocm3kg"
No merge if:
- dependency convergence fails
- banned javax dependencies appear
- Jersey resource smoke test fails
- auth filter smoke test fails
- exception mapper test fails
- CDI interceptor test fails
- policy/security tests fail
```

---

## 19. Production Failure Playbook

### Scenario A - App fails startup with unsatisfied dependency

```text id="7evncn"
1. Identify class being constructed.
2. Identify injection point.
3. Determine owner: HK2 or CDI.
4. Check corresponding binder/producer/bean.
5. Check qualifier/named binding.
6. Check dependency tree and namespace.
7. Add failing integration test before fix.
```

### Scenario B - Works local, fails Kubernetes

```text id="r209vt"
1. Compare app artifact/image version.
2. Compare APP_PROFILE.
3. Compare env vars and mounted files.
4. Check classpath inside image.
5. Check package scanning assumptions.
6. Check missing secret/config causing producer failure.
7. Check startup logs for registered resources/providers.
```

### Scenario C - Interceptor not firing

```text id="84tf20"
1. Confirm target bean is CDI-managed.
2. Confirm annotation is @InterceptorBinding.
3. Confirm retention is RUNTIME.
4. Confirm interceptor has @Interceptor and priority/enabling.
5. Confirm method call is not self-invocation.
6. Confirm test verifies side effect.
```

### Scenario D - Request context leak

```text id="pu2e1y"
1. Search for static/context/thread-local/MDC usage.
2. Check singleton fields.
3. Run concurrent request test with different tenants.
4. Verify MDC.clear in finally.
5. Move request data to explicit RequestContext parameter.
```

---

## 20. Anti-Patterns

| Anti-Pattern | Why Dangerous | Better |
|---|---|---|
| Add `@Inject` until compile passes | Tidak menjawab ownership/lifecycle | Map owner + binding. |
| `new Service()` inside resource | Bypass DI/interceptors | Constructor injection. |
| Store request context in singleton | Cross-request leak | Pass explicit context. |
| Use `CDI.current()` everywhere | Service locator hidden dependency | Boundary adapter only. |
| Use package scanning for security filters | Bisa miss silently | Explicit registration. |
| Mix `javax.*` and `jakarta.*` | Runtime class mismatch | One namespace per app generation. |
| Catch all exceptions in resource | Mapper tidak jalan | Let mapper handle domain exceptions. |
| Log full dependency/config/secret | Secret leakage | Safe startup summary. |
| Assume unit test proves DI wiring | Unit test bypasses container | Add integration smoke tests. |
| Use ThreadLocal as business context | Async/thread pool unsafe | Explicit context object. |

---

## 21. Quick Diagnostic Commands

```bash id="s7q4tf"
# Show relevant dependency tree
mvn dependency:tree \
  -Dincludes=org.glassfish.jersey,org.glassfish.hk2,org.jboss.weld,jakarta.ws.rs,jakarta.inject,jakarta.enterprise
```

```bash id="dwlxwy"
# Find old namespace imports
grep -R "import javax\." -n src/main/java src/test/java
```

```bash id="gr5wwq"
# Find manual construction of services
grep -R "new .*Service\|new .*Repository\|new .*Policy" -n src/main/java
```

```bash id="g3dwmu"
# Find ThreadLocal/MDC usage
grep -R "ThreadLocal\|MDC\.put\|MDC\.clear" -n src/main/java
```

```bash id="lg4s5o"
# Find resource/provider registration
grep -R "register(.*Filter\|register(.*Mapper\|register(new .*Binder" -n src/main/java
```

```bash id="n56qgp"
# Inspect final jar for namespace
jar tf target/*.jar | grep -E "javax/ws/rs|jakarta/ws/rs|org/glassfish/jersey"
```

## 22. Production Checklist

| Checklist | Status |
|---|---|
| Dependency tree reviewed for Jersey/HK2/CDI/Jakarta alignment | Wajib |
| No unintended `javax.*` dependencies in Jakarta app | Wajib |
| `ResourceConfig` explicitly registers critical filters/mappers/binders | Wajib |
| Every component has one clear owner | Wajib |
| No manual `new` for managed service/repository/policy | Wajib |
| No singleton/static field stores request/user/tenant context | Wajib |
| CDI interceptor activation covered by integration test | Wajib jika pakai interceptor |
| Request-scoped object not used outside active request | Wajib |
| MDC/ThreadLocal cleanup tested or reviewed | Wajib |
| Config loaded once and fail-fast | Wajib |
| Startup logs show non-secret runtime summary | Wajib |
| API smoke tests verify auth filter and error mapper active | Wajib |
| CI blocks dependency convergence/namespace regression | Wajib |

| Urutan | Fokus | Target Praktik |
|---:|---|---|
| 1 | Dependency tree | Bisa menemukan conflict Jersey/HK2/CDI/Jakarta dari Maven tree. |
| 2 | Namespace audit | Bisa memastikan `javax.*` dan `jakarta.*` tidak tercampur. |
| 3 | Object ownership | Bisa menentukan object dibuat HK2, CDI, manual, atau runtime. |
| 4 | HK2 binding diagnostics | Bisa memperbaiki missing binder/factory/scope. |
| 5 | CDI diagnostics | Bisa memperbaiki unsatisfied/ambiguous/request context inactive. |
| 6 | Interceptor diagnostics | Bisa memastikan CDI interceptor benar-benar firing. |
| 7 | Scope leak detection | Bisa menemukan singleton/static/thread-local/MDC leak. |
| 8 | CI guardrails | Bisa mencegah regression DI lewat tests dan dependency checks. |

# Cheatsheet - Jersey/JAX-RS Production Bootstrap Checklist  
## ResourceConfig, DI Modules, Config Validation, Provider Registration, Health Checks, Startup Logs, Deployment Smoke Tests

## 1. Scope

Cheatsheet ini fokus pada **bootstrap production** untuk aplikasi Jersey/JAX-RS:

```text id="4xt1cc"
Production Bootstrap
│
├─ ResourceConfig / Application
├─ DI module registration
├─ Runtime config loading
├─ Secret loading
├─ Provider/filter/mapper registration
├─ Serialization setup
├─ Security setup
├─ Health/readiness endpoints
├─ Startup validation
├─ Startup logs
├─ Deployment smoke tests
└─ Failure policy
```

Tujuan akhirnya: saat aplikasi start, kita bisa menjawab dengan jelas:

```text id="w9f5p6"
Apa yang diregister?
Config apa yang dipakai?
Provider/filter/mapper apa yang aktif?
Dependency apa yang berhasil dibuat?
Health/readiness apa yang siap?
Kalau ada config/dependency salah, apakah gagal startup secara eksplisit?
```

Jakarta RESTful Web Services 4.0 adalah bagian dari Jakarta EE 11 dan menyediakan API dasar untuk membangun RESTful web services. Untuk aplikasi Jersey modern, bootstrap harus memperhatikan kompatibilitas Jakarta REST/Jersey/HK2/CDI serta tidak mencampur namespace lama dan baru. ([Jakarta EE](https://jakarta.ee/specifications/restful-ws/4.0/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 2. Bootstrap Mental Model

```text id="dn2kqe"
Main / Servlet Container / App Server
    ↓
AppResourceConfig / Application
    ↓
RuntimeConfigLoader
    ↓
DeploymentInvariantValidator
    ↓
DI Binder / CDI Producer
    ↓
Resource / Provider Registration
    ↓
Health / Readiness / Diagnostics
    ↓
Application Ready
```

Rule utama:

```text id="cgmpgn"
Bootstrap harus deterministic.
Tidak boleh bergantung pada kebetulan package scanning untuk komponen critical.
Tidak boleh start jika config/security/dependency critical invalid.
```

---

## 3. Production Bootstrap Decomposition

| Area | Item | Fungsi | Failure yang Dicegah |
|---|---|---|---|
| Application root | `ResourceConfig` / `Application` | Titik komposisi JAX-RS/Jersey | Resource/filter/mapper tidak aktif |
| Runtime config | `AppConfig` | Memuat profile, DB, HTTP, security, feature flag | Prod pakai local/default config |
| Secret loading | `SecretResolver` | Membaca secret dari env/file/secret manager | Secret hardcoded / missing |
| DI modules | HK2 binder / CDI producer | Menyusun object graph | Unsatisfied dependency saat request pertama |
| Provider registration | filter, mapper, interceptor, body provider | Mengaktifkan HTTP pipeline | Security/error/logging bolong |
| Serialization | JSON-B/Jackson config | Mapping request/response konsisten | Format beda antar endpoint |
| Security | auth filter, authz filter, context | Reject unauthenticated/unauthorized | Endpoint public tidak sengaja |
| Health checks | liveness/readiness/startup | Memberi sinyal ke orchestrator | Traffic masuk sebelum siap |
| Startup logs | non-secret runtime summary | Audit deployment | Sulit debug environment mismatch |
| Smoke tests | API-level sanity | Validasi deployment | Rollout broken lolos |

---

## 4. ResourceConfig sebagai Composition Root

```java id="9t3ncv"
public final class AppResourceConfig extends ResourceConfig {

    public AppResourceConfig() {
        RuntimeResources resources = RuntimeBootstrap.load();

        register(new AppHk2Binder(resources));

        registerResources();
        registerProviders();
        registerFilters();
        registerInterceptors();
        registerExceptionMappers();
        registerHealthEndpoints();

        property("app.name", resources.appConfig().appName());
        property("app.profile", resources.appConfig().profile().name());
    }

    private void registerResources() {
        register(OrderResource.class);
        register(CustomerResource.class);
        register(HealthResource.class);
        register(InternalDiagnosticsResource.class);
    }

    private void registerProviders() {
        register(JsonProvider.class);
    }

    private void registerFilters() {
        register(CorrelationIdFilter.class);
        register(AuthenticationFilter.class);
        register(AuthorizationFilter.class);
        register(SecurityHeadersFilter.class);
        register(AccessLogFilter.class);
    }

    private void registerInterceptors() {
        register(RequestBodyLimitInterceptor.class);
        register(ResponseEnvelopeWriterInterceptor.class);
    }

    private void registerExceptionMappers() {
        register(ValidationExceptionMapper.class);
        register(AuthenticationExceptionMapper.class);
        register(AuthorizationExceptionMapper.class);
        register(DomainExceptionMapper.class);
        register(UnhandledExceptionMapper.class);
    }

    private void registerHealthEndpoints() {
        register(HealthResource.class);
    }
}
```

JAX-RS/Jakarta REST mengenal resources, providers, filters, interceptors, dan exception mappers sebagai bagian dari runtime extensibility. Untuk komponen production-critical seperti auth filter, exception mapper, dan security headers, explicit registration lebih aman daripada hanya mengandalkan package scanning. Jakarta REST juga mendefinisikan container/client filters, entity interceptors, priorities, name binding, dan exception mapping sebagai provider model. ([Jakarta EE](https://jakarta.ee/specifications/restful-ws/4.0/jakarta-restful-ws-spec-4.0.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 5. RuntimeBootstrap Pattern

```java id="b2m3cy"
public final class RuntimeBootstrap {

    private RuntimeBootstrap() {}

    public static RuntimeResources load() {
        StartupTimer timer = StartupTimer.start();

        AppConfig config = RuntimeConfigLoader.load();
        RuntimeSecretBundle secrets = RuntimeSecretLoader.load(config);
        PolicyConfig policy = PolicyLoader.load(config);
        TlsConfig tls = TlsConfigLoader.load(config, secrets);

        RuntimeResources resources = new RuntimeResources(
            config,
            secrets,
            policy,
            tls
        );

        DeploymentInvariantValidator.validate(resources);
        StartupSummaryLogger.log(resources, timer.elapsed());

        return resources;
    }
}
```

```java id="e1d75i"
public record RuntimeResources(
    AppConfig appConfig,
    RuntimeSecretBundle secrets,
    PolicyConfig policyConfig,
    TlsConfig tlsConfig
) {}
```

Rule: **load → parse → validate → log safe summary → inject**.

Jangan melakukan lazy config loading di resource/service karena error deployment baru muncul setelah traffic masuk.

---

## 6. AppConfig Production Invariant

```java id="ojp51n"
public record AppConfig(
    String appName,
    String version,
    RuntimeProfile profile,
    DbConfig database,
    HttpClientConfig httpClient,
    SecurityConfig security,
    FeatureFlags featureFlags
) {
    public void validate() {
        requireNonBlank(appName, "appName");
        requireNonBlank(version, "version");

        database.validate();
        httpClient.validate();
        security.validate();
        featureFlags.validate(profile);

        if (profile.isProductionLike()) {
            validateProductionLike();
        }
    }

    private void validateProductionLike() {
        if (database.url().contains("localhost")
            || database.url().contains("127.0.0.1")) {
            throw new IllegalStateException(
                "Production-like profile must not use localhost database"
            );
        }

        if (!security.authenticationRequired()) {
            throw new IllegalStateException(
                "Authentication must be required in production-like profile"
            );
        }

        if (featureFlags.debugEndpointsEnabled()) {
            throw new IllegalStateException(
                "Debug endpoints must be disabled in production-like profile"
            );
        }
    }

    private static void requireNonBlank(String value, String name) {
        if (value == null || value.isBlank()) {
            throw new IllegalStateException("Missing config: " + name);
        }
    }
}
```

### Invariant table

| Invariant | Local | Dev | Test | Staging | Prod |
|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| `APP_PROFILE` explicit | Optional | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes |
| DB config validated | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes |
| Secret required | Optional | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes |
| No localhost dependency | No | Prefer | No | Yes | Yes |
| Auth required | Optional | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes |
| Debug endpoint disabled | Optional | Prefer | Optional | Yes | Yes |
| Audit enabled | Optional | Yes | Optional | Yes | Yes |
| Safe error mapper active | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes |
| Startup log non-secret | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes |

---

## 7. DI Binder Bootstrap

### HK2 binder

```java id="fhm7kn"
public final class AppHk2Binder extends AbstractBinder {

    private final RuntimeResources resources;

    public AppHk2Binder(RuntimeResources resources) {
        this.resources = resources;
    }

    @Override
    protected void configure() {
        bind(resources.appConfig()).to(AppConfig.class);
        bind(resources.policyConfig()).to(PolicyConfig.class);
        bind(resources.tlsConfig()).to(TlsConfig.class);

        bind(OrderServiceImpl.class)
            .to(OrderService.class)
            .in(Singleton.class);

        bind(OrderRepositoryPostgres.class)
            .to(OrderRepository.class)
            .in(Singleton.class);

        bindFactory(DataSourceFactory.class)
            .to(DataSource.class)
            .in(Singleton.class);

        bindFactory(RequestContextFactory.class)
            .to(RequestContext.class)
            .in(RequestScoped.class);
    }
}
```

### CDI producer equivalent

```java id="z7xmm4"
@ApplicationScoped
public class RuntimeProducer {

    private final RuntimeResources resources =
        RuntimeBootstrap.load();

    @Produces
    @ApplicationScoped
    public AppConfig appConfig() {
        return resources.appConfig();
    }

    @Produces
    @ApplicationScoped
    public PolicyConfig policyConfig() {
        return resources.policyConfig();
    }

    @Produces
    @ApplicationScoped
    public DataSource dataSource(AppConfig config) {
        return DataSourceFactory.create(config.database());
    }
}
```

Production rule: **jangan load config dua kali**. Jika HK2 binder dan CDI producer sama-sama memanggil `RuntimeBootstrap.load()`, hasilnya bisa duplicate pool/client/config dan sulit debug.

---

## 8. Provider Registration Checklist

| Provider Type | Example | Register Explicit? | Reason |
|---|---|---:|---|
| Authentication filter | `AuthenticationFilter` | Yes | Security critical |
| Authorization filter | `AuthorizationFilter` | Yes | Security critical |
| Correlation filter | `CorrelationIdFilter` | Yes | Observability critical |
| Access log filter | `AccessLogFilter` | Yes | Audit/debug |
| Security headers filter | `SecurityHeadersFilter` | Yes | Baseline hardening |
| Exception mapper | `DomainExceptionMapper` | Yes | Error contract |
| Unknown exception mapper | `UnhandledExceptionMapper` | Yes | No stack trace leakage |
| JSON provider | `JsonProvider` | Yes | Serialization consistency |
| Reader/writer interceptor | body limit/envelope | Yes | Data handling consistency |
| Dynamic feature | feature gate/auth annotation | Yes | Conditional behavior |

---

## 9. Startup Failure Policy

Bootstrap should fail if critical dependency/config is invalid.

```text id="ea4hd4"
Fail startup if:
- required config missing
- production-like profile unsafe
- secret missing/weak
- DB pool cannot initialize when required
- migration state incompatible
- security provider not registered
- JSON provider incompatible
- required policy file invalid
- health endpoint cannot be registered
```

```java id="94dx8s"
public final class DeploymentInvariantValidator {

    public static void validate(RuntimeResources resources) {
        AppConfig config = resources.appConfig();

        config.validate();

        if (config.profile().isProductionLike()) {
            requireAuthentication(config);
            requireAudit(config);
            requireNoDebug(config);
            requireStrongSecrets(resources.secrets());
            requirePolicyVersion(resources.policyConfig());
        }
    }

    private static void requireAuthentication(AppConfig config) {
        if (!config.security().authenticationRequired()) {
            throw new IllegalStateException(
                "Authentication must be enabled in production-like profile"
            );
        }
    }

    private static void requireAudit(AppConfig config) {
        if (!config.security().auditEnabled()) {
            throw new IllegalStateException(
                "Audit must be enabled in production-like profile"
            );
        }
    }

    private static void requireNoDebug(AppConfig config) {
        if (config.featureFlags().debugEndpointsEnabled()) {
            throw new IllegalStateException(
                "Debug endpoints must be disabled in production-like profile"
            );
        }
    }

    private static void requireStrongSecrets(RuntimeSecretBundle secrets) {
        if (secrets.dbPassword().isDefaultOrWeak()) {
            throw new IllegalStateException(
                "Weak/default DB password is forbidden"
            );
        }
    }

    private static void requirePolicyVersion(PolicyConfig policy) {
        if (policy.policyVersion() == null || policy.policyVersion().isBlank()) {
            throw new IllegalStateException("Policy version is required");
        }
    }
}
```

---

## 10. Health Endpoint Strategy

Untuk Kubernetes/on-prem/cloud deployment, health endpoint harus dibedakan:

```text id="q8scm0"
Liveness
  = proses masih hidup / tidak deadlock fatal

Readiness
  = siap menerima traffic

Startup
  = masih dalam fase boot lambat, jangan kill dulu
```

Kubernetes mendefinisikan liveness, readiness, dan startup probes sebagai diagnostic periodik oleh kubelet; berdasarkan hasil probe, Kubernetes dapat me-restart container yang tidak sehat atau menghentikan pengiriman traffic ke container yang belum ready. ([Kubernetes](https://kubernetes.io/docs/concepts/workloads/pods/probes/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

### Health endpoint design

| Endpoint | Purpose | Check |
|---|---|---|
| `/health/live` | Apakah process hidup? | Minimal JVM/app alive |
| `/health/ready` | Apakah bisa menerima traffic? | DB, migration, critical downstream, config ready |
| `/health/startup` | Apakah boot masih berjalan? | Startup complete flag |
| `/internal/diagnostics/runtime` | Non-prod diagnostics | Registered providers, version, profile, non-secret config source |

### Jersey resource

```java id="rwq5gs"
@Path("/health")
@Produces(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
public final class HealthResource {

    private final ReadinessChecker readinessChecker;
    private final StartupState startupState;

    @Inject
    public HealthResource(ReadinessChecker readinessChecker,
                          StartupState startupState) {
        this.readinessChecker = readinessChecker;
        this.startupState = startupState;
    }

    @GET
    @Path("/live")
    public Response live() {
        return Response.ok(Map.of("status", "UP")).build();
    }

    @GET
    @Path("/ready")
    public Response ready() {
        HealthStatus status = readinessChecker.check();

        if (status.up()) {
            return Response.ok(status).build();
        }

        return Response.status(Response.Status.SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE)
            .entity(status)
            .build();
    }

    @GET
    @Path("/startup")
    public Response startup() {
        if (startupState.complete()) {
            return Response.ok(Map.of("status", "UP")).build();
        }

        return Response.status(Response.Status.SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE)
            .entity(Map.of("status", "STARTING"))
            .build();
    }
}
```

MicroProfile Health menyediakan cara standar bagi aplikasi untuk mengekspos status health ke sistem eksternal seperti Kubernetes; jika stack Anda memakai MicroProfile, pakai standar itu, tetapi aplikasi Jersey standalone tetap bisa memakai endpoint eksplisit seperti di atas. ([MicroProfile](https://microprofile.io/specifications/health/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 11. Readiness Checker

```java id="r3q82r"
public final class ReadinessChecker {

    private final DataSource dataSource;
    private final MigrationStateChecker migrationStateChecker;
    private final CriticalDependencyChecker dependencyChecker;

    public HealthStatus check() {
        List<HealthCheckResult> results = List.of(
            checkDatabase(),
            migrationStateChecker.check(),
            dependencyChecker.check()
        );

        boolean up = results.stream().allMatch(HealthCheckResult::up);

        return new HealthStatus(
            up ? "UP" : "DOWN",
            results
        );
    }

    private HealthCheckResult checkDatabase() {
        try (Connection connection = dataSource.getConnection();
             PreparedStatement statement = connection.prepareStatement("SELECT 1")) {

            statement.execute();
            return HealthCheckResult.up("database");
        } catch (SQLException e) {
            return HealthCheckResult.down("database", "DB_UNAVAILABLE");
        }
    }
}
```

### Readiness rules

| Check | Liveness | Readiness | Startup |
|---|---:|---:|---:|
| Process alive | Yes | Yes | Yes |
| DB reachable | No | Yes | Maybe |
| Migration compatible | No | Yes | Yes |
| Config loaded | No | Yes | Yes |
| Secret loaded | No | Yes | Yes |
| Critical downstream reachable | No | Maybe/Yes | Maybe |
| Optional downstream reachable | No | No | No |
| Thread pool saturation | Maybe | Yes | No |
| Disk full/temp unavailable | Maybe | Yes | No |

Jangan membuat liveness terlalu ketat. Kalau liveness mengecek DB dan DB sedang transient down, Kubernetes bisa me-restart semua pod tanpa menyelesaikan masalah.

---

## 12. Kubernetes Probe Example

```yaml id="tj0iov"
apiVersion: apps/v1
kind: Deployment
metadata:
  name: order-service
spec:
  template:
    spec:
      containers:
        - name: order-service
          image: registry.example.com/order-service:1.12.4
          ports:
            - containerPort: 8080
          startupProbe:
            httpGet:
              path: /health/startup
              port: 8080
            failureThreshold: 30
            periodSeconds: 5
          livenessProbe:
            httpGet:
              path: /health/live
              port: 8080
            periodSeconds: 10
            failureThreshold: 3
          readinessProbe:
            httpGet:
              path: /health/ready
              port: 8080
            periodSeconds: 5
            failureThreshold: 3
```

Kubernetes startup probe berguna untuk aplikasi yang butuh waktu inisialisasi lebih lama; liveness/readiness probe tidak perlu mulai menghukum container terlalu cepat selama startup belum selesai. Kubernetes docs terbaru menjelaskan konfigurasi probe untuk liveness, readiness, dan startup probe pada container. ([Kubernetes](https://kubernetes.io/docs/tasks/configure-pod-container/configure-liveness-readiness-startup-probes/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 13. Startup Logs - Safe Summary

Startup log harus cukup detail untuk troubleshooting, tapi tidak membocorkan secret.

```text id="81wo9c"
Starting order-service
version=1.12.4
profile=PROD
java.version=17
jersey.version=4.x
jakarta.rest.version=4.0
config.source=env+mounted-file
config.file=/etc/order-service/application.yaml
policy.name=order-approval-policy
policy.version=2026-07-01
db.url=jdbc:postgresql://postgres-prod:5432/orderdb
db.username=order_app
db.password=****
auth.enabled=true
audit.enabled=true
debugEndpointsEnabled=false
registered.resources=OrderResource,CustomerResource,HealthResource
registered.filters=CorrelationIdFilter,AuthenticationFilter,AuthorizationFilter
registered.mappers=ValidationExceptionMapper,DomainExceptionMapper,UnhandledExceptionMapper
startup.elapsedMs=1243
```

Jangan log:

```text id="a13fpu"
Authorization=Bearer ...
Cookie=session=...
DB_PASSWORD=...
JWT_PRIVATE_KEY=...
clientSecret=...
```

### Logger helper

```java id="s9ul5y"
public final class StartupSummaryLogger {

    public static void log(RuntimeResources resources, Duration elapsed) {
        AppConfig config = resources.appConfig();

        LOGGER.info(
            "Starting {} version={} profile={} config.source={} " +
            "db.url={} db.username={} db.password={} " +
            "auth.enabled={} audit.enabled={} startup.elapsedMs={}",
            config.appName(),
            config.version(),
            config.profile(),
            config.configSourceSummary(),
            config.database().url(),
            config.database().username(),
            "****",
            config.security().authenticationRequired(),
            config.security().auditEnabled(),
            elapsed.toMillis()
        );
    }
}
```

## 14. Deployment Smoke Tests

Smoke test setelah deployment harus menguji **bootstrap aktif**, bukan business flow lengkap.

```text id="bio367"
Deployment Smoke Test
│
├─ /health/live returns 200
├─ /health/ready returns 200
├─ anonymous protected endpoint returns 401
├─ invalid token returns 401
├─ authorized minimal endpoint returns 200
├─ unknown route returns safe 404
├─ domain error maps to expected JSON
├─ correlation ID returned
└─ startup log contains expected version/profile
```

### Example smoke script

```bash id="zaf16h"
#!/usr/bin/env bash
set -euo pipefail

BASE_URL="${BASE_URL:?BASE_URL required}"

curl -fsS "$BASE_URL/health/live"
curl -fsS "$BASE_URL/health/ready"

STATUS=$(curl -s -o /tmp/resp.json -w "%{http_code}" "$BASE_URL/orders/does-not-matter")
test "$STATUS" = "401"

STATUS=$(curl -s -o /tmp/resp.json -w "%{http_code}" \
  -H "Authorization: Bearer invalid-token" \
  "$BASE_URL/orders/does-not-matter")
test "$STATUS" = "401"

echo "Smoke tests passed"
```

### Authenticated smoke test

```bash id="6aujxp"
STATUS=$(curl -s -o /tmp/resp.json -w "%{http_code}" \
  -H "Authorization: Bearer $SMOKE_TEST_TOKEN" \
  -H "X-Tenant-Id: $SMOKE_TEST_TENANT" \
  -H "X-Correlation-Id: smoke-$(date +%s)" \
  "$BASE_URL/internal/whoami")

test "$STATUS" = "200"
```
