## 15. Internal Diagnostics Endpoint

Diagnostics endpoint berguna di dev/staging, tapi harus dikunci atau dimatikan di prod.

```java id="5cezsw"
@Path("/internal/diagnostics/runtime")
@Produces(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
public final class InternalDiagnosticsResource {

    private final AppConfig config;
    private final RuntimeRegistrySnapshot registrySnapshot;

    @Inject
    public InternalDiagnosticsResource(AppConfig config,
                                       RuntimeRegistrySnapshot registrySnapshot) {
        this.config = config;
        this.registrySnapshot = registrySnapshot;
    }

    @GET
    public Response runtime() {
        if (config.profile().isProductionLike()
            && !config.featureFlags().diagnosticsEndpointEnabled()) {
            throw new NotFoundException();
        }

        return Response.ok(Map.of(
            "appName", config.appName(),
            "version", config.version(),
            "profile", config.profile().name(),
            "resources", registrySnapshot.resources(),
            "filters", registrySnapshot.filters(),
            "mappers", registrySnapshot.mappers(),
            "configSources", config.configSources()
        )).build();
    }
}
```

Diagnostics response tidak boleh berisi secret, raw env, token, full config, atau internal network topology sensitive.

---

## 16. Bootstrap Test Matrix

| Test | Purpose |
|---|---|
| `AppResourceConfig` creates successfully | Basic bootstrap works |
| Required providers registered | Security/error/logging active |
| Missing config fails startup | Fail-fast works |
| Weak prod secret fails startup | Production invariant works |
| Debug endpoint disabled in prod | Safety invariant works |
| Health live returns UP | Process-level endpoint works |
| Health ready returns DOWN when DB down | Readiness honest |
| Auth filter rejects anonymous | Security filter active |
| Exception mapper returns safe JSON | Error mapping active |
| Startup summary masks secrets | Safe observability |
| Dependency tree has no `javax.*` regression | Namespace clean |

### JUnit-style bootstrap test

```java id="a7twsn"
@Test
void appResourceConfigMustRegisterCriticalProviders() {
    ResourceConfig config = new AppResourceConfig();

    Set<Class<?>> classes = config.getClasses();

    assertTrue(classes.contains(OrderResource.class));
    assertTrue(classes.contains(AuthenticationFilter.class));
    assertTrue(classes.contains(AuthorizationFilter.class));
    assertTrue(classes.contains(CorrelationIdFilter.class));
    assertTrue(classes.contains(DomainExceptionMapper.class));
    assertTrue(classes.contains(UnhandledExceptionMapper.class));
}
```

### Config validation test

```java id="42fggp"
@Test
void productionProfileMustRejectDebugEndpoints() {
    AppConfig config = validConfigBuilder()
        .profile(RuntimeProfile.PROD)
        .featureFlags(new FeatureFlags(
            true,  // newOrderFlow
            true,  // debugEndpointsEnabled
            true   // strictTenantValidation
        ))
        .build();

    IllegalStateException ex = assertThrows(
        IllegalStateException.class,
        config::validate
    );

    assertTrue(ex.getMessage().contains("Debug endpoints"));
}
```

---

## 17. Provider Ordering Checklist

| Concern | Extension Point | Priority |
|---|---|---|
| Correlation ID | Request filter | Very early |
| Authentication | Request filter | `Priorities.AUTHENTICATION` |
| Authorization | Request filter / DynamicFeature | `Priorities.AUTHORIZATION` |
| Request logging | Request/response filter | After correlation, around request |
| Body size / entity coding | Reader interceptor | Entity stage |
| Business exception mapping | ExceptionMapper | Type-specific |
| Unknown exception mapping | ExceptionMapper | Catch-all |
| Security headers | Response filter | Late response decoration |
| MDC cleanup | Response filter/finally | Always |

Critical point: response filter ordering is not the same mental model as request filters. Jakarta REST specifies provider priority ordering for filter/interceptor chains, and response-side chains are sorted in reverse direction compared to request-side chains. ([Jakarta EE](https://jakarta.ee/specifications/restful-ws/4.0/jakarta-restful-ws-spec-4.0.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 18. Production Bootstrap Failure Modes

| Symptom | Root Cause | Fix |
|---|---|---|
| App starts but auth not active | Filter not registered / scanning missed | Explicit registration + smoke test |
| First request fails DI | Lazy dependency creation | Eager startup validation / DI integration test |
| Pod receives traffic before DB ready | Readiness too shallow | DB/migration readiness check |
| Pod restarts when DB down | Liveness checks DB | Move DB check to readiness |
| Production uses local defaults | Missing profile guard | Production-like invariant |
| Secret appears in startup log | Raw config logging | SecretValue + safe summary |
| Error response leaks stack trace | Missing catch-all mapper | Register `UnhandledExceptionMapper` |
| Staging behavior differs from prod | Staging invariants weaker | Treat staging as production-like |
| Mapper/filter absent in one environment | Package scanning/classpath difference | Explicit registration |
| Rollout succeeds but endpoint broken | No smoke tests | Add deployment smoke gate |

---

## 19. CI/CD Gate

```text id="d74y57"
No deploy if:
- unit tests fail
- DI/bootstrap tests fail
- dependency convergence fails
- namespace audit fails
- config invariant tests fail
- security smoke tests fail
- migration compatibility check fails
```

### GitHub Actions sketch

```yaml id="94lw2e"
name: build-and-bootstrap-check

on:
  pull_request:
    branches: [ main ]

jobs:
  bootstrap:
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest

    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v4

      - uses: actions/setup-java@v4
        with:
          distribution: temurin
          java-version: '17'

      - name: Dependency tree
        run: mvn dependency:tree

      - name: Unit and bootstrap tests
        run: mvn test

      - name: Integration tests
        run: mvn verify -Pintegration
```

---

## 20. Production Bootstrap Checklist

| Area | Checklist |
|---|---|
| `ResourceConfig` | Explicitly registers resources, filters, mappers, providers, health endpoints |
| DI | HK2/CDI ownership clear; config created once; no duplicate producers/binders |
| Config | Loaded, parsed, typed, validated before traffic |
| Secrets | Loaded from env/file/secret manager; masked; no hardcoded fallback |
| Security | Auth/authz filters active; security headers active; protected endpoint smoke-tested |
| Error handling | Domain mapper and catch-all mapper active; no stack trace leakage |
| Context | Correlation/request context initialized and returned in response |
| Observability | Startup logs include version/profile/providers but no secret |
| Health | Liveness shallow; readiness checks critical dependencies |
| Kubernetes | Startup/liveness/readiness probes configured separately |
| Diagnostics | Internal diagnostics disabled or heavily restricted in prod |
| Tests | Bootstrap, config invariant, provider registration, smoke tests in CI |
| Deployment | Rollout blocked if smoke tests fail |
| Dependency | No unintended `javax.*`/Jakarta mismatch; dependency tree reviewed |

---

## 21. Roadmap Belajar Singkat

| Urutan | Fokus | Target Praktik |
|---:|---|---|
| 1 | Composition root | Bisa membuat `ResourceConfig` deterministic dan explicit. |
| 2 | Runtime bootstrap | Bisa load config/secret/policy sekali dan validate fail-fast. |
| 3 | DI modules | Bisa menyusun HK2/CDI object graph tanpa duplicate ownership. |
| 4 | Provider registration | Bisa memastikan filter/mapper/interceptor critical aktif. |
| 5 | Health checks | Bisa membedakan liveness, readiness, startup. |
| 6 | Startup observability | Bisa log summary aman untuk audit deployment. |
| 7 | Smoke tests | Bisa membuktikan deployment siap menerima traffic. |
| 8 | CI/CD gate | Bisa memblokir rollout jika bootstrap/security/regression gagal. |

# Cheatsheet - Jersey/JAX-RS ResourceConfig & Provider Registration Deep Dive  
## Packages vs Explicit Register, Provider Lifecycle, Priorities, DynamicFeature, Production Registration Tests

## 1. Scope

Cheatsheet ini membahas **ResourceConfig dan provider registration** di Jersey/JAX-RS:

```text id="6hnq4w"
ResourceConfig / Application
│
├─ Resource registration
│  ├─ explicit class registration
│  ├─ package scanning
│  └─ programmatic resource model
│
├─ Provider registration
│  ├─ filters
│  ├─ interceptors
│  ├─ exception mappers
│  ├─ message body readers/writers
│  ├─ context resolvers
│  └─ features / dynamic features
│
├─ DI registration
│  ├─ HK2 Binder
│  ├─ Factory / Supplier
│  └─ CDI bridge / producer boundary
│
├─ Ordering
│  ├─ @Priority
│  ├─ request chain
│  ├─ response chain
│  └─ entity chain
│
└─ Production tests
   ├─ registration smoke test
   ├─ provider activation test
   ├─ security filter test
   ├─ exception mapper test
   └─ dependency/version test
```

Jakarta REST menyebut filters dan entity interceptors sebagai **providers**; provider memperluas runtime JAX-RS, misalnya untuk request/response filtering, entity read/write interception, message body mapping, dan exception mapping. ([Jakarta EE](https://jakarta.ee/specifications/restful-ws/4.0/jakarta-restful-ws-spec-4.0?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 2. Mental Model

```text id="zd51hj"
ResourceConfig bukan sekadar tempat scan package.

ResourceConfig adalah composition root:
- resource mana yang aktif
- provider mana yang aktif
- filter mana yang aktif
- mapper mana yang aktif
- feature mana yang aktif
- binder/DI module mana yang aktif
- property runtime apa yang dipasang
```

Untuk production, bias yang aman:

```text id="0pnx3r"
Explicit registration untuk komponen critical.
Package scanning hanya untuk resource biasa atau dev convenience.
```

Komponen seperti authentication filter, authorization filter, exception mapper, security header filter, JSON provider, dan DI binder sebaiknya **registered explicitly**, bukan berharap ditemukan lewat scanning.

---

## 3. ResourceConfig vs Application

JAX-RS standard punya `jakarta.ws.rs.core.Application`; Jersey menyediakan `ResourceConfig` sebagai subclass/utility konfigurasi yang lebih ergonomic.

| Item | Role |
|---|---|
| `Application` | Standard JAX-RS application configuration. |
| `ResourceConfig` | Jersey-specific configuration helper untuk register class, singleton, properties, packages, features, binder. |
| `getClasses()` | Standard way untuk root resource, provider, feature classes. |
| `getSingletons()` | Mengembalikan instance singleton, tetapi di Jakarta REST 4.0 API ditandai deprecated; dokumentasi API menyebut automatic discovery atau `getClasses()` lebih disukai. ([Jakarta EE](https://jakarta.ee/specifications/restful-ws/4.0/apidocs/jakarta.ws.rs/jakarta/ws/rs/core/application?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) |
| `register(...)` | Jersey API untuk mendaftarkan resource/provider/feature/binder. |
| `packages(...)` | Jersey package scanning. |

### Minimal explicit config

```java id="9fhojd"
public final class AppResourceConfig extends ResourceConfig {

    public AppResourceConfig() {
        register(OrderResource.class);
        register(CustomerResource.class);

        register(CorrelationIdFilter.class);
        register(AuthenticationFilter.class);
        register(AuthorizationFilter.class);
        register(SecurityHeadersFilter.class);

        register(DomainExceptionMapper.class);
        register(UnhandledExceptionMapper.class);

        register(new AppBinder());
    }
}
```

---

## 4. Packages vs Explicit Register

### Package scanning

```java id="sfl8kp"
public final class AppResourceConfig extends ResourceConfig {

    public AppResourceConfig() {
        packages("com.company.order.api");
    }
}
```

### Explicit registration

```java id="17wukf"
public final class AppResourceConfig extends ResourceConfig {

    public AppResourceConfig() {
        register(OrderResource.class);
        register(CustomerResource.class);
        register(AuthenticationFilter.class);
        register(DomainExceptionMapper.class);
    }
}
```

### Decision table

| Concern | Package Scanning | Explicit Register |
|---|---:|---:|
| Banyak resource CRUD biasa | Bisa | Bisa |
| Authentication filter | Tidak disarankan | Ya |
| Authorization filter | Tidak disarankan | Ya |
| Exception mapper | Tidak disarankan | Ya |
| Security headers | Tidak disarankan | Ya |
| JSON provider | Tidak disarankan | Ya |
| DynamicFeature | Tidak disarankan | Ya |
| HK2 binder | Tidak | Ya |
| Testability | Sedang | Tinggi |
| Auditability | Rendah/sedang | Tinggi |
| Risiko missing provider | Lebih tinggi | Lebih rendah |

**Rule:** scanning itu discovery mechanism; explicit registration itu deployment contract.

---

## 5. Recommended Production Layout

```text id="grjfa7"
bootstrap
├─ AppResourceConfig.java
├─ AppBinder.java
├─ RuntimeBootstrap.java
├─ ProviderRegistry.java
└─ ResourceRegistry.java

api
├─ order
│  └─ OrderResource.java
├─ customer
│  └─ CustomerResource.java
└─ health
   └─ HealthResource.java

provider
├─ filter
│  ├─ CorrelationIdFilter.java
│  ├─ AuthenticationFilter.java
│  ├─ AuthorizationFilter.java
│  └─ SecurityHeadersFilter.java
├─ mapper
│  ├─ DomainExceptionMapper.java
│  ├─ ValidationExceptionMapper.java
│  └─ UnhandledExceptionMapper.java
├─ interceptor
│  ├─ RequestBodyLimitInterceptor.java
│  └─ ResponseEnvelopeWriterInterceptor.java
└─ feature
   ├─ FeatureGateDynamicFeature.java
   └─ SecuredEndpointDynamicFeature.java
```

### Registry split

```java id="jtk87a"
public final class ProviderRegistry {

    private ProviderRegistry() {}

    public static void registerAll(ResourceConfig config) {
        registerFilters(config);
        registerInterceptors(config);
        registerMappers(config);
        registerFeatures(config);
        registerSerialization(config);
    }

    private static void registerFilters(ResourceConfig config) {
        config.register(CorrelationIdFilter.class);
        config.register(AuthenticationFilter.class);
        config.register(AuthorizationFilter.class);
        config.register(SecurityHeadersFilter.class);
        config.register(AccessLogFilter.class);
    }

    private static void registerInterceptors(ResourceConfig config) {
        config.register(RequestBodyLimitInterceptor.class);
        config.register(ResponseEnvelopeWriterInterceptor.class);
    }

    private static void registerMappers(ResourceConfig config) {
        config.register(ValidationExceptionMapper.class);
        config.register(DomainExceptionMapper.class);
        config.register(UnhandledExceptionMapper.class);
    }

    private static void registerFeatures(ResourceConfig config) {
        config.register(FeatureGateDynamicFeature.class);
        config.register(SecuredEndpointDynamicFeature.class);
    }

    private static void registerSerialization(ResourceConfig config) {
        config.register(JsonProvider.class);
    }
}
```

```java id="5u987t"
public final class ResourceRegistry {

    private ResourceRegistry() {}

    public static void registerAll(ResourceConfig config) {
        config.register(OrderResource.class);
        config.register(CustomerResource.class);
        config.register(HealthResource.class);
        config.register(InternalDiagnosticsResource.class);
    }
}
```

```java id="g7vrbx"
public final class AppResourceConfig extends ResourceConfig {

    public AppResourceConfig() {
        RuntimeResources resources = RuntimeBootstrap.loadAndValidate();

        register(new AppBinder(resources));

        ResourceRegistry.registerAll(this);
        ProviderRegistry.registerAll(this);

        property("app.name", resources.appConfig().appName());
        property("app.profile", resources.appConfig().profile().name());
    }
}
```

---

## 6. Provider Types

| Provider Type | Interface / Annotation | Purpose |
|---|---|---|
| Resource | `@Path` | HTTP endpoint. |
| Request filter | `ContainerRequestFilter` | Pre-resource request processing. |
| Response filter | `ContainerResponseFilter` | Response decoration/logging/cleanup. |
| Reader interceptor | `ReaderInterceptor` | Around entity body read. |
| Writer interceptor | `WriterInterceptor` | Around entity body write. |
| Exception mapper | `ExceptionMapper<T>` | Map Java exception to HTTP response. |
| Message body reader | `MessageBodyReader<T>` | Deserialize HTTP body. |
| Message body writer | `MessageBodyWriter<T>` | Serialize HTTP body. |
| Context resolver | `ContextResolver<T>` | Provide contextual serialization/config object. |
| Feature | `Feature` | Register a set of providers/config programmatically. |
| DynamicFeature | `DynamicFeature` | Register filters/interceptors per resource method. |

Jakarta REST 4.0 menjelaskan bahwa filters/interceptors adalah providers dan bisa didaftarkan pada configurable runtime objects; provider priority dan name binding menentukan bagaimana mereka masuk ke chain. ([Jakarta EE](https://jakarta.ee/specifications/restful-ws/4.0/jakarta-restful-ws-spec-4.0.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 7. Provider Lifecycle

Pahami dua bentuk registration:

```java id="0tdd1h"
register(AuthenticationFilter.class);
```

vs

```java id="sj9v8z"
register(new AuthenticationFilter(tokenVerifier));
```

| Bentuk | Owner | DI Aktif? | Kapan Dipakai |
|---|---|---:|---|
| Register class | Jersey/HK2/CDI runtime | Ya, jika dependency tersedia | Default production choice. |
| Register instance | Anda membuat object | Terbatas / lifecycle manual | Untuk provider sederhana tanpa DI atau test. |
| Register binder | Jersey/HK2 | Ya untuk HK2 bindings | Dependency composition. |
| CDI producer | CDI/Weld | Ya untuk CDI beans | Standard Jakarta object graph. |

### Rule

```text id="l50kml"
Register class jika provider butuh DI/lifecycle.
Register instance hanya jika object benar-benar self-contained.
```

Anti-pattern:

```java id="3mavry"
// BAD jika AuthenticationFilter butuh DI/lifecycle/interceptor
register(new AuthenticationFilter(new TokenVerifier(...)));
```

Better:

```java id="opvsqe"
register(AuthenticationFilter.class);
register(new AppBinder(resources));
```

---

## 8. Priority & Ordering

JAX-RS/Jakarta REST provider priority menggunakan `@Priority`. Untuk request-side chain, priority lebih kecil berjalan lebih dulu. Untuk response-side chain, urutannya dibalik sehingga response filter dengan priority lebih besar dapat berjalan lebih dulu pada response path. Jakarta REST 4.0 specification menjelaskan provider priority untuk filter/interceptor chains, termasuk perbedaan ascending pada request/read/write chain dan descending pada response chain. ([Jakarta EE](https://jakarta.ee/specifications/restful-ws/4.0/jakarta-restful-ws-spec-4.0.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

### Example

```java id="z4xtjo"
@Provider
@Priority(Priorities.AUTHENTICATION)
public final class AuthenticationFilter implements ContainerRequestFilter {
    @Override
    public void filter(ContainerRequestContext request) {
        // authenticate
    }
}
```

```java id="z21oxf"
@Provider
@Priority(Priorities.AUTHORIZATION)
public final class AuthorizationFilter implements ContainerRequestFilter {
    @Override
    public void filter(ContainerRequestContext request) {
        // authorize
    }
}
```

### Suggested order

| Stage | Provider | Priority |
|---|---|---:|
| Request correlation setup | `CorrelationIdFilter` | Custom early, before auth |
| Authentication | `AuthenticationFilter` | `Priorities.AUTHENTICATION` |
| Authorization | `AuthorizationFilter` | `Priorities.AUTHORIZATION` |
| Request access log start | `AccessLogFilter` | After correlation |
| Body limit / body transform | `ReaderInterceptor` | `Priorities.ENTITY_CODER` or custom |
| Exception mapping | `ExceptionMapper` | Type specificity matters |
| Security headers | `SecurityHeadersFilter` | Response path |
| MDC cleanup | Response filter `finally` | Must always run |

### Production rule

```text id="6fl3oa"
Untuk provider critical, selalu beri @Priority eksplisit.
Jangan mengandalkan default priority atau urutan scanning.
```

---

## 9. Name Binding

Name binding membuat filter/interceptor hanya aktif untuk resource/method yang punya annotation tertentu.

```java id="0x7b0x"
@NameBinding
@Retention(RetentionPolicy.RUNTIME)
@Target({ElementType.TYPE, ElementType.METHOD})
public @interface RequireAdmin {}
```

```java id="35bgjj"
@Provider
@RequireAdmin
@Priority(Priorities.AUTHORIZATION)
public final class AdminOnlyFilter implements ContainerRequestFilter {

    @Override
    public void filter(ContainerRequestContext request) {
        PrincipalContext principal =
            (PrincipalContext) request.getProperty("principal");

        if (principal == null || !principal.hasRole("ADMIN")) {
            throw new ForbiddenException("Admin access required");
        }
    }
}
```

```java id="aqxhls"
@Path("/admin/orders")
@RequireAdmin
public final class AdminOrderResource {

    @GET
    public Response list() {
        return Response.ok().build();
    }
}
```

### Use name binding when

| Use Case | Cocok? |
|---|---:|
| Endpoint admin-only | Ya |
| Endpoint requires special audit | Ya |
| Endpoint has body logging disabled | Ya |
| Global auth filter | Tidak |
| Global correlation ID | Tidak |
| Global exception mapping | Tidak |

---

## 10. DynamicFeature

`DynamicFeature` cocok untuk conditional registration berbasis resource method/class metadata. Jakarta REST API mendefinisikan `DynamicFeature` sebagai provider yang bisa mendaftarkan filters/entity interceptors yang bound ke resource method, biasanya di-resolve saat deployment/resource method discovery. ([Jakarta EE](https://jakarta.ee/specifications/restful-ws/4.0/jakarta-restful-ws-spec-4.0?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

### Annotation with parameter

```java id="24z2jd"
@Retention(RetentionPolicy.RUNTIME)
@Target({ElementType.TYPE, ElementType.METHOD})
public @interface RequirePermission {
    String value();
}
```

### DynamicFeature

```java id="3zrpn8"
@Provider
public final class PermissionDynamicFeature implements DynamicFeature {

    @Override
    public void configure(ResourceInfo resourceInfo, FeatureContext context) {
        RequirePermission annotation = resolve(resourceInfo);

        if (annotation != null) {
            context.register(new PermissionFilter(annotation.value()));
        }
    }

    private RequirePermission resolve(ResourceInfo resourceInfo) {
        RequirePermission method =
            resourceInfo.getResourceMethod().getAnnotation(RequirePermission.class);

        if (method != null) {
            return method;
        }

        return resourceInfo.getResourceClass().getAnnotation(RequirePermission.class);
    }
}
```

### Filter instance

```java id="aoo1hh"
public final class PermissionFilter implements ContainerRequestFilter {

    private final String permission;

    public PermissionFilter(String permission) {
        this.permission = permission;
    }

    @Override
    public void filter(ContainerRequestContext request) {
        PrincipalContext principal =
            (PrincipalContext) request.getProperty("principal");

        if (principal == null || !principal.hasPermission(permission)) {
            throw new ForbiddenException("Access denied");
        }
    }
}
```

### Resource

```java id="4h7tw6"
@Path("/orders")
public final class OrderResource {

    @GET
    @Path("/{id}")
    @RequirePermission("order:read")
    public Response getOrder(@PathParam("id") String id) {
        return Response.ok().build();
    }
}
```

### NameBinding vs DynamicFeature

| Requirement | NameBinding | DynamicFeature |
|---|---:|---:|
| Simple marker annotation | Good | Bisa |
| Annotation punya parameter | Lemah | Good |
| Filter instance berbeda per method | Sulit | Good |
| Register based on method metadata | Terbatas | Good |
| Global behavior | Tidak | Tidak perlu |
| Runtime config-based conditional provider | Kadang | Good |

---

## 11. Feature

`Feature` cocok untuk mendaftarkan satu paket provider/config secara modular.

```java id="gfprbs"
public final class SecurityFeature implements Feature {

    @Override
    public boolean configure(FeatureContext context) {
        context.register(AuthenticationFilter.class);
        context.register(AuthorizationFilter.class);
        context.register(SecurityHeadersFilter.class);
        context.register(PermissionDynamicFeature.class);
        return true;
    }
}
```

```java id="m0j8z7"
public final class AppResourceConfig extends ResourceConfig {

    public AppResourceConfig() {
        register(new AppBinder(resources));
        register(SecurityFeature.class);
        register(JsonFeature.class);
        register(OrderResource.class);
    }
}
```

### Use Feature when

| Use Case | Reason |
|---|---|
| Security provider bundle | Auth/authz/header/dynamic feature registered together. |
| Observability bundle | Correlation/logging/metrics/tracing together. |
| Serialization bundle | JSON provider/context resolver configured together. |
| Multi-module app | Each module exposes its own feature. |

---

## 12. ExceptionMapper Registration

`ExceptionMapper<T>` memetakan exception Java ke HTTP response; API Jakarta REST 4.0 mendefinisikan `ExceptionMapper#toResponse`, dan provider mapper harus registered programmatically atau ditemukan via `@Provider`. Jika `toResponse` mengembalikan `null`, hasilnya `204 No Content`; jika mapper melempar runtime exception, hasilnya `500 Internal Server Error`. ([javadoc.io](https://javadoc.io/doc/jakarta.ws.rs/jakarta.ws.rs-api/latest/index.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

### Mapper hierarchy

```text id="v1m35z"
ValidationExceptionMapper
AuthenticationExceptionMapper
AuthorizationExceptionMapper
DomainExceptionMapper
ExternalDependencyExceptionMapper
UnhandledExceptionMapper
```

### Example

```java id="v5l13v"
@Provider
public final class DomainExceptionMapper implements ExceptionMapper<DomainException> {

    @Override
    public Response toResponse(DomainException exception) {
        return Response.status(statusOf(exception))
            .type(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
            .entity(ErrorResponse.safe(
                exception.code(),
                exception.safeMessage(),
                Correlation.currentId()
            ))
            .build();
    }

    private int statusOf(DomainException exception) {
        return switch (exception.category()) {
            case NOT_FOUND -> 404;
            case VALIDATION -> 400;
            case CONFLICT -> 409;
            case FORBIDDEN -> 403;
            default -> 500;
        };
    }
}
```

### Catch-all mapper

```java id="ugqq72"
@Provider
public final class UnhandledExceptionMapper implements ExceptionMapper<Throwable> {

    @Override
    public Response toResponse(Throwable exception) {
        LOGGER.error("Unhandled exception correlationId={}",
            Correlation.currentId(), exception);

        return Response.status(Response.Status.INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR)
            .type(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
            .entity(ErrorResponse.safe(
                "INTERNAL_ERROR",
                "Unexpected error",
                Correlation.currentId()
            ))
            .build();
    }
}
```

### Mapper rules

| Rule | Reason |
|---|---|
| Specific mapper before generic behavior | Avoid catch-all swallowing domain semantics. |
| Mapper must be defensive | Mapper failure becomes 500. |
| Do not expose raw exception message | Prevent info leak. |
| Include correlation ID | Debuggable without leaking internals. |
| Test each mapper via API-level test | Registration matters. |

---

## 13. MessageBody Provider / JSON Provider

Jersey bisa menggunakan JSON-B/Jackson provider tergantung dependency/config. Untuk production, jangan biarkan serialization behavior “terjadi kebetulan”.

```java id="8q2g6v"
@Provider
@Produces(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
@Consumes(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
public final class JsonProvider implements ContextResolver<ObjectMapper> {

    private final ObjectMapper mapper;

    public JsonProvider() {
        this.mapper = new ObjectMapper()
            .registerModule(new JavaTimeModule())
            .disable(SerializationFeature.WRITE_DATES_AS_TIMESTAMPS)
            .disable(DeserializationFeature.FAIL_ON_UNKNOWN_PROPERTIES);
    }

    @Override
    public ObjectMapper getContext(Class<?> type) {
        return mapper;
    }
}
```

### JSON registration rules

| Rule | Reason |
|---|---|
| Register JSON provider explicitly | Consistent serialization. |
| Support Java Time intentionally | Avoid timestamp/string mismatch. |
| Decide unknown property behavior | API compatibility policy. |
| Configure enum handling | Prevent accidental breaking change. |
| Test serialization edge cases | Date/time, null, unknown fields, validation. |

---

## 14. Resource Registration Strategy

### Explicit resources

```java id="06nopj"
public final class ResourceRegistry {

    public static void registerAll(ResourceConfig config) {
        config.register(OrderResource.class);
        config.register(CustomerResource.class);
        config.register(CaseResource.class);
        config.register(HealthResource.class);
    }
}
```

### Module-based registration

```java id="t9sgk6"
public interface ApiModule {
    void register(ResourceConfig config);
}
```

```java id="yyqx72"
public final class OrderApiModule implements ApiModule {

    @Override
    public void register(ResourceConfig config) {
        config.register(OrderResource.class);
        config.register(OrderExceptionMapper.class);
        config.register(OrderFeature.class);
    }
}
```

```java id="8y10sa"
public final class AppResourceConfig extends ResourceConfig {

    public AppResourceConfig() {
        List<ApiModule> modules = List.of(
            new OrderApiModule(),
            new CustomerApiModule(),
            new HealthApiModule()
        );

        modules.forEach(module -> module.register(this));
    }
}
```

### Module registration checklist

| Checklist | Why |
|---|---|
| Resource registered | Endpoint exists. |
| Mapper registered | Domain errors consistent. |
| Feature/filter registered | Security/feature gate active. |
| DTO validation provider registered | Input validation works. |
| Tests assert registration | Prevent accidental omission. |

---

## 15. Programmatic Resource Model

Use case: dynamic endpoints, generated endpoints, or highly controlled endpoint registration.

```java id="48rxei"
Resource.Builder resourceBuilder = Resource.builder()
    .path("/internal/ping");

resourceBuilder.addMethod("GET")
    .produces(MediaType.TEXT_PLAIN_TYPE)
    .handledBy((containerRequestContext) -> "pong");

Resource pingResource = resourceBuilder.build();

registerResources(pingResource);
```

Jersey `ResourceConfig` API supports registering programmatic resource models in addition to component classes. ([Eclipse EE4J](https://eclipse-ee4j.github.io/jersey.github.io/apidocs/3.1.10/jersey/org/glassfish/jersey/server/ResourceConfig.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

### Use carefully

| Good Use | Bad Use |
|---|---|
| Generated internal endpoints | Business CRUD endpoint biasa |
| Diagnostics in controlled runtime | Replacing normal resource classes |
| Framework/module infrastructure | Hiding API behavior from code search |
| Test-only resource | Production logic without documentation |

---

## 16. Application Properties

`ResourceConfig.property(...)` bisa dipakai untuk Jersey/runtime-level properties atau custom application properties yang perlu dibaca provider.

```java id="ifpq0y"
property("app.name", config.appName());
property("app.profile", config.profile().name());
property("jersey.config.server.tracing.type", "OFF");
```

### Rule

| Use `ResourceConfig.property` for | Do not use it for |
|---|---|
| Jersey runtime tuning | DB password |
| Provider-level flags | Raw application config map |
| App name/profile summary | Secret/config source of truth |
| Feature toggles for provider behavior | Business service config access everywhere |

Better:

```java id="9r2uqa"
bind(config).to(AppConfig.class);
```

Not:

```java id="o2jahw"
// BAD: config scattered as string properties
property("db.password", password);
```

---

## 17. Registration Tests

### Test ResourceConfig contains critical classes

```java id="i6k892"
@Test
void resourceConfigMustRegisterCriticalProviders() {
    ResourceConfig config = new AppResourceConfig();

    Set<Class<?>> classes = config.getClasses();

    assertTrue(classes.contains(OrderResource.class));
    assertTrue(classes.contains(AuthenticationFilter.class));
    assertTrue(classes.contains(AuthorizationFilter.class));
    assertTrue(classes.contains(CorrelationIdFilter.class));
    assertTrue(classes.contains(SecurityHeadersFilter.class));
    assertTrue(classes.contains(DomainExceptionMapper.class));
    assertTrue(classes.contains(UnhandledExceptionMapper.class));
}
```

### If using module registry

```java id="mcruzv"
@Test
void everyApiModuleMustRegisterAtLeastOneResource() {
    List<ApiModule> modules = ApiModules.productionModules();

    for (ApiModule module : modules) {
        ResourceConfig config = new ResourceConfig();
        module.register(config);

        assertFalse(
            config.getClasses().isEmpty(),
            "Module registered no classes: " + module.getClass().getName()
        );
    }
}
```

### Provider activation smoke test

```java id="sv3e8u"
@Test
void anonymousRequestMustBeRejectedByAuthenticationFilter() {
    given()
    .when()
        .get("/orders/123")
    .then()
        .statusCode(401);
}
```

### Exception mapper smoke test

```java id="yb9bi7"
@Test
void domainExceptionMustMapToSafeErrorResponse() {
    given()
        .auth().oauth2(validToken())
        .header("X-Tenant-Id", "tenant-a")
    .when()
        .get("/orders/not-found")
    .then()
        .statusCode(404)
        .body("code", equalTo("ORDER_NOT_FOUND"))
        .body("message", not(containsString("SQLException")))
        .body("correlationId", notNullValue());
}
```

---

## 18. Provider Ordering Tests

Untuk provider critical, test behavior lebih penting daripada introspeksi urutan.

### Authentication before authorization

```java id="07eq67"
@Test
void missingTokenMustReturn401Not403() {
    given()
        .header("X-Tenant-Id", "tenant-a")
    .when()
        .get("/admin/orders")
    .then()
        .statusCode(401);
}
```

Jika authorization filter berjalan sebelum authentication filter, endpoint bisa return `403` karena principal null. Test ini menangkap ordering salah.

### Correlation ID available on error

```java id="wrl908"
@Test
void correlationIdMustBeReturnedEvenOnAuthFailure() {
    given()
        .header("X-Correlation-Id", "corr-test-1")
    .when()
        .get("/orders/123")
    .then()
        .statusCode(401)
        .header("X-Correlation-Id", "corr-test-1");
}
```

Ini membuktikan correlation filter berjalan sebelum auth failure dan response filter/header logic tetap aktif.

---

## 19. Production Registration Invariants

| Invariant | Why |
|---|---|
| Auth filter registered in every production config | No accidental public API. |
| Catch-all exception mapper registered | No stack trace leak. |
| Security headers response filter registered | Baseline hardening. |
| Correlation filter registered before auth | Error response traceable. |
| JSON provider registered | Stable serialization. |
| Health resource registered | Orchestrator can probe. |
| Diagnostics endpoint disabled/restricted in prod | Prevent info disclosure. |
| DynamicFeature registered if annotations used | Annotation behavior active. |
| No duplicate provider registration | Avoid confusing behavior. |
| No package scanning for security-critical components | Deterministic startup. |

---

## 20. Failure Modes

| Symptom | Root Cause | Fix |
|---|---|---|
| Endpoint returns 404 unexpectedly | Resource not registered / package not scanned | Explicit register resource. |
| Auth filter not running | Filter not registered / priority/name binding wrong | Explicit register + smoke test. |
| Protected endpoint public | Missing annotation or DynamicFeature not registered | Annotation coverage test + register feature. |
| Error returns HTML/stack trace | Exception mapper missing | Register mapper explicitly. |
| Correlation ID absent on error | Correlation filter too late / response filter missing | Register early + response filter. |
| JSON date format inconsistent | Multiple JSON providers / default provider | Register one configured provider. |
| Body interceptor not invoked | Not registered / wrong media type / wrong extension point | Register explicitly, test payload. |
| DynamicFeature not applied | Feature not registered or annotation resolution wrong | Registration test + endpoint behavior test. |
| Provider dependency null | Registered instance manually bypassing DI | Register class and bind dependencies. |
| Works local, fails prod | Package scanning/classpath difference | Stop relying on scanning for critical providers. |

---

## 21. Anti-Patterns

| Anti-Pattern | Why Bad | Better |
|---|---|---|
| `packages("com.company")` only | Too broad, hidden registration | Explicit critical providers + limited scanning |
| Rely on `@Provider` for auth filter | Discovery failure can be silent | `register(AuthenticationFilter.class)` |
| Register provider instance with manual deps | Bypasses lifecycle/DI | Register class + binder/producer |
| No `@Priority` on filters | Ordering implementation-dependent | Explicit priority |
| Catch-all mapper only | Domain semantics lost | Specific mappers + catch-all |
| DynamicFeature for global auth | Overkill/wrong abstraction | Global request filter |
| NameBinding for global correlation | Wrong scope | Global filter |
| Store app config in ResourceConfig properties only | Stringly typed config | Typed `AppConfig` injection |
| Production diagnostics open | Info disclosure | Disable/restrict |
| No registration tests | Regression invisible | Bootstrap smoke tests |

---

## 22. Production Checklist

| Area | Checklist |
|---|---|
| Resource registration | All API resources explicitly registered or module-registered. |
| Security providers | Auth/authz/correlation/security headers explicitly registered. |
| Error providers | Specific mappers + catch-all mapper registered. |
| Serialization | One intentional JSON provider/context resolver registered. |
| Dynamic behavior | DynamicFeature registered and tested. |
| Name binding | Annotation + filter both present and tested. |
| Priority | Critical filters/interceptors have explicit `@Priority`. |
| DI | Provider classes registered, not manually instantiated, unless self-contained. |
| Health | Liveness/readiness/startup resources registered. |
| Diagnostics | Internal diagnostics disabled/restricted in production-like profiles. |
| Tests | ResourceConfig registration test + API activation smoke tests exist. |
| Build | Dependency tree checked for Jersey/Jakarta/HK2 consistency. |
| Runtime | Startup log lists registered critical providers without secrets. |

---

## 23. Roadmap Belajar Singkat

| Urutan | Fokus | Target Praktik |
|---:|---|---|
| 1 | `ResourceConfig` basics | Bisa register resource, provider, binder, property secara explicit. |
| 2 | Package vs explicit | Bisa menentukan mana yang boleh scanning dan mana wajib explicit. |
| 3 | Provider lifecycle | Bisa memilih register class vs instance dengan benar. |
| 4 | Priority/order | Bisa memastikan auth, correlation, logging, response header order benar. |
| 5 | NameBinding | Bisa membuat endpoint-specific filter. |
| 6 | DynamicFeature | Bisa membuat annotation parameter-driven provider registration. |
| 7 | ExceptionMapper | Bisa membuat mapper hierarchy dan catch-all yang aman. |
| 8 | Registration tests | Bisa mencegah provider critical hilang saat refactor. |

7/13/2026, 12:40:36 AM

# Cheatsheet - Jersey/JAX-RS Error Handling Architecture  
## Exception Taxonomy, Mapper Hierarchy, Safe Error Response, Correlation ID, Validation Errors, Downstream Failures, Auditability

## 1. Scope

Cheatsheet ini membahas desain **error handling architecture** untuk aplikasi Jersey/JAX-RS production:

```text id="0x9r42"
Error Handling Architecture
│
├─ Exception Taxonomy
│  ├─ domain exception
│  ├─ validation exception
│  ├─ authentication / authorization exception
│  ├─ conflict / state transition exception
│  ├─ downstream dependency exception
│  └─ unexpected exception
│
├─ Exception Mapping
│  ├─ ExceptionMapper<T>
│  ├─ mapper hierarchy
│  ├─ safe response body
│  └─ correlation ID propagation
│
├─ Error Response Contract
│  ├─ stable error code
│  ├─ safe message
│  ├─ HTTP status
│  ├─ details / field errors
│  ├─ correlation ID
│  └─ timestamp
│
├─ Observability
│  ├─ structured logs
│  ├─ metrics
│  ├─ trace span error
│  └─ audit event
│
└─ Failure Policy
   ├─ retryable vs non-retryable
   ├─ client fault vs server fault
   ├─ transient vs permanent
   └─ sensitive vs safe-to-expose
```

JAX-RS/Jakarta REST menyediakan `ExceptionMapper<T>` sebagai provider untuk memetakan Java exception menjadi HTTP `Response`; provider mapper harus diregister secara programmatic atau diberi `@Provider` agar ditemukan runtime. Jika `toResponse` mengembalikan `null`, hasilnya `204 No Content`; jika mapper melempar runtime exception, hasilnya `500 Internal Server Error`. ([Jakarta EE](https://jakarta.ee/specifications/restful-ws/4.0/apidocs/jakarta.ws.rs/jakarta/ws/rs/ext/exceptionmapper?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 2. Mental Model

```text id="772rsw"
Exception is internal control/diagnostic signal.
HTTP error response is external contract.
Audit event is compliance evidence.
Log event is operational evidence.
Metric is reliability signal.
```

Jangan mengirim exception mentah ke client. Client hanya butuh **status**, **stable error code**, **safe message**, dan **correlation ID**. Engineer internal butuh stack trace di log, bukan di response.

---

## 3. Error Flow

```text id="bokycc"
Resource / Service / Repository
    ↓ throws
DomainException / ValidationException / InfrastructureException
    ↓
ExceptionMapper<T>
    ↓
ErrorResponse
    ↓
HTTP Response
    ↓
Structured log + metric + audit event
```

Flow yang sehat:

```text id="fseu0s"
throw meaningful exception internally
map to safe external response
log technical details internally
return correlation ID externally
audit business/security-relevant failures
```

---

## 4. Exception Taxonomy

| Category | Example Exception | HTTP | Client Message | Retry? | Audit? |
|---|---|---:|---|---:|---:|
| Validation | `InputValidationException` | 400 | Field/input invalid | No | Optional |
| Authentication | `AuthenticationRequiredException` | 401 | Authentication required | Maybe after login/token refresh | Security audit |
| Authorization | `AccessDeniedException` | 403 | Access denied | No | Yes |
| Not Found | `EntityNotFoundException` | 404 | Resource not found | No | Optional/security-dependent |
| Conflict | `VersionConflictException` | 409 | Resource conflict | Maybe after refresh | Optional |
| Invalid state | `InvalidStateTransitionException` | 409/422 | Action not allowed in current state | No | Yes for workflow |
| Rate limit | `RateLimitExceededException` | 429 | Too many requests | Yes later | Optional |
| Downstream bad gateway | `DownstreamProtocolException` | 502 | Upstream service error | Maybe |
| Downstream unavailable | `DownstreamUnavailableException` | 503 | Service temporarily unavailable | Yes | Operational |
| Timeout | `DownstreamTimeoutException` | 504 | Upstream timeout | Yes | Operational |
| Unexpected | `Throwable` / `RuntimeException` | 500 | Unexpected error | Unknown | Operational |

Rule: taxonomy harus mengikuti **semantics**, bukan class library. `SQLException` tidak langsung berarti 500; bisa jadi 409 untuk unique constraint, 503 untuk DB unavailable, atau 500 untuk bug.

---

## 5. Error Response Contract

Opsi modern yang aman adalah menggunakan bentuk mirip **Problem Details**. RFC 9457 mendefinisikan “problem detail” sebagai format machine-readable untuk error HTTP API dan menggantikan RFC 7807. ([RFC Editor](https://www.rfc-editor.org/rfc/rfc9457.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

```json id="020i44"
{
  "type": "https://errors.company.com/order/not-found",
  "title": "Order not found",
  "status": 404,
  "code": "ORDER_NOT_FOUND",
  "detail": "Order not found",
  "correlationId": "7d7b3aa1-5c92-4a8a-aed2-4d0bda2e519c",
  "timestamp": "2026-07-13T08:15:30Z"
}
```

Untuk validation error:

```json id="aefv2m"
{
  "type": "https://errors.company.com/validation/invalid-request",
  "title": "Invalid request",
  "status": 400,
  "code": "VALIDATION_FAILED",
  "detail": "Request validation failed",
  "correlationId": "7d7b3aa1-5c92-4a8a-aed2-4d0bda2e519c",
  "timestamp": "2026-07-13T08:15:30Z",
  "errors": [
    {
      "field": "customerId",
      "code": "REQUIRED",
      "message": "customerId is required"
    },
    {
      "field": "amount",
      "code": "MIN_VALUE",
      "message": "amount must be greater than 0"
    }
  ]
}
```

### Field semantics

| Field | Purpose | Stable? |
|---|---|---:|
| `type` | URI/category identifier for error type | Yes |
| `title` | Short human-readable summary | Mostly |
| `status` | HTTP status code | Yes |
| `code` | Application-specific stable code | Yes |
| `detail` | Safe human-readable detail | Can vary |
| `correlationId` | Debug link to logs/traces | Per request |
| `timestamp` | Error response timestamp | Per response |
| `errors[]` | Field-level validation details | Shape stable |

---

## 6. Java Error Response Model

```java id="gbn5pj"
public record ErrorResponse(
    String type,
    String title,
    int status,
    String code,
    String detail,
    String correlationId,
    Instant timestamp,
    List<FieldError> errors
) {
    public static ErrorResponse of(
        String type,
        String title,
        int status,
        String code,
        String detail,
        String correlationId
    ) {
        return new ErrorResponse(
            type,
            title,
            status,
            code,
            detail,
            correlationId,
            Instant.now(),
            List.of()
        );
    }

    public static ErrorResponse validation(
        String correlationId,
        List<FieldError> errors
    ) {
        return new ErrorResponse(
            "https://errors.company.com/validation/invalid-request",
            "Invalid request",
            400,
            "VALIDATION_FAILED",
            "Request validation failed",
            correlationId,
            Instant.now(),
            List.copyOf(errors)
        );
    }
}
```

```java id="7fjskv"
public record FieldError(
    String field,
    String code,
    String message
) {}
```

### Rule

```text id="9a5d5d"
Error code adalah contract.
Exception message bukan contract.
Stack trace tidak pernah menjadi contract.
```

---

## 7. Domain Exception Model

```java id="661c7i"
public abstract class DomainException extends RuntimeException {

    private final String code;
    private final ErrorCategory category;
    private final String safeMessage;

    protected DomainException(
        String code,
        ErrorCategory category,
        String safeMessage
    ) {
        super(safeMessage);
        this.code = code;
        this.category = category;
        this.safeMessage = safeMessage;
    }

    public String code() {
        return code;
    }

    public ErrorCategory category() {
        return category;
    }

    public String safeMessage() {
        return safeMessage;
    }
}
```

```java id="xovohj"
public enum ErrorCategory {
    VALIDATION,
    NOT_FOUND,
    CONFLICT,
    FORBIDDEN,
    INVALID_STATE,
    RATE_LIMIT,
    DOWNSTREAM_UNAVAILABLE,
    DOWNSTREAM_TIMEOUT,
    INTERNAL
}
```

```java id="g04iqm"
public final class OrderNotFoundException extends DomainException {

    public OrderNotFoundException(String orderId) {
        super(
            "ORDER_NOT_FOUND",
            ErrorCategory.NOT_FOUND,
            "Order not found"
        );
    }
}
```

```java id="xzfqij"
public final class InvalidOrderStateException extends DomainException {

    public InvalidOrderStateException(String action, String currentState) {
        super(
            "ORDER_INVALID_STATE",
            ErrorCategory.INVALID_STATE,
            "Order is not in a valid state for this action"
        );
    }
}
```

Notice: constructor boleh menerima detail teknis/domain seperti `orderId`, `action`, `currentState`, tapi safe message tidak harus mengekspos semuanya.

## 8. Mapper Hierarchy

```text id="bbiq72"
ExceptionMapper hierarchy
│
├─ ConstraintViolationExceptionMapper
├─ JsonMappingExceptionMapper
├─ AuthenticationExceptionMapper
├─ AuthorizationExceptionMapper
├─ DomainExceptionMapper
├─ WebApplicationExceptionMapper
├─ DownstreamExceptionMapper
└─ UnhandledExceptionMapper
```

### Registration

```java id="q651pi"
public final class ProviderRegistry {

    public static void registerMappers(ResourceConfig config) {
        config.register(ConstraintViolationExceptionMapper.class);
        config.register(JsonMappingExceptionMapper.class);
        config.register(AuthenticationExceptionMapper.class);
        config.register(AuthorizationExceptionMapper.class);
        config.register(DomainExceptionMapper.class);
        config.register(DownstreamExceptionMapper.class);
        config.register(WebApplicationExceptionMapper.class);
        config.register(UnhandledExceptionMapper.class);
    }
}
```

### Rule

| Rule | Reason |
|---|---|
| Specific mapper untuk domain/validation/security | Error contract lebih jelas |
| Catch-all mapper tetap perlu | Mencegah stack trace/raw HTML |
| Mapper jangan terlalu kompleks | Mapper failure menjadi 500 |
| Mapper jangan akses dependency rapuh | Error handling harus reliable |
| Mapper harus safe by default | Jangan expose raw exception message |

---

## 9. DomainExceptionMapper

```java id="surh3m"
@Provider
public final class DomainExceptionMapper implements ExceptionMapper<DomainException> {

    @Override
    public Response toResponse(DomainException exception) {
        int status = statusOf(exception.category());
        String correlationId = Correlation.currentId();

        ErrorResponse body = ErrorResponse.of(
            typeOf(exception.code()),
            titleOf(exception.category()),
            status,
            exception.code(),
            exception.safeMessage(),
            correlationId
        );

        logDomainException(exception, status, correlationId);

        return Response.status(status)
            .type(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
            .entity(body)
            .build();
    }

    private int statusOf(ErrorCategory category) {
        return switch (category) {
            case VALIDATION -> 400;
            case NOT_FOUND -> 404;
            case CONFLICT -> 409;
            case FORBIDDEN -> 403;
            case INVALID_STATE -> 409;
            case RATE_LIMIT -> 429;
            case DOWNSTREAM_UNAVAILABLE -> 503;
            case DOWNSTREAM_TIMEOUT -> 504;
            case INTERNAL -> 500;
        };
    }

    private String typeOf(String code) {
        return "https://errors.company.com/" + code.toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT);
    }

    private String titleOf(ErrorCategory category) {
        return switch (category) {
            case VALIDATION -> "Validation failed";
            case NOT_FOUND -> "Resource not found";
            case CONFLICT -> "Resource conflict";
            case FORBIDDEN -> "Access denied";
            case INVALID_STATE -> "Invalid resource state";
            case RATE_LIMIT -> "Rate limit exceeded";
            case DOWNSTREAM_UNAVAILABLE -> "Service unavailable";
            case DOWNSTREAM_TIMEOUT -> "Gateway timeout";
            case INTERNAL -> "Internal error";
        };
    }

    private void logDomainException(
        DomainException exception,
        int status,
        String correlationId
    ) {
        if (status >= 500) {
            LOGGER.error(
                "Domain exception code={} status={} correlationId={}",
                exception.code(),
                status,
                correlationId,
                exception
            );
        } else {
            LOGGER.info(
                "Domain exception code={} status={} correlationId={}",
                exception.code(),
                status,
                correlationId
            );
        }
    }
}
```

---

## 10. Validation Error Handling

Jakarta Validation 3.1 adalah bagian dari Jakarta EE 11, dengan perubahan minor seperti rename dari Jakarta Bean Validation menjadi Jakarta Validation dan klarifikasi support Java records. ([Jakarta EE](https://jakarta.ee/specifications/bean-validation/3.1/jakarta-validation-spec-3.1.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

### ConstraintViolationException mapper

```java id="y27sxf"
@Provider
public final class ConstraintViolationExceptionMapper
    implements ExceptionMapper<ConstraintViolationException> {

    @Override
    public Response toResponse(ConstraintViolationException exception) {
        String correlationId = Correlation.currentId();

        List<FieldError> fieldErrors = exception.getConstraintViolations()
            .stream()
            .map(this::toFieldError)
            .sorted(Comparator.comparing(FieldError::field))
            .toList();

        ErrorResponse body = ErrorResponse.validation(
            correlationId,
            fieldErrors
        );

        LOGGER.info(
            "Validation failed count={} correlationId={}",
            fieldErrors.size(),
            correlationId
        );

        return Response.status(Response.Status.BAD_REQUEST)
            .type(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
            .entity(body)
            .build();
    }

    private FieldError toFieldError(ConstraintViolation<?> violation) {
        return new FieldError(
            normalizePath(violation.getPropertyPath().toString()),
            codeOf(violation),
            safeMessage(violation)
        );
    }

    private String normalizePath(String path) {
        return path == null || path.isBlank() ? "$" : path;
    }

    private String codeOf(ConstraintViolation<?> violation) {
        String annotationName = violation.getConstraintDescriptor()
            .getAnnotation()
            .annotationType()
            .getSimpleName();

        return annotationName.toUpperCase(Locale.ROOT);
    }

    private String safeMessage(ConstraintViolation<?> violation) {
        return violation.getMessage();
    }
}
```

### Validation status policy

| Failure | HTTP | Example |
|---|---:|---|
| JSON malformed | 400 | Invalid JSON syntax |
| JSON type mismatch | 400 | `amount` expected number |
| Bean validation violation | 400 | `customerId` required |
| Business invariant violation | 409/422 | Cannot approve closed order |
| Optimistic lock conflict | 409 | Version mismatch |
| Unsupported media type | 415 | Sent XML to JSON endpoint |
| Not acceptable | 406 | Client asks unsupported response type |

---

## 11. JSON Parsing / Mapping Errors

JSON parsing error jangan bocor internal parser message mentah. Mapper class tergantung provider yang dipakai, misalnya Jackson/Jersey entity provider.

```java id="kr7s27"
@Provider
public final class JsonMappingExceptionMapper
    implements ExceptionMapper<JsonProcessingException> {

    @Override
    public Response toResponse(JsonProcessingException exception) {
        String correlationId = Correlation.currentId();

        ErrorResponse body = ErrorResponse.of(
            "https://errors.company.com/request/malformed-json",
            "Malformed JSON request",
            400,
            "MALFORMED_JSON",
            "Request body is not valid JSON",
            correlationId
        );

        LOGGER.info(
            "Malformed JSON correlationId={} message={}",
            correlationId,
            exception.getOriginalMessage()
        );

        return Response.status(Response.Status.BAD_REQUEST)
            .type(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
            .entity(body)
            .build();
    }
}
```

### Jangan

```json id="y9f4c6"
{
  "message": "Cannot deserialize value of type `com.company.internal.SecretConfig` from String..."
}
```

### Lebih baik

```json id="gqu7og"
{
  "code": "MALFORMED_JSON",
  "detail": "Request body is not valid JSON",
  "correlationId": "..."
}
```

---

## 12. Authentication & Authorization Errors

| Condition | HTTP | Code | Message |
|---|---:|---|---|
| Missing credential | 401 | `AUTH_REQUIRED` | Authentication is required |
| Invalid token | 401 | `AUTH_INVALID` | Invalid authentication token |
| Expired token | 401 | `AUTH_EXPIRED` or generic `AUTH_INVALID` | Invalid authentication token |
| Valid identity, missing permission | 403 | `ACCESS_DENIED` | Access denied |
| Valid identity, wrong tenant | 403/404 | `TENANT_ACCESS_DENIED` | Access denied |
| Sensitive object existence | 404 | `RESOURCE_NOT_FOUND` | Resource not found |

### Mapper example

```java id="gdic2a"
@Provider
public final class AuthenticationExceptionMapper
    implements ExceptionMapper<AuthenticationException> {

    @Override
    public Response toResponse(AuthenticationException exception) {
        String correlationId = Correlation.currentId();

        ErrorResponse body = ErrorResponse.of(
            "https://errors.company.com/security/authentication",
            "Authentication required",
            401,
            exception.code(),
            "Authentication is required",
            correlationId
        );

        return Response.status(Response.Status.UNAUTHORIZED)
            .header(HttpHeaders.WWW_AUTHENTICATE, "Bearer")
            .type(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
            .entity(body)
            .build();
    }
}
```

Security error harus **minim informasi**. Jangan memberi tahu client detail seperti tenant valid yang dimiliki user, permission internal yang hilang, key ID token, atau parser error JWT mentah.

---

## 13. WebApplicationException Handling

JAX-RS `WebApplicationException` sering muncul dari resource/filter atau runtime JAX-RS. Jangan biarkan ia menghasilkan response HTML/default yang tidak konsisten.

```java id="d6dfgc"
@Provider
public final class WebApplicationExceptionMapper
    implements ExceptionMapper<WebApplicationException> {

    @Override
    public Response toResponse(WebApplicationException exception) {
        int status = exception.getResponse().getStatus();
        String correlationId = Correlation.currentId();

        ErrorResponse body = ErrorResponse.of(
            "https://errors.company.com/http/" + status,
            titleFor(status),
            status,
            codeFor(status),
            safeDetailFor(status),
            correlationId
        );

        log(status, exception, correlationId);

        return Response.status(status)
            .type(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
            .entity(body)
            .build();
    }

    private String titleFor(int status) {
        return switch (status) {
            case 400 -> "Bad request";
            case 401 -> "Authentication required";
            case 403 -> "Access denied";
            case 404 -> "Resource not found";
            case 405 -> "Method not allowed";
            case 406 -> "Not acceptable";
            case 415 -> "Unsupported media type";
            case 429 -> "Too many requests";
            default -> status >= 500 ? "Server error" : "Request error";
        };
    }

    private String codeFor(int status) {
        return "HTTP_" + status;
    }

    private String safeDetailFor(int status) {
        return switch (status) {
            case 400 -> "The request is invalid";
            case 401 -> "Authentication is required";
            case 403 -> "Access denied";
            case 404 -> "Resource not found";
            case 405 -> "HTTP method is not allowed";
            case 406 -> "Requested response type is not acceptable";
            case 415 -> "Request media type is not supported";
            case 429 -> "Too many requests";
            default -> status >= 500 ? "Unexpected server error" : "Request failed";
        };
    }

    private void log(int status, WebApplicationException exception, String correlationId) {
        if (status >= 500) {
            LOGGER.error("JAX-RS error status={} correlationId={}", status, correlationId, exception);
        } else {
            LOGGER.info("JAX-RS error status={} correlationId={}", status, correlationId);
        }
    }
}
```

---

## 14. Downstream Failure Mapping

Untuk microservice, error downstream harus dibedakan jelas.

```text id="tq6bna"
Current service receives request
    ↓
Calls downstream service
    ↓
Downstream returns error / timeout / invalid response
    ↓
Map to gateway-like error
```

| Downstream Failure | Internal Exception | HTTP to Client | Retry? |
|---|---|---:|---:|
| Connection refused | `DownstreamUnavailableException` | 503 | Yes |
| Timeout | `DownstreamTimeoutException` | 504 | Yes |
| Downstream 400 | `DownstreamRejectedRequestException` | 502 or mapped domain error | Usually no |
| Downstream 401/403 | `DownstreamAuthException` | 502/503 | Maybe operational |
| Downstream 404 | Depends | 404 or 502 | Depends |
| Downstream 409 | Depends | 409 | Maybe |
| Malformed downstream response | `DownstreamProtocolException` | 502 | Maybe |
| Circuit open | `DownstreamUnavailableException` | 503 | Yes |

### Downstream exception

```java id="d7i52m"
public final class DownstreamException extends RuntimeException {

    private final String dependencyName;
    private final DownstreamFailureType failureType;
    private final boolean retryable;

    public DownstreamException(
        String dependencyName,
        DownstreamFailureType failureType,
        boolean retryable,
        Throwable cause
    ) {
        super("Downstream failure: " + dependencyName + " " + failureType, cause);
        this.dependencyName = dependencyName;
        this.failureType = failureType;
        this.retryable = retryable;
    }

    public String dependencyName() {
        return dependencyName;
    }

    public DownstreamFailureType failureType() {
        return failureType;
    }

    public boolean retryable() {
        return retryable;
    }
}
```

```java id="b2tuc0"
public enum DownstreamFailureType {
    UNAVAILABLE,
    TIMEOUT,
    BAD_RESPONSE,
    AUTH_FAILURE,
    RATE_LIMITED
}
```

### Downstream mapper

```java id="82nn08"
@Provider
public final class DownstreamExceptionMapper
    implements ExceptionMapper<DownstreamException> {

    @Override
    public Response toResponse(DownstreamException exception) {
        int status = statusOf(exception.failureType());
        String correlationId = Correlation.currentId();

        ErrorResponse body = ErrorResponse.of(
            "https://errors.company.com/dependency/" + exception.failureType().name().toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT),
            titleOf(exception.failureType()),
            status,
            codeOf(exception.failureType()),
            "A required dependency is temporarily unavailable",
            correlationId
        );

        LOGGER.warn(
            "Downstream failure dependency={} type={} retryable={} status={} correlationId={}",
            exception.dependencyName(),
            exception.failureType(),
            exception.retryable(),
            status,
            correlationId,
            exception
        );

        return Response.status(status)
            .type(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
            .entity(body)
            .build();
    }

    private int statusOf(DownstreamFailureType type) {
        return switch (type) {
            case UNAVAILABLE -> 503;
            case TIMEOUT -> 504;
            case BAD_RESPONSE -> 502;
            case AUTH_FAILURE -> 502;
            case RATE_LIMITED -> 503;
        };
    }

    private String codeOf(DownstreamFailureType type) {
        return "DOWNSTREAM_" + type.name();
    }

    private String titleOf(DownstreamFailureType type) {
        return switch (type) {
            case UNAVAILABLE -> "Dependency unavailable";
            case TIMEOUT -> "Dependency timeout";
            case BAD_RESPONSE -> "Dependency protocol error";
            case AUTH_FAILURE -> "Dependency authentication failure";
            case RATE_LIMITED -> "Dependency rate limited";
        };
    }
}
```

---

## 15. Correlation ID in Errors

Error response wajib mengembalikan correlation ID agar user/support bisa melaporkan error tanpa melihat stack trace.

```java id="qhro13"
@Provider
@Priority(Priorities.HEADER_DECORATOR)
public final class CorrelationResponseFilter implements ContainerResponseFilter {

    @Override
    public void filter(ContainerRequestContext request,
                       ContainerResponseContext response) {
        Object correlationId = request.getProperty("correlationId");

        if (correlationId != null) {
            response.getHeaders().putSingle("X-Correlation-Id", correlationId.toString());
        }
    }
}
```

### Error body

```java id="7wck8c"
String correlationId = Correlation.currentId();

ErrorResponse body = ErrorResponse.of(
    type,
    title,
    status,
    code,
    safeMessage,
    correlationId
);
```

Rule:

```text id="5371pe"
Every error response must include:
- X-Correlation-Id header
- correlationId body field
- structured log with same correlationId
```

---

## 16. Logging Policy

| Status | Log Level | Include Stack Trace? | Reason |
|---|---|---:|---|
| 400 validation | INFO/DEBUG | No | Client input issue |
| 401/403 | INFO/WARN | No usually | Security signal |
| 404 expected | INFO/DEBUG | No | Common client path |
| 409 conflict | INFO | No | Business conflict |
| 429 rate limit | INFO/WARN | No | Traffic signal |
| 502/503/504 | WARN/ERROR | Yes for cause | Operational issue |
| 500 unexpected | ERROR | Yes | Bug/system failure |

### Structured log example

```text id="fax55x"
level=ERROR
event=api_error
status=500
code=INTERNAL_ERROR
correlationId=7d7b3aa1
path=/orders/123/approve
method=POST
actorId=user-123
tenantId=tenant-a
exceptionClass=NullPointerException
```

Jangan log:

```text id="fcpx8k"
Authorization=Bearer ...
Cookie=session=...
db.password=...
jwt.privateKey=...
full request body with PII
```

---

## 17. Auditability

Tidak semua error perlu audit, tapi beberapa harus.

| Error Type | Audit? | Why |
|---|---:|---|
| Authorization denied | Yes | Security decision evidence |
| Tenant denied | Yes | Cross-tenant attempt evidence |
| Invalid state transition | Yes | Workflow/regulatory defensibility |
| Approval rejected by policy | Yes | Decision evidence |
| Validation failed | Usually no | Too noisy, unless regulatory form submission |
| Authentication failed | Maybe aggregate/security audit | Brute force/token abuse detection |
| Downstream unavailable | No audit, operational log/metric | Not business decision |
| Unexpected 500 | Operational incident | Not normal audit event |

### Audit event

```java id="xmli60"
public record ErrorAuditEvent(
    String eventId,
    Instant occurredAt,
    String correlationId,
    String actorId,
    String tenantId,
    String action,
    String targetType,
    String targetId,
    String errorCode,
    int status,
    String decision,
    String reasonCode
) {}
```

Untuk regulatory/case-management, `InvalidStateTransitionException` sering harus diaudit karena mencerminkan attempt melakukan aksi yang tidak valid terhadap lifecycle object.

---

## 18. Safe vs Unsafe Error Detail

| Unsafe | Safe Alternative |
|---|---|
| `SQL syntax error near users.password` | `Unexpected error` |
| `Tenant tenant-b is not assigned to user user-a` | `Access denied` |
| `JWT signature invalid for kid abc123` | `Invalid authentication token` |
| `Order 123 belongs to another customer` | `Resource not found` or `Access denied` |
| Stack trace | Correlation ID |
| Downstream hostname/internal IP | `A required dependency is unavailable` |
| Raw validation exception with class names | Field-level validation messages |

### Rule

```text id="v9ql40"
External response answers:
"What should client know?"

Internal log answers:
"What should engineer know?"

Audit answers:
"What decision happened and why?"
```

---

## 19. Retryability Contract

Error response bisa memberi hint retry bila aman.

| HTTP | Retry? | Header |
|---|---:|---|
| 400 | No | None |
| 401 | After token refresh | Optional |
| 403 | No | None |
| 404 | No usually | None |
| 409 | Maybe after refresh | None |
| 429 | Yes later | `Retry-After` |
| 500 | Unknown | Usually none |
| 502 | Maybe | Optional |
| 503 | Yes later | `Retry-After` optional |
| 504 | Yes maybe | Optional |

```java id="nl3tup"
return Response.status(429)
    .header("Retry-After", "60")
    .type(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
    .entity(body)
    .build();
```

Jangan memberi retry hint untuk error yang tidak akan berhasil tanpa perubahan request/permission/state.

---

## 20. Mapper Testing Matrix

| Test | Expected |
|---|---|
| Domain not found | 404 + `ORDER_NOT_FOUND` |
| Domain conflict | 409 + stable conflict code |
| Validation violation | 400 + `errors[]` |
| Malformed JSON | 400 + `MALFORMED_JSON` |
| Missing auth | 401 + `WWW-Authenticate` |
| Access denied | 403 + safe body |
| Tenant denied | 403/404 + no tenant detail |
| Downstream timeout | 504 + dependency-safe message |
| Unknown exception | 500 + no stack trace |
| Mapper includes correlation ID | Header and body |
| Mapper does not log token/secret | Log assertion/review |
| Mapper does not return null | No accidental 204 |
| Mapper itself does not throw | Defensive test |

### API-level test

```java id="p1swur"
@Test
void domainExceptionMustReturnSafeErrorResponse() {
    given()
        .auth().oauth2(validToken())
        .header("X-Tenant-Id", "tenant-a")
        .header("X-Correlation-Id", "corr-123")
    .when()
        .get("/orders/not-found")
    .then()
        .statusCode(404)
        .header("X-Correlation-Id", "corr-123")
        .body("code", equalTo("ORDER_NOT_FOUND"))
        .body("correlationId", equalTo("corr-123"))
        .body("detail", equalTo("Order not found"))
        .body("detail", not(containsString("SQLException")));
}
```

### Unknown exception test

```java id="aq81e6"
@Test
void unknownExceptionMustNotExposeStackTrace() {
    given()
        .auth().oauth2(validToken())
        .header("X-Tenant-Id", "tenant-a")
    .when()
        .get("/internal/test/throw-null-pointer")
    .then()
        .statusCode(500)
        .body("code", equalTo("INTERNAL_ERROR"))
        .body("detail", equalTo("Unexpected error"))
        .body("detail", not(containsString("NullPointerException")));
}
```

## 21. Production Registration

```java id="bibj5q"
public final class ErrorHandlingFeature implements Feature {

    @Override
    public boolean configure(FeatureContext context) {
        context.register(ConstraintViolationExceptionMapper.class);
        context.register(JsonMappingExceptionMapper.class);
        context.register(AuthenticationExceptionMapper.class);
        context.register(AuthorizationExceptionMapper.class);
        context.register(DomainExceptionMapper.class);
        context.register(DownstreamExceptionMapper.class);
        context.register(WebApplicationExceptionMapper.class);
        context.register(UnhandledExceptionMapper.class);
        return true;
    }
}
```

```java id="sej0xx"
public final class AppResourceConfig extends ResourceConfig {

    public AppResourceConfig() {
        register(ErrorHandlingFeature.class);

        register(CorrelationIdFilter.class);
        register(AuthenticationFilter.class);
        register(AuthorizationFilter.class);

        register(OrderResource.class);
        register(HealthResource.class);
    }
}
```

Production-critical mapper sebaiknya explicit registration. Jangan bergantung penuh pada scanning untuk catch-all mapper.

---

## 22. Failure Modes

| Symptom | Root Cause | Fix |
|---|---|---|
| Client menerima HTML error page | Mapper tidak registered | Register mapper eksplisit |
| 500 response berisi stack trace | Catch-all mapper missing / debug mode | Safe `UnhandledExceptionMapper` |
| 404/403 bocor object existence | Error policy tidak konsisten | Definisikan exposure policy |
| Validation error sulit dipakai client | Tidak ada `errors[]` field-level | Buat validation mapper |
| Mapper return 204 | `toResponse` return `null` | Selalu return `Response` |
| Mapper menyebabkan 500 | Mapper throw exception | Mapper harus defensive |
| Downstream timeout jadi 500 | Tidak ada downstream taxonomy | Map timeout ke 504 |
| Unique constraint jadi 500 | SQL error tidak diterjemahkan | Map ke conflict/domain exception |
| Error tidak bisa dicari di log | Correlation ID tidak ada | Header/body/log correlation wajib |
| Security error bocor detail | Raw exception message dikirim | Safe message |
| Audit tidak merekam denied action | Audit hanya success | Audit deny/security/state failures |
| Client retry terus untuk 403 | Retryability tidak jelas | Jangan beri retry hint untuk non-retryable |

---

## 23. Production Checklist

| Area | Checklist |
|---|---|
| Error contract | Stable `code`, `status`, `detail`, `correlationId` |
| Problem details | Format konsisten, bisa mirip RFC 9457 |
| Mapper hierarchy | Specific mapper + catch-all mapper |
| Validation | Field-level validation errors |
| Security | 401/403/404 policy safe |
| Downstream | Timeout/unavailable/protocol errors dibedakan |
| Correlation | Header + body + log selalu sama |
| Logging | Stack trace hanya internal, secret/token tidak pernah masuk log |
| Audit | Authz denied, tenant denied, invalid state transition diaudit |
| Retryability | 429/503/504 punya policy jelas |
| Registration | Mapper explicit registered |
| Tests | API-level mapper tests + no-leak tests |
| Ops | Metrics per `status`/`code`/dependency |
| Compliance | Error decision dapat ditelusuri via audit/correlation ID |

---

## 24. Roadmap Belajar Singkat

| Urutan | Fokus | Target Praktik |
|---:|---|---|
| 1 | Exception taxonomy | Bisa membedakan validation, domain, security, downstream, unexpected. |
| 2 | Error response contract | Bisa membuat stable error body dengan correlation ID. |
| 3 | Mapper hierarchy | Bisa membuat mapper specific + catch-all. |
| 4 | Validation mapper | Bisa mengembalikan field-level errors. |
| 5 | Security error policy | Bisa membedakan 401/403/404 tanpa bocor detail. |
| 6 | Downstream mapping | Bisa map timeout/unavailable/protocol error dengan benar. |
| 7 | Observability | Bisa log/metric/trace error tanpa secret leakage. |
| 8 | Auditability | Bisa mencatat denied/security/state failures secara defensible. |
