## Response:
7/13/2026, 12:43:56 AM

# Cheatsheet - Jersey/JAX-RS Validation Architecture  
## Jakarta Validation, Resource Parameter Validation, DTO Validation, Business Rule Validation, Error Mapping, Testing Strategy

## 1. Scope

Cheatsheet ini membahas **validation architecture** untuk aplikasi Jersey/JAX-RS production:

```text id="m5uvgb"
Validation Architecture
│
├─ HTTP Boundary Validation
│  ├─ path parameter
│  ├─ query parameter
│  ├─ header parameter
│  ├─ request body syntax
│  └─ media type
│
├─ DTO / Contract Validation
│  ├─ Jakarta Validation annotations
│  ├─ nested object validation
│  ├─ collection validation
│  ├─ record validation
│  └─ custom constraint
│
├─ Application / Business Validation
│  ├─ state transition rule
│  ├─ ownership / tenant rule
│  ├─ domain invariant
│  ├─ duplicate / uniqueness check
│  └─ cross-entity consistency
│
├─ Persistence Validation
│  ├─ DB constraint
│  ├─ unique index
│  ├─ foreign key
│  ├─ check constraint
│  └─ optimistic locking
│
└─ Error Mapping
   ├─ ConstraintViolationExceptionMapper
   ├─ JsonMappingExceptionMapper
   ├─ DomainExceptionMapper
   └─ stable validation error response
```

Jakarta Validation 3.1 adalah release untuk Jakarta EE 11; spesifikasi ini mendefinisikan metadata model dan API untuk **JavaBean validation** dan **method validation**, serta mengklarifikasi dukungan untuk Java Records. ([Jakarta Validation 3.1](https://jakarta.ee/specifications/bean-validation/3.1/)) Jersey juga menyediakan modul Bean Validation support untuk dependency, configuration, registration, dan usage dalam aplikasi Jersey/JAX-RS. ([Jersey Bean Validation Support](https://eclipse-ee4j.github.io/jersey.github.io/documentation/latest/bean-validation.html))

---

## 2. Mental Model

```text id="xn1q47"
Validation bukan satu layer.

HTTP validation:
  "Request ini berbentuk benar?"

DTO validation:
  "Payload ini memenuhi contract API?"

Business validation:
  "Aksi ini sah menurut aturan domain?"

Persistence validation:
  "Data ini tetap konsisten secara database?"

Authorization:
  "Actor ini boleh melakukan aksi ini?"
```

Rule penting:

```text id="vkyky8"
Validation menjawab: apakah data/action valid?
Authorization menjawab: apakah actor boleh?
Conflict menjawab: apakah state/data sudah berubah?
```

Jangan mencampur semuanya menjadi `BadRequestException`.

---

## 3. Validation Decomposition Map

```text id="g6226z"
Jersey/JAX-RS Validation
│
├─ 1. Request Syntax Validation
│  ├─ malformed JSON
│  ├─ unsupported media type
│  ├─ unacceptable response type
│  └─ invalid primitive conversion
│
├─ 2. Resource Parameter Validation
│  ├─ @PathParam
│  ├─ @QueryParam
│  ├─ @HeaderParam
│  ├─ @BeanParam
│  └─ method parameter constraints
│
├─ 3. Request DTO Validation
│  ├─ @Valid
│  ├─ @NotNull
│  ├─ @NotBlank
│  ├─ @Size
│  ├─ @Pattern
│  ├─ @Email
│  ├─ nested DTO
│  └─ collection item validation
│
├─ 4. Custom Constraint Validation
│  ├─ annotation
│  ├─ ConstraintValidator
│  ├─ class-level constraint
│  └─ cross-field constraint
│
├─ 5. Service-Level Business Validation
│  ├─ state machine
│  ├─ duplicate check
│  ├─ limit / threshold
│  ├─ cross-entity rule
│  └─ temporal rule
│
├─ 6. Persistence-Level Validation
│  ├─ NOT NULL
│  ├─ UNIQUE
│  ├─ CHECK
│  ├─ FK
│  └─ version conflict
│
└─ 7. Error Contract
   ├─ stable code
   ├─ field errors
   ├─ safe message
   ├─ correlation ID
   └─ audit relevance
```

---

## 4. Validation Layer Decision Table

| Rule Type | Example | Layer | HTTP Status |
|---|---|---|---:|
| JSON malformed | Broken JSON syntax | Message body provider / mapper | 400 |
| Missing required field | `customerId` null | DTO validation | 400 |
| Invalid format | email / UUID / date format | DTO/resource param validation | 400 |
| Query param out of range | `limit > 100` | Resource parameter validation | 400 |
| Unsupported content type | `text/plain` instead of JSON | JAX-RS runtime | 415 |
| Business state invalid | approve closed order | Service/domain validation | 409 or 422 |
| Duplicate natural key | same order number | Service or DB constraint mapping | 409 |
| Optimistic lock mismatch | stale version | Service/persistence mapping | 409 |
| Tenant mismatch | actor tenant vs object tenant | Authorization/security policy | 403/404 |
| Missing permission | no `order:approve` | Authorization | 403 |
| DB constraint violated unexpectedly | bug or race | Persistence exception mapping | 409 or 500 |

---

## 5. Jakarta Validation Basics

Jakarta Validation provides constraints for object fields, method parameters, method return values, and constructors. The Jakarta EE tutorial describes it as validation for objects, object members, methods, and constructors. ([Jakarta EE Tutorial - Bean Validation](https://jakarta.ee/learn/docs/jakartaee-tutorial/current/beanvalidation/bean-validation/bean-validation.html))

### DTO example

```java id="mfl2mz"
public record CreateOrderRequest(

    @NotBlank(message = "customerId is required")
    String customerId,

    @NotNull(message = "amount is required")
    @DecimalMin(value = "0.01", message = "amount must be greater than zero")
    BigDecimal amount,

    @NotBlank(message = "currency is required")
    @Pattern(regexp = "^[A-Z]{3}$", message = "currency must be ISO-4217 code")
    String currency,

    @Size(max = 500, message = "note must not exceed 500 characters")
    String note,

    @Valid
    List<OrderLineRequest> lines
) {}
```

```java id="rwp3ak"
public record OrderLineRequest(

    @NotBlank(message = "productId is required")
    String productId,

    @Min(value = 1, message = "quantity must be at least 1")
    int quantity,

    @NotNull(message = "unitPrice is required")
    @DecimalMin(value = "0.00", message = "unitPrice must not be negative")
    BigDecimal unitPrice
) {}
```

### Resource usage

```java id="nqdla5"
@Path("/orders")
@Consumes(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
@Produces(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
public final class OrderResource {

    private final OrderService orderService;

    @Inject
    public OrderResource(OrderService orderService) {
        this.orderService = orderService;
    }

    @POST
    public Response createOrder(@Valid CreateOrderRequest request,
                                @Context ContainerRequestContext context) {
        RequestContext requestContext = RequestContexts.from(context);

        OrderDto result = orderService.createOrder(request, requestContext);

        return Response.status(Response.Status.CREATED)
            .entity(result)
            .build();
    }
}
```

---

## 6. Resource Parameter Validation

Parameter dari path/query/header juga perlu validasi.

```java id="l4n3v2"
@Path("/orders")
@Produces(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
public final class OrderResource {

    @GET
    @Path("/{orderId}")
    public Response getOrder(
        @PathParam("orderId")
        @NotBlank(message = "orderId is required")
        @Pattern(regexp = "^[A-Z0-9-]{8,64}$", message = "orderId format is invalid")
        String orderId,

        @HeaderParam("X-Tenant-Id")
        @NotBlank(message = "X-Tenant-Id is required")
        String tenantId
    ) {
        return Response.ok().build();
    }

    @GET
    public Response searchOrders(
        @QueryParam("limit")
        @DefaultValue("50")
        @Min(value = 1, message = "limit must be at least 1")
        @Max(value = 100, message = "limit must not exceed 100")
        int limit,

        @QueryParam("offset")
        @DefaultValue("0")
        @Min(value = 0, message = "offset must not be negative")
        int offset
    ) {
        return Response.ok().build();
    }
}
```

Jersey Bean Validation support menjelaskan dukungan Bean Validation dalam Jersey, termasuk dependency, configuration, registration, dan usage; untuk resource classes, JAX-RS/Jersey dapat memanfaatkan Bean Validation untuk parameter dan entity validation bila support dependency/registration benar. ([Jersey Bean Validation Support](https://eclipse-ee4j.github.io/jersey.github.io/documentation/latest/bean-validation.html))

---

## 7. `@BeanParam` Validation Pattern

Untuk query parameter kompleks, gunakan `@BeanParam`.

```java id="j1p0iy"
public class SearchOrdersParams {

    @QueryParam("status")
    @Pattern(
        regexp = "DRAFT|SUBMITTED|PENDING_APPROVAL|APPROVED|CLOSED",
        message = "status is invalid"
    )
    public String status;

    @QueryParam("limit")
    @DefaultValue("50")
    @Min(1)
    @Max(100)
    public int limit;

    @QueryParam("offset")
    @DefaultValue("0")
    @Min(0)
    public int offset;

    @HeaderParam("X-Tenant-Id")
    @NotBlank(message = "X-Tenant-Id is required")
    public String tenantId;
}
```

```java id="qgp6oj"
@GET
public Response search(@Valid @BeanParam SearchOrdersParams params) {
    SearchOrdersQuery query = SearchOrdersQuery.from(params);
    return Response.ok(orderService.search(query)).build();
}
```

### Rule

| Use `@BeanParam` when | Avoid when |
|---|---|
| Banyak query/header param | Param sedikit |
| Reusable filter/search object | DTO jadi mutable public fields tanpa kontrol |
| Ingin validasi query object | Business command kompleks lebih cocok object sendiri |

---

## 8. Nested Validation

`@Valid` harus dipakai agar object nested ikut divalidasi.

```java id="d74lbr"
public record CreateCustomerRequest(

    @NotBlank
    String name,

    @Valid
    AddressRequest address,

    @Valid
    List<ContactRequest> contacts
) {}
```

```java id="4e1qkh"
public record AddressRequest(

    @NotBlank
    String street,

    @NotBlank
    String city,

    @NotBlank
    String countryCode
) {}
```

Tanpa `@Valid`, constraint pada nested object bisa tidak dievaluasi.

### Collection item validation

```java id="g7myax"
public record BulkApproveRequest(

    @NotEmpty(message = "orderIds must not be empty")
    @Size(max = 100, message = "cannot approve more than 100 orders at once")
    List<
        @NotBlank(message = "orderId must not be blank")
        @Pattern(regexp = "^[A-Z0-9-]{8,64}$", message = "orderId format is invalid")
        String
    > orderIds
) {}
```

---

## 9. Validation Groups

Validation groups berguna untuk membedakan rule create vs update vs approve.

```java id="f8etck"
public interface OnCreate {}
public interface OnUpdate {}
public interface OnApprove {}
```

```java id="jg62j1"
public record OrderCommand(

    @Null(groups = OnCreate.class, message = "id must be absent on create")
    @NotNull(groups = OnUpdate.class, message = "id is required on update")
    String id,

    @NotBlank(groups = {OnCreate.class, OnUpdate.class})
    String customerId,

    @NotNull(groups = OnApprove.class)
    String approvalReason
) {}
```

### Manual validation in service/application layer

```java id="b4vfn9"
@ApplicationScoped
public class CommandValidator {

    private final Validator validator;

    @Inject
    public CommandValidator(Validator validator) {
        this.validator = validator;
    }

    public <T> void validate(T value, Class<?>... groups) {
        Set<ConstraintViolation<T>> violations = validator.validate(value, groups);

        if (!violations.isEmpty()) {
            throw new ConstraintViolationException(violations);
        }
    }
}
```

```java id="otbdym"
public OrderDto create(OrderCommand command) {
    commandValidator.validate(command, OnCreate.class);
    return createInternal(command);
}
```

### When to use groups

| Good Use | Caution |
|---|---|
| Same DTO reused carefully for create/update | Jangan bikin DTO terlalu polymorphic |
| Different validation phase | Jangan sembunyikan business rule kompleks |
| Service-level method validation | Pastikan error mapper konsisten |

Sering kali lebih jelas membuat DTO terpisah: `CreateOrderRequest`, `UpdateOrderRequest`, `ApproveOrderRequest`.

---

## 10. Custom Constraint

Gunakan custom constraint untuk rule format/shape yang reusable dan tidak butuh DB/service call.

### Annotation

```java id="mwsgvq"
@Documented
@Constraint(validatedBy = IsoCurrencyValidator.class)
@Target({ FIELD, PARAMETER, RECORD_COMPONENT })
@Retention(RUNTIME)
public @interface IsoCurrency {

    String message() default "currency must be a valid ISO-4217 currency code";

    Class<?>[] groups() default {};

    Class<? extends Payload>[] payload() default {};
}
```

### Validator

```java id="ngg71x"
public final class IsoCurrencyValidator
    implements ConstraintValidator<IsoCurrency, String> {

    private static final Set<String> CODES = Currency.getAvailableCurrencies()
        .stream()
        .map(Currency::getCurrencyCode)
        .collect(Collectors.toUnmodifiableSet());

    @Override
    public boolean isValid(String value, ConstraintValidatorContext context) {
        if (value == null || value.isBlank()) {
            return true; // let @NotBlank handle requiredness
        }

        return CODES.contains(value);
    }
}
```

### Usage

```java id="r94gr1"
public record CreateOrderRequest(

    @NotBlank
    @IsoCurrency
    String currency
) {}
```

### Custom constraint rule

| Rule | Reason |
|---|---|
| Validator should be side-effect-free | Validation may run multiple times. |
| Avoid DB call in field validator | Hard to test/cache; can cause performance issue. |
| Null usually valid | Let `@NotNull`/`@NotBlank` handle requiredness. |
| Use class-level constraint for cross-field | Field validator should not inspect unrelated fields. |

---

## 11. Cross-Field / Class-Level Constraint

Untuk rule seperti `startDate <= endDate`, gunakan class-level constraint.

```java id="kpua5u"
@ValidDateRange
public record ReportRequest(

    @NotNull
    LocalDate startDate,

    @NotNull
    LocalDate endDate
) {}
```

```java id="blo7cz"
@Documented
@Constraint(validatedBy = DateRangeValidator.class)
@Target({ TYPE, RECORD_COMPONENT })
@Retention(RUNTIME)
public @interface ValidDateRange {

    String message() default "startDate must be before or equal to endDate";

    Class<?>[] groups() default {};

    Class<? extends Payload>[] payload() default {};
}
```

```java id="p7b9w0"
public final class DateRangeValidator
    implements ConstraintValidator<ValidDateRange, ReportRequest> {

    @Override
    public boolean isValid(ReportRequest value, ConstraintValidatorContext context) {
        if (value == null || value.startDate() == null || value.endDate() == null) {
            return true;
        }

        if (!value.startDate().isAfter(value.endDate())) {
            return true;
        }

        context.disableDefaultConstraintViolation();
        context.buildConstraintViolationWithTemplate(
                "startDate must be before or equal to endDate"
            )
            .addPropertyNode("startDate")
            .addConstraintViolation();

        return false;
    }
}
```

Class-level constraint cocok untuk cross-field shape validation, bukan rule domain yang butuh state database.

---

## 12. Method Validation

Jakarta Validation mendukung method validation untuk parameter dan return value. Spesifikasi Jakarta Validation 3.1 tetap mendefinisikan API untuk JavaBean dan method validation, dan tutorial Jakarta EE juga menyebut validasi methods/constructors. ([Jakarta Validation 3.1](https://jakarta.ee/specifications/bean-validation/3.1/), [Jakarta EE Tutorial - Bean Validation](https://jakarta.ee/learn/docs/jakartaee-tutorial/current/beanvalidation/bean-validation/bean-validation.html))

```java id="s6842p"
@ApplicationScoped
public class OrderApplicationService {

    public OrderDto getOrder(
        @NotBlank String orderId,
        @NotBlank String tenantId
    ) {
        return doGetOrder(orderId, tenantId);
    }

    @NotNull
    public OrderDto approve(
        @NotNull @Valid ApproveOrderCommand command
    ) {
        return doApprove(command);
    }
}
```

### Caution

Method validation bergantung pada runtime/interceptor integration. Kalau service dibuat manual dengan `new`, validation method interceptor tidak otomatis aktif.

---

## 13. Business Rule Validation

Business validation tidak selalu cocok sebagai Jakarta Validation annotation.

```text id="xvh36t"
DTO validation:
  amount must be > 0

Business validation:
  amount must not exceed approval limit for this actor and order state

DTO validation:
  orderId format valid

Business validation:
  order exists, belongs to tenant, and can be approved now
```

### Domain validator

```java id="i46am7"
@ApplicationScoped
public class OrderBusinessValidator {

    public void validateCreate(CreateOrderCommand command, RequestContext context) {
        if (!context.tenantId().equals(command.tenantId())) {
            throw new DomainValidationException(
                "TENANT_MISMATCH",
                "Tenant mismatch"
            );
        }

        if (command.lines().isEmpty()) {
            throw new DomainValidationException(
                "ORDER_LINES_REQUIRED",
                "Order must contain at least one line"
            );
        }

        BigDecimal total = command.totalAmount();

        if (total.compareTo(BigDecimal.ZERO) <= 0) {
            throw new DomainValidationException(
                "ORDER_TOTAL_INVALID",
                "Order total must be greater than zero"
            );
        }
    }

    public void validateApprove(Order order, ApprovalCommand command) {
        if (order.status() != OrderStatus.PENDING_APPROVAL) {
            throw new InvalidStateTransitionException(
                "APPROVE_ORDER",
                order.status().name()
            );
        }

        if (command.reason() == null || command.reason().isBlank()) {
            throw new DomainValidationException(
                "APPROVAL_REASON_REQUIRED",
                "Approval reason is required"
            );
        }
    }
}
```

### Business validation status

| Condition | Suggested Status |
|---|---:|
| Field/input shape invalid | 400 |
| Business invariant violated by request content | 400 or 422 |
| State transition impossible | 409 |
| Duplicate natural key | 409 |
| Stale version | 409 |
| External dependency prevents validation | 503/504 |
| Actor not allowed | 403 |

---

## 14. DB Constraint as Final Guard

Database constraints tetap wajib meski aplikasi sudah validasi.

```sql id="ge5z4g"
ALTER TABLE orders
ADD CONSTRAINT orders_amount_positive CHECK (amount > 0);

ALTER TABLE orders
ADD CONSTRAINT orders_order_number_unique
UNIQUE (tenant_id, order_number);

ALTER TABLE orders
ADD CONSTRAINT orders_tenant_not_null
CHECK (tenant_id IS NOT NULL);
```

### Mapping DB errors

| DB Error | Domain Meaning | HTTP |
|---|---|---:|
| unique violation | duplicate order number | 409 |
| FK violation | referenced entity missing | 400/409 |
| check violation | invariant violated | 400/409/500 depending cause |
| not null violation | app bug or invalid request | 400/500 depending boundary |
| serialization failure | retryable conflict | 409/503 |
| deadlock | retryable server-side | 503 or retry internally |

Rule: DB constraint adalah **last line of defense**, bukan user-friendly validation layer utama.

---

## 15. Validation Error Response

Gunakan response shape konsisten.

```json id="vg9mg9"
{
  "type": "https://errors.company.com/validation/invalid-request",
  "title": "Invalid request",
  "status": 400,
  "code": "VALIDATION_FAILED",
  "detail": "Request validation failed",
  "correlationId": "corr-123",
  "timestamp": "2026-07-13T09:25:00Z",
  "errors": [
    {
      "field": "amount",
      "code": "DECIMAL_MIN",
      "message": "amount must be greater than zero"
    }
  ]
}
```

### `ConstraintViolationExceptionMapper`

```java id="ck6t7w"
@Provider
public final class ConstraintViolationExceptionMapper
    implements ExceptionMapper<ConstraintViolationException> {

    @Override
    public Response toResponse(ConstraintViolationException exception) {
        String correlationId = Correlation.currentId();

        List<FieldError> errors = exception.getConstraintViolations()
            .stream()
            .map(this::toFieldError)
            .sorted(Comparator
                .comparing(FieldError::field)
                .thenComparing(FieldError::code))
            .toList();

        ErrorResponse body = ErrorResponse.validation(
            correlationId,
            errors
        );

        return Response.status(Response.Status.BAD_REQUEST)
            .type(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
            .entity(body)
            .build();
    }

    private FieldError toFieldError(ConstraintViolation<?> violation) {
        return new FieldError(
            normalizePath(violation.getPropertyPath().toString()),
            codeOf(violation),
            violation.getMessage()
        );
    }

    private String normalizePath(String rawPath) {
        if (rawPath == null || rawPath.isBlank()) {
            return "$";
        }

        return rawPath
            .replace("arg0.", "")
            .replace("createOrder.", "");
    }

    private String codeOf(ConstraintViolation<?> violation) {
        return violation.getConstraintDescriptor()
            .getAnnotation()
            .annotationType()
            .getSimpleName()
            .toUpperCase(Locale.ROOT);
    }
}
```

---

## 16. JSON Syntax / Mapping Error Response

```java id="bmgiri"
@Provider
public final class JsonParseExceptionMapper
    implements ExceptionMapper<JsonProcessingException> {

    @Override
    public Response toResponse(JsonProcessingException exception) {
        String correlationId = Correlation.currentId();

        ErrorResponse body = ErrorResponse.of(
            "https://errors.company.com/request/malformed-json",
            "Malformed JSON request",
            400,
            "MALFORMED_JSON",
            "Request body is not valid JSON",
            correlationId
        );

        LOGGER.info(
            "Malformed JSON correlationId={} parserMessage={}",
            correlationId,
            exception.getOriginalMessage()
        );

        return Response.status(Response.Status.BAD_REQUEST)
            .type(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
            .entity(body)
            .build();
    }
}
```

Jangan kirim parser exception mentah ke client karena bisa berisi nama class internal atau detail struktur DTO.

---

## 17. Validation vs Authorization vs Conflict

| Scenario | Wrong Mapping | Better Mapping |
|---|---|---|
| Missing `customerId` | 500 | 400 |
| Invalid `limit=9999` | 500 | 400 |
| User lacks permission | 400 | 403 |
| Tenant mismatch | 400 | 403/404 |
| Order already approved | 400 | 409 |
| Duplicate order number | 500 | 409 |
| Stale version | 500 | 409 |
| External product service down while validating SKU | 400 | 503/504 |
| SKU format invalid | 500 | 400 |
| SKU not found | 400/404 depending resource semantics | 400 or 409 depending operation |

Rule:

```text id="e8ptdl"
400 = request shape/value invalid
403 = actor cannot do this
404 = resource not found or hidden
409 = request conflicts with current resource state
422 = semantically invalid but syntactically correct, if your API standard uses it
5xx = server/dependency failure
```

---

## 18. Validation Placement Pattern

```text id="desiw3"
Resource
  ↓ shape validation via Jakarta Validation
Command Mapper
  ↓ maps DTO to command
Service
  ↓ business validation + authorization + state checks
Repository
  ↓ persistence constraints / optimistic lock
Exception Mapper
  ↓ stable error response
```

### Resource

```java id="qfmivq"
@POST
public Response create(@Valid CreateOrderRequest request,
                       @Context ContainerRequestContext ctx) {
    RequestContext requestContext = RequestContexts.from(ctx);
    CreateOrderCommand command = CreateOrderCommand.from(request, requestContext);

    OrderDto result = orderService.create(command, requestContext);

    return Response.status(Response.Status.CREATED)
        .entity(result)
        .build();
}
```

### Service

```java id="fzs6k1"
public OrderDto create(CreateOrderCommand command, RequestContext context) {
    authorizationPolicy.requireCanCreateOrder(context.principal(), context.tenantId());

    businessValidator.validateCreate(command, context);

    Order order = Order.create(command);

    orderRepository.insert(order);

    auditPublisher.publish(OrderAuditEvent.created(order, context));

    return OrderDto.from(order);
}
```

---

## 19. Validation Testing Strategy

```text id="bfpxx9"
Validation Test Types
│
├─ DTO validation test
│  ├─ required fields
│  ├─ size/range/pattern
│  ├─ nested validation
│  └─ custom constraint
│
├─ Resource/API validation test
│  ├─ malformed JSON
│  ├─ invalid path/query/header
│  ├─ invalid body
│  └─ expected error response
│
├─ Business validation test
│  ├─ invalid state
│  ├─ duplicate
│  ├─ cross-entity rule
│  └─ temporal rule
│
├─ Persistence constraint test
│  ├─ unique constraint
│  ├─ check constraint
│  ├─ FK
│  └─ optimistic lock
│
└─ Error contract test
   ├─ stable code
   ├─ field errors
   ├─ no stack trace
   └─ correlation ID
```

### DTO validation unit test

```java id="f4sxct"
class CreateOrderRequestValidationTest {

    private final Validator validator =
        Validation.buildDefaultValidatorFactory().getValidator();

    @Test
    void amountMustBeGreaterThanZero() {
        CreateOrderRequest request = new CreateOrderRequest(
            "customer-1",
            BigDecimal.ZERO,
            "USD",
            null,
            List.of(new OrderLineRequest("product-1", 1, BigDecimal.TEN))
        );

        Set<ConstraintViolation<CreateOrderRequest>> violations =
            validator.validate(request);

        assertTrue(violations.stream()
            .anyMatch(v -> v.getPropertyPath().toString().equals("amount")));
    }
}
```

### API validation test

```java id="ldq3us"
@Test
void invalidCreateOrderRequestReturnsValidationErrors() {
    given()
        .auth().oauth2(validToken())
        .header("X-Tenant-Id", "tenant-a")
        .contentType(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
        .body("""
            {
              "customerId": "",
              "amount": 0,
              "currency": "US"
            }
        """)
    .when()
        .post("/orders")
    .then()
        .statusCode(400)
        .body("code", equalTo("VALIDATION_FAILED"))
        .body("errors.field", hasItems("customerId", "amount", "currency"));
}
```

### Business validation test

```java id="lcgqx0"
@Test
void cannotApproveClosedOrder() {
    Order order = orderWithStatus(OrderStatus.CLOSED);

    InvalidStateTransitionException ex = assertThrows(
        InvalidStateTransitionException.class,
        () -> businessValidator.validateApprove(order, approvalCommand())
    );

    assertEquals("ORDER_INVALID_STATE", ex.code());
}
```

---

## 20. Production Failure Modes

| Symptom | Root Cause | Fix |
|---|---|---|
| Invalid payload reaches service | Missing `@Valid` or validation support not registered | Add `@Valid`, ensure Jersey validation dependency/config |
| Nested DTO not validated | Missing nested `@Valid` | Add `@Valid` on nested object/collection |
| Query param invalid becomes 500 | Missing mapper for validation/conversion error | Add mapper and API tests |
| Business rule returns 400 inconsistently | No taxonomy | Define 400 vs 409 vs 403 policy |
| User sees parser class names | Raw JSON exception exposed | Safe JSON mapper |
| Duplicate order becomes 500 | DB unique violation not mapped | Convert to domain conflict |
| Validation annotation calls DB | ConstraintValidator doing heavy IO | Move to service-level validator |
| Method validation not active | Service not container-managed | Use CDI/HK2 integration or explicit validator |
| Error field path ugly | Raw property path exposed | Normalize path |
| Sensitive field in validation message | Message includes secret/internal detail | Use safe validation messages |
| Client cannot automate error handling | Unstable error messages only | Stable error codes |
| Validation differs local/prod | Provider dependency/config mismatch | Bootstrap and API validation tests |

---

## 21. Production Checklist

| Area | Checklist |
|---|---|
| DTO validation | `@Valid` used on request body and nested DTOs |
| Parameter validation | Path/query/header constraints defined |
| Custom constraints | Side-effect-free and tested |
| Business validation | State/cross-entity rules live in service/domain layer |
| DB constraints | Critical invariants enforced in database |
| Error mapping | Constraint violations mapped to stable field errors |
| JSON mapping | Malformed JSON mapped to safe 400 response |
| Status taxonomy | 400/403/404/409/422/5xx policy documented |
| Correlation | Validation errors include correlation ID |
| Logging | Validation logs do not expose secrets/PII unnecessarily |
| Testing | DTO, API, business, persistence validation tests exist |
| Runtime | Jersey validation support dependency/config present |
| Records | Java records validated correctly in current stack |
| CI | Validation regression tests block merge |

---

## 22. Roadmap Belajar Singkat

| Urutan | Fokus | Target Praktik |
|---:|---|---|
| 1 | Jakarta Validation basics | Bisa memakai `@NotNull`, `@NotBlank`, `@Size`, `@Pattern`, `@Valid`. |
| 2 | Resource parameter validation | Bisa validasi path/query/header params. |
| 3 | Nested/collection validation | Bisa memastikan nested DTO dan list item tervalidasi. |
| 4 | Custom constraints | Bisa membuat annotation + `ConstraintValidator`. |
| 5 | Business validation | Bisa memisahkan DTO validation dari domain/state validation. |
| 6 | Error mapping | Bisa membuat field-level validation error response yang stabil. |
| 7 | DB constraint mapping | Bisa map unique/check/FK/lock conflict ke error domain. |
| 8 | Testing strategy | Bisa membuat DTO/API/business/persistence validation regression tests. |

## Response:
7/13/2026, 6:11:26 AM

# Cheatsheet - Jersey/JAX-RS DTO & Mapper Architecture  
## Request/Response DTOs, Command Mapping, MapStruct, Validation Boundaries, Versioning, Anti-Corruption Layer

## 1. Scope

Seri ini membahas bagaimana mendesain **DTO dan mapper architecture** di aplikasi Jersey/JAX-RS agar boundary API, domain model, persistence model, dan downstream contract tidak saling bocor.

```text
HTTP Request
  ↓
Request DTO
  ↓ validation
Command / Query Object
  ↓ application service
Domain Model
  ↓ business rule / state transition
Persistence Model / External Model
  ↓
Response DTO
  ↓
HTTP Response
```

Tujuan utamanya: resource JAX-RS tetap tipis, validasi terjadi di boundary yang benar, domain model tidak terikat bentuk JSON, dan perubahan API contract bisa dikelola tanpa merusak service/domain layer.

MapStruct relevan di sini karena ia adalah annotation processor yang menghasilkan mapper Java saat compile-time; generated code memakai method invocation biasa sehingga type-safe, cepat, dan mudah dibaca. ([MapStruct](https://mapstruct.org/?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) Jersey juga mendukung Bean/Jakarta Validation untuk resource dan entity validation melalui modul Bean Validation support. ([Eclipse EE4J](https://eclipse-ee4j.github.io/jersey.github.io/documentation/latest/bean-validation.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 2. Mental Model

```text
DTO is contract shape.
Command is application intent.
Domain model is business truth.
Entity/Record is persistence shape.
External DTO is integration shape.
Mapper is translation boundary.
```

Jangan jadikan satu class untuk semua layer.

| Model | Layer | Tujuan | Boleh Punya Logic? |
|---|---|---|---:|
| Request DTO | API boundary | Bentuk JSON/input client | Tidak, hanya shape + validation |
| Response DTO | API boundary | Bentuk JSON/output client | Tidak |
| Command / Query | Application layer | Intent operasi | Sedikit invariant ringan |
| Domain model | Domain layer | State dan behavior bisnis | Ya |
| Persistence entity/record | Repository layer | Bentuk storage/query result | Tidak/seperlunya |
| External DTO | Integration layer | Contract downstream/upstream | Tidak |
| Mapper | Boundary translator | Konversi antar model | Mapping logic, bukan business decision |

Rule senior-level:

```text
Resource maps HTTP → DTO → command.
Service handles command → domain behavior.
Repository maps domain/query → database.
Mapper never becomes policy engine.
```

---

## 3. Decomposition Map

```text
DTO & Mapper Architecture
│
├─ 1. API DTO Design
│  ├─ request DTO
│  ├─ response DTO
│  ├─ error DTO
│  ├─ pagination DTO
│  └─ versioned DTO
│
├─ 2. Validation Boundary
│  ├─ Jakarta Validation
│  ├─ JSON parsing validation
│  ├─ resource parameter validation
│  ├─ nested DTO validation
│  └─ business validation separation
│
├─ 3. Application Mapping
│  ├─ request DTO → command
│  ├─ query params → query object
│  ├─ command → domain factory
│  └─ domain → response DTO
│
├─ 4. Domain Boundary
│  ├─ no JAX-RS annotations in domain
│  ├─ no JSON annotations in domain
│  ├─ no persistence annotations in domain
│  └─ no downstream DTO in domain
│
├─ 5. Mapper Implementation
│  ├─ manual mapper
│  ├─ MapStruct mapper
│  ├─ decorator / after mapping
│  ├─ context-aware mapping
│  └─ collection mapping
│
├─ 6. Anti-Corruption Layer
│  ├─ external API DTO mapping
│  ├─ downstream error mapping
│  ├─ enum/status translation
│  └─ compatibility shielding
│
├─ 7. Versioning
│  ├─ additive response changes
│  ├─ request compatibility
│  ├─ v1/v2 DTOs
│  ├─ deprecated fields
│  └─ mapper coexistence
│
└─ 8. Testing
   ├─ mapper unit test
   ├─ API serialization test
   ├─ validation test
   ├─ contract regression test
   └─ backward compatibility test
```

---

## 4. Layering Pattern

```text
api
├─ dto
│  ├─ request
│  ├─ response
│  └─ error
├─ mapper
│  └─ OrderApiMapper
└─ resource
   └─ OrderResource

application
├─ command
│  └─ CreateOrderCommand
├─ query
│  └─ SearchOrdersQuery
└─ service
   └─ OrderApplicationService

domain
├─ model
│  └─ Order
├─ value
│  └─ Money
└─ policy
   └─ OrderAccessPolicy

infrastructure
├─ persistence
│  ├─ OrderRecord
│  └─ OrderRepositoryPostgres
└─ external
   ├─ CustomerClientDto
   └─ CustomerClientMapper
```

Core invariant:

```text
api layer may know application layer.
application layer may know domain layer.
domain layer should not know api/infrastructure layer.
infrastructure maps to/from domain.
```

---

## 5. Request DTO Design

```java
public record CreateOrderRequest(

    @NotBlank(message = "customerId is required")
    String customerId,

    @NotNull(message = "amount is required")
    @DecimalMin(value = "0.01", message = "amount must be greater than zero")
    BigDecimal amount,

    @NotBlank(message = "currency is required")
    @Pattern(regexp = "^[A-Z]{3}$", message = "currency must be ISO-4217 code")
    String currency,

    @Valid
    @NotEmpty(message = "lines must not be empty")
    List<CreateOrderLineRequest> lines,

    @Size(max = 500, message = "note must not exceed 500 characters")
    String note
) {}
```

```java
public record CreateOrderLineRequest(

    @NotBlank(message = "productId is required")
    String productId,

    @Min(value = 1, message = "quantity must be at least 1")
    int quantity,

    @NotNull(message = "unitPrice is required")
    @DecimalMin(value = "0.00", message = "unitPrice must not be negative")
    BigDecimal unitPrice
) {}
```

Jakarta Validation 3.1 mendefinisikan metadata model dan API untuk JavaBean dan method validation, menargetkan Jakarta EE 11, dan mengklarifikasi support Java Records. ([Jakarta EE](https://jakarta.ee/specifications/bean-validation/3.1/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

### Request DTO rules

| Rule | Alasan |
|---|---|
| Pakai record untuk immutable request shape | Cocok untuk Java 17+ dan API DTO sederhana |
| Pakai wrapper DTO, jangan expose domain langsung | Domain tidak terikat JSON contract |
| Validasi shape di DTO | Required, format, size, range |
| Jangan simpan business decision di DTO | Business rule butuh service/domain state |
| Jangan expose persistence ID internal bila tidak perlu | Contract stability dan security |

---

## 6. Response DTO Design

```java
public record OrderResponse(
    String id,
    String status,
    String customerId,
    MoneyResponse total,
    List<OrderLineResponse> lines,
    Instant createdAt,
    Instant updatedAt,
    Map<String, LinkResponse> links
) {}
```

```java
public record OrderLineResponse(
    String productId,
    String productName,
    int quantity,
    MoneyResponse unitPrice,
    MoneyResponse subtotal
) {}
```

```java
public record MoneyResponse(
    BigDecimal amount,
    String currency
) {}
```

```java
public record LinkResponse(
    String href,
    String method
) {}
```

### Response DTO rules

| Rule | Alasan |
|---|---|
| Response DTO boleh berbeda dari domain | API contract tidak harus sama dengan internal model |
| Jangan expose internal enum mentah tanpa policy | Enum bisa berubah di domain |
| Jangan expose field sensitive by default | Field-level authorization |
| Tambahkan link/action bila membantu client | Bisa mendukung workflow/action availability |
| Gunakan format waktu konsisten | ISO-8601 biasanya lebih aman |

---

## 7. Command Mapping

Request DTO bukan command. Command adalah intent aplikasi yang sudah membawa context penting.

```java
public record CreateOrderCommand(
    String customerId,
    Money total,
    List<CreateOrderLineCommand> lines,
    String note,
    String tenantId,
    String actorId,
    String correlationId
) {}
```

```java
public record CreateOrderLineCommand(
    String productId,
    int quantity,
    Money unitPrice
) {}
```

```java
public record Money(
    BigDecimal amount,
    String currency
) {
    public Money {
        if (amount == null || amount.signum() < 0) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("amount must not be negative");
        }
        if (currency == null || currency.isBlank()) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("currency is required");
        }
    }
}
```

```java
public final class OrderCommandMapper {

    public CreateOrderCommand toCommand(
        CreateOrderRequest request,
        RequestContext context
    ) {
        List<CreateOrderLineCommand> lines = request.lines()
            .stream()
            .map(line -> new CreateOrderLineCommand(
                line.productId(),
                line.quantity(),
                new Money(line.unitPrice(), request.currency())
            ))
            .toList();

        BigDecimal totalAmount = lines.stream()
            .map(line -> line.unitPrice().amount()
                .multiply(BigDecimal.valueOf(line.quantity())))
            .reduce(BigDecimal.ZERO, BigDecimal::add);

        return new CreateOrderCommand(
            request.customerId(),
            new Money(totalAmount, request.currency()),
            lines,
            request.note(),
            context.tenantId(),
            context.actorId(),
            context.correlationId()
        );
    }
}
```

Kenapa command perlu context? Karena operasi application layer hampir selalu butuh tenant, actor, correlation ID, dan authorization/audit boundary.

---

## 8. Resource Pattern

```java
@Path("/orders")
@Consumes(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
@Produces(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
public final class OrderResource {

    private final OrderApplicationService orderService;
    private final OrderCommandMapper commandMapper;
    private final OrderApiMapper apiMapper;

    @Inject
    public OrderResource(
        OrderApplicationService orderService,
        OrderCommandMapper commandMapper,
        OrderApiMapper apiMapper
    ) {
        this.orderService = orderService;
        this.commandMapper = commandMapper;
        this.apiMapper = apiMapper;
    }

    @POST
    public Response createOrder(
        @Valid CreateOrderRequest request,
        @Context ContainerRequestContext rawContext
    ) {
        RequestContext context = RequestContexts.from(rawContext);

        CreateOrderCommand command = commandMapper.toCommand(request, context);

        Order order = orderService.create(command, context);

        OrderResponse response = apiMapper.toResponse(order, context);

        return Response.status(Response.Status.CREATED)
            .entity(response)
            .build();
    }
}
```

Resource responsibilities:

```text
Do:
- receive HTTP input
- rely on validation
- extract request context
- map DTO → command
- call application service
- map result → response

Do not:
- query repository directly
- perform authorization rule deeply
- perform state transition directly
- expose domain object directly
- catch all exceptions manually
```

---

## 9. Manual Mapper Pattern

Manual mapper bagus saat mapping mengandung rule kecil yang perlu dibaca jelas.

```java
public final class OrderApiMapper {

    public OrderResponse toResponse(Order order, RequestContext context) {
        return new OrderResponse(
            order.id().value(),
            mapStatus(order.status()),
            order.customerId().value(),
            toMoney(order.total()),
            order.lines().stream()
                .map(this::toLineResponse)
                .toList(),
            order.createdAt(),
            order.updatedAt(),
            linksFor(order, context)
        );
    }

    private OrderLineResponse toLineResponse(OrderLine line) {
        return new OrderLineResponse(
            line.productId().value(),
            line.productName(),
            line.quantity(),
            toMoney(line.unitPrice()),
            toMoney(line.subtotal())
        );
    }

    private MoneyResponse toMoney(Money money) {
        return new MoneyResponse(money.amount(), money.currency());
    }

    private String mapStatus(OrderStatus status) {
        return switch (status) {
            case DRAFT -> "draft";
            case SUBMITTED -> "submitted";
            case PENDING_APPROVAL -> "pending_approval";
            case APPROVED -> "approved";
            case CLOSED -> "closed";
        };
    }

    private Map<String, LinkResponse> linksFor(Order order, RequestContext context) {
        Map<String, LinkResponse> links = new LinkedHashMap<>();

        links.put("self", new LinkResponse("/orders/" + order.id().value(), "GET"));

        if (order.canBeApprovedBy(context.actorId())) {
            links.put(
                "approve",
                new LinkResponse("/orders/" + order.id().value() + "/approve", "POST")
            );
        }

        return links;
    }
}
```

### Manual mapper cocok ketika

| Kondisi | Alasan |
|---|---|
| Mapping sedikit tapi business-adjacent | Lebih mudah dibaca |
| Perlu conditional field-level authorization | Butuh context/policy |
| Perlu link/action availability | Tidak sekadar property copy |
| Mapping enum/status butuh stabilisasi contract | Explicit switch lebih aman |
| Response shape tidak mirip domain | Manual lebih jelas |

---

## 10. MapStruct Pattern

MapStruct cocok untuk mapping yang banyak bersifat structural: property copy, nested mapping, collection mapping, enum mapping, record/bean mapping.

```java
@Mapper(componentModel = "jakarta")
public interface OrderStructMapper {

    @Mapping(target = "status", expression = "java(order.status().name().toLowerCase())")
    @Mapping(target = "total", source = "total")
    OrderResponse toResponse(Order order);

    MoneyResponse toResponse(Money money);

    OrderLineResponse toResponse(OrderLine line);
}
```

MapStruct adalah code generator berbasis annotation processor untuk mapping antar Java bean, dan generated mapping code menggunakan plain method invocations sehingga type-safe dan mudah dipahami. ([MapStruct](https://mapstruct.org/?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) Repository resmi MapStruct juga menyebut support untuk Java 16+ records. ([GitHub](https://github.com/mapstruct/mapstruct?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

### Maven setup

```xml
<dependencies>
    <dependency>
        <groupId>org.mapstruct</groupId>
        <artifactId>mapstruct</artifactId>
        <version>${mapstruct.version}</version>
    </dependency>
</dependencies>

<build>
    <plugins>
        <plugin>
            <groupId>org.apache.maven.plugins</groupId>
            <artifactId>maven-compiler-plugin</artifactId>
            <configuration>
                <source>17</source>
                <target>17</target>
                <annotationProcessorPaths>
                    <path>
                        <groupId>org.mapstruct</groupId>
                        <artifactId>mapstruct-processor</artifactId>
                        <version>${mapstruct.version}</version>
                    </path>
                </annotationProcessorPaths>
            </configuration>
        </plugin>
    </plugins>
</build>
```

### MapStruct rules

| Rule | Alasan |
|---|---|
| Pakai untuk deterministic structural mapping | Compile-time safe dan cepat |
| Jangan sembunyikan business decision di expression rumit | Mapper bukan policy engine |
| Gunakan `unmappedTargetPolicy = ERROR` untuk critical mapping | Field baru tidak silently hilang |
| Test generated mapping via unit test | Mencegah regression contract |
| Hindari mapping terlalu magic | Mapper harus bisa diaudit |

---

## 11. Strict Mapper Config

```java
@MapperConfig(
    componentModel = "jakarta",
    unmappedTargetPolicy = ReportingPolicy.ERROR,
    unmappedSourcePolicy = ReportingPolicy.WARN,
    nullValueCheckStrategy = NullValueCheckStrategy.ALWAYS
)
public interface ApiMapperConfig {}
```

```java
@Mapper(config = ApiMapperConfig.class)
public interface OrderMapper {

    @Mapping(target = "id", expression = "java(order.id().value())")
    @Mapping(target = "status", expression = "java(toApiStatus(order.status()))")
    OrderResponse toResponse(Order order);

    default String toApiStatus(OrderStatus status) {
        return switch (status) {
            case DRAFT -> "draft";
            case SUBMITTED -> "submitted";
            case PENDING_APPROVAL -> "pending_approval";
            case APPROVED -> "approved";
            case CLOSED -> "closed";
        };
    }
}
```

Strict mapping penting untuk API DTO. Jika field baru ditambahkan ke response, build sebaiknya gagal sampai mapper secara eksplisit memutuskan cara mengisi field tersebut.

---

## 12. Context-Aware Mapping

Kadang response field tergantung permission/tenant/context. Jangan sembunyikan logic terlalu dalam.

### Manual context-aware mapper

```java
public final class OrderResponseMapper {

    private final FieldAccessPolicy fieldAccessPolicy;

    public OrderResponseMapper(FieldAccessPolicy fieldAccessPolicy) {
        this.fieldAccessPolicy = fieldAccessPolicy;
    }

    public OrderDetailResponse toDetail(Order order, PrincipalContext principal) {
        return new OrderDetailResponse(
            order.id().value(),
            order.status().name(),
            order.customerId().value(),
            order.total().amount(),
            fieldAccessPolicy.canViewMargin(principal, order)
                ? order.margin().amount()
                : null,
            fieldAccessPolicy.canViewInternalNote(principal, order)
                ? order.internalNote()
                : null
        );
    }
}
```

### MapStruct with context

```java
@Mapper(config = ApiMapperConfig.class)
public interface OrderMapper {

    @Mapping(target = "margin", expression = "java(marginIfAllowed(order, principal))")
    OrderDetailResponse toDetail(Order order, @Context PrincipalContext principal);

    default BigDecimal marginIfAllowed(Order order, @Context PrincipalContext principal) {
        if (principal.hasPermission("order:field:margin:read")) {
            return order.margin().amount();
        }
        return null;
    }
}
```

Rule: kalau context-aware mapping mulai panjang, pindahkan ke manual mapper atau policy + mapper combination.

---

## 13. API DTO Versioning

```text
v1 DTO:
  OrderResponseV1
    id
    status
    amount

v2 DTO:
  OrderResponseV2
    id
    status
    total { amount, currency }
    links
```

### Package layout

```text
api
├─ v1
│  ├─ OrderResourceV1
│  ├─ OrderResponseV1
│  └─ OrderMapperV1
└─ v2
   ├─ OrderResourceV2
   ├─ OrderResponseV2
   └─ OrderMapperV2
```

### Mapper coexistence

```java
@Mapper(config = ApiMapperConfig.class)
public interface OrderMapperV1 {
    OrderResponseV1 toResponse(Order order);
}
```

```java
@Mapper(config = ApiMapperConfig.class)
public interface OrderMapperV2 {
    OrderResponseV2 toResponse(Order order);
}
```

### Versioning rules

| Change | Compatibility |
|---|---|
| Add optional response field | Usually backward-compatible |
| Remove response field | Breaking |
| Rename field | Breaking |
| Change field type | Breaking |
| Add required request field | Breaking |
| Add optional request field | Usually compatible |
| Change enum values | Can be breaking |
| Change status semantics | Often breaking even if JSON same |

---

## 14. Anti-Corruption Layer for External Systems

Jangan pakai DTO downstream langsung sebagai domain model.

```text
External Customer API DTO
  ↓
CustomerClientMapper
  ↓
CustomerSnapshot / Domain Value
  ↓
OrderService
```

### External DTO

```java
public record CustomerApiResponse(
    String customer_id,
    String display_name,
    String segment,
    String risk_score
) {}
```

### Internal value

```java
public record CustomerSnapshot(
    CustomerId id,
    String displayName,
    CustomerSegment segment,
    RiskScore riskScore
) {}
```

### Mapper

```java
public final class CustomerClientMapper {

    public CustomerSnapshot toSnapshot(CustomerApiResponse response) {
        return new CustomerSnapshot(
            new CustomerId(response.customer_id()),
            response.display_name(),
            mapSegment(response.segment()),
            mapRiskScore(response.risk_score())
        );
    }

    private CustomerSegment mapSegment(String value) {
        return switch (value) {
            case "ENT" -> CustomerSegment.ENTERPRISE;
            case "SMB" -> CustomerSegment.SMALL_BUSINESS;
            default -> CustomerSegment.UNKNOWN;
        };
    }

    private RiskScore mapRiskScore(String value) {
        if (value == null || value.isBlank()) {
            return RiskScore.unknown();
        }
        return RiskScore.of(new BigDecimal(value));
    }
}
```

Anti-corruption rules:

| Rule | Alasan |
|---|---|
| External DTO tidak masuk domain | Downstream contract bisa berubah |
| Translate enum/status eksplisit | Jangan bocor kode eksternal |
| Normalize missing/unknown values | Domain harus punya semantics sendiri |
| Map downstream errors ke domain/infrastructure exception | Jangan expose downstream internals |
| Test mapper dengan fixture contract | Mencegah regression integrasi |

---

## 15. Persistence Mapper

Jika pakai MyBatis/PostgreSQL, query result biasanya berupa record/entity persistence. Jangan expose langsung ke API.

```java
public record OrderRecord(
    String id,
    String tenantId,
    String customerId,
    String status,
    BigDecimal totalAmount,
    String currency,
    Instant createdAt,
    Instant updatedAt
) {}
```

```java
public final class OrderPersistenceMapper {

    public Order toDomain(OrderRecord record, List<OrderLineRecord> lines) {
        return Order.rehydrate(
            new OrderId(record.id()),
            new TenantId(record.tenantId()),
            new CustomerId(record.customerId()),
            mapStatus(record.status()),
            new Money(record.totalAmount(), record.currency()),
            lines.stream().map(this::toDomainLine).toList(),
            record.createdAt(),
            record.updatedAt()
        );
    }

    public OrderRecord toRecord(Order order) {
        return new OrderRecord(
            order.id().value(),
            order.tenantId().value(),
            order.customerId().value(),
            mapStatus(order.status()),
            order.total().amount(),
            order.total().currency(),
            order.createdAt(),
            order.updatedAt()
        );
    }

    private OrderStatus mapStatus(String status) {
        return switch (status) {
            case "DRAFT" -> OrderStatus.DRAFT;
            case "SUBMITTED" -> OrderStatus.SUBMITTED;
            case "PENDING_APPROVAL" -> OrderStatus.PENDING_APPROVAL;
            case "APPROVED" -> OrderStatus.APPROVED;
            case "CLOSED" -> OrderStatus.CLOSED;
            default -> throw new IllegalStateException("Unknown order status: " + status);
        };
    }

    private String mapStatus(OrderStatus status) {
        return status.name();
    }
}
```

### Persistence mapping rules

| Rule | Alasan |
|---|---|
| DB status → domain enum explicit | DB value corruption cepat ketahuan |
| Domain object rehydrate lewat factory | Invariant domain tetap terkendali |
| Persistence record tidak dipakai di API response | Avoid leakage |
| Tenant ID wajib masuk record/domain | Tenant isolation |
| Mapping unknown value fail-fast | Jangan diam-diam map ke default |

---

## 16. Partial Update / PATCH Mapping

PATCH raw-to-domain sering berbahaya. Gunakan patch DTO + field-level authorization.

```java
public record PatchOrderRequest(
    Optional<String> status,
    Optional<String> note,
    Optional<BigDecimal> discount
) {}
```

```java
public final class PatchOrderMapper {

    public PatchOrderCommand toCommand(
        String orderId,
        PatchOrderRequest request,
        RequestContext context
    ) {
        return new PatchOrderCommand(
            orderId,
            request.status(),
            request.note(),
            request.discount(),
            context.tenantId(),
            context.actorId()
        );
    }
}
```

```java
public final class PatchOrderService {

    public Order patch(PatchOrderCommand command, PrincipalContext principal) {
        Order order = repository.findByIdAndTenant(
            command.orderId(),
            command.tenantId()
        ).orElseThrow(OrderNotFoundException::new);

        if (command.status().isPresent()) {
            policy.requireCanUpdateField(principal, order, "status");
            order.changeStatus(OrderStatus.fromApi(command.status().get()));
        }

        if (command.note().isPresent()) {
            policy.requireCanUpdateField(principal, order, "note");
            order.updateNote(command.note().get());
        }

        if (command.discount().isPresent()) {
            policy.requireCanUpdateField(principal, order, "discount");
            order.applyDiscount(command.discount().get());
        }

        repository.save(order);
        return order;
    }
}
```

PATCH rules:

| Rule | Alasan |
|---|---|
| Jangan map patch langsung ke entity/domain via reflection | Field-level auth bisa terlewat |
| Optional membedakan absent vs null | Semantics update lebih jelas |
| Whitelist field yang boleh diubah | Prevent mass assignment |
| Per-field policy check | Sensitive field protection |
| Audit changed fields | Defensible change trail |

---

## 17. Mass Assignment Risk

Anti-pattern:

```java
// BAD
public Response update(Map<String, Object> body) {
    objectMapper.updateValue(orderEntity, body);
    repository.save(orderEntity);
    return Response.ok().build();
}
```

Risiko:

```text
client can set:
- tenantId
- ownerId
- approvalStatus
- internalRiskScore
- createdBy
- systemFlag
```

Better:

```java
public record UpdateOrderRequest(
    @Size(max = 500)
    String note,

    @Valid
    List<UpdateOrderLineRequest> lines
) {}
```

Rule: external client hanya boleh mengirim field yang memang bagian dari public contract.

---

## 18. Mapper Testing Strategy

```text
Mapper Tests
│
├─ Request DTO → Command
│  ├─ context copied
│  ├─ money calculated
│  ├─ tenant/actor included
│  └─ invalid assumptions tested
│
├─ Domain → Response DTO
│  ├─ status mapping
│  ├─ nested list mapping
│  ├─ field-level visibility
│  └─ links/actions
│
├─ Persistence → Domain
│  ├─ enum mapping
│  ├─ tenant included
│  ├─ unknown status fails
│  └─ rehydration invariant
│
├─ External DTO → Internal
│  ├─ unknown enum handling
│  ├─ missing value handling
│  └─ downstream compatibility
│
└─ Versioned DTO
   ├─ v1 shape stable
   ├─ v2 shape stable
   └─ backward compatibility
```

### Example mapper test

```java
@Test
void createOrderRequestMustMapToCommandWithContext() {
    CreateOrderRequest request = new CreateOrderRequest(
        "customer-1",
        new BigDecimal("100.00"),
        "USD",
        List.of(new CreateOrderLineRequest(
            "product-1",
            2,
            new BigDecimal("50.00")
        )),
        "urgent"
    );

    RequestContext context = new RequestContext(
        "req-1",
        "corr-1",
        "actor-1",
        "tenant-a"
    );

    CreateOrderCommand command =
        new OrderCommandMapper().toCommand(request, context);

    assertEquals("customer-1", command.customerId());
    assertEquals("tenant-a", command.tenantId());
    assertEquals("actor-1", command.actorId());
    assertEquals(new BigDecimal("100.00"), command.total().amount());
}
```

### API serialization test

```java
@Test
void orderResponseJsonShapeMustRemainStable() throws Exception {
    OrderResponse response = fixtureOrderResponse();

    String json = objectMapper.writeValueAsString(response);

    assertThat(json).contains("\"id\"");
    assertThat(json).contains("\"status\"");
    assertThat(json).contains("\"total\"");
    assertThat(json).doesNotContain("internalRiskScore");
}
```

---

## 19. Common Failure Modes

| Symptom | Root Cause | Fix |
|---|---|---|
| Domain object expose internal field in JSON | Resource returns domain directly | Always map domain → response DTO |
| Client can update forbidden field | PATCH/PUT maps raw body to entity | Whitelist request DTO + field policy |
| DTO validation not triggered | Missing `@Valid` / validation module | Add validation support and tests |
| Response breaks after domain enum rename | API status derived from enum name | Explicit enum-to-API mapping |
| Mapper silently drops new field | Lenient MapStruct config | `unmappedTargetPolicy = ERROR` |
| Business rule hidden in mapper | Mapper too smart | Move to service/policy/domain |
| Persistence enum unknown mapped to default | Overly permissive mapper | Fail fast on unknown DB value |
| External API change breaks domain | External DTO used directly | Anti-corruption mapper |
| v1 API changes when domain changes | Shared DTO/domain | Version-specific DTO/mapper |
| Sensitive field appears in response | No field-level mapper policy | Context-aware mapping/policy |

---

## 20. Production Checklist

| Area | Checklist |
|---|---|
| API boundary | Request/response DTOs separate from domain and persistence |
| Validation | Request DTOs validated with Jakarta Validation |
| Command mapping | Request DTO + context mapped to application command |
| Domain isolation | Domain has no JAX-RS/JSON/persistence annotations |
| Persistence mapping | DB record/entity mapped explicitly to domain |
| External integration | External DTO isolated behind anti-corruption mapper |
| MapStruct | Strict unmapped target policy for critical API DTOs |
| Enum/status | Explicit mapping, no accidental `enum.name()` contract unless intentional |
| PATCH/update | Whitelist fields and enforce field-level authorization |
| Versioning | v1/v2 DTOs and mappers coexist when breaking changes happen |
| Security | Sensitive fields mapped conditionally or omitted |
| Testing | Mapper tests, serialization tests, validation tests, version regression tests |
| Audit | Commands carry actor/tenant/correlation where needed |

---

## 21. Roadmap Belajar Singkat

| Urutan | Fokus | Target Praktik |
|---:|---|---|
| 1 | DTO separation | Bisa membedakan request DTO, response DTO, command, domain, persistence model |
| 2 | Request DTO validation | Bisa memasang Jakarta Validation di request body dan nested DTO |
| 3 | Command mapping | Bisa mengubah DTO + request context menjadi command |
| 4 | Domain → response mapping | Bisa membuat response DTO tanpa bocor internal |
| 5 | MapStruct strict mapping | Bisa memakai generated mapper dengan compile-time safety |
| 6 | Persistence mapping | Bisa map MyBatis/PostgreSQL record ke domain |
| 7 | Anti-corruption layer | Bisa isolasi downstream DTO dari domain |
| 8 | Versioning & tests | Bisa menjaga API contract saat domain berubah |
