## Response:
7/13/2026, 6:14:32 AM

Saya lanjut ke seri CDI: producer, qualifier, alternative, scope/lifecycle, dan cara mengetes wiring dengan Weld agar tidak hanya “compile”, tapi benar-benar aktif saat runtime.

# Cheatsheet - CDI Producers, Qualifiers, Alternatives, and Weld Testing  
## Producer Methods, Producer Fields, Disposer, Qualifier Strategy, Alternatives, Test Doubles, Weld/JUnit Testing

## 1. Scope

Seri ini membahas CDI/Weld sebagai **application composition model** untuk aplikasi Jersey/JAX-RS:

```text id="ulz7tx"
CDI / Weld Composition
│
├─ Producer
│  ├─ @Produces method
│  ├─ @Produces field
│  ├─ disposer
│  └─ runtime-created object
│
├─ Qualifier
│  ├─ multiple implementations
│  ├─ multiple DataSource
│  ├─ multiple HTTP client
│  └─ explicit dependency meaning
│
├─ Alternative
│  ├─ test double
│  ├─ environment-specific implementation
│  ├─ mock/stub/fake
│  └─ controlled replacement
│
├─ Scope & Lifecycle
│  ├─ @ApplicationScoped
│  ├─ @RequestScoped
│  ├─ @Dependent
│  ├─ @PostConstruct
│  └─ @PreDestroy / disposer
│
└─ Weld Testing
   ├─ CDI boot in unit/component test
   ├─ producer test
   ├─ qualifier resolution test
   ├─ alternative activation test
   └─ interceptor/event test
```

CDI 4.1 adalah spesifikasi CDI untuk Jakarta EE 11; spesifikasi ini mencakup bean, injection, scopes, producers, alternatives, interceptors, decorators, events, dan lifecycle. CDI 4.1 juga memindahkan detail integrasi Jakarta EE ke spesifikasi Platform/Web/Core Profile, jadi runtime integration tetap perlu diperhatikan di Jersey/Weld deployment. ([Jakarta EE](https://jakarta.ee/specifications/cdi/4.1/jakarta-cdi-spec-4.1?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 2. Mental Model

```text id="a7jrxh"
Producer answers:
"Bagaimana object non-CDI atau object yang butuh custom initialization masuk ke CDI?"

Qualifier answers:
"Kalau ada beberapa kandidat dependency, yang mana yang dimaksud?"

Alternative answers:
"Bagaimana mengganti implementation secara terkontrol?"

Weld test answers:
"Apakah wiring CDI benar-benar bekerja, bukan hanya compile?"
```

Rule utama:

```text id="e8yhz4"
Constructor injection untuk normal bean.
Producer untuk object yang butuh custom creation.
Qualifier untuk ambiguity yang sah.
Alternative untuk replacement yang disengaja.
Test CDI wiring dengan container, bukan hanya mock manual.
```

---

## 3. CDI Building Blocks

| Building Block | Fungsi | Contoh |
|---|---|---|
| `@Inject` | Meminta dependency | Service inject repository |
| `@ApplicationScoped` | Satu instance logical per app | Service stateless, config, policy |
| `@RequestScoped` | Satu instance per request | Request context, per-request accumulator |
| `@Dependent` | Lifecycle mengikuti injection target | Helper ringan |
| `@Produces` | Membuat object injectable | `DataSource`, `AppConfig`, HTTP client |
| `@Disposes` | Cleanup object producer | close HTTP client/pool |
| `@Qualifier` | Membedakan kandidat dependency | primary vs replica DB |
| `@Alternative` | Replacement implementation | fake repository test |
| `@Priority` | Enable alternative/interceptor globally | selected alternative |
| `@PostConstruct` | Lifecycle callback after injection | init cache |
| `@PreDestroy` | Lifecycle callback before destroy | cleanup resource |

Producer method berguna saat object yang ingin diinjeksi bukan bean biasa, concrete type-nya bisa berubah runtime, atau object butuh custom initialization yang tidak cocok di constructor bean. ([Jakarta EE](https://jakarta.ee/learn/docs/jakartaee-tutorial/current/cdi/cdi-adv/cdi-adv.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) Weld docs juga menekankan producer method sebagai cara umum mengintegrasikan object non-bean ke CDI environment. ([JBoss Documentation](https://docs.jboss.org/weld/reference/latest/en-US/html/producermethods.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 4. Producer Pattern: AppConfig

`AppConfig` biasanya dibuat dari env var, system property, mounted file, dan secret source. Ini bukan bean yang dibuat otomatis oleh CDI; lebih aman dibuat via producer.

```java id="7r7t2k"
@ApplicationScoped
public class RuntimeConfigProducer {

    private final AppConfig config = RuntimeConfigLoader.loadAndValidate();

    @Produces
    @ApplicationScoped
    public AppConfig appConfig() {
        return config;
    }

    @Produces
    @ApplicationScoped
    public DbConfig dbConfig() {
        return config.database();
    }

    @Produces
    @ApplicationScoped
    public SecurityConfig securityConfig() {
        return config.security();
    }
}
```

### Kenapa producer?

```text id="4vw5di"
AppConfig needs:
- custom loading
- parsing
- validation
- profile invariant
- secret masking
- fail-fast startup behavior
```

Jangan load config di banyak service.

```java id="6zqrlt"
// BAD
@ApplicationScoped
public class OrderService {
    private final AppConfig config = RuntimeConfigLoader.loadAndValidate();
}
```

Better:

```java id="n47sip"
@ApplicationScoped
public class OrderService {

    private final AppConfig config;

    @Inject
    public OrderService(AppConfig config) {
        this.config = config;
    }
}
```

---

## 5. Producer Pattern: DataSource

```java id="b0p5fb"
@ApplicationScoped
public class DataSourceProducer {

    @Produces
    @ApplicationScoped
    public DataSource dataSource(AppConfig config) {
        HikariConfig hikari = new HikariConfig();

        hikari.setJdbcUrl(config.database().url());
        hikari.setUsername(config.database().username());
        hikari.setPassword(config.database().password().reveal());
        hikari.setMaximumPoolSize(config.database().maxPoolSize());

        return new HikariDataSource(hikari);
    }

    public void closeDataSource(@Disposes DataSource dataSource) {
        if (dataSource instanceof HikariDataSource hikari) {
            hikari.close();
        }
    }
}
```

### Producer rules untuk infrastructure object

| Rule | Alasan |
|---|---|
| Producer class harus CDI-managed | Kalau producer class tidak ditemukan CDI, producer tidak aktif |
| Producer harus return interface bila mungkin | `DataSource`, bukan concrete pool type di service |
| Cleanup harus jelas | Pool/client harus ditutup saat shutdown |
| Jangan log secret saat create object | JDBC password/token/private key |
| Fail fast untuk config invalid | Jangan tunggu request pertama |
| Hindari multiple producer untuk same type tanpa qualifier | Akan menyebabkan ambiguous dependency |

---

## 6. Producer Pattern: HTTP Client

```java id="erwqu8"
@ApplicationScoped
public class HttpClientProducer {

    @Produces
    @ApplicationScoped
    public Client jerseyClient(AppConfig config) {
        ClientConfig clientConfig = new ClientConfig();

        clientConfig.property(
            ClientProperties.CONNECT_TIMEOUT,
            Math.toIntExact(config.httpClient().connectTimeout().toMillis())
        );

        clientConfig.property(
            ClientProperties.READ_TIMEOUT,
            Math.toIntExact(config.httpClient().readTimeout().toMillis())
        );

        return ClientBuilder.newClient(clientConfig)
            .register(CorrelationClientRequestFilter.class);
    }

    public void closeClient(@Disposes Client client) {
        client.close();
    }
}
```

### Service usage

```java id="8gme5u"
@ApplicationScoped
public class CustomerClient {

    private final Client client;
    private final AppConfig config;

    @Inject
    public CustomerClient(Client client, AppConfig config) {
        this.client = client;
        this.config = config;
    }

    public CustomerSnapshot getCustomer(String customerId, RequestContext context) {
        return client
            .target(config.customerService().baseUrl())
            .path("/customers/{id}")
            .resolveTemplate("id", customerId)
            .request(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
            .header("X-Correlation-Id", context.correlationId())
            .get(CustomerSnapshot.class);
    }
}
```

---

## 7. Qualifier Strategy

Qualifier digunakan ketika ada lebih dari satu bean dengan type yang sama.

```text id="btjrzx"
Without qualifier:
  DataSource
    ├─ primaryDataSource
    └─ readReplicaDataSource

CDI cannot know which one you mean.
```

### Qualifier definitions

```java id="x1eflg"
@Qualifier
@Retention(RUNTIME)
@Target({FIELD, PARAMETER, METHOD, TYPE})
public @interface PrimaryDatabase {}
```

```java id="b4n0u6"
@Qualifier
@Retention(RUNTIME)
@Target({FIELD, PARAMETER, METHOD, TYPE})
public @interface ReadReplicaDatabase {}
```

### Producer with qualifiers

```java id="t8mjmp"
@ApplicationScoped
public class DatabaseProducer {

    @Produces
    @PrimaryDatabase
    @ApplicationScoped
    public DataSource primaryDataSource(AppConfig config) {
        return DataSourceFactory.create(config.primaryDatabase());
    }

    @Produces
    @ReadReplicaDatabase
    @ApplicationScoped
    public DataSource readReplicaDataSource(AppConfig config) {
        return DataSourceFactory.create(config.readReplicaDatabase());
    }

    public void closePrimary(@Disposes @PrimaryDatabase DataSource dataSource) {
        close(dataSource);
    }

    public void closeReplica(@Disposes @ReadReplicaDatabase DataSource dataSource) {
        close(dataSource);
    }

    private void close(DataSource dataSource) {
        if (dataSource instanceof AutoCloseable closeable) {
            try {
                closeable.close();
            } catch (Exception e) {
                throw new IllegalStateException("Failed to close DataSource", e);
            }
        }
    }
}
```

### Injection

```java id="zb1a3o"
@ApplicationScoped
public class OrderRepositoryPostgres {

    private final DataSource primary;
    private final DataSource replica;

    @Inject
    public OrderRepositoryPostgres(
        @PrimaryDatabase DataSource primary,
        @ReadReplicaDatabase DataSource replica
    ) {
        this.primary = primary;
        this.replica = replica;
    }
}
```

---

## 8. Qualifier Design Rules

| Rule | Good | Bad |
|---|---|---|
| Name by meaning | `@PrimaryDatabase` | `@Db1` |
| Name by role | `@PaymentClient` | `@ClientA` |
| Avoid string names | Type-safe annotation | `"primary"` |
| Use only for real ambiguity | Multiple `DataSource` | One implementation only |
| Keep qualifier orthogonal | `@ReadReplicaDatabase` | `@FastAndNewAndBlue` |

### Qualifier examples

| Need | Qualifier |
|---|---|
| Primary DB vs read replica | `@PrimaryDatabase`, `@ReadReplicaDatabase` |
| Internal vs external HTTP client | `@InternalClient`, `@ExternalClient` |
| Payment service client | `@PaymentClient` |
| Audit publisher type | `@SyncAudit`, `@AsyncAudit` |
| Crypto key material | `@JwtVerificationKey`, `@MtlsClientKey` |
| Runtime clock | `@SystemClock`, `@TestClock` |

---

## 9. Qualifier with Member Values

Kadang qualifier butuh parameter.

```java id="7ptfbj"
@Qualifier
@Retention(RUNTIME)
@Target({FIELD, PARAMETER, METHOD, TYPE})
public @interface Downstream {
    String value();
}
```

```java id="q5qmie"
@Produces
@Downstream("customer")
@ApplicationScoped
public Client customerClient(AppConfig config) {
    return HttpClientFactory.create(config.customerService());
}
```

```java id="bggsg6"
@Produces
@Downstream("payment")
@ApplicationScoped
public Client paymentClient(AppConfig config) {
    return HttpClientFactory.create(config.paymentService());
}
```

```java id="e2l8qv"
@Inject
public CustomerGateway(@Downstream("customer") Client client) {
    this.client = client;
}
```

### Caution

Qualifier dengan string value tetap bisa typo. Untuk dependency critical, beberapa tim lebih memilih annotation terpisah agar refactor lebih aman.

---

## 10. Alternatives

Alternative dipakai untuk mengganti implementation secara eksplisit.

```java id="s8o2u7"
public interface AuditPublisher {
    void publish(AuditEvent event);
}
```

### Production implementation

```java id="6ewnyz"
@ApplicationScoped
public class KafkaAuditPublisher implements AuditPublisher {

    @Override
    public void publish(AuditEvent event) {
        // publish to Kafka
    }
}
```

### Test alternative

```java id="k7n7ll"
@Alternative
@Priority(1)
@ApplicationScoped
public class InMemoryAuditPublisher implements AuditPublisher {

    private final List<AuditEvent> events = new CopyOnWriteArrayList<>();

    @Override
    public void publish(AuditEvent event) {
        events.add(event);
    }

    public List<AuditEvent> events() {
        return List.copyOf(events);
    }
}
```

`@Alternative` bagus untuk test double, tapi jangan membuat production behavior bergantung pada alternative yang aktif secara “ajaib”. Gunakan aktivasi eksplisit dan test.

---

## 11. Alternatives vs Qualifiers vs Producer

| Need | Best Tool |
|---|---|
| Ada dua dependency valid bersamaan | Qualifier |
| Satu dependency diganti untuk test | Alternative |
| Object butuh custom creation | Producer |
| Runtime memilih concrete implementation berdasarkan config | Producer atau selected alternative |
| Butuh fake repository di test | Alternative |
| Butuh primary + replica DB | Qualifier |
| Butuh DataSource dari config | Producer |
| Butuh mock per test method | Weld test extension / synthetic bean / alternative |

### Anti-pattern

```java id="7gg5k5"
// BAD: service chooses implementation manually
if (profile.equals("test")) {
    this.publisher = new InMemoryAuditPublisher();
} else {
    this.publisher = new KafkaAuditPublisher();
}
```

Better:

```java id="idwjx3"
@Inject
public OrderService(AuditPublisher auditPublisher) {
    this.auditPublisher = auditPublisher;
}
```

Lalu pilih implementation di composition/test layer.

---

## 12. Producer + Qualifier for Secret Material

```java id="nozz8t"
@Qualifier
@Retention(RUNTIME)
@Target({FIELD, PARAMETER, METHOD})
public @interface JwtVerificationKey {}
```

```java id="5fxwkx"
@ApplicationScoped
public class KeyMaterialProducer {

    @Produces
    @JwtVerificationKey
    @ApplicationScoped
    public PublicKey jwtVerificationKey(AppConfig config) {
        Path keyPath = config.security().jwtVerificationKeyPath();

        String pem = ExternalFileLoader.readUtf8(keyPath, 64 * 1024);

        return PemKeyParser.parsePublicKey(pem);
    }
}
```

```java id="3wkooh"
@ApplicationScoped
public class JwtTokenVerifier implements TokenVerifier {

    private final PublicKey verificationKey;

    @Inject
    public JwtTokenVerifier(@JwtVerificationKey PublicKey verificationKey) {
        this.verificationKey = verificationKey;
    }
}
```

### Rule

```text id="5hrqe2"
Inject parsed key material.
Do not let every verifier/client read files independently.
```

---

## 13. Producer Lifecycle and Cleanup

Gunakan disposer untuk resource yang perlu cleanup.

```java id="63m1km"
@ApplicationScoped
public class ExternalClientProducer {

    @Produces
    @ApplicationScoped
    public CloseableHttpClient closeableHttpClient(AppConfig config) {
        return HttpClients.custom()
            .setDefaultRequestConfig(RequestConfig.custom()
                .setConnectTimeout(Timeout.ofMilliseconds(
                    config.httpClient().connectTimeout().toMillis()
                ))
                .setResponseTimeout(Timeout.ofMilliseconds(
                    config.httpClient().readTimeout().toMillis()
                ))
                .build())
            .build();
    }

    public void dispose(@Disposes CloseableHttpClient client) throws IOException {
        client.close();
    }
}
```

### Lifecycle checklist

| Object | Scope | Cleanup |
|---|---|---|
| `AppConfig` | `@ApplicationScoped` | No |
| `DataSource` | `@ApplicationScoped` | Yes |
| HTTP client | `@ApplicationScoped` | Yes |
| ObjectMapper | `@ApplicationScoped` | No |
| Token verifier | `@ApplicationScoped` | Usually no |
| Request context | `@RequestScoped` or explicit param | End of request |
| Temporary helper | `@Dependent` | Follows owner |

---

## 14. Scope Pitfalls

| Pitfall | Example | Fix |
|---|---|---|
| `@ApplicationScoped` holds request state | Field `currentTenantId` | Pass context explicitly |
| `@RequestScoped` injected into async task | Worker thread accesses request bean | Copy immutable context |
| Producer returns mutable singleton config | Config modified by service | Use immutable records |
| `@Dependent` resource not closed | Produced closeable object without disposer | Add disposer or app scope |
| Multiple producers same type no qualifier | Ambiguous dependency | Add qualifier |
| Alternative active in production accidentally | `@Priority` test fake on main classpath | Separate test source/profile |
| Request-scoped producer used at startup | Context inactive | Use application-scoped boot resources |

---

## 15. CDI Events - When Useful

CDI events bisa berguna untuk decoupling internal application events.

```java id="12qvlw"
public record OrderApprovedEvent(
    String orderId,
    String tenantId,
    String actorId,
    String correlationId,
    Instant occurredAt
) {}
```

```java id="in4ijs"
@ApplicationScoped
public class OrderService {

    private final Event<OrderApprovedEvent> event;

    @Inject
    public OrderService(Event<OrderApprovedEvent> event) {
        this.event = event;
    }

    public void approve(ApproveOrderCommand command) {
        // domain operation
        event.fire(new OrderApprovedEvent(
            command.orderId(),
            command.tenantId(),
            command.actorId(),
            command.correlationId(),
            Instant.now()
        ));
    }
}
```

```java id="dxbz9u"
@ApplicationScoped
public class AuditObserver {

    public void onOrderApproved(@Observes OrderApprovedEvent event) {
        // write audit or publish outbox
    }
}
```

### Caution

| Jangan pakai CDI event untuk | Kenapa |
|---|---|
| Cross-service messaging | CDI event in-process only |
| Critical durable integration | Process crash loses event unless persisted |
| Hidden business flow | Sulit trace jika berlebihan |
| Replacing explicit use-case logic | Mengaburkan control flow |

Untuk audit/regulatory, sering lebih aman memakai explicit `AuditPublisher` atau outbox, bukan hanya in-memory CDI event.

---

## 16. Weld Testing Strategy

Weld Testing Extensions menyediakan tool cepat untuk CDI unit/component testing, termasuk JUnit 4, JUnit 5, dan Spock extensions. Dokumentasi project juga mencatat perubahan versi: extension 6.x mendukung Weld 7.0/CDI 5.0, dan artifact JUnit 5 direnamai dari `weld-junit5` menjadi `weld-junit-jupiter`; untuk stack CDI 4.1/Weld 6, pilih versi yang kompatibel dengan runtime Anda, bukan sekadar versi terbaru. ([GitHub](https://github.com/weld/weld-testing?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

### Test pyramid CDI

```text id="tajc35"
CDI Tests
│
├─ Plain unit test
│  └─ no container, constructor injection manually
│
├─ Weld component test
│  └─ boot selected CDI beans
│
├─ Jersey + CDI test
│  └─ resource/filter/provider integration
│
└─ Full integration test
   └─ app + DB + external test doubles
```

---

## 17. Weld JUnit Test: Basic Producer Resolution

Contoh konseptual untuk mengetes producer dan injection.

```java id="ni48ys"
@EnableWeld
class RuntimeConfigProducerTest {

    @WeldSetup
    WeldInitiator weld = WeldInitiator
        .from(RuntimeConfigProducer.class, OrderService.class)
        .build();

    @Inject
    AppConfig appConfig;

    @Inject
    OrderService orderService;

    @Test
    void appConfigMustBeProduced() {
        assertNotNull(appConfig);
        assertNotNull(orderService);
    }
}
```

### Test target

| Test | Membuktikan |
|---|---|
| `AppConfig` injectable | Producer aktif |
| `OrderService` injectable | Constructor dependency resolvable |
| Config validation fails on bad config | Fail-fast |
| No duplicate config instance | Producer lifecycle benar |
| Secret masked | Safe logging |

---

## 18. Weld Test: Qualifier Resolution

```java id="ddads0"
@EnableWeld
class DatabaseProducerTest {

    @WeldSetup
    WeldInitiator weld = WeldInitiator
        .from(
            TestAppConfigProducer.class,
            DatabaseProducer.class,
            OrderRepositoryPostgres.class
        )
        .build();

    @Inject
    @PrimaryDatabase
    DataSource primary;

    @Inject
    @ReadReplicaDatabase
    DataSource replica;

    @Inject
    OrderRepositoryPostgres repository;

    @Test
    void primaryAndReplicaMustResolveDifferentBeans() {
        assertNotNull(primary);
        assertNotNull(replica);
        assertNotSame(primary, replica);
        assertNotNull(repository);
    }
}
```

### Apa yang dicegah?

```text id="plm2yx"
- ambiguous DataSource
- wrong qualifier
- missing producer
- repository accidentally using primary for reads
- test passing without CDI container
```

---

## 19. Weld Test: Alternative

```java id="l6gmle"
@Alternative
@Priority(1)
@ApplicationScoped
public class FakeAuditPublisher implements AuditPublisher {

    private final List<AuditEvent> events = new CopyOnWriteArrayList<>();

    @Override
    public void publish(AuditEvent event) {
        events.add(event);
    }

    public List<AuditEvent> events() {
        return List.copyOf(events);
    }
}
```

```java id="x8djgl"
@EnableWeld
class OrderServiceAuditTest {

    @WeldSetup
    WeldInitiator weld = WeldInitiator
        .from(
            OrderService.class,
            FakeOrderRepository.class,
            FakeAuditPublisher.class,
            OrderAccessPolicy.class
        )
        .build();

    @Inject
    OrderService orderService;

    @Inject
    AuditPublisher auditPublisher;

    @Test
    void orderApprovalMustPublishAuditEvent() {
        orderService.approve(command());

        FakeAuditPublisher fake = (FakeAuditPublisher) auditPublisher;

        assertTrue(fake.events().stream()
            .anyMatch(event -> event.action().equals("ORDER_APPROVED")));
    }
}
```

### Alternative test rules

| Rule | Reason |
|---|---|
| Test fake berada di `src/test/java` | Tidak masuk production artifact |
| Alternative aktif eksplisit | Tidak magic |
| Test assert fake benar-benar dipakai | Mencegah real publisher terpanggil |
| Jangan aktifkan fake via production profile | Risiko deployment |

---

## 20. Weld Test: Interceptor Firing

```java id="ab7so3"
@InterceptorBinding
@Retention(RUNTIME)
@Target({TYPE, METHOD})
public @interface Audited {
    String action();
}
```

```java id="xli73m"
@Audited(action = "APPROVE_ORDER")
@ApplicationScoped
public class OrderApprovalFacade {

    public void approve(ApproveOrderCommand command) {
        // approve
    }
}
```

```java id="do4ic3"
@Interceptor
@Audited(action = "")
@Priority(Interceptor.Priority.APPLICATION)
public class AuditInterceptor {

    @Inject
    AuditSink auditSink;

    @AroundInvoke
    public Object around(InvocationContext context) throws Exception {
        Object result = context.proceed();

        Audited audited = context.getMethod().getAnnotation(Audited.class);
        auditSink.record(audited.action());

        return result;
    }
}
```

```java id="u9ih2r"
@Test
void auditInterceptorMustFire() {
    facade.approve(command());

    assertTrue(auditSink.actions().contains("APPROVE_ORDER"));
}
```

### Interceptor pitfall

| Pitfall | Fix |
|---|---|
| Method dipanggil via `this.method()` | Panggil lewat CDI proxy / facade |
| Target dibuat manual `new` | Biarkan CDI create bean |
| Annotation missing `@InterceptorBinding` | Tambahkan binding |
| Retention bukan runtime | `@Retention(RUNTIME)` |
| Interceptor tidak enabled | `@Priority` atau config |

---

## 21. Producer Test Doubles

Kadang alternative class tidak cukup. Gunakan test producer.

```java id="3531sz"
@ApplicationScoped
public class TestClockProducer {

    @Produces
    @ApplicationScoped
    public Clock fixedClock() {
        return Clock.fixed(
            Instant.parse("2026-07-13T00:00:00Z"),
            ZoneOffset.UTC
        );
    }
}
```

```java id="cl90x5"
@ApplicationScoped
public class OrderNumberGenerator {

    private final Clock clock;

    @Inject
    public OrderNumberGenerator(Clock clock) {
        this.clock = clock;
    }

    public String generate() {
        return "ORD-" + LocalDate.now(clock).format(DateTimeFormatter.BASIC_ISO_DATE);
    }
}
```

### Test

```java id="avv76i"
@Test
void orderNumberMustUseInjectedClock() {
    String value = generator.generate();

    assertEquals("ORD-20260713", value);
}
```

### Use cases

| Test producer | Use |
|---|---|
| Fixed `Clock` | Deterministic temporal test |
| Fake `DataSource` | Repository component test |
| In-memory audit sink | Audit assertion |
| Stub downstream client | Service test |
| Test `AppConfig` | Profile/config invariant test |

---

## 22. CDI + Jersey Integration Boundary

Untuk aplikasi Jersey/JAX-RS, CDI test saja belum cukup. Pastikan resource/provider bisa memakai CDI-managed service.

```text id="9qwsg4"
Weld component test proves:
  CDI graph works.

Jersey test proves:
  ResourceConfig/provider/filter/mapper registration works.

Jersey + CDI test proves:
  HTTP boundary can reach CDI graph.
```

### Minimum integration smoke cases

| Case | Expected |
|---|---|
| Resource injects CDI service | Endpoint returns 200 |
| Auth filter injects token verifier | Missing token returns 401 |
| Exception mapper injects config/audit if needed | Error maps correctly |
| CDI interceptor fires from resource call | Audit recorded |
| Request context does not leak | Concurrent request test |

---

## 23. Common Failure Modes

| Symptom | Root Cause | Fix |
|---|---|---|
| `UnsatisfiedResolutionException` | No bean/producer found | Add bean scope or producer |
| `AmbiguousResolutionException` | Multiple beans same type | Add qualifier |
| Fake publisher used in prod | Test alternative on main classpath | Move to test source |
| `DataSource` not closed | No disposer | Add `@Disposes` |
| Config loaded twice | Multiple producer instances/loaders | Single bootstrap producer |
| Request state leaks | App-scoped bean stores request state | Pass context explicitly |
| Interceptor not firing | Manual object/self-invocation | CDI-managed facade |
| Producer throws late | Lazy initialization | Validate during bootstrap/test |
| Test passes without CDI | Manual construction bypasses wiring | Add Weld component test |
| Jersey resource cannot inject CDI bean | Integration/bridge missing | Add Jersey+CDI integration test |

---

## 24. Production Checklist

| Area | Checklist |
|---|---|
| Producers | Only used for object needing custom creation |
| Config | Produced once, immutable, validated |
| DataSource/client | Produced app-scoped and disposed cleanly |
| Qualifiers | Used for multiple implementations/resources |
| Alternatives | Test-only alternatives separated from production |
| Scope | No request data stored in application-scoped bean |
| Lifecycle | Closeable resources have disposer |
| Interceptor | CDI interceptor activation tested |
| Events | CDI events not used as durable messaging |
| Testing | Weld tests cover producers, qualifiers, alternatives |
| Jersey integration | Resource/filter/mapper can access CDI graph |
| Dependencies | CDI/Weld test extension version aligned with CDI/Weld runtime |
| CI | CDI wiring tests block merge |

---

## 25. Roadmap Belajar Singkat

| Urutan | Fokus | Target Praktik |
|---:|---|---|
| 1 | Producer basics | Bisa expose `AppConfig`, `DataSource`, HTTP client ke CDI |
| 2 | Disposer | Bisa cleanup resource saat shutdown |
| 3 | Qualifier | Bisa resolve multiple implementations secara type-safe |
| 4 | Alternative | Bisa mengganti dependency untuk test tanpa ubah service |
| 5 | Scope correctness | Bisa menghindari request state leak |
| 6 | Interceptor testing | Bisa memastikan AOP CDI benar-benar aktif |
| 7 | Weld component test | Bisa boot CDI graph di unit/component test |
| 8 | Jersey + CDI integration | Bisa membuktikan HTTP boundary memakai CDI graph |

7/13/2026, 6:16:58 AM

Saya lanjut ke HK2 cookbook: binder, factory, scope, request context factory, provider registration, dan failure pattern yang paling sering muncul di Jersey standalone.

# Cheatsheet - HK2 Binder / Factory Cookbook  
## AbstractBinder, Factory/Supplier, Scopes, Request Context Factory, Singleton Safety, Provider Injection, Diagnostics

## 1. Scope

Seri ini fokus pada **HK2 sebagai dependency injection layer bawaan Jersey**, terutama untuk aplikasi Jersey/JAX-RS standalone atau aplikasi yang masih HK2-heavy.

```text id="v7v3hk"
HK2 Cookbook
│
├─ Binder
│  ├─ bind implementation to interface
│  ├─ bind instance
│  ├─ bind factory / supplier
│  └─ named binding
│
├─ Scope
│  ├─ singleton
│  ├─ per lookup
│  ├─ request scoped
│  └─ custom lifecycle caution
│
├─ Factory Pattern
│  ├─ AppConfig factory
│  ├─ DataSource factory
│  ├─ RequestContext factory
│  ├─ TokenVerifier factory
│  └─ external client factory
│
├─ Jersey Boundary
│  ├─ ResourceConfig
│  ├─ resources
│  ├─ filters
│  ├─ mappers
│  └─ providers
│
└─ Diagnostics
   ├─ missing binder
   ├─ wrong scope
   ├─ duplicate binding
   ├─ request context leak
   └─ Jersey 3/4 package migration
```

Jersey documentation menjelaskan custom injection/lifecycle management sebagai skenario umum DI di Jersey, dengan pola: implement provider/factory/supplier, bind melalui `AbstractBinder`, lalu register binder ke `ResourceConfig`. ([Eclipse EE4J](https://eclipse-ee4j.github.io/jersey.github.io/documentation/latest31x/ioc.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Catatan versi penting: Jersey 4.0.x adalah jalur Jakarta REST 4.0/Jakarta EE 11, dan roadmap Jersey mencatat perubahan besar bahwa class injection-related seperti `AbstractBinder` dipindahkan ke package/module terkait HK2; jadi import/artifact harus dicek sesuai versi project. ([GitHub](https://github.com/eclipse-ee4j/jersey/wiki/Road-Map?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 2. Mental Model

```text id="1zzssd"
ResourceConfig
  = composition root Jersey

AbstractBinder
  = deklarasi object graph HK2

Factory / Supplier
  = cara membuat object yang butuh runtime config/context

Scope
  = lifecycle object

Provider / Filter / Mapper
  = HTTP boundary extension

Service / Repository / Policy
  = application dependencies
```

Rule utama:

```text id="qylfrr"
HK2 bagus untuk wiring Jersey boundary.
Jangan biarkan HK2 menjadi tempat menyimpan request state global.
Jangan simpan mutable request/user/tenant context di singleton.
```

---

## 3. Basic Binder Pattern

```java id="iwlmkn"
public final class AppResourceConfig extends ResourceConfig {

    public AppResourceConfig() {
        RuntimeResources resources = RuntimeBootstrap.loadAndValidate();

        register(new AppBinder(resources));

        register(OrderResource.class);
        register(AuthenticationFilter.class);
        register(DomainExceptionMapper.class);
    }
}
```

```java id="d5l4zl"
public final class AppBinder extends AbstractBinder {

    private final RuntimeResources resources;

    public AppBinder(RuntimeResources resources) {
        this.resources = resources;
    }

    @Override
    protected void configure() {
        bind(resources.appConfig()).to(AppConfig.class);
        bind(resources.policyConfig()).to(PolicyConfig.class);

        bind(OrderServiceImpl.class)
            .to(OrderService.class)
            .in(Singleton.class);

        bind(OrderRepositoryPostgres.class)
            .to(OrderRepository.class)
            .in(Singleton.class);
    }
}
```

### Binder responsibility

| Responsibility | Example |
|---|---|
| Bind config object | `bind(appConfig).to(AppConfig.class)` |
| Bind service interface | `bind(OrderServiceImpl.class).to(OrderService.class)` |
| Bind repository interface | `bind(OrderRepositoryPostgres.class).to(OrderRepository.class)` |
| Bind factory-created object | `bindFactory(DataSourceFactory.class).to(DataSource.class)` |
| Bind request context | `bindFactory(RequestContextFactory.class).to(RequestContext.class)` |
| Define lifecycle | `.in(Singleton.class)` / request / per lookup |

---

## 4. Basic Service Binding

```java id="yrzgnu"
public interface OrderService {
    OrderDto getOrder(String orderId, RequestContext context);
}
```

```java id="dp7bpy"
public final class OrderServiceImpl implements OrderService {

    private final OrderRepository repository;
    private final OrderAccessPolicy policy;
    private final AuditPublisher auditPublisher;

    @Inject
    public OrderServiceImpl(
        OrderRepository repository,
        OrderAccessPolicy policy,
        AuditPublisher auditPublisher
    ) {
        this.repository = repository;
        this.policy = policy;
        this.auditPublisher = auditPublisher;
    }

    @Override
    public OrderDto getOrder(String orderId, RequestContext context) {
        Order order = repository.findByIdAndTenant(orderId, context.tenantId())
            .orElseThrow(() -> new OrderNotFoundException(orderId));

        policy.requireCanRead(context.principal(), order);

        auditPublisher.publish(AuditEvent.read(order, context));

        return OrderDto.from(order);
    }
}
```

```java id="c96w38"
@Override
protected void configure() {
    bind(OrderServiceImpl.class)
        .to(OrderService.class)
        .in(Singleton.class);

    bind(OrderRepositoryPostgres.class)
        .to(OrderRepository.class)
        .in(Singleton.class);

    bind(OrderAccessPolicy.class)
        .to(OrderAccessPolicy.class)
        .in(Singleton.class);

    bind(KafkaAuditPublisher.class)
        .to(AuditPublisher.class)
        .in(Singleton.class);
}
```

### Rule

| Rule | Reason |
|---|---|
| Bind by interface untuk service/repository | Testability dan replaceability |
| Constructor injection | Dependency eksplisit |
| Singleton hanya untuk stateless/thread-safe object | Service/repo biasanya aman jika tidak simpan request state |
| Jangan `new` dependency di service | Bypass DI |
| Jangan inject raw HTTP context ke service | Service jadi sulit dites dan rawan leak |

---

## 5. Scope Cookbook

HK2/Jersey custom injection docs menyebut default scope untuk Jersey binder mirip HK2 yaitu `PerLookup`, sehingga scope perlu dibuat eksplisit untuk object penting. ([Eclipse EE4J](https://eclipse-ee4j.github.io/jersey.github.io/documentation/latest31x/ioc.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

| Scope | Kapan Dipakai | Contoh | Risiko |
|---|---|---|---|
| Singleton | Stateless, thread-safe, mahal dibuat | service, repository, config, client pool | Leak jika simpan request state |
| PerLookup | Object ringan per injection/lookup | helper stateless kecil | Bisa banyak instance |
| Request scoped | Object berlaku per HTTP request | `RequestContext` | Context inactive jika dipakai di luar request |
| Manual instance binding | Object sudah dibuat saat bootstrap | `AppConfig`, `PolicyConfig` | Lifecycle manual |
| Factory-created singleton | Object perlu config saat dibuat | `DataSource`, HTTP client | Cleanup harus jelas |

### Singleton-safe object

```java id="ibv2ys"
public final class OrderAccessPolicy {

    public AuthorizationDecision canRead(
        PrincipalContext principal,
        Order order
    ) {
        // no mutable request state stored
    }
}
```

### Singleton-dangerous object

```java id="8x8e7l"
// BAD
public final class CurrentUserHolder {

    private String currentUserId;

    public void setCurrentUserId(String currentUserId) {
        this.currentUserId = currentUserId;
    }

    public String currentUserId() {
        return currentUserId;
    }
}
```

---

## 6. Bind Existing Instance

Untuk immutable config yang sudah dibuat saat bootstrap:

```java id="b08oxo"
RuntimeResources resources = RuntimeBootstrap.loadAndValidate();

register(new AbstractBinder() {
    @Override
    protected void configure() {
        bind(resources.appConfig()).to(AppConfig.class);
        bind(resources.policyConfig()).to(PolicyConfig.class);
        bind(resources.tlsConfig()).to(TlsConfig.class);
    }
});
```

### Cocok untuk

| Object | Reason |
|---|---|
| `AppConfig` | Immutable, validated once |
| `PolicyConfig` | Versioned, immutable |
| `TlsConfig` | Runtime material metadata |
| `RuntimeProfile` | Enum/simple value |
| `BuildInfo` | Static deployment metadata |

### Jangan untuk

| Object | Reason |
|---|---|
| Request context | Berbeda per request |
| Mutable accumulator | Cross-request leak |
| Raw secret as `String` | Bisa bocor via logs/toString |
| Closeable pool tanpa lifecycle owner | Cleanup tidak jelas |

---

## 7. Factory Pattern: DataSource

```java id="zkq4cf"
public final class DataSourceFactory implements Factory<DataSource> {

    private final AppConfig config;

    @Inject
    public DataSourceFactory(AppConfig config) {
        this.config = config;
    }

    @Override
    public DataSource provide() {
        HikariConfig hikari = new HikariConfig();

        hikari.setJdbcUrl(config.database().url());
        hikari.setUsername(config.database().username());
        hikari.setPassword(config.database().password().reveal());
        hikari.setMaximumPoolSize(config.database().maxPoolSize());

        return new HikariDataSource(hikari);
    }

    @Override
    public void dispose(DataSource dataSource) {
        if (dataSource instanceof HikariDataSource hikari) {
            hikari.close();
        }
    }
}
```

```java id="8ojchu"
@Override
protected void configure() {
    bindFactory(DataSourceFactory.class)
        .to(DataSource.class)
        .in(Singleton.class);
}
```

### Factory rules

| Rule | Reason |
|---|---|
| Factory boleh inject config | Factory HK2-managed |
| Factory should validate enough | Fail early |
| Singleton untuk pool/client | Jangan buat pool per request |
| `dispose` untuk closeable object | Cleanup |
| Jangan log password/token | Secret safety |

---

## 8. Factory Pattern: HTTP Client

```java id="2v01tc"
public final class JerseyClientFactory implements Factory<Client> {

    private final AppConfig config;

    @Inject
    public JerseyClientFactory(AppConfig config) {
        this.config = config;
    }

    @Override
    public Client provide() {
        ClientConfig clientConfig = new ClientConfig();

        clientConfig.property(
            ClientProperties.CONNECT_TIMEOUT,
            Math.toIntExact(config.httpClient().connectTimeout().toMillis())
        );

        clientConfig.property(
            ClientProperties.READ_TIMEOUT,
            Math.toIntExact(config.httpClient().readTimeout().toMillis())
        );

        return ClientBuilder.newClient(clientConfig)
            .register(CorrelationClientRequestFilter.class);
    }

    @Override
    public void dispose(Client client) {
        client.close();
    }
}
```

```java id="0na9zf"
@Override
protected void configure() {
    bindFactory(JerseyClientFactory.class)
        .to(Client.class)
        .in(Singleton.class);
}
```

### Jangan

```java id="l0abhf"
// BAD: membuat Client baru per method call
public CustomerDto getCustomer(String id) {
    Client client = ClientBuilder.newClient();
    return client.target(baseUrl).path(id).request().get(CustomerDto.class);
}
```

Better: inject singleton client atau typed downstream gateway.

---

## 9. RequestContext Factory

Request context adalah contoh paling penting untuk scope.

```java id="3ckk6w"
public record RequestContext(
    String requestId,
    String correlationId,
    String tenantId,
    PrincipalContext principal,
    Instant startedAt
) {}
```

Factory-nya bisa membaca property yang disiapkan oleh filter.

```java id="89vr6n"
public final class RequestContextFactory implements Factory<RequestContext> {

    private final Provider<ContainerRequestContext> requestContextProvider;

    @Inject
    public RequestContextFactory(
        Provider<ContainerRequestContext> requestContextProvider
    ) {
        this.requestContextProvider = requestContextProvider;
    }

    @Override
    public RequestContext provide() {
        ContainerRequestContext raw = requestContextProvider.get();

        RequestContext context =
            (RequestContext) raw.getProperty("requestContext");

        if (context == null) {
            throw new IllegalStateException(
                "RequestContext has not been initialized by filter"
            );
        }

        return context;
    }

    @Override
    public void dispose(RequestContext instance) {
        // no-op, immutable context
    }
}
```

```java id="uwj8hj"
@Override
protected void configure() {
    bindFactory(RequestContextFactory.class)
        .to(RequestContext.class)
        .in(RequestScoped.class);
}
```

### Request filter prepares context

```java id="jvo9xt"
@Provider
@Priority(Priorities.AUTHENTICATION - 100)
public final class RequestContextInitializationFilter
    implements ContainerRequestFilter {

    @Override
    public void filter(ContainerRequestContext request) {
        String correlationId = firstNonBlank(
            request.getHeaderString("X-Correlation-Id"),
            UUID.randomUUID().toString()
        );

        String tenantId = request.getHeaderString("X-Tenant-Id");

        RequestContext context = new RequestContext(
            UUID.randomUUID().toString(),
            correlationId,
            tenantId,
            PrincipalContext.anonymous(),
            Instant.now()
        );

        request.setProperty("requestContext", context);
        MDC.put("correlationId", correlationId);
    }
}
```

### Caution

| Pitfall | Fix |
|---|---|
| Factory dipanggil sebelum filter set property | Prioritize filter early |
| Context stored in singleton | Pass explicitly or request scope |
| Async uses request-scoped context after request | Copy immutable context |
| Missing cleanup MDC | Clear in response filter/finally |

---

## 10. Authenticated Principal Binding

Biasanya principal dibuat oleh authentication filter setelah token diverifikasi.

```java id="da2lvj"
public record PrincipalContext(
    String subject,
    Set<String> roles,
    Set<String> permissions,
    Set<String> tenantIds
) {
    public static PrincipalContext anonymous() {
        return new PrincipalContext(
            "anonymous",
            Set.of(),
            Set.of(),
            Set.of()
        );
    }

    public boolean hasPermission(String permission) {
        return permissions.contains(permission);
    }
}
```

```java id="kni0me"
@Provider
@Priority(Priorities.AUTHENTICATION)
public final class AuthenticationFilter implements ContainerRequestFilter {

    private final TokenVerifier tokenVerifier;

    @Inject
    public AuthenticationFilter(TokenVerifier tokenVerifier) {
        this.tokenVerifier = tokenVerifier;
    }

    @Override
    public void filter(ContainerRequestContext request) {
        String authorization = request.getHeaderString(HttpHeaders.AUTHORIZATION);

        if (authorization == null || !authorization.startsWith("Bearer ")) {
            throw new AuthenticationRequiredException();
        }

        TokenClaims claims = tokenVerifier.verify(authorization.substring("Bearer ".length()));

        RequestContext oldContext =
            (RequestContext) request.getProperty("requestContext");

        RequestContext authenticatedContext = new RequestContext(
            oldContext.requestId(),
            oldContext.correlationId(),
            oldContext.tenantId(),
            claims.toPrincipal(),
            oldContext.startedAt()
        );

        request.setProperty("requestContext", authenticatedContext);
    }
}
```

### Binding TokenVerifier

```java id="27xgmk"
@Override
protected void configure() {
    bind(JwtTokenVerifier.class)
        .to(TokenVerifier.class)
        .in(Singleton.class);
}
```

---

## 11. Factory Pattern: TokenVerifier with Key Material

```java id="ojjw9m"
public final class JwtTokenVerifierFactory implements Factory<TokenVerifier> {

    private final AppConfig config;

    @Inject
    public JwtTokenVerifierFactory(AppConfig config) {
        this.config = config;
    }

    @Override
    public TokenVerifier provide() {
        PublicKey verificationKey = PemKeyParser.parsePublicKey(
            ExternalFileLoader.readUtf8(
                config.security().jwtVerificationKeyPath(),
                64 * 1024
            )
        );

        return new JwtTokenVerifier(
            verificationKey,
            config.security().issuer(),
            config.security().audience()
        );
    }

    @Override
    public void dispose(TokenVerifier instance) {
        // no-op
    }
}
```

```java id="hco3db"
@Override
protected void configure() {
    bindFactory(JwtTokenVerifierFactory.class)
        .to(TokenVerifier.class)
        .in(Singleton.class);
}
```

Rule: key material dibaca/parse sekali saat startup, bukan setiap request.

---

## 12. Multiple Implementation Binding

HK2 bisa memakai named binding untuk membedakan implementation.

```java id="2xe1li"
@Override
protected void configure() {
    bind(PrimaryOrderRepository.class)
        .to(OrderRepository.class)
        .named("primary")
        .in(Singleton.class);

    bind(ReadReplicaOrderRepository.class)
        .to(OrderRepository.class)
        .named("replica")
        .in(Singleton.class);
}
```

Injection dengan name:

```java id="s2j13s"
public final class OrderQueryService {

    private final OrderRepository replicaRepository;

    @Inject
    public OrderQueryService(@Named("replica") OrderRepository replicaRepository) {
        this.replicaRepository = replicaRepository;
    }
}
```

### Caution

| Issue | Recommendation |
|---|---|
| String name typo | Centralize constants or prefer separate interface |
| Too many named variants | Consider CDI qualifier if moving to CDI |
| Primary/replica behavior hidden | Encode in class/service names |
| Wrong repo injected | Add integration test |

Alternative untuk Java-only type safety:

```java id="4ptydp"
public interface PrimaryOrderRepository extends OrderRepository {}
public interface ReplicaOrderRepository extends OrderRepository {}
```

---

## 13. Bind Factory by Contract

Jika factory sendiri butuh dependency:

```java id="ebz52f"
public final class AuditPublisherFactory implements Factory<AuditPublisher> {

    private final AppConfig config;
    private final Client client;

    @Inject
    public AuditPublisherFactory(AppConfig config, Client client) {
        this.config = config;
        this.client = client;
    }

    @Override
    public AuditPublisher provide() {
        if (config.audit().mode() == AuditMode.IN_MEMORY) {
            return new InMemoryAuditPublisher();
        }

        if (config.audit().mode() == AuditMode.HTTP) {
            return new HttpAuditPublisher(client, config.audit().endpoint());
        }

        throw new IllegalStateException("Unsupported audit mode");
    }

    @Override
    public void dispose(AuditPublisher instance) {
        if (instance instanceof AutoCloseable closeable) {
            try {
                closeable.close();
            } catch (Exception e) {
                throw new IllegalStateException("Failed to close AuditPublisher", e);
            }
        }
    }
}
```

```java id="8xg9or"
@Override
protected void configure() {
    bindFactory(AuditPublisherFactory.class)
        .to(AuditPublisher.class)
        .in(Singleton.class);
}
```

### Good use

| Use Case | Kenapa |
|---|---|
| Implementation dipilih dari config | Factory centralizes runtime decision |
| Object butuh multiple infra deps | Factory injected by HK2 |
| Closeable implementation | dispose |
| Production vs local mode | Runtime profile decision |

### Caution

Jangan jadikan factory sebagai service locator besar yang tahu semua object. Kalau factory makin panjang, pecah menjadi producer/factory spesifik.

---

## 14. Provider Injection

Filter, mapper, dan resource bisa menerima dependency dari HK2 jika class registered dan dependency bound.

```java id="j05xkr"
@Provider
public final class DomainExceptionMapper
    implements ExceptionMapper<DomainException> {

    private final ErrorResponseFactory errorResponseFactory;
    private final AuditPublisher auditPublisher;

    @Inject
    public DomainExceptionMapper(
        ErrorResponseFactory errorResponseFactory,
        AuditPublisher auditPublisher
    ) {
        this.errorResponseFactory = errorResponseFactory;
        this.auditPublisher = auditPublisher;
    }

    @Override
    public Response toResponse(DomainException exception) {
        ErrorResponse response =
            errorResponseFactory.from(exception, Correlation.currentId());

        if (exception.auditRelevant()) {
            auditPublisher.publish(AuditEvent.error(exception));
        }

        return Response.status(response.status())
            .entity(response)
            .type(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
            .build();
    }
}
```

```java id="hcxnkl"
@Override
protected void configure() {
    bind(ErrorResponseFactory.class)
        .to(ErrorResponseFactory.class)
        .in(Singleton.class);

    bindFactory(AuditPublisherFactory.class)
        .to(AuditPublisher.class)
        .in(Singleton.class);
}
```

### Provider registration rule

```java id="d2wxcc"
// Good
register(DomainExceptionMapper.class);

// Risky if mapper needs DI
register(new DomainExceptionMapper(...));
```

Register class agar lifecycle/injection dikelola container.

---

## 15. Resource Injection

```java id="wwv8td"
@Path("/orders")
@Produces(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
@Consumes(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
public final class OrderResource {

    private final OrderService orderService;
    private final OrderCommandMapper commandMapper;

    @Inject
    public OrderResource(
        OrderService orderService,
        OrderCommandMapper commandMapper
    ) {
        this.orderService = orderService;
        this.commandMapper = commandMapper;
    }

    @POST
    public Response create(
        @Valid CreateOrderRequest request,
        @Context ContainerRequestContext rawContext
    ) {
        RequestContext context =
            (RequestContext) rawContext.getProperty("requestContext");

        CreateOrderCommand command = commandMapper.toCommand(request, context);

        OrderDto result = orderService.create(command, context);

        return Response.status(Response.Status.CREATED)
            .entity(result)
            .build();
    }
}
```

```java id="2tj1m4"
@Override
protected void configure() {
    bind(OrderCommandMapper.class)
        .to(OrderCommandMapper.class)
        .in(Singleton.class);

    bind(OrderServiceImpl.class)
        .to(OrderService.class)
        .in(Singleton.class);
}
```

### Resource rule

| Rule | Reason |
|---|---|
| Constructor injection | Dependency jelas |
| Resource tetap tipis | Business logic di service |
| Ambil raw `@Context` hanya di boundary | Jangan bocor ke service |
| Pass immutable `RequestContext` | Testable dan aman |
| Jangan store request context as field | Resource lifecycle bisa berbeda dari asumsi |

---

## 16. Per-Request Object vs Explicit Context

Pilihan A: inject request-scoped `RequestContext`.

```java id="jv5ven"
@Path("/orders")
public final class OrderResource {

    private final OrderService orderService;
    private final RequestContext requestContext;

    @Inject
    public OrderResource(
        OrderService orderService,
        RequestContext requestContext
    ) {
        this.orderService = orderService;
        this.requestContext = requestContext;
    }
}
```

Pilihan B: extract dari request property dan pass explicit.

```java id="p7mtgc"
RequestContext context =
    (RequestContext) rawContext.getProperty("requestContext");
```

### Decision

| Approach | Pros | Cons |
|---|---|---|
| Inject request-scoped context | Clean resource signature | Perlu scope/integration benar |
| Extract property explicitly | Very clear boundary | Sedikit boilerplate |
| ThreadLocal global | Convenient | Rawan leak, hindari |
| Inject raw `ContainerRequestContext` ke service | Easy | Service terikat JAX-RS, hindari |

Rekomendasi praktis: untuk service layer, tetap pass `RequestContext` eksplisit sebagai parameter.

---

## 17. Module-Oriented Binder

Saat aplikasi membesar, pecah binder per module.

```java id="c0zv4h"
public final class AppBinder extends AbstractBinder {

    private final RuntimeResources resources;

    public AppBinder(RuntimeResources resources) {
        this.resources = resources;
    }

    @Override
    protected void configure() {
        install(new RuntimeBinder(resources));
        install(new OrderModuleBinder());
        install(new CustomerModuleBinder());
        install(new SecurityBinder());
        install(new ObservabilityBinder());
    }
}
```

```java id="nq0020"
public final class OrderModuleBinder extends AbstractBinder {

    @Override
    protected void configure() {
        bind(OrderServiceImpl.class)
            .to(OrderService.class)
            .in(Singleton.class);

        bind(OrderRepositoryPostgres.class)
            .to(OrderRepository.class)
            .in(Singleton.class);

        bind(OrderCommandMapper.class)
            .to(OrderCommandMapper.class)
            .in(Singleton.class);

        bind(OrderAccessPolicy.class)
            .to(OrderAccessPolicy.class)
            .in(Singleton.class);
    }
}
```

### Module binder rules

| Rule | Reason |
|---|---|
| Module owns its service/repo/mapper/policy binding | Easy review |
| Runtime/infrastructure binder separate | Avoid giant binder |
| Security/observability binder explicit | Critical components visible |
| Avoid duplicate binding same contract | Ambiguity/wrong implementation |
| Test module binder separately | Faster diagnostics |

---

## 18. Local/Test Binder Override

Untuk local/test, bisa replace dependency lewat binder khusus.

```java id="uexqs1"
public final class TestAppBinder extends AbstractBinder {

    @Override
    protected void configure() {
        bind(FakeOrderRepository.class)
            .to(OrderRepository.class)
            .in(Singleton.class);

        bind(InMemoryAuditPublisher.class)
            .to(AuditPublisher.class)
            .in(Singleton.class);

        bind(FixedClockFactory.class)
            .to(Clock.class)
            .in(Singleton.class);
    }
}
```

### Caution

Jangan register production binder dan test binder yang bind contract sama tanpa strategi override jelas.

```java id="pcp4px"
// BAD: duplicate OrderRepository binding can cause confusing behavior
register(new ProductionOrderBinder());
register(new TestOrderBinder());
```

Better:

```java id="2hgvug"
if (profile == RuntimeProfile.TEST) {
    register(new TestOrderBinder());
} else {
    register(new ProductionOrderBinder());
}
```

Atau pisahkan `ResourceConfig` untuk test.

---

## 19. JerseyTest Pattern

```java id="jl6t77"
public final class OrderResourceTest extends JerseyTest {

    @Override
    protected Application configure() {
        RuntimeResources resources = TestRuntimeResources.create();

        return new ResourceConfig()
            .register(new TestAppBinder(resources))
            .register(OrderResource.class)
            .register(DomainExceptionMapper.class)
            .register(AuthenticationFilter.class);
    }

    @Test
    void getOrderReturnsOrder() {
        Response response = target("/orders/order-1")
            .request()
            .header("Authorization", "Bearer test-token")
            .header("X-Tenant-Id", "tenant-a")
            .get();

        assertEquals(200, response.getStatus());
    }
}
```

### Apa yang dites?

| Test | Membuktikan |
|---|---|
| Resource inject service | HK2 binding aktif |
| Filter inject verifier | Provider injection aktif |
| Mapper inject factory/audit | Mapper registration + DI aktif |
| Fake repo used | Test binder benar |
| Error mapped | ExceptionMapper aktif |
| Request context exists | Filter + factory order benar |

---

## 20. Startup Binding Validation

HK2 binding error sering baru muncul saat request pertama. Tambahkan bootstrap/smoke test.

```java id="ng5z5l"
@Test
void appResourceConfigMustStart() {
    ResourceConfig config = new AppResourceConfig();

    assertTrue(config.getClasses().contains(OrderResource.class));
    assertTrue(config.getClasses().contains(AuthenticationFilter.class));
}
```

Lebih kuat: start JerseyTest lalu call endpoint protected.

```java id="3kz682"
@Test
void protectedEndpointMustReturn401WithoutToken() {
    Response response = target("/orders/order-1")
        .request()
        .get();

    assertEquals(401, response.getStatus());
}
```

Ini membuktikan:

```text id="eotcl1"
ResourceConfig works
Filter registered
Filter dependency injectable
Exception mapper may be active
HTTP pipeline starts
```

---

## 21. Diagnostics: Unsatisfied Dependency

Typical symptom:

```text id="h75j62"
UnsatisfiedDependencyException:
There was no object available for injection at ...
```

Checklist:

| Check | Action |
|---|---|
| Contract bound? | Cari `bind(...).to(MissingType.class)` |
| Binder registered? | Cek `register(new AppBinder(...))` |
| Provider registered as class? | Jangan `new` manual jika butuh DI |
| Wrong namespace/import? | `javax.inject` vs `jakarta.inject` |
| Version mismatch? | `mvn dependency:tree` |
| Duplicate module? | Cek multiple Jersey/HK2 versions |
| Object CDI-only? | HK2 tidak otomatis tahu CDI bean |
| Factory itself injectable? | Factory dependencies bound? |

Command:

```bash id="v5n30v"
mvn dependency:tree \
  -Dincludes=org.glassfish.jersey,org.glassfish.hk2,jakarta.inject,javax.inject
```

HK2 adalah lightweight dynamic dependency injection framework, dan Jersey menggunakan HK2 untuk banyak lifecycle/injection scenario di runtime Jersey; karena itu dependency tree Jersey/HK2 perlu konsisten saat terjadi error classloading atau `NoSuchMethodError`. ([Eclipse EE4J](https://eclipse-ee4j.github.io/glassfish-hk2/api-overview.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 22. Diagnostics: Wrong Scope

| Symptom | Likely Scope Issue | Fix |
|---|---|---|
| User/tenant bocor antar request | Singleton menyimpan request state | Remove field, pass context |
| DataSource dibuat berkali-kali | Factory not singleton | `.in(Singleton.class)` |
| RequestContext null | Factory called before filter | Filter priority/order |
| Context inactive async | Request-scoped object used outside request | Copy immutable context |
| Flaky tests under concurrency | Mutable singleton | Make stateless/immutable |
| Performance buruk | Heavy object PerLookup | Singleton factory |

### Search code smell

```bash id="tvju0l"
grep -R "static .*Context\|ThreadLocal\|currentTenant\|currentUser" -n src/main/java
```

---

## 23. Diagnostics: Jersey 4 Migration

Jersey roadmap menyebut Jersey 4.0.0 sebagai Jakarta REST 4.0 compliant release dan mencatat injection-related classes seperti `AbstractBinder` pindah ke package/module HK2-related. ([GitHub](https://github.com/eclipse-ee4j/jersey/wiki/Road-Map?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

### Migration checklist

| Check | Why |
|---|---|
| `AbstractBinder` import berubah? | Jersey 4 package/module move |
| HK2 dependency tersedia? | Binder class mungkin ada di artifact berbeda |
| `jakarta.inject.Inject` konsisten? | Jakarta namespace |
| Tidak ada `javax.ws.rs.*` | Jakarta REST 4 stack pakai `jakarta.*` |
| Dependency tree single Jersey generation | Avoid binary conflict |
| Tests compile and runtime start | Compile saja tidak cukup |

Command:

```bash id="0fr4b9"
grep -R "import org.glassfish.jersey.internal.inject.AbstractBinder\|import org.glassfish.hk2" -n src/main/java
```

```bash id="tn3hno"
grep -R "import javax\." -n src/main/java src/test/java
```

---

## 24. Anti-Patterns

| Anti-Pattern | Why Bad | Better |
|---|---|---|
| Giant `AppBinder` 1000 lines | Sulit review dan debug | Module binder |
| Bind everything singleton blindly | Request state leak | Scope by lifecycle |
| Factory loads config repeatedly | Inconsistent, slow | Bootstrap once |
| Provider registered as instance with manual deps | DI/lifecycle bypass | Register class |
| Raw `String` secret bound everywhere | Secret leak risk | `SecretValue` |
| Service injects `ContainerRequestContext` | JAX-RS leaks into service | Explicit `RequestContext` |
| ThreadLocal request context | Async/thread pool leak | Immutable context parameter |
| Duplicate binding for same contract | Wrong dependency ambiguity | One owner/binding |
| Test binder accidentally used in prod | Security/data loss | Separate test config/source |
| Package scanning for critical providers | Hidden missing provider | Explicit registration |

---

## 25. Production Checklist

| Area | Checklist |
|---|---|
| Binder registration | `AppBinder` explicitly registered in `ResourceConfig` |
| Module structure | Runtime/security/order/customer binders separated |
| Service/repo | Bound by interface, singleton if stateless |
| Config | Immutable instance bound once |
| DataSource/client | Factory-created singleton with cleanup |
| RequestContext | Request-scoped or explicit parameter, never singleton field |
| Provider injection | Filters/mappers registered as classes |
| Secrets | Wrapped/masked, not raw string everywhere |
| Scope | Heavy objects singleton, request data request-scoped/explicit |
| Diagnostics | JerseyTest smoke verifies bindings |
| Version | Jersey/HK2/Jakarta dependency tree aligned |
| Migration | Imports checked for Jersey 4/HK2 package move |
| Tests | Missing binding, auth filter, exception mapper, request context tested |
