## Response:
7/13/2026, 6:19:54 AM

Saya lanjut ke feature flag dan profile-driven behavior: fokusnya bukan “if profile == prod”, tapi bagaimana runtime behavior tetap aman, testable, auditable, dan tidak membuka celah security.

# Cheatsheet - Feature Flag & Profile-Driven Runtime Behavior  
## Runtime Profile, Safe Feature Flags, Targeting Context, Rollout Policy, Kill Switch, Auditability

## 1. Scope

Seri ini membahas bagaimana mengatur **runtime behavior** aplikasi Jersey/JAX-RS berdasarkan:

```text id="s0ue6m"
Runtime Behavior Control
│
├─ Runtime Profile
│  ├─ LOCAL
│  ├─ DEV
│  ├─ TEST
│  ├─ STAGING
│  └─ PROD
│
├─ Static Runtime Config
│  ├─ env var
│  ├─ JVM property
│  ├─ mounted file
│  ├─ ConfigMap
│  └─ Secret
│
├─ Feature Flags
│  ├─ release flag
│  ├─ experiment flag
│  ├─ ops flag
│  ├─ permission/entitlement flag
│  └─ kill switch
│
├─ Evaluation Context
│  ├─ tenant
│  ├─ actor
│  ├─ environment
│  ├─ service
│  └─ request/correlation
│
└─ Governance
   ├─ owner
   ├─ expiry
   ├─ audit
   ├─ rollback
   └─ test matrix
```

**Twelve-Factor App** menyarankan config disimpan di environment variable agar bisa berubah antar deploy tanpa mengubah code dan lebih kecil kemungkinan masuk repository dibanding file config custom. Untuk Kubernetes, ConfigMap adalah object untuk data non-confidential key-value, dan Pod bisa menggunakannya sebagai environment variable, command-line arg, atau file volume; Secret harus dipakai untuk sensitive value, bukan ConfigMap. ([12factor](https://12factor.net/config?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 2. Mental Model

```text id="u3cj4e"
Runtime Profile answers:
"Environment ini apa?"

Static Config answers:
"Nilai runtime stabil saat startup apa?"

Feature Flag answers:
"Behavior mana yang aktif untuk konteks tertentu?"

Policy answers:
"Apakah behavior ini aman untuk actor/tenant/state ini?"

Audit answers:
"Siapa mengaktifkan apa, kapan, untuk siapa, dan efeknya apa?"
```

Rule utama:

```text id="wv8be3"
Feature flag bukan pengganti authorization.
Feature flag bukan pengganti config validation.
Feature flag bukan alasan membuat behavior production tidak testable.
```

---

## 3. Profile vs Config vs Feature Flag

| Mechanism | Dipakai Untuk | Lifecycle | Contoh |
|---|---|---|---|
| Runtime profile | Kelas environment | Saat startup | `LOCAL`, `DEV`, `STAGING`, `PROD` |
| Static config | Dependency/runtime parameter | Saat startup/restart | DB URL, timeout, endpoint |
| Secret | Sensitive runtime value | Startup/rotation controlled | DB password, private key |
| Feature flag | Behavior toggle | Bisa static/dynamic | new order flow, strict validation |
| Kill switch | Emergency disable | Dynamic/fast | disable downstream call |
| Permission | Actor capability | Authz decision | `order:approve` |
| Policy | Domain decision | Runtime/business state | approve amount limit |

### Decision table

| Concern | Profile | Config | Feature Flag | Authorization |
|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| DB URL | No | Yes | No | No |
| Enable debug endpoint | Profile guard | Config possible | Dangerous | Admin-only if any |
| New order flow rollout | No | Maybe | Yes | Still required |
| User can approve order | No | No | No | Yes |
| Tenant allowed beta feature | No | Maybe | Yes + policy | Possibly entitlement |
| Disable downstream integration | No | Maybe | Kill switch | No |
| Max approval amount | No | Config/policy | No usually | Policy |

---

## 4. RuntimeProfile Model

```java id="62vlt8"
public enum RuntimeProfile {
    LOCAL,
    DEV,
    TEST,
    STAGING,
    PROD;

    public static RuntimeProfile parse(String value) {
        if (value == null || value.isBlank()) {
            return LOCAL;
        }

        return RuntimeProfile.valueOf(
            value.trim().toUpperCase(Locale.ROOT)
        );
    }

    public boolean isProductionLike() {
        return this == STAGING || this == PROD;
    }

    public boolean allowsDebugBehavior() {
        return this == LOCAL || this == DEV || this == TEST;
    }

    public boolean allowsUnsafeDefaults() {
        return this == LOCAL || this == TEST;
    }
}
```

### Production-like invariant

```java id="t78htp"
public final class ProfileInvariantValidator {

    public static void validate(AppConfig config) {
        RuntimeProfile profile = config.profile();

        if (!profile.isProductionLike()) {
            return;
        }

        if (config.featureFlags().debugEndpointsEnabled()) {
            throw new IllegalStateException(
                "Debug endpoints must be disabled in production-like profiles"
            );
        }

        if (!config.security().strictTenantValidationEnabled()) {
            throw new IllegalStateException(
                "Strict tenant validation must be enabled in production-like profiles"
            );
        }

        if (!config.security().authenticationRequired()) {
            throw new IllegalStateException(
                "Authentication must be required in production-like profiles"
            );
        }
    }
}
```

---

## 5. Feature Flag Taxonomy

| Type | Purpose | Example | Risk |
|---|---|---|---|
| Release flag | Rollout fitur baru | `newOrderFlow` | Medium/high |
| Experiment flag | A/B behavior | `searchRankingExperiment` | Medium |
| Ops flag | Operational control | `disableCustomerLookup` | High |
| Kill switch | Emergency disable | `paymentSubmissionDisabled` | Critical |
| Permission/entitlement flag | Tenant/customer capability | `tenantCanUseBulkApprove` | High |
| Migration flag | Switch old/new data path | `readFromNewSchema` | Critical |
| Debug flag | Diagnostics | `diagnosticsEndpointEnabled` | Critical in prod |
| Security flag | Auth/security behavior | `strictTenantValidation` | Critical |

Rule:

```text id="k7en1f"
Semakin dekat flag ke security, data write path, migration, atau money movement,
semakin ketat governance, audit, dan test-nya.
```

---

## 6. Static FeatureFlags Object

Untuk flag sederhana yang dibaca saat startup:

```java id="fvzftt"
public record FeatureFlags(
    boolean newOrderFlowEnabled,
    boolean strictTenantValidationEnabled,
    boolean diagnosticsEndpointEnabled,
    boolean downstreamCustomerLookupEnabled,
    boolean bulkApprovalEnabled
) {
    public void validate(RuntimeProfile profile) {
        if (profile.isProductionLike() && diagnosticsEndpointEnabled) {
            throw new IllegalStateException(
                "diagnosticsEndpointEnabled must be false in production-like profile"
            );
        }

        if (profile.isProductionLike() && !strictTenantValidationEnabled) {
            throw new IllegalStateException(
                "strictTenantValidationEnabled must be true in production-like profile"
            );
        }
    }
}
```

### Loader

```java id="t8159b"
public final class FeatureFlagLoader {

    public static FeatureFlags load(ConfigSource source) {
        return new FeatureFlags(
            source.booleanValue("FEATURE_NEW_ORDER_FLOW", "feature.newOrderFlow", false),
            source.booleanValue("FEATURE_STRICT_TENANT_VALIDATION", "feature.strictTenantValidation", true),
            source.booleanValue("FEATURE_DIAGNOSTICS_ENDPOINT", "feature.diagnosticsEndpoint", false),
            source.booleanValue("FEATURE_CUSTOMER_LOOKUP", "feature.customerLookup", true),
            source.booleanValue("FEATURE_BULK_APPROVAL", "feature.bulkApproval", false)
        );
    }
}
```

Kubernetes mendukung `env` dan `envFrom` untuk menyetel environment variable container; `env` menyetel entry individual, sedangkan `envFrom` mengambil banyak key dari ConfigMap atau Secret. ([Kubernetes](https://kubernetes.io/docs/tasks/inject-data-application/define-environment-variable-container/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 7. Dynamic Feature Flag Model

Untuk flag yang perlu targeting/rollout dinamis, gunakan abstraction.

```java id="mklp6p"
public interface FeatureFlagClient {

    boolean booleanFlag(
        String flagKey,
        boolean defaultValue,
        FeatureEvaluationContext context
    );

    String stringFlag(
        String flagKey,
        String defaultValue,
        FeatureEvaluationContext context
    );

    int intFlag(
        String flagKey,
        int defaultValue,
        FeatureEvaluationContext context
    );
}
```

```java id="so7t29"
public record FeatureEvaluationContext(
    String profile,
    String serviceName,
    String tenantId,
    String actorId,
    Set<String> actorRoles,
    String correlationId,
    Map<String, Object> attributes
) {}
```

OpenFeature mendefinisikan API vendor-agnostic untuk feature flagging; konsep **evaluation context** menyediakan informasi ambient untuk evaluasi flag, termasuk targeting/rule-based evaluation. OpenFeature juga punya **hooks** yang berjalan pada tahap `before`, `after`, `error`, dan `finally` untuk menambahkan behavior seperti logging, telemetry, atau validasi hasil evaluasi. ([GitHub](https://github.com/open-feature/java-sdk?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 8. Evaluation Context dari RequestContext

```java id="oc02ix"
public final class FeatureContextFactory {

    private final AppConfig config;

    @Inject
    public FeatureContextFactory(AppConfig config) {
        this.config = config;
    }

    public FeatureEvaluationContext from(RequestContext requestContext) {
        PrincipalContext principal = requestContext.principal();

        return new FeatureEvaluationContext(
            config.profile().name(),
            config.appName(),
            requestContext.tenantId(),
            principal.subject(),
            principal.roles(),
            requestContext.correlationId(),
            Map.of(
                "permissions", principal.permissions(),
                "requestId", requestContext.requestId()
            )
        );
    }
}
```

### Context rules

| Rule | Reason |
|---|---|
| Sertakan tenant | Rollout sering tenant-based |
| Sertakan actor/role hanya yang perlu | Hindari over-sharing PII |
| Sertakan profile/service | Behavior environment-aware |
| Sertakan correlation ID | Debug/audit |
| Jangan kirim token mentah | Secret leakage |
| Jangan kirim full request body | PII/security risk |
| Jangan jadikan context authorization source tunggal | Tetap pakai policy/authz |

---

## 9. FeatureFlagService Pattern

```java id="gi5nhl"
@ApplicationScoped
public class FeatureFlagService {

    private final FeatureFlagClient client;
    private final FeatureContextFactory contextFactory;

    @Inject
    public FeatureFlagService(
        FeatureFlagClient client,
        FeatureContextFactory contextFactory
    ) {
        this.client = client;
        this.contextFactory = contextFactory;
    }

    public boolean isNewOrderFlowEnabled(RequestContext context) {
        return client.booleanFlag(
            "order.new-flow.enabled",
            false,
            contextFactory.from(context)
        );
    }

    public boolean isBulkApprovalEnabled(RequestContext context) {
        return client.booleanFlag(
            "order.bulk-approval.enabled",
            false,
            contextFactory.from(context)
        );
    }

    public boolean isCustomerLookupEnabled(RequestContext context) {
        return client.booleanFlag(
            "dependency.customer-lookup.enabled",
            true,
            contextFactory.from(context)
        );
    }
}
```

### Rule

```text id="sm9n4d"
Resource/service jangan memanggil raw flag client dengan string key tersebar.
Buat method domain-specific seperti isNewOrderFlowEnabled().
```

---

## 10. Usage in Service Layer

```java id="ijrnmp"
@ApplicationScoped
public class OrderApplicationService {

    private final FeatureFlagService featureFlags;
    private final OrderServiceV1 oldFlow;
    private final OrderServiceV2 newFlow;
    private final AuditPublisher auditPublisher;

    @Inject
    public OrderApplicationService(
        FeatureFlagService featureFlags,
        OrderServiceV1 oldFlow,
        OrderServiceV2 newFlow,
        AuditPublisher auditPublisher
    ) {
        this.featureFlags = featureFlags;
        this.oldFlow = oldFlow;
        this.newFlow = newFlow;
        this.auditPublisher = auditPublisher;
    }

    public OrderDto create(CreateOrderCommand command, RequestContext context) {
        boolean newFlowEnabled =
            featureFlags.isNewOrderFlowEnabled(context);

        auditPublisher.publish(FeatureDecisionAuditEvent.of(
            "order.new-flow.enabled",
            newFlowEnabled,
            context
        ));

        if (newFlowEnabled) {
            return newFlow.create(command, context);
        }

        return oldFlow.create(command, context);
    }
}
```

### Caution

| Problem | Better |
|---|---|
| Flag checked in many places | Centralize in service/facade |
| Flag controls tiny fragments everywhere | Use strategy object |
| No audit for high-risk flag | Publish feature decision event |
| Different code paths untested | Test both flag on/off |
| Flag changes during request | Snapshot decision once per operation |

---

## 11. Strategy Pattern for Feature Flags

Untuk behavior besar, hindari `if` tersebar.

```java id="fz8h4d"
public interface OrderCreationStrategy {
    OrderDto create(CreateOrderCommand command, RequestContext context);
}
```

```java id="m1c21n"
@ApplicationScoped
public class LegacyOrderCreationStrategy implements OrderCreationStrategy {
    @Override
    public OrderDto create(CreateOrderCommand command, RequestContext context) {
        // old behavior
    }
}
```

```java id="a8n593"
@ApplicationScoped
public class NewOrderCreationStrategy implements OrderCreationStrategy {
    @Override
    public OrderDto create(CreateOrderCommand command, RequestContext context) {
        // new behavior
    }
}
```

```java id="wrrzbm"
@ApplicationScoped
public class OrderCreationStrategyResolver {

    private final FeatureFlagService featureFlags;
    private final LegacyOrderCreationStrategy legacy;
    private final NewOrderCreationStrategy modern;

    @Inject
    public OrderCreationStrategyResolver(
        FeatureFlagService featureFlags,
        LegacyOrderCreationStrategy legacy,
        NewOrderCreationStrategy modern
    ) {
        this.featureFlags = featureFlags;
        this.legacy = legacy;
        this.modern = modern;
    }

    public OrderCreationStrategy resolve(RequestContext context) {
        if (featureFlags.isNewOrderFlowEnabled(context)) {
            return modern;
        }

        return legacy;
    }
}
```

---

## 12. Kill Switch Pattern

Kill switch harus cepat, aman, dan fail-safe.

```java id="e8uy4y"
public final class CustomerLookupDisabledException extends DomainException {
    public CustomerLookupDisabledException() {
        super(
            "CUSTOMER_LOOKUP_DISABLED",
            ErrorCategory.DOWNSTREAM_UNAVAILABLE,
            "Customer lookup is temporarily unavailable"
        );
    }
}
```

```java id="tw7xxy"
@ApplicationScoped
public class CustomerLookupService {

    private final FeatureFlagService featureFlags;
    private final CustomerClient customerClient;

    @Inject
    public CustomerLookupService(
        FeatureFlagService featureFlags,
        CustomerClient customerClient
    ) {
        this.featureFlags = featureFlags;
        this.customerClient = customerClient;
    }

    public CustomerSnapshot getCustomer(String customerId, RequestContext context) {
        if (!featureFlags.isCustomerLookupEnabled(context)) {
            throw new CustomerLookupDisabledException();
        }

        return customerClient.getCustomer(customerId, context);
    }
}
```

### Kill switch rules

| Rule | Reason |
|---|---|
| Default harus aman | Dependency-critical biasanya default true/false sesuai failure mode |
| Error response harus jelas tapi safe | Client tahu retry/temporary unavailable |
| Audit/metric decision | Ops bisa lihat impact |
| Jangan bypass authorization | Kill switch hanya behavior availability |
| Test both states | Flag on/off wajib tested |

---

## 13. Profile-Driven Registration vs Runtime Flag

Kadang behavior diputuskan saat bootstrap, bukan per request.

```java id="i36j7p"
public final class AppBinder extends AbstractBinder {

    private final AppConfig config;

    public AppBinder(AppConfig config) {
        this.config = config;
    }

    @Override
    protected void configure() {
        if (config.profile() == RuntimeProfile.LOCAL) {
            bind(InMemoryAuditPublisher.class)
                .to(AuditPublisher.class)
                .in(Singleton.class);
        } else {
            bind(KafkaAuditPublisher.class)
                .to(AuditPublisher.class)
                .in(Singleton.class);
        }
    }
}
```

### Use bootstrap decision when

| Use Case | Reason |
|---|---|
| In-memory vs Kafka audit in local | Dependency availability |
| Fake downstream in test | Test isolation |
| DB implementation | Infrastructure choice |
| Diagnostics endpoint registration | Avoid registering in prod |
| Mock token verifier for test | Test-only runtime |

### Use runtime flag when

| Use Case | Reason |
|---|---|
| Tenant-based rollout | Per tenant decision |
| Gradual feature enablement | Dynamic rollout |
| Kill switch | Needs quick toggle |
| Experiment | Per actor/request targeting |
| Operational dependency bypass | Runtime control |

---

## 14. ConfigMap Update Semantics

Kubernetes ConfigMap bisa dikonsumsi sebagai environment variable atau volume file; perubahan ConfigMap tidak otomatis mengubah environment variable process yang sudah berjalan. Untuk mounted ConfigMap volume, Kubernetes dapat memperbarui projected files, tetapi aplikasi tetap harus membaca ulang file atau di-restart agar behavior berubah jika app hanya membaca saat startup. Kubernetes juga menyediakan tutorial khusus untuk update konfigurasi via ConfigMap pada aplikasi berjalan. ([Kubernetes](https://kubernetes.io/docs/concepts/configuration/configmap/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

### Practical rule

```text id="mluths"
Env var config:
  change requires pod restart.

Mounted file config:
  file may update, but app must support reload.

Dynamic flag service:
  app/client/provider controls refresh behavior.
```

### Decision

| Need | Best Approach |
|---|---|
| DB URL/password | Restart-controlled config/secret |
| Timeout change | Restart or controlled reload |
| Emergency kill switch | Dynamic flag |
| Tenant rollout | Dynamic flag |
| Debug endpoint | Bootstrap registration + prod invariant |
| Security strictness | Startup invariant, not dynamic off switch |
| Authorization permission | Authz policy, not feature flag |

---

## 15. Flag Defaults

Default value adalah security decision.

```java id="ksft11"
boolean enabled = client.booleanFlag(
    "order.bulk-approval.enabled",
    false, // safe default
    context
);
```

### Default policy table

| Flag | Safe Default | Reason |
|---|---:|---|
| New write path | `false` | Avoid untested writes |
| New read path | `false` or fallback old | Availability |
| Strict tenant validation | `true` | Security |
| Diagnostics endpoint | `false` | Security |
| Bulk approval | `false` | High-impact action |
| Customer lookup enabled | Depends | If disabled blocks functionality |
| Payment submission enabled | Depends, but kill switch explicit | Business-critical |
| Debug logging | `false` | Info leakage |

Rule:

```text id="i249xx"
Jika flag provider down, aplikasi harus default ke behavior yang paling aman,
bukan behavior yang paling nyaman.
```

---

## 16. Flag Governance Model

```java id="77e5dp"
public record FeatureFlagDefinition(
    String key,
    String description,
    String owner,
    FeatureFlagType type,
    boolean defaultValue,
    RiskLevel riskLevel,
    LocalDate createdAt,
    LocalDate expiresAt,
    boolean auditDecision
) {}
```

```java id="ao8x2v"
public enum FeatureFlagType {
    RELEASE,
    EXPERIMENT,
    OPS,
    KILL_SWITCH,
    ENTITLEMENT,
    MIGRATION,
    DEBUG,
    SECURITY
}
```

```java id="gks7zh"
public enum RiskLevel {
    LOW,
    MEDIUM,
    HIGH,
    CRITICAL
}
```

### Governance table

| Metadata | Required? | Why |
|---|---:|---|
| Key | Yes | Stable reference |
| Owner | Yes | Accountability |
| Description | Yes | Review clarity |
| Default value | Yes | Failure behavior |
| Risk level | Yes | Test/audit strictness |
| Expiry date | Yes for release/experiment | Prevent flag debt |
| Audit decision | Yes for high-risk | Compliance |
| Rollback plan | Yes for high/critical | Ops readiness |
| Test cases | Yes | CI confidence |

---

## 17. Flag Registry

```java id="ypr7mm"
public final class FeatureFlagRegistry {

    public static final FeatureFlagDefinition NEW_ORDER_FLOW =
        new FeatureFlagDefinition(
            "order.new-flow.enabled",
            "Enable new order creation flow",
            "order-platform-team",
            FeatureFlagType.RELEASE,
            false,
            RiskLevel.HIGH,
            LocalDate.parse("2026-07-01"),
            LocalDate.parse("2026-09-01"),
            true
        );

    public static final FeatureFlagDefinition BULK_APPROVAL =
        new FeatureFlagDefinition(
            "order.bulk-approval.enabled",
            "Enable bulk approval operation",
            "order-platform-team",
            FeatureFlagType.ENTITLEMENT,
            false,
            RiskLevel.CRITICAL,
            LocalDate.parse("2026-07-01"),
            LocalDate.parse("2026-12-31"),
            true
        );

    private static final List<FeatureFlagDefinition> ALL =
        List.of(NEW_ORDER_FLOW, BULK_APPROVAL);

    public static List<FeatureFlagDefinition> all() {
        return ALL;
    }
}
```

### Registry test

```java id="jnk1n6"
@Test
void everyHighRiskFlagMustHaveOwnerExpiryAndAudit() {
    for (FeatureFlagDefinition flag : FeatureFlagRegistry.all()) {
        if (flag.riskLevel() == RiskLevel.HIGH
            || flag.riskLevel() == RiskLevel.CRITICAL) {

            assertFalse(flag.owner().isBlank(), flag.key());
            assertNotNull(flag.expiresAt(), flag.key());
            assertTrue(flag.auditDecision(), flag.key());
        }
    }
}
```

## 18. Auditing Feature Decisions

Untuk high-risk flag, audit keputusan flag.

```java id="8qfj1g"
public record FeatureDecisionAuditEvent(
    String flagKey,
    boolean enabled,
    String tenantId,
    String actorId,
    String correlationId,
    Instant occurredAt
) {
    public static FeatureDecisionAuditEvent of(
        String flagKey,
        boolean enabled,
        RequestContext context
    ) {
        return new FeatureDecisionAuditEvent(
            flagKey,
            enabled,
            context.tenantId(),
            context.actorId(),
            context.correlationId(),
            Instant.now()
        );
    }
}
```

```java id="m634l3"
public boolean highRiskBooleanFlag(
    FeatureFlagDefinition definition,
    RequestContext context
) {
    boolean enabled = client.booleanFlag(
        definition.key(),
        definition.defaultValue(),
        contextFactory.from(context)
    );

    if (definition.auditDecision()) {
        auditPublisher.publish(FeatureDecisionAuditEvent.of(
            definition.key(),
            enabled,
            context
        ));
    }

    return enabled;
}
```

### Audit when

| Flag Type | Audit Decision? |
|---|---:|
| Release flag low risk | Optional |
| Experiment flag UI-only | Optional |
| Ops kill switch | Yes |
| Entitlement flag | Yes |
| Migration write path | Yes |
| Security flag | Yes |
| Debug flag | Yes if ever accessible |
| Payment/order approval path | Yes |

---

## 19. Feature Flag Testing Matrix

| Test | Flag Off | Flag On |
|---|---|---|
| New order flow | Legacy flow executed | New flow executed |
| Bulk approval | 404/403/feature disabled | Allowed if permission valid |
| Customer lookup kill switch | Returns 503/safe error | Calls downstream |
| Strict tenant validation | Must be true in prod | Active |
| Diagnostics endpoint | Not found in prod | Only local/dev if allowed |
| Field visibility | Sensitive field hidden | Visible only if also authorized |
| Migration read path | Old schema read | New schema read |
| Audit decision | Audit off optional | Audit on for high-risk |

### Unit test

```java id="5dgryz"
@Test
void newOrderFlowFlagOffUsesLegacyStrategy() {
    FeatureFlagService flags = new FakeFeatureFlagService()
        .with("order.new-flow.enabled", false);

    OrderCreationStrategyResolver resolver =
        new OrderCreationStrategyResolver(flags, legacy, modern);

    assertSame(legacy, resolver.resolve(requestContext()));
}
```

### API test

```java id="x97jym"
@Test
void bulkApprovalDisabledMustReturnFeatureDisabled() {
    fakeFlags.with("order.bulk-approval.enabled", false);

    given()
        .auth().oauth2(validTokenWithPermission("order:bulk-approve"))
        .header("X-Tenant-Id", "tenant-a")
        .contentType(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
        .body("""
            { "orderIds": ["ORD-1", "ORD-2"] }
        """)
    .when()
        .post("/orders/bulk-approve")
    .then()
        .statusCode(403)
        .body("code", equalTo("FEATURE_DISABLED"));
}
```

## 20. Flag + Authorization Anti-Confusion

Feature flag says feature exists for context. Authorization says actor may perform action.

```java id="2tssw5"
public BulkApprovalResult bulkApprove(
    BulkApproveCommand command,
    RequestContext context
) {
    if (!featureFlags.isBulkApprovalEnabled(context)) {
        throw new FeatureDisabledException("order.bulk-approval.enabled");
    }

    authorizationPolicy.requirePermission(
        context.principal(),
        "order:bulk-approve"
    );

    return bulkApprovalService.approve(command, context);
}
```

### Wrong

```java id="6b3oqf"
// BAD
if (featureFlags.isBulkApprovalEnabled(context)) {
    approveOrders();
}
```

Masalah: tenant/actor yang mendapat flag otomatis bisa melakukan aksi tanpa permission check.

### Correct

```text id="e4896o"
Feature enabled?
  ↓ yes
Actor authorized?
  ↓ yes
Object/state valid?
  ↓ yes
Execute action
```

---

## 21. Flag Debt Management

Feature flag yang tidak dibersihkan akan menjadi technical debt.

```text id="wb47zq"
Flag lifecycle
│
├─ Proposed
├─ Implemented off by default
├─ Tested on/off
├─ Enabled in dev
├─ Enabled in staging
├─ Gradual production rollout
├─ Fully enabled
├─ Code old path removed
└─ Flag archived
```

### Cleanup checklist

| Question | Action |
|---|---|
| Flag fully rolled out? | Remove old path |
| Old path unused? | Delete code/tests |
| Flag expired? | Create cleanup ticket |
| Flag controls security behavior? | Review before removing |
| Flag still in config provider? | Archive/delete |
| Tests still cover both paths? | Remove obsolete tests after cleanup |

---

## 22. Dynamic vs Static Flag Failure Modes

| Symptom | Root Cause | Fix |
|---|---|---|
| Prod behavior berubah tanpa deploy dan tanpa audit | Dynamic flag no governance | Audit flag changes/decisions |
| Tenant dapat fitur tanpa izin | Flag treated as authz | Keep authorization check |
| Feature flag provider down opens risky feature | Unsafe default | Safe default false/true based on risk |
| Flag checked inconsistently | String key scattered | Central `FeatureFlagService` |
| Stale flag code remains years | No expiry owner | Registry with expiry |
| Debug endpoint enabled prod | Profile invariant missing | Fail startup |
| ConfigMap changed but app unchanged | App reads env at startup | Restart or implement reload |
| Mounted file changed but behavior unchanged | No reload policy | Restart/controlled reload |
| Kill switch too slow | Static env flag | Dynamic provider or fast config path |
| Tests only flag-on | Missing flag-off regression | Matrix tests |

---

## 23. Production Checklist

| Area | Checklist |
|---|---|
| Runtime profile | `LOCAL/DEV/TEST/STAGING/PROD` explicit and validated |
| Config | Static config via env/file/ConfigMap, not code |
| Secret | Secret separated from non-secret config |
| Feature service | Centralized domain-specific methods, no scattered string keys |
| Evaluation context | Tenant/actor/profile/correlation included safely |
| Defaults | Safe defaults defined for every flag |
| Governance | Owner, expiry, risk, rollback plan for high-risk flags |
| Authorization | Feature flags never replace permission checks |
| Kill switch | Tested and observable |
| Audit | High-risk flag decisions audited |
| Tests | On/off matrix for critical paths |
| Cleanup | Flag debt tracked and expired flags removed |
| Kubernetes | Understand env vs mounted file update semantics |
| Startup | Production-like invariant blocks unsafe debug/security flags |

---

## 24. Roadmap Belajar Singkat

| Urutan | Fokus | Target Praktik |
|---:|---|---|
| 1 | Profile vs config vs flag | Bisa memilih mekanisme yang tepat |
| 2 | Static feature flags | Bisa load flag dari env/config dengan invariant |
| 3 | Dynamic flag abstraction | Bisa membuat `FeatureFlagClient` dan `FeatureFlagService` |
| 4 | Evaluation context | Bisa target tenant/actor tanpa bocor PII/token |
| 5 | Kill switch | Bisa mematikan dependency/behavior secara aman |
| 6 | Authorization separation | Bisa memastikan flag tidak mengganti permission |
| 7 | Audit & governance | Bisa audit high-risk feature decision |
| 8 | Testing matrix | Bisa test flag on/off di unit/API/integration tests |

## Response:
7/13/2026, 6:21:48 AM

Saya lanjut ke integration testing: memisahkan plain unit test, JerseyTest, Weld CDI test, dan full bootstrap test agar wiring resource/filter/mapper/config benar-benar terbukti jalan.

# Cheatsheet - JerseyTest + Weld + Bootstrap Integration Testing  
## ResourceConfig, HK2 Binder, CDI/Weld Graph, Provider Activation, Testcontainers PostgreSQL, CI Smoke Tests

## 1. Scope

Seri ini membahas strategi testing agar aplikasi Jersey/JAX-RS tidak hanya **unit-testable**, tetapi benar-benar terbukti:

```text id="7acddq"
Testing Architecture
│
├─ Plain Unit Test
│  └─ business logic tanpa container
│
├─ Mapper / Policy / Validator Test
│  └─ pure Java deterministic test
│
├─ Weld CDI Component Test
│  └─ producer, qualifier, alternative, interceptor, event
│
├─ JerseyTest
│  └─ ResourceConfig, resource, filter, mapper, provider
│
├─ Jersey + CDI/HK2 Integration Test
│  └─ HTTP boundary bisa reach service graph
│
├─ DB Integration Test
│  └─ PostgreSQL/Testcontainers, repository, migration
│
└─ Deployment Smoke Test
   └─ health, auth, mapper, correlation, config invariant
```

Jersey Test Framework memang ditujukan untuk testing aplikasi Jersey/JAX-RS dan terutama berbasis JUnit, serta cocok untuk Maven-based setup. Dokumentasi Jersey juga menjelaskan framework ini sebagai tool untuk menjalankan test server-side component Jersey dengan konfigurasi test runtime. ([Eclipse EE4J](https://eclipse-ee4j.github.io/jersey.github.io/documentation/latest/test-framework.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 2. Mental Model

```text id="6jmk3z"
Unit test answers:
"Logic class ini benar?"

Weld test answers:
"CDI graph ini bisa boot dan dependency resolve?"

JerseyTest answers:
"HTTP pipeline Jersey ini benar?"

DB integration test answers:
"Query, transaction, migration, dan constraint benar?"

Deployment smoke test answers:
"Artifact yang dideploy benar-benar siap menerima traffic?"
```

Golden rule:

```text id="cenp77"
Jangan membuktikan runtime behavior hanya dengan unit test.

Filter, mapper, DI binder, provider registration, request context,
dan JSON serialization harus dites di level JerseyTest / integration test.
```

---

## 3. Test Pyramid untuk Jersey/JAX-RS

| Layer | Tool | Test Target | Cepat? | Perlu Container? |
|---|---|---|---:|---:|
| Unit | JUnit | service, policy, mapper, validator | Ya | Tidak |
| CDI component | Weld JUnit | producer, qualifier, alternative, interceptor | Ya/sedang | CDI only |
| Jersey resource | JerseyTest | resource, filter, mapper, JSON provider | Sedang | Jersey test container |
| DB integration | Testcontainers PostgreSQL | repository, SQL, migration, transaction | Lebih lambat | Docker |
| App integration | Jersey + DB + test binder | resource-to-db flow | Lebih lambat | Jersey + Docker |
| Deployment smoke | curl/REST client | deployed app health/auth/error | Cepat | Real deployment |

Testcontainers for Java mendukung JUnit tests dengan container ringan/throwaway untuk dependency seperti database, browser, atau service lain yang bisa berjalan dalam Docker container; PostgreSQL module juga disediakan untuk menjalankan PostgreSQL container dalam test. ([Testcontainers](https://java.testcontainers.org/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 4. Recommended Maven Test Split

```text id="vvp1fk"
src/test/java
├─ unit
│  ├─ OrderAccessPolicyTest
│  ├─ OrderMapperTest
│  └─ OrderValidatorTest
│
├─ cdi
│  ├─ RuntimeConfigProducerTest
│  ├─ DatabaseProducerTest
│  └─ AuditInterceptorTest
│
├─ jersey
│  ├─ OrderResourceJerseyTest
│  ├─ AuthenticationFilterJerseyTest
│  └─ ErrorMapperJerseyTest
│
└─ integration
   ├─ OrderRepositoryPostgresIT
   ├─ OrderApiPostgresIT
   └─ MigrationCompatibilityIT
```

### Maven convention

| Test Name | Plugin | Purpose |
|---|---|---|
| `*Test` | Surefire | Unit, mapper, policy, CDI component, Jersey light tests |
| `*IT` | Failsafe | DB/container/full integration tests |
| `*SmokeTest` | CI/deploy stage | Deployed service smoke test |

```xml id="9x3zgt"
<build>
    <plugins>
        <plugin>
            <groupId>org.apache.maven.plugins</groupId>
            <artifactId>maven-surefire-plugin</artifactId>
            <version>${maven-surefire-plugin.version}</version>
        </plugin>

        <plugin>
            <groupId>org.apache.maven.plugins</groupId>
            <artifactId>maven-failsafe-plugin</artifactId>
            <version>${maven-failsafe-plugin.version}</version>
            <executions>
                <execution>
                    <goals>
                        <goal>integration-test</goal>
                        <goal>verify</goal>
                    </goals>
                </execution>
            </executions>
        </plugin>
    </plugins>
</build>
```

---

## 5. Plain Unit Test: No Container

Service/policy/mapper harus tetap bisa dites tanpa Jersey/HK2/CDI.

```java id="h1rdku"
class OrderAccessPolicyTest {

    private final OrderAccessPolicy policy = new OrderAccessPolicy();

    @Test
    void ownerCanReadOwnOrder() {
        PrincipalContext principal = principal(
            "user-a",
            Set.of("order:read:own"),
            Set.of("tenant-a")
        );

        Order order = order(
            "order-1",
            "tenant-a",
            "user-a",
            OrderStatus.SUBMITTED
        );

        AuthorizationDecision decision =
            policy.canRead(principal, order);

        assertTrue(decision.allowed());
    }
}
```

### Unit-testable design

| Good | Bad |
|---|---|
| Constructor injection | Static service locator |
| Explicit `RequestContext` parameter | Hidden ThreadLocal |
| Plain Java policy object | Policy embedded in filter/resource |
| Mapper separate class | Mapping hidden in resource |
| Fake repository | Real DB required for every test |

---

## 6. Weld CDI Component Test

Weld testing cocok untuk membuktikan CDI graph: producer, qualifier, alternative, interceptor, event.

Weld Testing Extensions menyediakan ekstensi untuk JUnit 4, JUnit 5, dan Spock agar CDI components bisa dites dengan Weld; repository resminya juga mencatat dukungan JUnit 5 melalui module/extension khusus. ([GitHub](https://github.com/weld/weld-testing/blob/master/junit5/README.md?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

### Example: producer resolution

```java id="88hkpp"
@EnableWeld
class RuntimeProducerTest {

    @WeldSetup
    WeldInitiator weld = WeldInitiator
        .from(
            TestAppConfigProducer.class,
            DataSourceProducer.class,
            OrderRepositoryPostgres.class
        )
        .build();

    @Inject
    AppConfig appConfig;

    @Inject
    DataSource dataSource;

    @Inject
    OrderRepositoryPostgres repository;

    @Test
    void cdiGraphMustResolveInfrastructureBeans() {
        assertNotNull(appConfig);
        assertNotNull(dataSource);
        assertNotNull(repository);
    }
}
```

### Weld test target

| Test | Membuktikan |
|---|---|
| `AppConfig` injectable | Producer aktif |
| `DataSource` injectable | Producer + config benar |
| Qualified datasource resolve | Qualifier benar |
| Alternative fake aktif | Test replacement benar |
| Interceptor fires | CDI AOP aktif |
| CDI event observed | Event wiring aktif |

---

## 7. Weld Test: Qualifier Resolution

```java id="bk9n3n"
@EnableWeld
class DatabaseQualifierTest {

    @WeldSetup
    WeldInitiator weld = WeldInitiator
        .from(
            TestAppConfigProducer.class,
            DatabaseProducer.class,
            OrderRepositoryPostgres.class
        )
        .build();

    @Inject
    @PrimaryDatabase
    DataSource primary;

    @Inject
    @ReadReplicaDatabase
    DataSource replica;

    @Test
    void primaryAndReplicaMustResolveSeparately() {
        assertNotNull(primary);
        assertNotNull(replica);
        assertNotSame(primary, replica);
    }
}
```

### Failure yang ditangkap

| Failure | Gejala |
|---|---|
| Missing producer | `UnsatisfiedResolutionException` |
| Multiple `DataSource` tanpa qualifier | `AmbiguousResolutionException` |
| Wrong qualifier | Injection gagal |
| Test accidentally pakai real prod config | Config invariant gagal |
| DataSource dibuat dua kali tidak sengaja | Instance assertion gagal |

---

## 8. Weld Test: Alternative / Test Double

```java id="wej4pp"
@Alternative
@Priority(1)
@ApplicationScoped
public class InMemoryAuditPublisher implements AuditPublisher {

    private final List<AuditEvent> events = new CopyOnWriteArrayList<>();

    @Override
    public void publish(AuditEvent event) {
        events.add(event);
    }

    public List<AuditEvent> events() {
        return List.copyOf(events);
    }
}
```

```java id="3g2ulj"
@EnableWeld
class OrderServiceAuditTest {

    @WeldSetup
    WeldInitiator weld = WeldInitiator
        .from(
            OrderApplicationService.class,
            FakeOrderRepository.class,
            OrderAccessPolicy.class,
            InMemoryAuditPublisher.class
        )
        .build();

    @Inject
    OrderApplicationService service;

    @Inject
    AuditPublisher auditPublisher;

    @Test
    void approvalMustPublishAuditEvent() {
        service.approve(approveCommand(), requestContext());

        InMemoryAuditPublisher fake =
            (InMemoryAuditPublisher) auditPublisher;

        assertTrue(fake.events().stream()
            .anyMatch(event -> event.action().equals("ORDER_APPROVED")));
    }
}
```

Rule: test alternative harus berada di `src/test/java`, bukan production classpath.

---

## 9. JerseyTest: Basic ResourceConfig Test

JerseyTest biasanya dilakukan dengan override `configure()` untuk mengembalikan `Application` atau `ResourceConfig`.

```java id="tyj83a"
class OrderResourceJerseyTest extends JerseyTest {

    @Override
    protected Application configure() {
        RuntimeResources resources = TestRuntimeResources.valid();

        return new ResourceConfig()
            .register(new TestAppBinder(resources))
            .register(OrderResource.class)
            .register(CorrelationIdFilter.class)
            .register(AuthenticationFilter.class)
            .register(AuthorizationFilter.class)
            .register(DomainExceptionMapper.class)
            .register(UnhandledExceptionMapper.class)
            .register(JsonProvider.class);
    }

    @Test
    void getOrderReturns200() {
        Response response = target("/orders/order-1")
            .request()
            .header(HttpHeaders.AUTHORIZATION, "Bearer test-token")
            .header("X-Tenant-Id", "tenant-a")
            .get();

        assertEquals(200, response.getStatus());
    }
}
```

### JerseyTest target

| Test | Membuktikan |
|---|---|
| Endpoint returns 200 | Resource registered + DI jalan |
| Missing token returns 401 | Auth filter aktif |
| Invalid permission returns 403 | Authz filter aktif |
| Domain exception returns JSON | ExceptionMapper aktif |
| Correlation header returned | Correlation filter aktif |
| JSON body parsed | JSON provider aktif |
| Validation error returns 400 | Validation + mapper aktif |

---

## 10. Test Binder Pattern

Untuk JerseyTest, gunakan binder khusus test.

```java id="w52fn5"
public final class TestAppBinder extends AbstractBinder {

    private final RuntimeResources resources;

    public TestAppBinder(RuntimeResources resources) {
        this.resources = resources;
    }

    @Override
    protected void configure() {
        bind(resources.appConfig()).to(AppConfig.class);

        bind(FakeOrderRepository.class)
            .to(OrderRepository.class)
            .in(Singleton.class);

        bind(OrderApplicationService.class)
            .to(OrderApplicationService.class)
            .in(Singleton.class);

        bind(OrderAccessPolicy.class)
            .to(OrderAccessPolicy.class)
            .in(Singleton.class);

        bind(InMemoryAuditPublisher.class)
            .to(AuditPublisher.class)
            .in(Singleton.class);

        bind(TestTokenVerifier.class)
            .to(TokenVerifier.class)
            .in(Singleton.class);
    }
}
```

### Test binder rules

| Rule | Reason |
|---|---|
| Jangan pakai production DB by default | Test resource harus deterministic |
| Fake token verifier | Test auth pipeline tanpa real IdP |
| Fake/in-memory audit | Bisa assert event |
| Config test eksplisit | Tidak pakai local machine env |
| Jangan register duplicate binding | Ambiguous/wrong dependency |

---

## 11. Auth Filter Test

```java id="5x6av7"
class AuthenticationFilterJerseyTest extends JerseyTest {

    @Override
    protected Application configure() {
        return new ResourceConfig()
            .register(new TestAppBinder(TestRuntimeResources.valid()))
            .register(OrderResource.class)
            .register(CorrelationIdFilter.class)
            .register(AuthenticationFilter.class)
            .register(AuthenticationExceptionMapper.class);
    }

    @Test
    void missingTokenMustReturn401() {
        Response response = target("/orders/order-1")
            .request()
            .header("X-Tenant-Id", "tenant-a")
            .get();

        assertEquals(401, response.getStatus());
        assertNotNull(response.getHeaderString("X-Correlation-Id"));
    }

    @Test
    void invalidTokenMustReturn401() {
        Response response = target("/orders/order-1")
            .request()
            .header(HttpHeaders.AUTHORIZATION, "Bearer invalid")
            .header("X-Tenant-Id", "tenant-a")
            .get();

        assertEquals(401, response.getStatus());
    }
}
```

### Kenapa penting?

```text id="lzf3xd"
Tanpa JerseyTest:
- filter bisa lupa registered
- priority bisa salah
- exception mapper bisa tidak aktif
- response header bisa hilang
```

---

## 12. Error Mapper Test

```java id="aa4vii"
class ErrorMapperJerseyTest extends JerseyTest {

    @Override
    protected Application configure() {
        return new ResourceConfig()
            .register(TestErrorResource.class)
            .register(CorrelationIdFilter.class)
            .register(DomainExceptionMapper.class)
            .register(UnhandledExceptionMapper.class)
            .register(JsonProvider.class);
    }

    @Path("/test/errors")
    public static class TestErrorResource {

        @GET
        @Path("/domain")
        public Response domainError() {
            throw new OrderNotFoundException("order-404");
        }

        @GET
        @Path("/unexpected")
        public Response unexpectedError() {
            throw new NullPointerException("simulated");
        }
    }

    @Test
    void domainExceptionMustReturnSafeJson() {
        ErrorResponse response = target("/test/errors/domain")
            .request()
            .header("X-Correlation-Id", "corr-1")
            .get(ErrorResponse.class);

        assertEquals("ORDER_NOT_FOUND", response.code());
        assertEquals("corr-1", response.correlationId());
    }

    @Test
    void unexpectedExceptionMustNotExposeStackTrace() {
        Response response = target("/test/errors/unexpected")
            .request()
            .get();

        assertEquals(500, response.getStatus());

        String body = response.readEntity(String.class);
        assertFalse(body.contains("NullPointerException"));
        assertFalse(body.contains("simulated"));
    }
}
```

---

## 13. Validation Test with JerseyTest

```java id="xxllf6"
class OrderValidationJerseyTest extends JerseyTest {

    @Override
    protected Application configure() {
        return new ResourceConfig()
            .register(new TestAppBinder(TestRuntimeResources.valid()))
            .register(OrderResource.class)
            .register(ConstraintViolationExceptionMapper.class)
            .register(JsonProvider.class);
    }

    @Test
    void invalidRequestMustReturnFieldErrors() {
        Response response = target("/orders")
            .request(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON_TYPE)
            .header(HttpHeaders.AUTHORIZATION, "Bearer test-token")
            .header("X-Tenant-Id", "tenant-a")
            .post(Entity.json("""
                {
                  "customerId": "",
                  "amount": 0,
                  "currency": "US",
                  "lines": []
                }
            """));

        assertEquals(400, response.getStatus());

        ErrorResponse body = response.readEntity(ErrorResponse.class);

        assertEquals("VALIDATION_FAILED", body.code());
        assertTrue(body.errors().stream()
            .anyMatch(error -> error.field().contains("customerId")));
    }
}
```

---

## 14. Provider Registration Test

Jangan hanya test endpoint. Test juga composition root.

```java id="jbrd91"
class ResourceConfigRegistrationTest {

    @Test
    void productionConfigMustRegisterCriticalProviders() {
        ResourceConfig config = new AppResourceConfig(
            TestRuntimeResources.valid()
        );

        Set<Class<?>> classes = config.getClasses();

        assertTrue(classes.contains(OrderResource.class));
        assertTrue(classes.contains(AuthenticationFilter.class));
        assertTrue(classes.contains(AuthorizationFilter.class));
        assertTrue(classes.contains(CorrelationIdFilter.class));
        assertTrue(classes.contains(DomainExceptionMapper.class));
        assertTrue(classes.contains(UnhandledExceptionMapper.class));
        assertTrue(classes.contains(HealthResource.class));
    }
}
```

### Registration invariant

| Critical Component | Test |
|---|---|
| `AuthenticationFilter` | Must be registered |
| `AuthorizationFilter` | Must be registered |
| `CorrelationIdFilter` | Must be registered |
| `UnhandledExceptionMapper` | Must be registered |
| `JsonProvider` | Must be registered |
| `HealthResource` | Must be registered |
| `AppBinder` / CDI integration | Must be registered |
| `SecurityHeadersFilter` | Must be registered |

---

## 15. Jersey + CDI Integration Test

CDI component test membuktikan CDI graph. JerseyTest membuktikan Jersey pipeline. Tapi integrasi Jersey + CDI perlu test sendiri.

```text id="aip3ej"
Resource
  ↓ injected service
CDI/HK2 bridge
  ↓
CDI service
  ↓
CDI producer/repository/policy
```

### Test target

| Case | Expected |
|---|---|
| Resource injects CDI service | Endpoint 200 |
| Filter injects CDI token verifier | 401/200 behavior correct |
| Mapper injects CDI error factory | Error JSON correct |
| CDI interceptor on service fires | Audit/log event exists |
| CDI request-scoped bean usable | Only within request |

Pseudo-pattern:

```java id="5mdiw0"
class JerseyCdiIntegrationTest extends JerseyTest {

    @Override
    protected Application configure() {
        return new ResourceConfig()
            .register(CdiIntegrationFeature.class)
            .register(OrderResource.class)
            .register(AuthenticationFilter.class)
            .register(DomainExceptionMapper.class);
    }

    @Test
    void resourceMustReachCdiManagedService() {
        Response response = target("/orders/order-1")
            .request()
            .header(HttpHeaders.AUTHORIZATION, "Bearer test-token")
            .header("X-Tenant-Id", "tenant-a")
            .get();

        assertEquals(200, response.getStatus());
    }
}
```

Catatan: detail `CdiIntegrationFeature` bergantung pada versi Jersey/Weld/project. Yang penting: jangan berasumsi CDI aktif hanya karena `@Inject` compile.

---

## 16. Testcontainers PostgreSQL Repository Test

Gunakan Testcontainers untuk repository/SQL yang butuh PostgreSQL asli.

```java id="cm8ge3"
@Testcontainers
class OrderRepositoryPostgresIT {

    @Container
    static PostgreSQLContainer<?> postgres =
        new PostgreSQLContainer<>("postgres:16")
            .withDatabaseName("orderdb")
            .withUsername("order_app")
            .withPassword("order_password");

    private OrderRepositoryPostgres repository;

    @BeforeEach
    void setUp() {
        DataSource dataSource = DataSourceFactory.create(
            postgres.getJdbcUrl(),
            postgres.getUsername(),
            postgres.getPassword()
        );

        MigrationRunner.run(dataSource);

        repository = new OrderRepositoryPostgres(dataSource);
    }

    @Test
    void findByIdAndTenantMustNotReturnOtherTenantOrder() {
        repository.insert(order("order-1", "tenant-a", "user-a"));
        repository.insert(order("order-2", "tenant-b", "user-b"));

        Optional<Order> result =
            repository.findByIdAndTenant("order-2", "tenant-a");

        assertTrue(result.isEmpty());
    }
}
```

### DB integration targets

| Test | Membuktikan |
|---|---|
| `findByIdAndTenant` | Tenant isolation |
| Unique constraint | Duplicate maps to 409 |
| FK constraint | Referential integrity |
| Check constraint | DB invariant |
| Migration runs cleanly | Schema compatible |
| Transaction rollback | Consistency |
| PL/pgSQL function behavior | Server-side logic correct |

---

## 17. Full API + PostgreSQL Integration Test

```java id="kf5p2v"
@Testcontainers
class OrderApiPostgresIT extends JerseyTest {

    @Container
    static PostgreSQLContainer<?> postgres =
        new PostgreSQLContainer<>("postgres:16")
            .withDatabaseName("orderdb")
            .withUsername("order_app")
            .withPassword("order_password");

    @Override
    protected Application configure() {
        AppConfig config = TestAppConfigBuilder.valid()
            .dbUrl(postgres.getJdbcUrl())
            .dbUsername(postgres.getUsername())
            .dbPassword(SecretValue.of(postgres.getPassword()))
            .build();

        RuntimeResources resources =
            TestRuntimeResources.from(config);

        return new ResourceConfig()
            .register(new IntegrationAppBinder(resources))
            .register(OrderResource.class)
            .register(CorrelationIdFilter.class)
            .register(AuthenticationFilter.class)
            .register(AuthorizationFilter.class)
            .register(DomainExceptionMapper.class)
            .register(UnhandledExceptionMapper.class)
            .register(JsonProvider.class);
    }

    @BeforeEach
    void migrateAndSeed() {
        MigrationRunner.run(dataSourceFrom(postgres));
        TestDataSeeder.seedOrders(dataSourceFrom(postgres));
    }

    @Test
    void apiMustNotReturnOtherTenantOrder() {
        Response response = target("/orders/order-tenant-b")
            .request()
            .header(HttpHeaders.AUTHORIZATION, "Bearer tenant-a-user")
            .header("X-Tenant-Id", "tenant-a")
            .get();

        assertEquals(404, response.getStatus());
    }
}
```

### Kapan perlu full API + DB?

| Need | Full integration? |
|---|---:|
| Test HTTP status only | Tidak selalu |
| Test filter/mapper only | JerseyTest cukup |
| Test SQL tenant boundary from API | Ya |
| Test migration compatibility | Ya |
| Test transactional write path | Ya |
| Test actual Postgres function/procedure | Ya |

---

## 18. Bootstrap Invariant Test

Config production-like harus fail-fast.

```java id="dhp2xz"
class BootstrapInvariantTest {

    @Test
    void prodMustRejectDebugEndpoint() {
        AppConfig config = TestAppConfigBuilder.valid()
            .profile(RuntimeProfile.PROD)
            .featureFlags(new FeatureFlags(
                true,  // newOrderFlow
                true,  // diagnosticsEndpointEnabled
                true   // strictTenantValidation
            ))
            .build();

        IllegalStateException ex = assertThrows(
            IllegalStateException.class,
            () -> DeploymentInvariantValidator.validate(
                TestRuntimeResources.from(config)
            )
        );

        assertTrue(ex.getMessage().contains("diagnostics"));
    }

    @Test
    void prodMustRejectLocalhostDatabase() {
        AppConfig config = TestAppConfigBuilder.valid()
            .profile(RuntimeProfile.PROD)
            .dbUrl("jdbc:postgresql://localhost:5432/orderdb")
            .build();

        assertThrows(
            IllegalStateException.class,
            () -> config.validate()
        );
    }
}
```

---

## 19. Startup Smoke Test

```java id="qk16le"
class AppStartupJerseyTest extends JerseyTest {

    @Override
    protected Application configure() {
        return new AppResourceConfig(TestRuntimeResources.valid());
    }

    @Test
    void liveHealthMustReturnUp() {
        Response response = target("/health/live")
            .request()
            .get();

        assertEquals(200, response.getStatus());
    }

    @Test
    void protectedEndpointMustRejectAnonymous() {
        Response response = target("/orders/order-1")
            .request()
            .get();

        assertEquals(401, response.getStatus());
    }

    @Test
    void unknownEndpointMustReturnSafe404() {
        Response response = target("/missing")
            .request()
            .get();

        assertEquals(404, response.getStatus());
        assertFalse(response.readEntity(String.class).contains("Exception"));
    }
}
```

## 20. Parameterized Matrix Tests

JUnit Jupiter mendukung parameterized tests, yaitu satu method test bisa dieksekusi beberapa kali dengan argument berbeda; ini cocok untuk authorization, validation, feature flag, dan state transition matrix. ([JUnit User Guide](https://docs.junit.org/5.0.2/api/org/junit/jupiter/params/ParameterizedTest.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

```java id="wlsn2q"
@ParameterizedTest
@CsvSource({
    "VIEWER, order:read:own, true",
    "VIEWER, order:read:any, false",
    "APPROVER, order:approve:low-risk, true",
    "APPROVER, order:approve:high-risk, false",
    "SUPERVISOR, order:approve:high-risk, true"
})
void permissionMatrixMustBeStable(
    Role role,
    String permission,
    boolean expected
) {
    Set<String> permissions =
        RolePermissionRegistry.permissionsFor(Set.of(role));

    assertEquals(expected, permissions.contains(permission));
}
```

### Matrix yang wajib

| Matrix | Values |
|---|---|
| Authorization | role, permission, tenant, object owner, state |
| Feature flag | on/off/default/provider-down |
| Validation | missing/invalid/boundary values |
| Error mapper | exception type/status/code |
| Profile invariant | local/dev/test/staging/prod |
| Repository tenant | same tenant/different tenant |

---

## 21. Test Data Strategy

```text id="hyox1x"
Tenants:
  tenant-a
  tenant-b

Users:
  user-a-owner-tenant-a
  user-b-owner-tenant-a
  user-c-owner-tenant-b
  supervisor-tenant-a
  admin-tenant-a

Orders:
  order-a-owned-by-user-a
  order-b-owned-by-user-b-same-tenant
  order-c-owned-by-user-c-other-tenant
  order-closed
  order-pending-low-risk
  order-pending-high-risk
```

### Fixture builder

```java id="7sdt7k"
public final class TestFixtures {

    public static PrincipalContext ownerTenantA() {
        return new PrincipalContext(
            "user-a",
            Set.of("ORDER_USER"),
            Set.of("order:read:own"),
            Set.of("tenant-a")
        );
    }

    public static RequestContext requestTenantA() {
        return new RequestContext(
            "req-1",
            "corr-1",
            "tenant-a",
            ownerTenantA(),
            Instant.parse("2026-07-13T00:00:00Z")
        );
    }

    public static Order orderOwnedByUserBInTenantA() {
        return Order.rehydrate(
            new OrderId("order-b"),
            new TenantId("tenant-a"),
            new UserId("user-b"),
            OrderStatus.SUBMITTED
        );
    }
}
```

Rule: test data harus sengaja mencakup **same tenant different owner** dan **different tenant**. Itu dua case yang sering menangkap BOLA/tenant leak.

---

## 22. What to Mock, Fake, or Containerize

| Dependency | Unit Test | JerseyTest | Integration Test |
|---|---|---|---|
| Repository | Fake/mock | Fake or Testcontainers | Testcontainers |
| Token verifier | Fake | Fake | Fake or test IdP |
| Audit publisher | In-memory fake | In-memory fake | Outbox/Testcontainers if needed |
| HTTP downstream | Fake client | Stub/fake | WireMock/container if needed |
| DB | No | No or Testcontainers | Testcontainers |
| Config | Test builder | Test runtime resources | Test runtime resources |
| Clock | Fixed fake | Fixed fake | Fixed fake |
| Feature flags | Fake flag client | Fake flag client | Fake/dedicated test provider |

### Rule

```text id="270b0d"
Unit test isolates logic.
JerseyTest verifies HTTP pipeline.
Integration test verifies real external boundary.
```

---

## 23. CI Strategy

```text id="sba12i"
Pull Request CI:
  mvn test
    - unit
    - mapper
    - policy
    - CDI component
    - JerseyTest light

Merge / Nightly / Pre-deploy:
  mvn verify -Pintegration
    - Testcontainers PostgreSQL
    - migration
    - repository
    - API + DB integration

Deployment:
  smoke test against deployed endpoint
    - health
    - auth
    - error mapper
    - correlation
```

### GitHub Actions sketch

```yaml id="0pno5c"
name: test

on:
  pull_request:
    branches: [ main ]

jobs:
  unit-and-jersey:
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest

    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v4

      - uses: actions/setup-java@v4
        with:
          distribution: temurin
          java-version: '17'

      - name: Unit, CDI, and Jersey tests
        run: mvn test

  integration:
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    needs: unit-and-jersey

    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v4

      - uses: actions/setup-java@v4
        with:
          distribution: temurin
          java-version: '17'

      - name: Integration tests
        run: mvn verify -Pintegration
```

## 24. Deployment Smoke Script

```bash id="4lrpli"
#!/usr/bin/env bash
set -euo pipefail

BASE_URL="${BASE_URL:?BASE_URL is required}"
TOKEN="${SMOKE_TEST_TOKEN:-}"

curl -fsS "$BASE_URL/health/live" > /dev/null
curl -fsS "$BASE_URL/health/ready" > /dev/null

STATUS=$(curl -s -o /tmp/response.json -w "%{http_code}" \
  "$BASE_URL/orders/order-1")

test "$STATUS" = "401"

STATUS=$(curl -s -o /tmp/response.json -w "%{http_code}" \
  -H "Authorization: Bearer invalid-token" \
  "$BASE_URL/orders/order-1")

test "$STATUS" = "401"

if [ -n "$TOKEN" ]; then
  STATUS=$(curl -s -o /tmp/response.json -w "%{http_code}" \
    -H "Authorization: Bearer $TOKEN" \
    -H "X-Tenant-Id: tenant-a" \
    -H "X-Correlation-Id: smoke-$(date +%s)" \
    "$BASE_URL/internal/whoami")

  test "$STATUS" = "200"
fi

echo "Smoke test passed"
```

### Smoke test target

| Check | Expected |
|---|---|
| `/health/live` | 200 |
| `/health/ready` | 200 |
| Protected endpoint no token | 401 |
| Invalid token | 401 |
| Valid smoke endpoint | 200 |
| Error response | JSON, no stack trace |
| Correlation ID | Header returned |

## 25. Failure Modes

| Symptom | Root Cause | Test yang Menangkap |
|---|---|---|
| Unit tests pass, runtime DI fails | No Jersey/CDI integration test | JerseyTest / Weld test |
| Auth filter tidak jalan | Provider not registered | Auth JerseyTest |
| Exception mapper tidak aktif | Mapper not registered | Error mapper JerseyTest |
| CDI producer tidak ditemukan | Bean discovery/integration issue | Weld test |
| Qualifier ambiguous | Multiple beans same type | Weld qualifier test |
| Resource bisa akses cross-tenant object | Repo query missing tenant | API + DB integration |
| Migration rusak | Schema incompatible | Testcontainers migration test |
| Prod pakai debug endpoint | Missing invariant | Bootstrap invariant test |
| Config secret/log leak | Unsafe startup logger | Log/snapshot review test |
| Feature flag only tested on | Missing off/default/provider-down tests | Matrix tests |

---

| Area | Checklist |
|---|---|
| Unit tests | Policy, mapper, validator, command mapping covered |
| Weld tests | Producers, qualifiers, alternatives, interceptors covered |
| JerseyTest | Resource/filter/mapper/provider activation covered |
| Bootstrap tests | Config/profile/secret invariants covered |
| DB integration | PostgreSQL repository, migration, constraints covered |
| Security | 401/403/BOLA/tenant tests covered |
| Validation | DTO/resource param/malformed JSON tests covered |
| Error handling | Domain/unexpected/downstream mapper tests covered |
| Feature flags | On/off/default/failure tests covered |
| Smoke tests | Health/auth/error/correlation tested post-deploy |
| CI split | Fast tests on PR, integration on merge/pre-deploy |
| Test data | Same-tenant/different-owner and cross-tenant cases included |
