# Cheatsheet - Final Production Hardening & Anti-Pattern Review  
## Jersey/JAX-RS DI, Contexts, AOP, Profiles, Properties, Env, File Resources

## 1. Scope

Seri penutup ini merangkum **production hardening** untuk area yang sudah dibahas:

```text id="ilxfso"
Production Hardening Scope
│
├─ Bootstrap & ResourceConfig
├─ HK2 / CDI ownership
├─ Config, profile, env, secret
├─ Request context propagation
├─ AOP / filters / interceptors
├─ Security / authorization / BOLA prevention
├─ Error handling / validation
├─ DTO / mapper boundary
├─ Health checks / startup logs
├─ Testing / CI guardrails
└─ Anti-pattern review
```

Goal-nya: aplikasi Jersey/JAX-RS tidak hanya “jalan”, tetapi **deterministic, secure-by-default, observable, testable, dan siap production**.

---

## 2. Final Mental Model

```text id="uz48x8"
Production-ready Jersey/JAX-RS app =
    explicit bootstrap
  + clear DI ownership
  + immutable config
  + safe secret loading
  + request context discipline
  + explicit provider registration
  + object-level authorization
  + safe error contract
  + health/readiness correctness
  + automated regression tests
```

OWASP Authorization Cheat Sheet menekankan **least privilege**, **deny by default**, dan validasi permission pada setiap request. Untuk API, OWASP API Security 2023 juga menempatkan Broken Object Level Authorization sebagai risiko utama karena attacker bisa memanipulasi object ID di path, query, header, atau payload. ([OWASP Cheat Sheet Series](https://cheatsheetseries.owasp.org/cheatsheets/Authorization_Cheat_Sheet.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 3. Production Architecture Checklist

| Area | Production Standard |
|---|---|
| `ResourceConfig` | Explicit registration untuk resource, filter, mapper, provider, feature, binder |
| DI ownership | Setiap object punya owner jelas: HK2, CDI, manual, atau runtime |
| Config | Typed, immutable, fail-fast, loaded once |
| Secret | Tidak di Git/JAR/Dockerfile/log; injected via env/file/secret manager |
| Profile | `LOCAL/DEV/TEST/STAGING/PROD` jelas dan tervalidasi |
| Request context | Immutable, explicit, tidak disimpan di singleton |
| Security | Authentication, authorization, tenant isolation, object-level checks |
| AOP | JAX-RS filter untuk HTTP boundary; CDI interceptor untuk business method |
| Error handling | Stable error code, safe message, correlation ID |
| Validation | DTO/resource validation terpisah dari business validation |
| DTO mapping | Request/response DTO tidak bocor domain/persistence |
| Health | Liveness ringan, readiness mengecek dependency critical |
| Tests | Unit, Weld, JerseyTest, DB integration, smoke test |
| CI/CD | Dependency, namespace, bootstrap, security regression checks |

Jakarta REST mendefinisikan provider seperti filters, entity interceptors, message body providers, dan exception mappers sebagai extension point runtime JAX-RS; karena itu provider critical sebaiknya diregister dan dites secara eksplisit. ([Jakarta EE](https://jakarta.ee/specifications/restful-ws/4.0/jakarta-restful-ws-spec-4.0.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 4. Golden Rules

```text id="kgjbaq"
1. Explicit over implicit.
2. Fail fast over silent fallback.
3. Typed config over stringly config.
4. Immutable context over ThreadLocal magic.
5. Service policy over resource-only authorization.
6. Safe error response over raw exception.
7. Test runtime wiring, not only business logic.
8. Secret never enters artifact or log.
9. Profile guards must block unsafe production behavior.
10. Every object ID access requires authorization.
```

---

## 5. Bootstrap Hardening

### Target bootstrap flow

```text id="rnlqir"
AppResourceConfig
  ↓
RuntimeBootstrap.load()
  ↓
Config + Secret + Policy load
  ↓
DeploymentInvariantValidator
  ↓
DI binder / CDI producer
  ↓
Resource/provider/filter/mapper registration
  ↓
Health/startup ready
```

### Hardened `ResourceConfig`

```java id="ueykt1"
public final class AppResourceConfig extends ResourceConfig {

    public AppResourceConfig() {
        RuntimeResources resources = RuntimeBootstrap.loadAndValidate();

        register(new AppBinder(resources));

        registerResources();
        registerSecurityProviders();
        registerObservabilityProviders();
        registerErrorMappers();
        registerSerializationProviders();
        registerHealthResources();

        property("app.name", resources.appConfig().appName());
        property("app.profile", resources.appConfig().profile().name());
    }

    private void registerResources() {
        register(OrderResource.class);
        register(CustomerResource.class);
        register(HealthResource.class);
    }

    private void registerSecurityProviders() {
        register(CorrelationIdFilter.class);
        register(AuthenticationFilter.class);
        register(AuthorizationFilter.class);
        register(SecurityHeadersFilter.class);
    }

    private void registerObservabilityProviders() {
        register(AccessLogFilter.class);
    }

    private void registerErrorMappers() {
        register(ValidationExceptionMapper.class);
        register(AuthenticationExceptionMapper.class);
        register(AuthorizationExceptionMapper.class);
        register(DomainExceptionMapper.class);
        register(UnhandledExceptionMapper.class);
    }

    private void registerSerializationProviders() {
        register(JsonProvider.class);
    }

    private void registerHealthResources() {
        register(HealthResource.class);
    }
}
```

### Bootstrap anti-patterns

| Anti-Pattern | Risk | Fix |
|---|---|---|
| `packages("com.company")` untuk semua | Critical provider bisa miss diam-diam | Explicit register critical providers |
| Config lazy-loaded di service | Error baru muncul saat request | Load + validate saat startup |
| Secret hardcoded di default properties | Secret ikut artifact | Secret source runtime |
| Production fallback ke local | Prod bisa pakai config dev | Production invariant |
| Mapper/filter hanya mengandalkan scanning | Error/security behavior tidak deterministic | Register explicit |

---

## 6. DI Ownership Hardening

```text id="y80v12"
Setiap class harus punya satu owner:

Resource / Filter / Mapper
  → Jersey/HK2 boundary

Service / Repository / Policy
  → CDI or HK2, choose one

Config / DataSource / Client
  → producer/factory/bootstrap

RequestContext
  → request-bound or explicit parameter

DTO / Command / Domain Value
  → manually created plain objects
```

### Ownership checklist

| Question | Good Answer |
|---|---|
| Siapa membuat object ini? | HK2/CDI/factory/manual jelas |
| Scope object ini apa? | Singleton/request/dependent/per-lookup jelas |
| Apakah object punya mutable request state? | Tidak jika singleton |
| Apakah object butuh cleanup? | Ada disposer/factory dispose |
| Apakah object dibuat dengan `new` tapi berharap DI? | Tidak |
| Apakah service bisa dites tanpa container? | Ya |

### DI anti-patterns

| Anti-Pattern | Why Bad |
|---|---|
| Service dibuat manual `new` di resource | DI, lifecycle, interceptor bypass |
| Service inject `ContainerRequestContext` | Service terikat HTTP/JAX-RS |
| HK2 dan CDI sama-sama mengelola class yang sama | Duplicate ownership |
| Singleton menyimpan `currentUser` / `tenantId` | Cross-request leak |
| Producer/factory memuat config berkali-kali | Inconsistent runtime behavior |
| Alternative test masuk production classpath | Fake dependency bisa aktif di prod |

---

## 7. Config, Profile, Secret Hardening

### Recommended config precedence

```text id="04z91t"
1. Explicit runtime override
2. Environment variable
3. JVM system property
4. External mounted file
5. Classpath default
6. Hardcoded safe default only for non-critical values
```

Twelve-Factor App menganjurkan config berada di environment agar terpisah dari code dan dapat berubah antar deploy tanpa rebuild artifact; untuk Kubernetes, ConfigMap cocok untuk non-secret config dan Secret untuk sensitive value. ([OWASP Cheat Sheet Series](https://cheatsheetseries.owasp.org/cheatsheets/Authorization_Cheat_Sheet.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

### Production invariant example

```java id="tws6a4"
public final class DeploymentInvariantValidator {

    public static void validate(AppConfig config) {
        if (!config.profile().isProductionLike()) {
            return;
        }

        require(config.security().authenticationRequired(),
            "Authentication must be enabled");

        require(config.security().strictTenantValidationEnabled(),
            "Strict tenant validation must be enabled");

        require(!config.featureFlags().diagnosticsEndpointEnabled(),
            "Diagnostics endpoint must be disabled");

        require(!config.database().url().contains("localhost"),
            "Production DB must not use localhost");

        require(!config.database().password().isDefaultOrWeak(),
            "Production DB password must not be default/weak");
    }

    private static void require(boolean condition, String message) {
        if (!condition) {
            throw new IllegalStateException(message);
        }
    }
}
```

### Secret hardening checklist

| Rule | Status |
|---|---|
| Secret tidak masuk Git | Wajib |
| Secret tidak masuk Dockerfile `ENV` | Wajib |
| Secret tidak masuk classpath resource | Wajib |
| Secret tidak muncul di startup log | Wajib |
| Secret dibungkus `SecretValue` yang masked | Wajib |
| Secret bisa dirotate tanpa rebuild artifact | Wajib |
| Secret source per environment jelas | Wajib |

---

## 8. Request Context Hardening

### Correct model

```text id="suzd40"
Inbound request
  ↓
Correlation filter creates correlationId/requestId
  ↓
Authentication filter creates principal
  ↓
Tenant validation creates trusted tenant context
  ↓
Immutable RequestContext passed to service
  ↓
Audit/log/error use same correlationId
  ↓
MDC cleared in finally/response filter
```

### Immutable context

```java id="w5qkaa"
public record RequestContext(
    String requestId,
    String correlationId,
    String tenantId,
    PrincipalContext principal,
    Instant startedAt
) {}
```

### Context anti-patterns

| Anti-Pattern | Risk |
|---|---|
| `static RequestContext current` | Cross-request leak |
| `ThreadLocal` tanpa cleanup | Thread pool leak |
| MDC tanpa `clear()` | Log request A muncul di request B |
| RequestContext disimpan di singleton service | User/tenant leak |
| Raw HTTP context dipakai di repository | Layering rusak |
| Async task pakai request-scoped bean | Context inactive / stale |

### Safe MDC pattern

```java id="i9o8ec"
try {
    MDC.put("correlationId", context.correlationId());
    MDC.put("tenantId", context.tenantId());

    chain.proceed();
} finally {
    MDC.clear();
}
```

---

## 9. Security & Authorization Hardening

### Authorization execution order

```text id="b7ioh2"
Authenticate actor
  ↓
Validate tenant
  ↓
Load object using tenant boundary
  ↓
Evaluate permission / ownership / ACL / state policy
  ↓
Audit allow or deny
  ↓
Execute action
```

### Correct object-level access

```java id="0zmucr"
public OrderDto getOrder(String orderId, RequestContext context) {
    Order order = repository.findByIdAndTenant(orderId, context.tenantId())
        .orElseThrow(() -> new OrderNotFoundException(orderId));

    authorizationPolicy.requireCanRead(
        context.principal(),
        order
    );

    auditPublisher.publish(AuditEvent.readAllowed(order, context));

    return mapper.toDto(order, context);
}
```

### Dangerous pattern

```java id="di4208"
// BAD
public OrderDto getOrder(String orderId) {
    Order order = repository.findById(orderId).orElseThrow();
    return mapper.toDto(order);
}
```

OWASP API1:2023 menjelaskan bahwa BOLA sering terjadi ketika attacker mengubah object ID dalam request; karena itu setiap endpoint yang menerima object ID harus punya object-level authorization check, bukan sekadar autentikasi. ([OWASP Foundation](https://owasp.org/API-Security/editions/2023/en/0xa1-broken-object-level-authorization/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

### Security checklist

| Checklist | Status |
|---|---|
| Semua endpoint object-ID punya BOLA test | Wajib |
| Semua repository query tenant-sensitive pakai tenant boundary | Wajib |
| Service-level authorization ada, bukan hanya filter | Wajib |
| Field-level sensitive response dites | Wajib |
| PATCH/update field whitelist | Wajib |
| Audit deny untuk authz/tenant/state violation | Wajib |
| 401/403/404 semantics terdokumentasi | Wajib |
| Feature flag tidak mengganti permission | Wajib |

---

## 10. AOP / Provider Hardening

### Use correct extension point

| Concern | Correct Place |
|---|---|
| Correlation ID | `ContainerRequestFilter` + response filter |
| Authentication | Request filter |
| Endpoint authorization | Filter / `DynamicFeature` / service policy |
| Business audit | CDI interceptor or explicit service call |
| Transaction boundary | Service/use-case boundary |
| Error mapping | `ExceptionMapper` |
| Body size/transformation | Reader/writer interceptor |
| Security headers | Response filter |
| JSON config | Message body provider / context resolver |

### Provider ordering rule

```text id="8vdcz3"
Correlation before authentication.
Authentication before authorization.
Authorization before resource method.
ExceptionMapper handles errors.
Security headers and correlation ID added on response.
MDC cleanup always happens.
```

Jakarta REST specifies providers, filters, entity interceptors, and provider chains as runtime extension points; production code should not rely on accidental scanning or undocumented ordering for critical security behavior. ([Jakarta EE](https://jakarta.ee/specifications/restful-ws/4.0/jakarta-restful-ws-spec-4.0.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 11. Error Handling Hardening

### Error response contract

```json id="fnw4dw"
{
  "type": "https://errors.company.com/order/not-found",
  "title": "Order not found",
  "status": 404,
  "code": "ORDER_NOT_FOUND",
  "detail": "Order not found",
  "correlationId": "corr-123",
  "timestamp": "2026-07-13T10:00:00Z"
}
```

### Error hardening checklist

| Rule | Status |
|---|---|
| Stable `code` exists | Wajib |
| `correlationId` in body/header/log | Wajib |
| No stack trace in response | Wajib |
| No raw exception message for 5xx/security errors | Wajib |
| Validation errors include field errors | Wajib |
| Catch-all mapper registered | Wajib |
| Downstream timeout/unavailable mapped correctly | Wajib |
| Security errors do not reveal internal detail | Wajib |

### Dangerous errors

| Unsafe | Safe |
|---|---|
| `SQLException: relation users_password...` | `Unexpected error` |
| `JWT kid abc invalid` | `Invalid authentication token` |
| `Tenant tenant-b not assigned to user-a` | `Access denied` |
| Stack trace in response | Correlation ID |
| Raw downstream host/IP | `Required dependency unavailable` |

---

## 12. Validation & DTO Boundary Hardening

### Correct flow

```text id="495dg0"
HTTP JSON
  ↓
Request DTO + Jakarta Validation
  ↓
Command object + RequestContext
  ↓
Service business validation
  ↓
Domain operation
  ↓
Response DTO
```

### DTO hardening rules

| Rule | Reason |
|---|---|
| Request DTO separate from domain | API contract independent |
| Response DTO separate from persistence | Avoid internal leakage |
| `@Valid` on nested DTO | Nested validation active |
| Business validation in service/domain | Needs state/context |
| MapStruct strict unmapped target policy | New field not silently ignored |
| PATCH uses whitelist DTO | Prevent mass assignment |
| Field-level authorization before response mapping | Prevent sensitive data leak |

### Mass assignment anti-pattern

```java id="4d8lr9"
// BAD
objectMapper.updateValue(orderEntity, rawRequestMap);
```

Better:

```java id="dl2omk"
public record UpdateOrderRequest(
    @Size(max = 500)
    String note,
    List<UpdateOrderLineRequest> lines
) {}
```

---

## 13. Health & Readiness Hardening

### Probe semantics

| Probe | Meaning | Should Check |
|---|---|---|
| Liveness | Process should be restarted if unhealthy | Minimal app/JVM alive |
| Readiness | Pod can receive traffic | DB, migration, critical dependency |
| Startup | App still booting | Startup complete flag |

Kubernetes documents liveness, readiness, and startup probes separately; liveness can trigger restarts, while readiness controls whether traffic is sent to a pod. ([Kubernetes](https://kubernetes.io/docs/tasks/configure-pod-container/configure-liveness-readiness-startup-probes/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

### Health anti-patterns

| Anti-Pattern | Risk | Fix |
|---|---|---|
| Liveness checks DB | DB outage restarts all pods | Put DB in readiness |
| Readiness always 200 | Traffic sent before ready | Check critical dependencies |
| Startup probe missing for slow app | Pod killed during startup | Add startup probe |
| Health endpoint requires auth from kubelet | Probe fails | Separate internal probe route/network policy |
| Health leaks config/secret | Info disclosure | Minimal health response |

---

## 14. Observability Hardening

### Required correlation path

```text id="qr9kvo"
Request header X-Correlation-Id
  ↓
RequestContext.correlationId
  ↓
MDC/log fields
  ↓
Error response body/header
  ↓
Audit event
  ↓
Outbound HTTP/Kafka headers
```

### Startup safe summary

```text id="y3a7m3"
app=order-service
version=1.12.4
profile=PROD
java=17
config.source=env+mounted-file
db.url=jdbc:postgresql://postgres-prod:5432/orderdb
db.username=order_app
db.password=****
auth.enabled=true
audit.enabled=true
registered.filters=CorrelationIdFilter,AuthenticationFilter,AuthorizationFilter
registered.mappers=DomainExceptionMapper,UnhandledExceptionMapper
```

### Do not log

```text id="7xzy1l"
Authorization bearer token
Cookie/session
DB password
JWT private key
Full request body with PII
Raw secret file content
```

---

## 15. Test & CI Hardening

### Minimum test suite

| Test Type | Must Cover |
|---|---|
| Unit | policy, mapper, validator, command mapping |
| Weld/CDI | producer, qualifier, alternative, interceptor |
| JerseyTest | resource, filter, mapper, provider, validation |
| DB integration | tenant boundary, constraints, migration |
| Security matrix | BOLA, tenant isolation, permission/state |
| Feature flag matrix | on/off/default/provider-down |
| Bootstrap invariant | prod/staging safety |
| Smoke test | health, auth, error, correlation |

### CI gates

```text id="vaojve"
No merge/deploy if:
- dependency convergence fails
- javax/jakarta namespace regression appears
- bootstrap tests fail
- security tests fail
- BOLA/tenant tests fail
- error mapper tests fail
- validation tests fail
- integration tests fail
- deployment smoke tests fail
```

### Useful checks

```bash id="c4na29"
mvn dependency:tree \
  -Dincludes=org.glassfish.jersey,org.glassfish.hk2,jakarta.ws.rs,jakarta.inject,jakarta.enterprise
```

```bash id="mjwjo4"
grep -R "import javax\." -n src/main/java src/test/java
```

```bash id="f48l63"
grep -R "new .*Service\|new .*Repository\|ThreadLocal\|MDC.put" -n src/main/java
```

---

## 16. Final Anti-Pattern Review

| Anti-Pattern | Production Risk | Better Pattern |
|---|---|---|
| Resource contains business logic | Hard to test, authz bypass | Thin resource + service |
| Repository called directly from resource | Tenant/policy bypass | Service owns use case |
| `findById(id)` for tenant object | BOLA/cross-tenant leak | `findByIdAndTenant(id, tenant)` |
| Feature flag replaces permission | Unauthorized access | Flag + authorization |
| `ThreadLocal` request context | Async/thread leak | Immutable context parameter |
| Singleton stores user/tenant | Cross-request leakage | Stateless singleton |
| Raw exception to client | Info disclosure | Safe mapper |
| Secret in properties/JAR | Secret exposure | Runtime secret source |
| Config fallback to local in prod | Misdeployment | Production invariant |
| Package scanning for auth filter | Missing security provider | Explicit registration |
| DTO = domain = DB entity | Contract/internal leakage | Separate DTO/domain/persistence |
| Mapper contains business policy | Hidden logic | Service/policy layer |
| Liveness checks DB | Restart storm | DB in readiness |
| Unit tests only | Runtime wiring untested | JerseyTest/Weld/integration |
| No field-level auth tests | Sensitive response leak | Mapper/policy tests |
| No audit deny path | Weak forensic evidence | Audit allow/deny decisions |

---

## 17. Production Readiness Scorecard

| Category | Pass Criteria |
|---|---|
| Bootstrap | App fails fast on invalid prod config |
| DI | Object ownership documented and tested |
| Config | Typed config, no secret leak, source precedence known |
| Security | Authn/authz/tenant/BOLA tests pass |
| Context | Correlation/tenant/principal propagated safely |
| Error | Stable safe error body with correlation ID |
| Validation | DTO + business + DB validation layered |
| DTO | Domain/persistence not exposed |
| Observability | Logs/metrics/audit have correlation ID |
| Health | Liveness/readiness/startup correctly separated |
| Tests | Unit + CDI + Jersey + DB + smoke covered |
| CI/CD | Security and bootstrap regressions block merge/deploy |

---

## 18. Final Hardening Checklist

| Checklist | Status |
|---|---|
| Critical providers explicitly registered | Wajib |
| HK2/CDI ownership clear | Wajib |
| No accidental `javax.*` in Jakarta stack | Wajib |

## 19. Practical Review Questions untuk Codebase Baru

Gunakan ini saat onboarding ke repository perusahaan:

```text id="qpr0hc"
1. Di mana AppResourceConfig/Application didefinisikan?
2. Provider critical apa saja yang explicit registered?
3. Apakah HK2, CDI, atau campuran?
4. Apakah ada object yang dibuat manual tapi butuh DI?
5. Bagaimana AppConfig dimuat dan divalidasi?
6. Di mana secret berasal?
7. Apakah prod bisa fallback ke local config?
8. Bagaimana RequestContext dibuat dan dibersihkan?
9. Apakah service menerima RequestContext eksplisit?
10. Apakah repository query tenant-aware?
11. Apakah semua endpoint object-ID punya authz check?
12. Apakah error response aman dan konsisten?
13. Apakah DTO terpisah dari domain/persistence?
14. Apakah PATCH terlindungi dari mass assignment?
15. Apakah health readiness mengecek DB/migration?
16. Apakah CI menjalankan JerseyTest dan integration test?
17. Apakah ada smoke test setelah deployment?
18. Apakah feature flag punya owner, expiry, dan test on/off?
```
